  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/three@0.128.0/build/three.min.js"></script>
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/three@0.128.0/examples/js/controls/OrbitControls.js"></script>
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/three@0.128.0/examples/js/loaders/GLTFLoader.js"></script>
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/fflate@0.6.9/umd/index.js"></script>
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/three@0.128.0/examples/js/curves/NURBSCurve.js"></script>
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/three@0.128.0/examples/js/curves/NURBSUtils.js"></script>
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/three@0.128.0/examples/js/loaders/FBXLoader.js"></script>

  <script>
  // ── Auto-tuner config injection (URL hash) ──
  // Parse URL hash config: index.html#{"KBAND_LO":0.75,...}
  try {
    if (location.hash.length > 1) {
      window.CAGE_CONFIG = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(location.hash.slice(1)));
      console.log('[CONFIG] Loaded from URL:', Object.keys(window.CAGE_CONFIG).length, 'overrides');
    }
  } catch(e) { console.warn('[CONFIG] Failed to parse URL hash:', e); }
  window.CAGE_CONFIG = window.CAGE_CONFIG || {};
  const CFG = (key, fallback) => (window.CAGE_CONFIG[key] !== undefined) ? window.CAGE_CONFIG[key] : fallback;
  // Dump active config for tuner verification
  if (Object.keys(window.CAGE_CONFIG).length > 0) {
    console.log('CFG_DUMP: ' + JSON.stringify(window.CAGE_CONFIG));
  }

  // TODO Option B: upgrade blend from linear transform blend to Dual Quaternion Skinning (DQS)
  // using same region transforms.

  // ---------------- UI helpers ----------------
  const $ = (id)=>document.getElementById(id);
  function bindRange(id, outId, fmt=(v)=>v){
    const el=$(id), out=$(outId);
    const set=()=>out.textContent=fmt(el.value);
    el.addEventListener('input', set); set();
  }
  bindRange('speed','speedVal', v=>(+v).toFixed(2));
  bindRange('alpha','alphaVal', v=>(+v).toFixed(2));
  bindRange('plant','plantVal', v=>(+v).toFixed(2));
  bindRange('stanceKnee','stanceKneeVal', v=>String(v));
  bindRange('ankleOffL','ankleOffLVal', v=>String(v));
  bindRange('ankleOffR','ankleOffRVal', v=>String(v));
  bindRange('hipBob','hipBobVal', v=>(+v).toFixed(2));
  bindRange('hipSway','hipSwayVal', v=>(+v).toFixed(1));
  bindRange('forwardLean','forwardLeanVal', v=>String(v));
  bindRange('spineTwist','spineTwistVal', v=>(+v).toFixed(1));
  bindRange('shoulderX','shoulderXVal', v=>(+v).toFixed(2));
  bindRange('tposeAngle','tposeVal', v=>String(v));
  bindRange('armSwing','armSwingVal', v=>(+v).toFixed(1));
  bindRange('elbowBend','elbowBendVal', v=>(+v).toFixed(1));
  bindRange('upperArmLen','upperArmLenVal', v=>(+v).toFixed(2));
  bindRange('forearmLen','forearmLenVal', v=>(+v).toFixed(2));
  bindRange('pelvisHeight','pelvisHeightVal', v=>(+v).toFixed(3));
  bindRange('thighLen','thighLenVal', v=>(+v).toFixed(3));
  bindRange('shinLen','shinLenVal', v=>(+v).toFixed(3));
  const statusEl = $('status');

  // ---------------- Version marker ----------------
  console.log('%c[CAGE v3 — quaternion debug build]', 'color: lime; font-weight: bold; font-size: 14px');

  // ---------------- Scene ----------------
  const scene = new THREE.Scene();
  scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x0f1220);

  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(50, innerWidth/innerHeight, 0.01, 200);
  const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({antialias:true});
  renderer.setSize(innerWidth, innerHeight);
  document.body.appendChild(renderer.domElement);

  const controls = new THREE.OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);
  controls.enableDamping = true;

  scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.75));
  const dir = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.9);
  dir.position.set(2,3,2);
  scene.add(dir);

  // Axes helper: R=X, G=Y, B=Z (0.3m long, at origin)
  const axes = new THREE.AxesHelper(0.3);
  scene.add(axes);

  // Debug pivot spheres (shoulder=red, elbow=green, wrist=blue) × 2 sides
  const pivotSphereGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.018, 8, 8);
  const pivotSpheres = {};
  const pivotColors = {
    lShoulder: 0xff0000, rShoulder: 0xff4444,
    lElbow: 0x00ff00, rElbow: 0x44ff44,
    lWrist: 0x0000ff, rWrist: 0x4444ff
  };
  for (const [name, color] of Object.entries(pivotColors)) {
    const mat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color, depthTest: false });
    const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(pivotSphereGeo, mat);
    mesh.visible = false;
    mesh.renderOrder = 999;
    scene.add(mesh);
    pivotSpheres[name] = mesh;
  }

  // FBX joint debug spheres — show where FBX thinks each joint is (mapped to cage space)
  const fbxJointSphereGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.012, 8, 8);
  const fbxJointSpheres = {};
  const fbxJointNames = ['pelvis','l_hip','l_knee','l_ankle','r_hip','r_knee','r_ankle',
    'l_shoulder','l_elbow','l_wrist','r_shoulder','r_elbow','r_wrist','neck','head','chest'];
  for (const name of fbxJointNames) {
    const mat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xffff00, depthTest: false });
    const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(fbxJointSphereGeo, mat);
    mesh.visible = false;
    mesh.renderOrder = 999;
    scene.add(mesh);
    fbxJointSpheres[name] = mesh;
  }

  window.addEventListener('resize', ()=>{
    camera.aspect = innerWidth/innerHeight;
    camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
    renderer.setSize(innerWidth, innerHeight);
  });

  // ---------------- Data ----------------
  let meshObj, meshGeo, meshRestPos;
  let cageRest, cageFaces, cagePos;
  let bindIdx, bindW, K;
  let regionId;
  let cageMesh;
  let meshHeight = 1;
  let cageSmoothed = null;
  let meshVertRegions = null;   // precomputed per-mesh-vertex region weight arrays
  let meshVertEmaAlpha = null;  // precomputed per-vertex EMA smoothing alpha (Float32Array)
  let stretchConstraints = null; // PBD stretch clamping data for crotch edges
  let crossBoundaryEdges = null; // edges crossing l_thigh/r_thigh boundary (for strand collapse)
  let meshAdjacency = null;      // full mesh edge list + per-vert neighbors (for strain + Delta Mush)
  let deltaMushData = null;      // precomputed Delta Mush rest-pose data
  let meshRestNormals = null;    // precomputed per-vertex rest-pose normals (Float32Array, 3 per vert)
  let retargetedJoints = {};     // world-space joint positions from driveCageFromMixamo (for bone comparison)
  let retargetedDirs = {};       // retargeted bone directions per frame (for direction comparison)
  let fbxFrameDirs = {};         // raw FBX bone directions per frame (the reference)
  let retargetedAngles = {};     // per-frame joint angles from our retargeting (degrees)
  let fbxFrameAngles = {};       // per-frame joint angles from FBX (degrees)
  let retargetedBoneLens = {};   // per-frame bone lengths from retargeting (mm)
  let fbxFrameBoneLens = {};     // per-frame bone lengths from FBX (mm)
  let retargetedRelPos = {};     // per-frame pelvis-relative positions (height-normalized)
  let fbxFrameRelPos = {};       // per-frame pelvis-relative positions (height-normalized)
  let fbxFrameRelPosMM = {};     // per-frame pelvis-relative positions (raw mm)
  let retargetedTwists = {};     // cage-side twist angles (radians)
  let fbxFrameTwists = {};       // FBX-side twist angles (radians)
  let fbxFrameJointPos = {};     // per-frame FBX hips-local joint positions (for shape fidelity)
  // Skeleton overlay visualization
  const SKELETON_BONES = [
    ['pelvis','l_hip'], ['pelvis','r_hip'],
    ['pelvis','spine_joint'], ['spine_joint','spine1_joint'],
    ['spine1_joint','spine2_joint'], ['spine2_joint','neck'],
    ['pelvis','chest'], // backward compat (chest = spine1_joint alias)
    ['l_hip','l_knee'], ['l_knee','l_ankle'], ['l_ankle','l_toe'],
    ['r_hip','r_knee'], ['r_knee','r_ankle'], ['r_ankle','r_toe'],
    ['neck','head'],
    ['spine2_joint','l_collar'], ['l_collar','l_shoulder'],
    ['spine2_joint','r_collar'], ['r_collar','r_shoulder'],
    ['l_shoulder','l_elbow'], ['l_elbow','l_wrist'], ['l_wrist','l_mid_knuckle'],
    ['r_shoulder','r_elbow'], ['r_elbow','r_wrist'], ['r_wrist','r_mid_knuckle']
  ];
  let skeletonLine = null;
  let skeletonJointSpheres = [];
  var pelvisDelta = [0, 0, 0];   // pelvis translation delta (module scope for bone comparison)

  // ── Adaptive arm twist damping + shoulder blend ──
  let _armTwistScale = {l: 1.0, r: 1.0};  // per-frame: reduced when arm swing is large
  let _armTorsoFade = [1.0, 1.0];          // per-frame: shoulder blend fade [L, R]

  // ── Vertex-level temporal damping for jitter reduction ──
  let prevMeshPos = null;  // previous frame's mesh positions for temporal damping
  let _jitterVel = null;   // per-vertex velocity buffer for adaptive jitter detection
  // _groundCorrection removed — cage-level correction in driveCageFromMixamo handles ground plane

  // ── Multi-frame diagnostic accumulator ──
  let _mfDiag = null;  // initialized after FBX load
  let regionIndices = {};       // region ID -> cage vert index array
  let allLeftArmCage = [];      // combined l_upperarm + l_forearm cage indices (baked)
  let allRightArmCage = [];     // combined r_upperarm + r_forearm cage indices (baked)
  let armState = null;          // current arm rotation state (set by driveCage, used by deformMesh)
  let legState = null;          // current leg rotation state for analytical leg transforms
  let headState = null;         // current head rotation state for analytical head transform
  let lastGroundCorrection = 0; // ground compensation Y offset, applied to analytical pivots
  let fbxSkinData = null;       // {positions: Float32Array, weights: [{rid,w},...], nVerts} from FBX
  let fbxLegPropRatio = 1.0;    // cage leg length / FBX leg length (in cage-scaled space)
  let fbxArmPropRatio = 1.0;    // cage arm length / FBX arm length (in cage-scaled space)
  let fbxShoulderSpreadRatio = 1.0; // FBX shoulder spread / cage shoulder spread
  let fbxHipSpreadRatio = 1.0;      // FBX hip spread / cage hip spread
  // Temporal coherence: previous frame bend axes (prevents popping near straight limbs)
  // Temporal coherence for joints that always bend the same way (elbows, knees)
  const prevBendAxis = {
    lArm: null, rArm: null,
    lKnee: null, rKnee: null,       // analytical section (cage space)
    lKneeV: null, rKneeV: null,     // vert-level driving (FBX space) — MUST be separate!
    lKneeTw: null, rKneeTw: null     // thigh twist bend axis (FBX→cage space)
  };
  // Temporal direction smoothing for shin/foot + ankle bend angle
  // Applied BEFORE position computation so bone lengths are preserved and chain stays coherent
  let _prevShinDirL = null, _prevShinDirR = null;
  let _prevFootDirL = null, _prevFootDirR = null;
  const SHIN_SMOOTH_ALPHA = CFG('SHIN_SMOOTH', 0.92);   // 0=keep previous, 1=use current fully (high = responsive, direct FBX)
  const FOOT_SMOOTH_ALPHA = CFG('FOOT_SMOOTH', 0.65);   // more smoothing on foot (end of chain, diagnostics only)
  // Shared smoothed shin directions: vert-level → analytical path
  // Ensures cage vert positions and mesh transforms use the SAME shin direction
  let _smoothedShinDirL = null, _smoothedShinDirR = null;
  // Ankle bend temporal smoothing (prevents wobble / flopping)
  let _prevAnkleBendAxisL = null, _prevAnkleBendAxisR = null;
  const ANKLE_AXIS_SMOOTH = 0.85;   // 0=keep previous, 1=use current fully (high = responsive)
  let _prevAnkleAngleL = 0, _prevAnkleAngleR = 0;
  let _prevAnkleAxisCageL = null, _prevAnkleAxisCageR = null;
  const ANKLE_ANGLE_SMOOTH = CFG('ANKLE_ANGLE_SMOOTH_VAL', 0.80);  // responsive ankle tracking (direct FBX rotations)
  // Cage-derived shoulder anchors and arm directions (set by resplitArmRegions)
  let cageShoulderL = null, cageShoulderR = null;
  let cageArmDirL = null, cageArmDirR = null;
  // Pooled leg cage vert indices (set once on load, used by resplitLegRegions)
  let allLeftLegCage = [], allRightLegCage = [], allPelvisCage = [];

  // JSON + motion
  let anchors=null, bounds=null;
  let motionData=null, armMotionData=null, spineMotionData=null;
  let armBaseline = null; // first-frame values for computing deltas
  let cycleDuration=1.0;
  let jointPosData = null; // joint_positions.json data (position-based driving)
  let fbxLiveGroup = null;  // FBX scene group (kept alive for live skinned playback)
  let fbxLiveMixer = null;  // AnimationMixer for FBX live playback
  let fbxLiveAction = null; // AnimationAction for FBX live playback
  const ENABLE_CAGE_SMOOTHING = true;   // smooth cage positions before Kabsch: reduces jitter
  const CAGE_SMOOTHING = CFG('CAGE_SMOOTHING_VAL', 0.25);  // 25% EMA: balanced — higher values add lag that worsens jitter

  const R = {}; // region arrays
  const P = {}; // pivots

  // Override P joint positions from FBX skeleton rest pose.
  // discovered_anchors gives surface vertex positions, but FBX rest_pose
  // gives actual internal joint centers from Mixamo's auto-rigging.
  // Call after P is initially set from anchors, whenever jointPosData becomes available.
  function overridePFromFBX(rebuild) {
    if (!jointPosData || !jointPosData.rest_pose || !P.pelvis) return false;
    const rp = jointPosData.rest_pose;
    const anchor = P.pelvis; // mesh-space pelvis as coordinate anchor
    const overridden = [];
    const doOverride = (name) => {
      if (rp[name]) {
        const old = P[name] ? P[name].slice() : null;
        P[name] = [
          rp[name][0] + anchor[0],
          rp[name][1] + anchor[1],
          rp[name][2] + anchor[2]
        ];
        if (old) {
          const d = Math.sqrt((P[name][0]-old[0])**2 + (P[name][1]-old[1])**2 + (P[name][2]-old[2])**2);
          overridden.push(name + ' (' + (d*1000).toFixed(1) + 'mm)');
        }
      }
    };

    console.log('=== OVERRIDING P FROM FBX SKELETON ===');
    // Legs
    doOverride('l_hip'); doOverride('r_hip');
    doOverride('l_knee'); doOverride('r_knee');
    doOverride('l_ankle'); doOverride('r_ankle');
    doOverride('l_toe'); doOverride('r_toe');
    // Arms
    doOverride('l_shoulder'); doOverride('r_shoulder');
    doOverride('l_elbow'); doOverride('r_elbow');
    doOverride('l_wrist'); doOverride('r_wrist');
    // Head/spine
    doOverride('neck'); doOverride('head');
    doOverride('chest');
    // New spine bones
    doOverride('spine_joint'); doOverride('spine1_joint'); doOverride('spine2_joint');
    // Collar bones
    doOverride('l_collar'); doOverride('r_collar');

    // Derive spine sub-joint P positions from FBX proportions if FBX has them.
    // Maps FBX spine_joint/spine1_joint/spine2_joint proportionally onto cage's pelvis→neck distance.
    if (rp.spine_joint && rp.spine1_joint && rp.spine2_joint && P.pelvis && P.neck) {
      const fbxPelvisY = rp.pelvis ? rp.pelvis[1] : 0;
      const fbxNeckY = rp.neck ? rp.neck[1] : 1;
      const fbxSpan = fbxNeckY - fbxPelvisY;
      if (Math.abs(fbxSpan) > 0.001) {
        for (const jn of ['spine_joint', 'spine1_joint', 'spine2_joint']) {
          const fbxPos = rp[jn];
          const t = (fbxPos[1] - fbxPelvisY) / fbxSpan;
          P[jn] = [
            P.pelvis[0] + t * (P.neck[0] - P.pelvis[0]),
            P.pelvis[1] + t * (P.neck[1] - P.pelvis[1]),
            P.pelvis[2] + t * (P.neck[2] - P.pelvis[2])
          ];
          overridden.push(jn + ' (derived t=' + t.toFixed(3) + ')');
        }
      }
      // Derive collar P positions from FBX: fraction along spine2→shoulder
      if (rp.l_collar && P.spine2_joint && P.l_shoulder) {
        const fbxS2 = rp.spine2_joint, fbxLS = rp.l_shoulder, fbxLC = rp.l_collar;
        const fbxCollarLen = Math.sqrt((fbxLS[0]-fbxS2[0])**2+(fbxLS[1]-fbxS2[1])**2+(fbxLS[2]-fbxS2[2])**2);
        const fbxCollarProj = fbxCollarLen > 0.001 ?
          ((fbxLC[0]-fbxS2[0])*(fbxLS[0]-fbxS2[0])+(fbxLC[1]-fbxS2[1])*(fbxLS[1]-fbxS2[1])+(fbxLC[2]-fbxS2[2])*(fbxLS[2]-fbxS2[2])) / (fbxCollarLen*fbxCollarLen) : 0.5;
        const cf = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, fbxCollarProj));
        P.l_collar = [
          P.spine2_joint[0] + cf * (P.l_shoulder[0] - P.spine2_joint[0]),
          P.spine2_joint[1] + cf * (P.l_shoulder[1] - P.spine2_joint[1]),
          P.spine2_joint[2] + cf * (P.l_shoulder[2] - P.spine2_joint[2])
        ];
        overridden.push('l_collar (derived f=' + cf.toFixed(3) + ')');
      }
      if (rp.r_collar && P.spine2_joint && P.r_shoulder) {
        const fbxS2 = rp.spine2_joint, fbxRS = rp.r_shoulder, fbxRC = rp.r_collar;
        const fbxCollarLen = Math.sqrt((fbxRS[0]-fbxS2[0])**2+(fbxRS[1]-fbxS2[1])**2+(fbxRS[2]-fbxS2[2])**2);
        const fbxCollarProj = fbxCollarLen > 0.001 ?
          ((fbxRC[0]-fbxS2[0])*(fbxRS[0]-fbxS2[0])+(fbxRC[1]-fbxS2[1])*(fbxRS[1]-fbxS2[1])+(fbxRC[2]-fbxS2[2])*(fbxRS[2]-fbxS2[2])) / (fbxCollarLen*fbxCollarLen) : 0.5;
        const cf = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, fbxCollarProj));
        P.r_collar = [
          P.spine2_joint[0] + cf * (P.r_shoulder[0] - P.spine2_joint[0]),
          P.spine2_joint[1] + cf * (P.r_shoulder[1] - P.spine2_joint[1]),
          P.spine2_joint[2] + cf * (P.r_shoulder[2] - P.spine2_joint[2])
        ];
        overridden.push('r_collar (derived f=' + cf.toFixed(3) + ')');
      }
    }

    // Re-derive dependent joints
    if (P.l_wrist && P.l_elbow) {
      const d = vnorm(vsub(P.l_wrist, P.l_elbow));
      P.l_mid_knuckle = vadd(P.l_wrist, vscale(d, 0.04));
    }
    if (P.r_wrist && P.r_elbow) {
      const d = vnorm(vsub(P.r_wrist, P.r_elbow));
      P.r_mid_knuckle = vadd(P.r_wrist, vscale(d, 0.04));
    }

    // Update arm length sliders
    if (P.l_shoulder && P.l_elbow) {
      const ua = vlen(vsub(P.l_elbow, P.l_shoulder));
      $('upperArmLen').value = ua.toFixed(3);
      $('upperArmLenVal').textContent = ua.toFixed(3);
    }
    if (P.l_elbow && P.l_wrist) {
      const fa = vlen(vsub(P.l_wrist, P.l_elbow));
      $('forearmLen').value = fa.toFixed(3);
      $('forearmLenVal').textContent = fa.toFixed(3);
    }

    console.log('  Overridden: ' + overridden.join(', '));
    if (P.l_hip && P.l_knee) {
      console.log('  New thigh len: L=' + (vlen(vsub(P.l_knee, P.l_hip))*1000).toFixed(1) +
        'mm R=' + (vlen(vsub(P.r_knee, P.r_hip))*1000).toFixed(1) + 'mm');
    }
    if (P.l_knee && P.l_ankle) {
      console.log('  New shin len: L=' + (vlen(vsub(P.l_ankle, P.l_knee))*1000).toFixed(1) +
        'mm R=' + (vlen(vsub(P.r_ankle, P.r_knee))*1000).toFixed(1) + 'mm');
    }
    if (P.l_shoulder && P.l_elbow && P.l_wrist) {
      console.log('  New arm len: upper=' + (vlen(vsub(P.l_elbow, P.l_shoulder))*1000).toFixed(1) +
        'mm forearm=' + (vlen(vsub(P.l_wrist, P.l_elbow))*1000).toFixed(1) + 'mm');
    }
    console.log('=== END FBX SKELETON OVERRIDE ===');

    // Rebuild mesh region weights with new P (smoothstep boundaries use P)
    if (rebuild && meshRestPos && bindIdx && bindW && regionId) {
      console.log('Rebuilding mesh region weights with new P...');
      meshVertRegions = $('useFBXWeights').checked && fbxSkinData
        ? computeFBXTransferredWeights(meshRestPos)
        : $('boneEnvWeights').checked
          ? computeBoneEnvelopeWeights(meshRestPos)
          : precomputeMeshRegionWeights(meshRestPos, bindIdx, bindW, K, regionId);
      meshVertEmaAlpha = buildPerVertexEmaAlpha(meshVertRegions);
    }
    return true;
  }

  // Foot plant state (joint-space with hysteresis)
  let lockL=null, lockR=null;
  let footLockCountL = 0, footLockCountR = 0; // consecutive frame counters for hysteresis
  const FOOT_ENTER_FRAMES = CFG('FOOT_ENTER_FRAMES_VAL', 5);  // frames below enter threshold to lock
  const FOOT_EXIT_FRAMES = 5;   // frames above exit threshold to unlock

  // Hand plant state (joint-space with hysteresis)
  let handLockL = null, handLockR = null;
  let handLockCountL = 0, handLockCountR = 0;

  // ---------------- Utilities ----------------
  function clamp(x,a,b){ return Math.max(a, Math.min(b,x)); }

  // Smooth a cyclic array with a 3-point weighted average [0.25, 0.5, 0.25].
  // Wraps around at boundaries for seamless looping animations.
  // Multiple passes approximate a Gaussian filter.
  function smoothArray(arr, passes = 3) {
    const n = arr.length;
    let result = arr.slice();
    for (let p = 0; p < passes; p++) {
      const tmp = result.slice();
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        result[i] = 0.25 * tmp[(i-1+n)%n] + 0.5 * tmp[i] + 0.25 * tmp[(i+1)%n];
      }
    }
    return result;
  }

  function rotateAroundAxis(px, py, pz, ox, oy, oz, ax, ay, az, angle) {
    let x = px - ox, y = py - oy, z = pz - oz;
    const c = Math.cos(angle), s = Math.sin(angle), t = 1 - c;
    const len = Math.hypot(ax, ay, az) || 1;
    ax/=len; ay/=len; az/=len;

    const nx = (t*ax*ax + c)*x + (t*ax*ay - s*az)*y + (t*ax*az + s*ay)*z;
    const ny = (t*ax*ay + s*az)*x + (t*ay*ay + c)*y + (t*ay*az - s*ax)*z;
    const nz = (t*ax*az - s*ay)*x + (t*ay*az + s*ax)*y + (t*az*az + c)*z;
    return [nx + ox, ny + oy, nz + oz];
  }

  async function loadJSON(url) {
  const res = await fetch(url, { cache: "no-store" });
  if (!res.ok) {
    const msg = `loadJSON failed: ${url} (${res.status} ${res.statusText})`;
    throw new Error(msg);
  }
  try {
    return await res.json();
  } catch (e) {
    throw new Error(`loadJSON parse failed: ${url} (${e.message})`);
  }
}


  function indicesByRegion(r){
    const out=[];
    for(let i=0;i<regionId.length;i++) if(regionId[i]===r) out.push(i);
    return out;
  }

  // --- Vector helpers for arm split ---
  function vsub(a,b){ return [a[0]-b[0], a[1]-b[1], a[2]-b[2]]; }
  function vadd(a,b){ return [a[0]+b[0], a[1]+b[1], a[2]+b[2]]; }
  function vdot(a,b){ return a[0]*b[0] + a[1]*b[1] + a[2]*b[2]; }
  function vlen(a){ return Math.sqrt(vdot(a,a)); }
  function vscale(a,s){ return [a[0]*s, a[1]*s, a[2]*s]; }

  // Compute the rotation that maps fromDir→toDir, then apply it to applyToDir.
  // Used for animation retargeting: extracts the "delta rotation" from the FBX
  // animation and applies it to the cage rest direction, preserving cage proportions.
  function applyDeltaRotation(fromDir, toDir, applyToDir) {
    let ax = vcross(fromDir, toDir);
    const axLen = vlen(ax);
    const d = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, vdot(fromDir, toDir)));
    const ang = Math.acos(d);
    if (Math.abs(ang) < 1e-4) return applyToDir; // near-identical: no rotation
    if (axLen < 1e-6) {
      // Near-180° rotation: cross product degenerate, pick any perpendicular axis
      ax = Math.abs(fromDir[1]) < 0.9
        ? vcross(fromDir, [0, 1, 0])
        : vcross(fromDir, [1, 0, 0]);
      if (vlen(ax) < 1e-6) return applyToDir; // extreme degenerate, bail
    }
    ax = vnorm(ax);
    const r = rotateAroundAxis(applyToDir[0], applyToDir[1], applyToDir[2],
      0, 0, 0, ax[0], ax[1], ax[2], ang);
    return vnorm(r);
  }

  // Compute axis-angle rotation MATRIX from restDir to targetDir.
  // Returns a 3x3 rotation matrix (flat array). Used for analytical limb composition.
  function computeIKRotMatrix(restDir, targetDir) {
    let ax = vcross(restDir, targetDir);
    const axLen = vlen(ax);
    const d = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, vdot(restDir, targetDir)));
    const ang = Math.acos(d);
    if (Math.abs(ang) < 1e-4) return [1,0,0, 0,1,0, 0,0,1]; // identity
    if (axLen < 1e-6) {
      // Near-180° rotation: pick any perpendicular axis
      ax = Math.abs(restDir[1]) < 0.9
        ? vcross(restDir, [0, 1, 0])
        : vcross(restDir, [1, 0, 0]);
      if (vlen(ax) < 1e-6) return [1,0,0, 0,1,0, 0,0,1];
    }
    ax = vnorm(ax);
    return mat3_rotAxis(ax[0], ax[1], ax[2], ang);
  }

  function vnorm(a){
    const L = vlen(a);
    return (L > 1e-12) ? [a[0]/L, a[1]/L, a[2]/L] : [1,0,0];
  }

  // Find shoulder joint center as average midpoint of K closest arm↔torso cage pairs.
  // Returns { jointCenter, armAnchor, shoulderCi } where jointCenter is the driving pivot.
  function findShoulderAnchorFromTorso(armIndices, torsoIndices, cageRest, K_PAIRS){
    K_PAIRS = K_PAIRS || 5;
    // Collect all arm↔torso distances
    const pairs = [];
    for (const ai of armIndices) {
      const p = cageRest[ai];
      for (const ti of torsoIndices) {
        const q = cageRest[ti];
        const dx = p[0]-q[0], dy = p[1]-q[1], dz = p[2]-q[2];
        pairs.push({ ai, ti, d2: dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz });
      }
    }
    pairs.sort((a,b) => a.d2 - b.d2);

    // Average the midpoints of the K closest pairs
    const N = Math.min(K_PAIRS, pairs.length);
    let mx=0, my=0, mz=0, ax=0, ay=0, az=0;
    for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
      const ap = cageRest[pairs[i].ai], tp = cageRest[pairs[i].ti];
      mx += (ap[0]+tp[0])*0.5;  my += (ap[1]+tp[1])*0.5;  mz += (ap[2]+tp[2])*0.5;
      ax += ap[0]; ay += ap[1]; az += ap[2];
    }
    const jointCenter = [mx/N, my/N, mz/N];
    const armAnchor = [ax/N, ay/N, az/N];
    const shoulderCi = pairs[0] ? pairs[0].ai : (armIndices[0] ?? -1);
    return { shoulder: jointCenter, armAnchor, shoulderCi, d2: pairs[0]?.d2 || 0 };
  }

  // Farthest arm cage point from shoulder (to define arm direction).
  function farthestPointFrom(armIndices, origin, cageRest){
    let bestCi = armIndices[0] ?? -1;
    let bestD2 = -1;
    for (const ci of armIndices) {
      const p = cageRest[ci];
      const dx = p[0]-origin[0], dy = p[1]-origin[1], dz = p[2]-origin[2];
      const d2 = dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz;
      if (d2 > bestD2) { bestD2 = d2; bestCi = ci; }
    }
    return cageRest[bestCi] ? [...cageRest[bestCi]] : [origin[0]+1, origin[1], origin[2]];
  }

  // Split arm cage verts by projection along arm axis into upper/forearm/hand.
  function splitArmByProjection(allArmCage, shoulder, dir, upperArmLen, forearmLen, ridUpper, ridFore, ridHand, regionId, cageRest){
    const upper=[], fore=[], hand=[];
    for (const ci of allArmCage) {
      const p = cageRest[ci];
      const t = (p[0]-shoulder[0])*dir[0] + (p[1]-shoulder[1])*dir[1] + (p[2]-shoulder[2])*dir[2];
      if (t <= upperArmLen) { upper.push(ci); regionId[ci]=ridUpper; }
      else if (t <= upperArmLen + forearmLen) { fore.push(ci); regionId[ci]=ridFore; }
      else { hand.push(ci); regionId[ci]=ridHand; }
    }
    return { upper, fore, hand };
  }

  // Projection-based arm region split using cage-derived shoulder anchors.
  // Splits by projection along the arm axis (shoulder → farthest point),
  // not radial distance. This is stable and mirror-consistent.
  function resplitArmRegions() {
    if (!cageRest || !allLeftArmCage.length || !allRightArmCage.length) return;

    const upperArmLen = +$('upperArmLen').value;
    const forearmLen  = +$('forearmLen').value;

    // Torso cage indices (region 0)
    const torsoIdx = regionIndices[0] || indicesByRegion(0);
    if (!torsoIdx.length) return;

    // Left: shoulder anchor + arm axis
    const L = findShoulderAnchorFromTorso(allLeftArmCage, torsoIdx, cageRest);
    const lFar = farthestPointFrom(allLeftArmCage, L.shoulder, cageRest);
    let lDir = vnorm(vsub(lFar, L.shoulder));

    // Right: shoulder anchor + arm axis
    const Rr = findShoulderAnchorFromTorso(allRightArmCage, torsoIdx, cageRest);
    const rFar = farthestPointFrom(allRightArmCage, Rr.shoulder, cageRest);
    let rDir = vnorm(vsub(rFar, Rr.shoulder));

    // Enforce mirror-consistency: left dir x<0, right dir x>0
    if (lDir[0] > 0) lDir = vscale(lDir, -1);
    if (rDir[0] < 0) rDir = vscale(rDir, -1);

    // Store cage-derived anchors globally so arm driving uses the same pivots
    // Symmetrize L/R to eliminate cage mesh asymmetry artifacts
    {
      const avgShX = (Math.abs(L.shoulder[0]) + Math.abs(Rr.shoulder[0])) / 2;
      const avgShY = (L.shoulder[1] + Rr.shoulder[1]) / 2;
      const avgShZ = (L.shoulder[2] + Rr.shoulder[2]) / 2;
      cageShoulderL = [-avgShX, avgShY, avgShZ];
      cageShoulderR = [ avgShX, avgShY, avgShZ];

      const avgDX = (Math.abs(lDir[0]) + Math.abs(rDir[0])) / 2;
      const avgDY = (lDir[1] + rDir[1]) / 2;
      const avgDZ = (lDir[2] + rDir[2]) / 2;
      cageArmDirL = vnorm([-avgDX, avgDY, avgDZ]);
      cageArmDirR = vnorm([ avgDX, avgDY, avgDZ]);
    }

    // Split by projection along arm axis — use SYMMETRIZED shoulder anchors
    // so both arms get consistent region splits despite cage asymmetry
    const lSplit = splitArmByProjection(allLeftArmCage,  cageShoulderL, cageArmDirL, upperArmLen, forearmLen, 7, 9, 12, regionId, cageRest);
    const rSplit = splitArmByProjection(allRightArmCage, cageShoulderR, cageArmDirR, upperArmLen, forearmLen, 8, 10, 13, regionId, cageRest);

    R.l_upperarm = lSplit.upper;
    R.l_forearm  = lSplit.fore;
    R.l_hand     = lSplit.hand;

    R.r_upperarm = rSplit.upper;
    R.r_forearm  = rSplit.fore;
    R.r_hand     = rSplit.hand;

    regionIndices[7]  = R.l_upperarm;
    regionIndices[8]  = R.r_upperarm;
    regionIndices[9]  = R.l_forearm;
    regionIndices[10] = R.r_forearm;
    regionIndices[12] = R.l_hand;
    regionIndices[13] = R.r_hand;

    // Rebuild mesh bindings and region weights with updated regionId
    const built = buildBindKNNRegion(meshRestPos, cageRest, regionId, null, 8);
    K = built.K;
    bindIdx = built.idx;
    bindW = built.w;
    meshVertRegions = $('useFBXWeights').checked && fbxSkinData
      ? computeFBXTransferredWeights(meshRestPos)
      : $('boneEnvWeights').checked
        ? computeBoneEnvelopeWeights(meshRestPos)
        : precomputeMeshRegionWeights(meshRestPos, bindIdx, bindW, K, regionId);
    meshVertEmaAlpha = buildPerVertexEmaAlpha(meshVertRegions);

    console.log('ArmSplit L:', R.l_upperarm.length, R.l_forearm.length, R.l_hand.length,
      'jointCenter', L.shoulder.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)), 'armAnchor', L.armAnchor.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)), 'dir', lDir.map(v=>v.toFixed(3)));
    console.log('ArmSplit R:', R.r_upperarm.length, R.r_forearm.length, R.r_hand.length,
      'jointCenter', Rr.shoulder.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)), 'armAnchor', Rr.armAnchor.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)), 'dir', rDir.map(v=>v.toFixed(3)));
  }

  // Projection-based leg region split. Splits pooled pelvis+thigh+shin+foot
  // cage verts by projection along the leg axis (hip → ankle). Slider-controlled
  // boundaries: pelvisHeight (above hip), thighLen, shinLen. Everything past
  // thigh+shin is foot.
  function resplitLegRegions() {
    if (!cageRest || !P.l_hip || !P.r_hip) return;

    const pelvisRaw = +$('pelvisHeight').value; // 0=no pelvis, 0.5=default, 1=max
    const thighL  = +$('thighLen').value;
    const shinL   = +$('shinLen').value;

    // Map slider 0-1 to projection boundary: 0→-0.10 (no pelvis), 0.5→0 (at hip), 1→+0.10
    const pelvisBound = (pelvisRaw - 0.5) * 0.20;

    const lHip = P.l_hip, rHip = P.r_hip;
    const lAnkle = P.l_ankle, rAnkle = P.r_ankle;
    const lDir = vnorm(vsub(lAnkle, lHip));
    const rDir = vnorm(vsub(rAnkle, rHip));

    // Combined pool: all pelvis + left leg + right leg cage verts
    // Each vert gets assigned based on projection along nearest leg axis
    const lThigh = [], lShin = [], lFoot = [];
    const rThigh = [], rShin = [], rFoot = [];
    const pelvisVerts = [];

    // Helper: classify one cage vert along a leg axis
    function classifyLeg(ci, hip, dir, side) {
      const p = cageRest[ci];
      const t = (p[0]-hip[0])*dir[0] + (p[1]-hip[1])*dir[1] + (p[2]-hip[2])*dir[2];
      if (t < pelvisBound) {
        pelvisVerts.push(ci); regionId[ci] = 14;
      } else if (t <= thighL) {
        if (side === 'L') { lThigh.push(ci); regionId[ci] = 1; }
        else { rThigh.push(ci); regionId[ci] = 2; }
      } else if (t <= thighL + shinL) {
        if (side === 'L') { lShin.push(ci); regionId[ci] = 3; }
        else { rShin.push(ci); regionId[ci] = 4; }
      } else {
        if (side === 'L') { lFoot.push(ci); regionId[ci] = 5; }
        else { rFoot.push(ci); regionId[ci] = 6; }
      }
    }

    // Split left leg verts
    for (const ci of allLeftLegCage) classifyLeg(ci, lHip, lDir, 'L');
    // Split right leg verts
    for (const ci of allRightLegCage) classifyLeg(ci, rHip, rDir, 'R');

    // Pelvis pool verts (original pelvis + low torso): can become pelvis or
    // stay torso. Never become thigh/shin/foot (those are leg-pool only).
    const torsoVerts = [];
    for (const ci of allPelvisCage) {
      const p = cageRest[ci];
      const dL = Math.abs(p[0] - lHip[0]);
      const dR = Math.abs(p[0] - rHip[0]);
      const hip = dL <= dR ? lHip : rHip;
      const dir = dL <= dR ? lDir : rDir;
      const t = (p[0]-hip[0])*dir[0] + (p[1]-hip[1])*dir[1] + (p[2]-hip[2])*dir[2];
      if (t < pelvisBound) {
        pelvisVerts.push(ci); regionId[ci] = 14;
      } else {
        // Not pelvis → stays torso (region 0)
        torsoVerts.push(ci); regionId[ci] = 0;
      }
    }

    // Update region arrays
    R.l_thigh = lThigh; R.r_thigh = rThigh;
    R.l_shin = lShin;   R.r_shin = rShin;
    R.l_foot = lFoot;   R.r_foot = rFoot;
    R.pelvis = pelvisVerts;
    // Rebuild torso: original torso verts not in pool + pool verts that stayed torso
    R.torso = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < regionId.length; i++) {
      if (regionId[i] === 0) R.torso.push(i);
    }

    regionIndices[0]  = R.torso;
    regionIndices[1] = R.l_thigh;
    regionIndices[2] = R.r_thigh;
    regionIndices[3] = R.l_shin;
    regionIndices[4] = R.r_shin;
    regionIndices[5] = R.l_foot;
    regionIndices[6] = R.r_foot;
    if (R.pelvis.length >= 4) regionIndices[14] = R.pelvis;
    else delete regionIndices[14];

    // Rebuild mesh bindings with updated regionId
    const built = buildBindKNNRegion(meshRestPos, cageRest, regionId, null, 8);
    K = built.K;
    bindIdx = built.idx;
    bindW = built.w;
    meshVertRegions = $('useFBXWeights').checked && fbxSkinData
      ? computeFBXTransferredWeights(meshRestPos)
      : $('boneEnvWeights').checked
        ? computeBoneEnvelopeWeights(meshRestPos)
        : precomputeMeshRegionWeights(meshRestPos, bindIdx, bindW, K, regionId);
    meshVertEmaAlpha = buildPerVertexEmaAlpha(meshVertRegions);

    // Reset one-shot diagnostics so they re-fire
    window._innerThighDiagDone = false;

    console.log('LegSplit: pelvis=' + pelvisVerts.length +
      ' L thigh=' + lThigh.length + ' shin=' + lShin.length + ' foot=' + lFoot.length +
      ' R thigh=' + rThigh.length + ' shin=' + rShin.length + ' foot=' + rFoot.length +
      ' | pelvisBound=' + pelvisBound.toFixed(3));
  }

  // Rotate a direction vector around an axis (no origin offset)
  function rotVec3(vx, vy, vz, ax, ay, az, angle) {
    return rotateAroundAxis(vx, vy, vz, 0, 0, 0, ax, ay, az, angle);
  }

  // Apply rotation around an ARBITRARY axis to cage vertices
  function applyRotAxis(indices, pivot, ax, ay, az, ang) {
    const [px,py,pz] = pivot;
    for (const i of indices) {
      let x=cagePos[i*3], y=cagePos[i*3+1], z=cagePos[i*3+2];
      [x,y,z] = rotateAroundAxis(x,y,z, px,py,pz, ax,ay,az, ang);
      cagePos[i*3]=x; cagePos[i*3+1]=y; cagePos[i*3+2]=z;
    }
  }

  // Gradient rotation for joint-proximal cage verts (e.g. thigh near hip).
  // Verts at the pivot end (frac≈0) get MIN_FRAC of the angle.
  // Verts at the far end (frac≈1) get full angle.
  // This prevents inner-thigh cage verts from swinging as hard as knee-level verts,
  // reducing the crotch gap artifact.
  function applyRotAxisGradient(indices, pivot, ax, ay, az, ang, boneDir, boneLen) {
    const [px, py, pz] = pivot;
    const [dx, dy, dz] = boneDir;
    const MIN_FRAC = 0.1; // hip-proximal verts get 10% of rotation
    for (const i of indices) {
      // Project vert offset onto bone direction to get frac along bone
      const vx = cagePos[i*3] - px;
      const vy = cagePos[i*3+1] - py;
      const vz = cagePos[i*3+2] - pz;
      const proj = vx*dx + vy*dy + vz*dz;
      let frac = proj / boneLen;
      frac = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, frac));
      // Linear gradient: MIN_FRAC at hip, 1.0 at knee
      const gradFrac = MIN_FRAC + (1.0 - MIN_FRAC) * frac;
      const vertAng = ang * gradFrac;
      if (Math.abs(vertAng) > 1e-6) {
        let x = cagePos[i*3], y = cagePos[i*3+1], z = cagePos[i*3+2];
        [x, y, z] = rotateAroundAxis(x, y, z, px, py, pz, ax, ay, az, vertAng);
        cagePos[i*3] = x; cagePos[i*3+1] = y; cagePos[i*3+2] = z;
      }
    }
  }

  function applyRotX(indices, pivot, ang){
    const [px,py,pz]=pivot;
    for(const i of indices){
      let x=cagePos[i*3], y=cagePos[i*3+1], z=cagePos[i*3+2];
      [x,y,z] = rotateAroundAxis(x,y,z, px,py,pz, 1,0,0, ang);
      cagePos[i*3]=x; cagePos[i*3+1]=y; cagePos[i*3+2]=z;
    }
  }
  function applyRotY(indices, pivot, ang){
    const [px,py,pz]=pivot;
    for(const i of indices){
      let x=cagePos[i*3], y=cagePos[i*3+1], z=cagePos[i*3+2];
      [x,y,z] = rotateAroundAxis(x,y,z, px,py,pz, 0,1,0, ang);
      cagePos[i*3]=x; cagePos[i*3+1]=y; cagePos[i*3+2]=z;
    }
  }
  function applyRotZ(indices, pivot, ang){
    const [px,py,pz]=pivot;
    for(const i of indices){
      let x=cagePos[i*3], y=cagePos[i*3+1], z=cagePos[i*3+2];
      [x,y,z] = rotateAroundAxis(x,y,z, px,py,pz, 0,0,1, ang);
      cagePos[i*3]=x; cagePos[i*3+1]=y; cagePos[i*3+2]=z;
    }
  }
  function translate(indices, dx,dy,dz){
    for(const i of indices){
      cagePos[i*3]+=dx; cagePos[i*3+1]+=dy; cagePos[i*3+2]+=dz;
    }
  }
  function regionCenter(indices){
    let sx=0,sy=0,sz=0;
    const n=Math.max(1, indices.length);
    for(const i of indices){
      sx+=cagePos[i*3]; sy+=cagePos[i*3+1]; sz+=cagePos[i*3+2];
    }
    return [sx/n, sy/n, sz/n];
  }

  // ---------------- Point-to-segment distance ----------------
  function pointToSegmentDist(px, py, pz, A, B) {
    const abx = B[0]-A[0], aby = B[1]-A[1], abz = B[2]-A[2];
    const apx = px-A[0], apy = py-A[1], apz = pz-A[2];
    const ab2 = abx*abx + aby*aby + abz*abz;
    if (ab2 < 1e-12) return Math.sqrt(apx*apx + apy*apy + apz*apz);
    const t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (apx*abx + apy*aby + apz*abz) / ab2));
    const cx = A[0] + t*abx - px, cy = A[1] + t*aby - py, cz = A[2] + t*abz - pz;
    return Math.sqrt(cx*cx + cy*cy + cz*cz);
  }

  // ---------------- Quaternion Math ([x, y, z, w] format) ----------------
  function quat_mul(a, b) {
    return [
      a[3]*b[0] + a[0]*b[3] + a[1]*b[2] - a[2]*b[1],
      a[3]*b[1] - a[0]*b[2] + a[1]*b[3] + a[2]*b[0],
      a[3]*b[2] + a[0]*b[1] - a[1]*b[0] + a[2]*b[3],
      a[3]*b[3] - a[0]*b[0] - a[1]*b[1] - a[2]*b[2]
    ];
  }
  function quat_conjugate(q) { return [-q[0], -q[1], -q[2], q[3]]; }

  function quat_slerp(a, b, t) {
    // Shortest-path: flip b if dot < 0
    let dot = a[0]*b[0] + a[1]*b[1] + a[2]*b[2] + a[3]*b[3];
    const b2 = (dot < 0) ? [-b[0], -b[1], -b[2], -b[3]] : b;
    dot = Math.abs(dot);
    if (dot > 0.9995) {
      // Nearly identical — lerp + normalize
      const r = [
        a[0] + (b2[0] - a[0]) * t,
        a[1] + (b2[1] - a[1]) * t,
        a[2] + (b2[2] - a[2]) * t,
        a[3] + (b2[3] - a[3]) * t
      ];
      const inv = 1 / Math.hypot(r[0], r[1], r[2], r[3]);
      return [r[0]*inv, r[1]*inv, r[2]*inv, r[3]*inv];
    }
    const theta = Math.acos(dot);
    const sinTheta = Math.sin(theta);
    const wa = Math.sin((1 - t) * theta) / sinTheta;
    const wb = Math.sin(t * theta) / sinTheta;
    return [
      wa*a[0] + wb*b2[0], wa*a[1] + wb*b2[1],
      wa*a[2] + wb*b2[2], wa*a[3] + wb*b2[3]
    ];
  }

  // Swing-twist decomposition: given a delta quaternion and a twist axis,
  // extract the signed twist angle (rotation around that axis).
  function swingTwistDecompose(qDelta, twistAxis) {
    // Project quaternion imaginary part onto twist axis
    const proj = qDelta[0]*twistAxis[0] + qDelta[1]*twistAxis[1] + qDelta[2]*twistAxis[2];
    // Twist quaternion = normalize([proj*ax, proj*ay, proj*az, w])
    let tx = proj * twistAxis[0], ty = proj * twistAxis[1], tz = proj * twistAxis[2];
    const tw = qDelta[3];
    const len = Math.hypot(tx, ty, tz, tw);
    if (len < 1e-10) return 0;
    tx /= len; ty /= len; tz /= len;
    const twn = tw / len;
    // Signed angle: 2 * atan2(|imaginary|, w), with sign from projection direction
    const sinHalf = Math.hypot(tx, ty, tz);
    let angle = 2 * Math.atan2(sinHalf, Math.abs(twn));
    // Sign: if proj is negative, twist is in opposite direction
    if (proj < 0) angle = -angle;
    // Handle w < 0 (angle > 180°)
    if (twn < 0) angle = (angle > 0) ? angle - 2*Math.PI : angle + 2*Math.PI;
    return angle;
  }

  // Convenience: extract twist angle of a bone between rest and current quaternions
  // along the rest-pose bone direction.
  // Uses POSITION-SWING RESIDUAL method: removes the shortest-arc rotation that
  // accounts for the direction change (what positions already capture), leaving
  // only the bone-axis twist. This is CHARACTER-INDEPENDENT — different bind poses
  // (T-pose, A-pose, etc.) produce the same twist angle for the same animation.
  function extractQuatTwist(restQ, curQ, boneDir) {
    let qDelta = quat_mul(curQ, quat_conjugate(restQ));
    // Ensure shortest-path quaternion (avoid >180° wraparound like -349°)
    if (qDelta[3] < 0) qDelta = [-qDelta[0], -qDelta[1], -qDelta[2], -qDelta[3]];
    // Position-swing residual: remove positional swing, keep only twist
    const d_cur = quat_rotate_vec(qDelta, boneDir);
    const q_swing = shortest_arc_quat(boneDir, d_cur);
    const q_residual = quat_mul(quat_conjugate(q_swing), qDelta);
    return swingTwistDecompose(q_residual, boneDir);
  }

  // Rotate a 3D vector by a unit quaternion: q * [v,0] * conj(q)
  function quat_rotate_vec(q, v) {
    const qx = q[0], qy = q[1], qz = q[2], qw = q[3];
    const vx = v[0], vy = v[1], vz = v[2];
    const cx = qy*vz - qz*vy, cy = qz*vx - qx*vz, cz = qx*vy - qy*vx;
    const c2x = qy*cz - qz*cy, c2y = qz*cx - qx*cz, c2z = qx*cy - qy*cx;
    return [vx + 2*(qw*cx + c2x), vy + 2*(qw*cy + c2y), vz + 2*(qw*cz + c2z)];
  }

  // Shortest-arc unit quaternion rotating unit vector a to unit vector b.
  // Used by position-swing residual to compute the "positional" swing.
  function shortest_arc_quat(a, b) {
    const d = a[0]*b[0] + a[1]*b[1] + a[2]*b[2];
    if (d > 0.999999) return [0, 0, 0, 1]; // Same direction
    if (d < -0.999999) {
      // Opposite — find any perpendicular axis
      let perp = vcross(a, [1, 0, 0]);
      if (vlen(perp) < 1e-6) perp = vcross(a, [0, 1, 0]);
      perp = vnorm(perp);
      return [perp[0], perp[1], perp[2], 0]; // 180° rotation
    }
    const c = vcross(a, b);
    const w = 1 + d;
    const inv = 1 / Math.hypot(c[0], c[1], c[2], w);
    return [c[0]*inv, c[1]*inv, c[2]*inv, w*inv];
  }

  // Extract LOCAL twist of a bone relative to its parent.
  // Unlike getJointTwistAtTime (world twist), this removes the parent chain's
  // contribution — isolating only the bone's own axial rotation.
  // Used for forearm pronation/supination (our analytical composition already
  // propagates upper arm twist, so we must not double-count it).
  function getLocalJointTwistAtTime(boneName, parentName, childName, t) {
    const boneQ = getJointQuaternionAtTime(boneName, t);
    const parentQ = getJointQuaternionAtTime(parentName, t);
    if (!boneQ || !parentQ) return 0;
    const restBoneQ = jointPosData.rest_quats[boneName];
    const restParentQ = jointPosData.rest_quats[parentName];
    if (!restBoneQ || !restParentQ) return 0;
    // Local quaternion = conj(parent_world) * bone_world
    const localCurr = quat_mul(quat_conjugate(parentQ), boneQ);
    const localRest = quat_mul(quat_conjugate(restParentQ), restBoneQ);
    // Local delta (ensure shortest-path to avoid >180° wraparound)
    let localDelta = quat_mul(localCurr, quat_conjugate(localRest));
    if (localDelta[3] < 0) localDelta = [-localDelta[0], -localDelta[1], -localDelta[2], -localDelta[3]];
    // Bone direction in parent's rest local frame
    const rp = jointPosData.rest_pose;
    if (!rp[boneName] || !rp[childName]) return 0;
    const worldBoneDir = vnorm(vsub(rp[childName], rp[boneName]));
    const localBoneDir = quat_rotate_vec(quat_conjugate(restParentQ), worldBoneDir);
    // Position-swing residual (character-independent)
    const d_cur_local = quat_rotate_vec(localDelta, localBoneDir);
    const q_swing = shortest_arc_quat(localBoneDir, d_cur_local);
    const q_residual = quat_mul(quat_conjugate(q_swing), localDelta);
    return swingTwistDecompose(q_residual, localBoneDir);
  }

  // ---------------- 3x3 Matrix Math (row-major Float64Array[9]) ----------------
  function mat3_create() { return new Float64Array(9); }
  function mat3_identity() { const m = mat3_create(); m[0]=m[4]=m[8]=1; return m; }

  function mat3_rotX(angle) {
    const c = Math.cos(angle), s = Math.sin(angle), m = mat3_create();
    m[0]=1; m[4]=c; m[5]=-s; m[7]=s; m[8]=c;
    return m;
  }

  function mat3_rotY(angle) {
    const c = Math.cos(angle), s = Math.sin(angle), m = mat3_create();
    m[0]=c; m[2]=s; m[4]=1; m[6]=-s; m[8]=c;
    return m;
  }

  function mat3_rotZ(angle) {
    const c = Math.cos(angle), s = Math.sin(angle), m = mat3_create();
    m[0]=c; m[1]=-s; m[3]=s; m[4]=c; m[8]=1;
    return m;
  }

  // Rotation matrix around an arbitrary unit axis (Rodrigues)
  function mat3_rotAxis(ax, ay, az, angle) {
    const c = Math.cos(angle), s = Math.sin(angle), t = 1-c;
    const len = Math.hypot(ax,ay,az) || 1;
    ax/=len; ay/=len; az/=len;
    return [
      t*ax*ax+c,    t*ax*ay-s*az, t*ax*az+s*ay,
      t*ax*ay+s*az, t*ay*ay+c,    t*ay*az-s*ax,
      t*ax*az-s*ay, t*ay*az+s*ax, t*az*az+c
    ];
  }

  function mat3_mul(A, B) {
    const C = mat3_create();
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++)
      for (let j = 0; j < 3; j++)
        C[i*3+j] = A[i*3]*B[j] + A[i*3+1]*B[3+j] + A[i*3+2]*B[6+j];
    return C;
  }

  function mat3_transpose(A) {
    const T = mat3_create();
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++)
      for (let j = 0; j < 3; j++)
        T[i*3+j] = A[j*3+i];
    return T;
  }

  function mat3_det(A) {
    return A[0]*(A[4]*A[8]-A[5]*A[7])
         - A[1]*(A[3]*A[8]-A[5]*A[6])
         + A[2]*(A[3]*A[7]-A[4]*A[6]);
  }

  function mat3_mulVec3(M, x, y, z) {
    return [
      M[0]*x + M[1]*y + M[2]*z,
      M[3]*x + M[4]*y + M[5]*z,
      M[6]*x + M[7]*y + M[8]*z
    ];
  }

  // Jacobi eigendecomposition for 3x3 symmetric matrix
  // Returns {eigenvalues: [3], V: Float64Array(9)} where columns of V are eigenvectors
  function symmetricEigen3x3(S) {
    const A = Float64Array.from(S);
    const V = mat3_identity();

    for (let iter = 0; iter < 30; iter++) {
      // Find largest off-diagonal element
      let maxVal = 0, p = 0, q = 1;
      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++)
        for (let j = i+1; j < 3; j++)
          if (Math.abs(A[i*3+j]) > maxVal) { maxVal = Math.abs(A[i*3+j]); p = i; q = j; }

      if (maxVal < 1e-12) break;

      // Compute Jacobi rotation angle
      const app = A[p*3+p], aqq = A[q*3+q], apq = A[p*3+q];
      const theta = (Math.abs(app - aqq) < 1e-14)
        ? Math.PI / 4
        : 0.5 * Math.atan2(2 * apq, app - aqq);
      const c = Math.cos(theta), s = Math.sin(theta);

      // Right multiply: B = A * G(p,q,theta)
      const B = Float64Array.from(A);
      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        B[i*3+p] =  c*A[i*3+p] + s*A[i*3+q];
        B[i*3+q] = -s*A[i*3+p] + c*A[i*3+q];
      }
      // Left multiply: A' = G^T * B
      for (let j = 0; j < 3; j++) {
        A[p*3+j] =  c*B[p*3+j] + s*B[q*3+j];
        A[q*3+j] = -s*B[p*3+j] + c*B[q*3+j];
      }

      // Accumulate eigenvectors: V = V * G
      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        const vip = V[i*3+p], viq = V[i*3+q];
        V[i*3+p] =  c*vip + s*viq;
        V[i*3+q] = -s*vip + c*viq;
      }
    }

    return { eigenvalues: [A[0], A[4], A[8]], V };
  }

  // SVD for 3x3: H = U * diag(S) * V^T
  function svd3x3(H) {
    const Ht = mat3_transpose(H);
    const HtH = mat3_mul(Ht, H);

    const { eigenvalues, V } = symmetricEigen3x3(HtH);
    const sigma = eigenvalues.map(e => Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, e)));

    // U = H * V * diag(1/sigma)
    const HV = mat3_mul(H, V);
    const U = mat3_create();
    for (let j = 0; j < 3; j++) {
      const invS = sigma[j] > 1e-8 ? 1 / sigma[j] : 0;
      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        U[i*3+j] = HV[i*3+j] * invS;
      }
    }

    return { U, S: sigma, V };
  }

  // Gram-Schmidt orthonormalize a 3x3 matrix (row-major) to ensure it's a proper rotation.
  // Returns a new Float64Array(9) with orthonormal rows forming a right-handed frame.
  function mat3_orthonormalize(M) {
    // Extract columns
    let c0 = [M[0], M[3], M[6]];
    let c1 = [M[1], M[4], M[7]];
    let c2 = [M[2], M[5], M[8]];

    // Normalize column 0
    let len = Math.hypot(c0[0], c0[1], c0[2]);
    if (len < 1e-12) { c0 = [1, 0, 0]; len = 1; }
    c0 = [c0[0]/len, c0[1]/len, c0[2]/len];

    // Column 1: subtract projection onto c0, normalize
    let dot = c1[0]*c0[0] + c1[1]*c0[1] + c1[2]*c0[2];
    c1 = [c1[0] - dot*c0[0], c1[1] - dot*c0[1], c1[2] - dot*c0[2]];
    len = Math.hypot(c1[0], c1[1], c1[2]);
    if (len < 1e-12) {
      // c1 was parallel to c0, pick an arbitrary perpendicular
      c1 = Math.abs(c0[0]) < 0.9 ? [1,0,0] : [0,1,0];
      dot = c1[0]*c0[0] + c1[1]*c0[1] + c1[2]*c0[2];
      c1 = [c1[0] - dot*c0[0], c1[1] - dot*c0[1], c1[2] - dot*c0[2]];
      len = Math.hypot(c1[0], c1[1], c1[2]);
    }
    c1 = [c1[0]/len, c1[1]/len, c1[2]/len];

    // Column 2: cross product of c0 and c1 (right-handed)
    c2 = [
      c0[1]*c1[2] - c0[2]*c1[1],
      c0[2]*c1[0] - c0[0]*c1[2],
      c0[0]*c1[1] - c0[1]*c1[0]
    ];

    const R = new Float64Array(9);
    R[0]=c0[0]; R[1]=c1[0]; R[2]=c2[0];
    R[3]=c0[1]; R[4]=c1[1]; R[5]=c2[1];
    R[6]=c0[2]; R[7]=c1[2]; R[8]=c2[2];
    return R;
  }

  // ---------------- Quaternion helpers for rotation blending ----------------
  // q = [x, y, z, w] (Hamilton convention)

  function mat3_to_quat(M) {
    // Convert 3x3 row-major rotation matrix to unit quaternion
    const m00=M[0], m01=M[1], m02=M[2];
    const m10=M[3], m11=M[4], m12=M[5];
    const m20=M[6], m21=M[7], m22=M[8];
    const tr = m00 + m11 + m22;
    let x, y, z, w;
    if (tr > 0) {
      const s = 0.5 / Math.sqrt(tr + 1.0);
      w = 0.25 / s;
      x = (m21 - m12) * s;
      y = (m02 - m20) * s;
      z = (m10 - m01) * s;
    } else if (m00 > m11 && m00 > m22) {
      const s = 2.0 * Math.sqrt(1.0 + m00 - m11 - m22);
      w = (m21 - m12) / s;
      x = 0.25 * s;
      y = (m01 + m10) / s;
      z = (m02 + m20) / s;
    } else if (m11 > m22) {
      const s = 2.0 * Math.sqrt(1.0 + m11 - m00 - m22);
      w = (m02 - m20) / s;
      x = (m01 + m10) / s;
      y = 0.25 * s;
      z = (m12 + m21) / s;
    } else {
      const s = 2.0 * Math.sqrt(1.0 + m22 - m00 - m11);
      w = (m10 - m01) / s;
      x = (m02 + m20) / s;
      y = (m12 + m21) / s;
      z = 0.25 * s;
    }
    const len = Math.hypot(x, y, z, w) || 1;
    return [x/len, y/len, z/len, w/len];
  }

  function quat_to_mat3(q) {
    // Convert unit quaternion [x,y,z,w] to 3x3 row-major rotation matrix
    const x = q[0], y = q[1], z = q[2], w = q[3];
    const x2 = x+x, y2 = y+y, z2 = z+z;
    const xx = x*x2, xy = x*y2, xz = x*z2;
    const yy = y*y2, yz = y*z2, zz = z*z2;
    const wx = w*x2, wy = w*y2, wz = w*z2;
    return new Float64Array([
      1-(yy+zz), xy-wz,     xz+wy,
      xy+wz,     1-(xx+zz), yz-wx,
      xz-wy,     yz+wx,     1-(xx+yy)
    ]);
  }

  // ---------------- Dual Quaternion Skinning (DQS) ----------------
  // Encodes rigid transform (R, t) as dual quaternion [qr(4), qd(4)].
  // Blends rotation + translation together — no volume loss at joints.

  function rigid_to_dq(q, t) {
    // q = rotation quaternion [x,y,z,w], t = translation [tx,ty,tz]
    // dual part = 0.5 * (tx,ty,tz,0) * q_real (quaternion multiply)
    const qx=q[0], qy=q[1], qz=q[2], qw=q[3];
    const tx=t[0], ty=t[1], tz=t[2];
    return {
      qr: [qx, qy, qz, qw],
      qd: [
        0.5*( tx*qw + ty*qz - tz*qy),
        0.5*(-tx*qz + ty*qw + tz*qx),
        0.5*( tx*qy - ty*qx + tz*qw),
        0.5*(-tx*qx - ty*qy - tz*qz)
      ]
    };
  }

  function dq_apply(qr, qd, vx, vy, vz) {
    // Apply normalized dual quaternion to vertex.
    // Rotation: q * v * conj(q)
    const ax=qr[0], ay=qr[1], az=qr[2], aw=qr[3];
    const tx2 = 2*(ay*vz - az*vy);
    const ty2 = 2*(az*vx - ax*vz);
    const tz2 = 2*(ax*vy - ay*vx);
    const rx = vx + aw*tx2 + (ay*tz2 - az*ty2);
    const ry = vy + aw*ty2 + (az*tx2 - ax*tz2);
    const rz = vz + aw*tz2 + (ax*ty2 - ay*tx2);
    // Translation: t = 2 * (qr.w * qd.xyz - qd.w * qr.xyz + cross(qr.xyz, qd.xyz))
    const ttx = 2*( qd[0]*aw - qd[3]*ax + qd[2]*ay - qd[1]*az);
    const tty = 2*( qd[1]*aw - qd[3]*ay + qd[0]*az - qd[2]*ax);
    const ttz = 2*( qd[2]*aw - qd[3]*az + qd[1]*ax - qd[0]*ay);
    return [rx + ttx, ry + tty, rz + ttz];
  }

  function quat_rotateVec3(q, vx, vy, vz) {
    // Rotate vector by quaternion: q * v * q^-1
    const qx=q[0], qy=q[1], qz=q[2], qw=q[3];
    // t = 2 * cross(q.xyz, v)
    const tx = 2*(qy*vz - qz*vy);
    const ty = 2*(qz*vx - qx*vz);
    const tz = 2*(qx*vy - qy*vx);
    return [
      vx + qw*tx + (qy*tz - qz*ty),
      vy + qw*ty + (qz*tx - qx*tz),
      vz + qw*tz + (qx*ty - qy*tx)
    ];
  }

  // Kabsch algorithm: best-fit rigid transform (R, t) mapping rest → current
  // for the cage vertices at given indices.
  // Always produces a valid rotation — no fallbacks to identity.
  function computeRigidTransformKabsch(indices, regionId) {
    const n = indices.length;

    // Compute centroids
    let c0x=0, c0y=0, c0z=0, ctx=0, cty=0, ctz=0;
    for (const i of indices) {
      c0x += cageRest[i][0]; c0y += cageRest[i][1]; c0z += cageRest[i][2];
      ctx += cagePos[i*3];   cty += cagePos[i*3+1]; ctz += cagePos[i*3+2];
    }
    c0x/=n; c0y/=n; c0z/=n;
    ctx/=n; cty/=n; ctz/=n;

    // Build covariance H = Σ (rest - c0)(current - ct)^T
    const H = mat3_create();
    for (const i of indices) {
      const dx0 = cageRest[i][0]-c0x, dy0 = cageRest[i][1]-c0y, dz0 = cageRest[i][2]-c0z;
      const dxt = cagePos[i*3]-ctx,   dyt = cagePos[i*3+1]-cty, dzt = cagePos[i*3+2]-ctz;

      H[0] += dx0*dxt; H[1] += dx0*dyt; H[2] += dx0*dzt;
      H[3] += dy0*dxt; H[4] += dy0*dyt; H[5] += dy0*dzt;
      H[6] += dz0*dxt; H[7] += dz0*dyt; H[8] += dz0*dzt;
    }

    const { U, S, V } = svd3x3(H);

    // Fix degenerate SVD: when points are nearly coplanar, one singular value
    // is near zero, making that column of U all zeros (from invS=0 in svd3x3).
    // Reconstruct it as the cross product of the other two columns.
    const sMax = Math.max(S[0], S[1], S[2]);
    if (sMax > 1e-10) {
      let minI = 0;
      if (S[1] < S[minI]) minI = 1;
      if (S[2] < S[minI]) minI = 2;

      if (S[minI] < sMax * 1e-3) {
        const a = (minI + 1) % 3, b = (minI + 2) % 3;
        U[0*3+minI] = U[1*3+a]*U[2*3+b] - U[2*3+a]*U[1*3+b];
        U[1*3+minI] = U[2*3+a]*U[0*3+b] - U[0*3+a]*U[2*3+b];
        U[2*3+minI] = U[0*3+a]*U[1*3+b] - U[1*3+a]*U[0*3+b];
      }
    }

    // R = V * U^T
    const Ut = mat3_transpose(U);
    let R = mat3_mul(V, Ut);

    // Fix reflection: ensure det(R) > 0
    if (mat3_det(R) < 0) {
      const Vfix = Float64Array.from(V);
      let minI = 0;
      if (S[1] < S[minI]) minI = 1;
      if (S[2] < S[minI]) minI = 2;
      Vfix[0*3+minI] *= -1;
      Vfix[1*3+minI] *= -1;
      Vfix[2*3+minI] *= -1;
      R = mat3_mul(Vfix, Ut);
    }

    // Always orthonormalize R to guarantee a valid rotation matrix.
    // This handles any numerical drift from SVD without ever falling back to identity.
    R = mat3_orthonormalize(R);

    // t = centroid_current - R * centroid_rest
    const Rc0 = mat3_mulVec3(R, c0x, c0y, c0z);
    const t = [ctx-Rc0[0], cty-Rc0[1], ctz-Rc0[2]];

    return { R, t, cRest: [c0x, c0y, c0z], cCurr: [ctx, cty, ctz] };
  }

  // Compute world-space rigid transforms for all regions (independent Kabsch per region).
  // Each region's cage verts are already driven with full hierarchical rotation by
  // driveCageFromMixamo(), so world-space Kabsch recovers the composed transform directly.
  function computeAllRegionTransforms() {
    const transforms = {};
    const posMode = armState && armState.positionBased;
    const legAnalytical = legState && legState.positionBased;
    for (const rid in regionIndices) {
      const r = +rid;
      if (r === 5 || r === 6) continue;   // feet use shin/analytical
      // Arm regions ALWAYS use analytical composition (Kabsch unreliable on thin clusters)
      if (r === 7 || r === 8) continue;
      if (r === 9 || r === 10) continue;
      if (r === 12 || r === 13) continue;
      // Spine sub-regions: ALL analytical (no Kabsch)
      if (r === 0 || r === 14 || r === 15 || r === 16 || r === 17) continue;
      // Collar, neck, toe: analytical
      if (r === 18 || r === 19 || r === 20 || r === 21 || r === 22) continue;
      // Leg regions: use analytical in position mode
      if (legAnalytical && (r === 1 || r === 2)) continue;
      if (legAnalytical && (r === 3 || r === 4)) continue;
      if (headState && r === 11) continue;
      const indices = regionIndices[rid];
      if (indices && indices.length) {
        transforms[r] = computeRigidTransformKabsch(indices, r);
        if (r === 3 || r === 4) transforms[r]._source = 'kabsch';
      }
    }

    // ── ANALYTICAL SPINE / TORSO TRANSFORMS ──
    // All spine sub-regions (0, 14, 15, 16, 17) use analytical bone transforms
    // computed directly from retargeted joint positions — no Kabsch.
    if (retargetedJoints && retargetedJoints.pelvis) {
      const rj = retargetedJoints;
      // Helper: compute analytical rigid transform for a bone segment
      const boneXform = (parentKey, childKey, fallbackChildKey) => {
        const pRest = P[parentKey], cRest = P[childKey] || P[fallbackChildKey];
        const pCur = rj[parentKey], cCur = rj[childKey] || rj[fallbackChildKey];
        if (!pRest || !cRest || !pCur || !cCur) return null;
        return computeBoneRigidTransform(pRest, cRest, pCur, cCur);
      };

      // Spine sub-regions: each uses its own bone segment
      // Region 14 (pelvis): pelvis→spine_joint (or pelvis→chest fallback)
      transforms[14] = boneXform('pelvis', 'spine_joint', 'chest') || boneXform('pelvis', 'chest');
      // Region 0 (spine): spine_joint→spine1_joint (or pelvis→chest fallback)
      transforms[0] = boneXform('spine_joint', 'spine1_joint') || boneXform('pelvis', 'chest');
      // Region 15 (spine1): spine1_joint→spine2_joint
      if (P.spine1_joint) {
        transforms[15] = boneXform('spine1_joint', 'spine2_joint') || transforms[0];
      }
      // Region 16 (spine2): spine2_joint→neck
      if (P.spine2_joint) {
        transforms[16] = boneXform('spine2_joint', 'neck') || transforms[0];
      }
      // Region 17 (chest): spine2_joint→neck (same bone as spine2 but covers upper verts)
      if (P.spine2_joint) {
        transforms[17] = boneXform('spine2_joint', 'neck') || transforms[0];
      }

      // Fallback: if no spine sub-joints available, use pelvis→chest for region 0
      if (!transforms[0]) {
        transforms[0] = boneXform('pelvis', 'chest');
      }
      if (!transforms[14]) {
        transforms[14] = transforms[0];
      }

      // Region 18 (neck): neck→head
      if (P.neck && P.head && rj.neck && rj.head) {
        transforms[18] = boneXform('neck', 'head');
      }
      // Region 19 (l_collar): spine2→l_shoulder
      if (P.spine2_joint && P.l_shoulder && rj.spine2_joint && rj.l_shoulder) {
        transforms[19] = boneXform('spine2_joint', 'l_shoulder');
      } else if (transforms[17]) {
        transforms[19] = transforms[17]; // fallback to chest
      }
      // Region 20 (r_collar): spine2→r_shoulder
      if (P.spine2_joint && P.r_shoulder && rj.spine2_joint && rj.r_shoulder) {
        transforms[20] = boneXform('spine2_joint', 'r_shoulder');
      } else if (transforms[17]) {
        transforms[20] = transforms[17]; // fallback to chest
      }
      // Region 21 (l_toe): use foot transform (will be overridden if toe data exists)
      // Region 22 (r_toe): use foot transform
      // (these get set later when feet transforms are computed)

      // ── BODY ROTATION OVERRIDE ──
      // The analytical bone transforms use computeIKRotMatrix (minimum rotation from
      // bone direction) which captures lean but NOT twist. AND the retargetedJoints
      // positions rotate around pelvisW (translated pelvis) while cage verts rotate
      // around P.pelvis (rest pelvis) — different rotation centers cause the analytical
      // transform's translation to not match the actual cage vertex positions.
      // These two issues combine to create 82mm+ hip pivot error, 117mm+ shoulder error.
      //
      // Fix: Override pelvis/spine transforms with the actual body rotation matrices
      // (R_bodyLeg for lower body, R_bodyUpper for upper body) and compute translations
      // that match the cage vertex pipeline: translate by pelvisDelta then rotate around
      // P.pelvis. This guarantees the analytical transforms match the cage exactly.
      if (legState && legState.R_bodyLeg) {
        const pp = P.pelvis;
        const pd = legState.pelvisDelta;

        // Helper: compute transform that matches cage vertex pipeline.
        // Cage does: v' = R * (v_rest + pelvisDelta - P.pelvis) + P.pelvis
        //          = R * v_rest + (P.pelvis - R*P.pelvis + R*pelvisDelta)
        const bodyTransform = (R_body) => {
          const Rpp = mat3_mulVec3(R_body, pp[0], pp[1], pp[2]);
          const Rpd = mat3_mulVec3(R_body, pd[0], pd[1], pd[2]);
          return {
            R: R_body,
            t: [pp[0]-Rpp[0]+Rpd[0], pp[1]-Rpp[1]+Rpd[1], pp[2]-Rpp[2]+Rpd[2]]
          };
        };

        // Pelvis (region 14): use R_bodyLeg (lean + hip twist)
        // This fixes the 82mm hip pivot displacement — confirmed thigh error drops 43→10mm.
        transforms[14] = bodyTransform(legState.R_bodyLeg);

        // All spine regions (0, 15, 16, 17): use R_bodyUpper (lean + spine twist).
        // The analytical bone transforms miss twist (computeIKRotMatrix only captures direction)
        // AND have wrong translation (retargetedJoints rotates around pelvisW, cage around P.pelvis).
        // Override with bodyTransform matches the cage vertex pipeline exactly.
        // This fixes torso stretch from 41x to 20x by making all spine transforms consistent.
        // The head bone-vs-cage error increases (2.6→39mm) but that's a diagnostic artifact:
        // the retargetedJoints reference lacks twist, while the mesh is now correctly positioned.
        // Head mesh quality is preserved (bulge 5.3mm GOOD, inversions unchanged).
        if (legState.R_bodyUpper) {
          const upperT = bodyTransform(legState.R_bodyUpper);
          if (transforms[0])  transforms[0]  = upperT;
          if (transforms[15]) transforms[15] = upperT;
          if (transforms[16]) transforms[16] = upperT;
          if (transforms[17]) transforms[17] = upperT;
        }
      }
    }

    // ── ANALYTICAL LEG TRANSFORMS (position mode) ──
    // Uses stored body-rotated hip pivots (movedHipL/R) instead of Kabsch-derived
    // pivots. Using exact pivots eliminates cage-mesh misalignment at the hip joint.
    // Use pelvis transform as parent for hip joint placement.
    const legParent = transforms[14] || transforms[0];
    if (legAnalytical && legParent) {
      const R_t = legParent.R, t_t = legParent.t;
      const gc = lastGroundCorrection; // ground comp Y offset (applied to cage, propagate to mesh)

      // One-shot: compare Kabsch-derived vs stored pivot
      if (!window._pivotDiagDone) {
        window._pivotDiagDone = true;
        const hipL = P.l_hip;
        const hipT = mat3_mulVec3(R_t, hipL[0], hipL[1], hipL[2]);
        const kabschHip = [hipT[0]+t_t[0], hipT[1]+t_t[1], hipT[2]+t_t[2]];
        const mhL = legState.movedHipL || hipL;
        const storedHip = [mhL[0], mhL[1]+gc, mhL[2]];
        const diff = Math.sqrt((kabschHip[0]-storedHip[0])**2+(kabschHip[1]-storedHip[1])**2+(kabschHip[2]-storedHip[2])**2);
        console.log('=== PIVOT COMPARISON (Kabsch vs stored) ===');
        console.log('  L hip Kabsch: [' + kabschHip.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)).join(',') + ']');
        console.log('  L hip stored: [' + storedHip.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)).join(',') + ']');
        console.log('  displacement: ' + diff.toFixed(4) + ' (' + (diff*100).toFixed(1) + '% of mesh if ~1.0 scale)');
        if (armState && armState.movedShoulderL) {
          const shL = cageShoulderL;
          const shT = mat3_mulVec3(R_t, shL[0], shL[1], shL[2]);
          const kabschSh = [shT[0]+t_t[0], shT[1]+t_t[1], shT[2]+t_t[2]];
          const msL = armState.movedShoulderL;
          const storedSh = [msL[0], msL[1]+gc, msL[2]];
          const shDiff = Math.sqrt((kabschSh[0]-storedSh[0])**2+(kabschSh[1]-storedSh[1])**2+(kabschSh[2]-storedSh[2])**2);
          console.log('  L shoulder Kabsch: [' + kabschSh.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)).join(',') + ']');
          console.log('  L shoulder stored: [' + storedSh.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)).join(',') + ']');
          console.log('  displacement: ' + shDiff.toFixed(4));
        }
        // Shin transform source diagnostic (deferred to after transforms[1] exists)
        if (transforms[3]) {
          console.log('  L shin transform source: ' + (transforms[3]._source || 'analytical'));
        }
      }

      // Left thigh: R_thigh = legIK directly (NOT composed with R_t).
      // R_thigh already includes body lean via bodyRotDir in the rest direction.
      // HIERARCHICAL PIVOT: derive hip position from torso Kabsch so torso and thigh
      // transforms agree at the hip joint (same as FBX bone hierarchy).
      {
        const R_thigh = legState.lThighR;
        const R_noTwist = legState.lSwingOnlyR;
        const hip = P.l_hip;
        // Hierarchical pivot: where the torso Kabsch places the hip joint.
        // This guarantees torso & thigh agree at the hip (zero seam).
        const kabschHip = mat3_mulVec3(R_t, hip[0], hip[1], hip[2]);
        const hipWorld = [kabschHip[0]+t_t[0], kabschHip[1]+t_t[1], kabschHip[2]+t_t[2]];
        const Rth_hip = mat3_mulVec3(R_thigh, hip[0], hip[1], hip[2]);
        const Rnt_hip = mat3_mulVec3(R_noTwist, hip[0], hip[1], hip[2]);
        const lkn = P.l_knee;
        const thighVec = [lkn[0]-hip[0], lkn[1]-hip[1], lkn[2]-hip[2]];
        const thighLen = Math.sqrt(thighVec[0]**2 + thighVec[1]**2 + thighVec[2]**2);
        transforms[1] = {
          R: R_thigh,
          t: [hipWorld[0]-Rth_hip[0], hipWorld[1]-Rth_hip[1], hipWorld[2]-Rth_hip[2]],
          R_noTwist: R_noTwist,
          t_noTwist: [hipWorld[0]-Rnt_hip[0], hipWorld[1]-Rnt_hip[1], hipWorld[2]-Rnt_hip[2]],
          _hip: hip, _thighDir: [thighVec[0]/thighLen, thighVec[1]/thighLen, thighVec[2]/thighLen], _thighLen: thighLen,
          cRest: transforms[0].cRest, cCurr: transforms[0].cCurr
        };

        // Left shin: analytical chain (R_kneeBend * R_thigh) with hierarchical pivot at knee.
        // Same architecture as arms — each segment follows its parent.
        {
          const knee = P.l_knee;
          // Where thigh places the knee (hierarchical chain: torso→thigh→knee):
          const kneeT = mat3_mulVec3(R_thigh, knee[0], knee[1], knee[2]);
          const kneeWorld = [kneeT[0]+transforms[1].t[0], kneeT[1]+transforms[1].t[1], kneeT[2]+transforms[1].t[2]];

          const Rb = mat3_rotAxis(legState.lKneeBendAxis[0], legState.lKneeBendAxis[1], legState.lKneeBendAxis[2], legState.lKneeBendAngle);
          const R_shin = mat3_mul(Rb, R_thigh);
          const Rsh_knee = mat3_mulVec3(R_shin, knee[0], knee[1], knee[2]);
          // Bend gradient: at knee use thigh rotation (zero seam with thigh region),
          // smoothly transition to full shin rotation (with knee bend) at ankle.
          // Same pattern as forearm bend gradient at the elbow.
          const la = P.l_ankle;
          const lsv = [la[0]-knee[0], la[1]-knee[1], la[2]-knee[2]];
          const lsLen = Math.sqrt(lsv[0]*lsv[0]+lsv[1]*lsv[1]+lsv[2]*lsv[2]);
          transforms[3] = {
            R: R_shin,
            t: [kneeWorld[0]-Rsh_knee[0], kneeWorld[1]-Rsh_knee[1], kneeWorld[2]-Rsh_knee[2]],
            cRest: transforms[0].cRest, cCurr: transforms[0].cCurr,
            R_noBend: R_thigh,
            t_noBend: transforms[1].t,
            _kneeRest: [knee[0], knee[1], knee[2]],
            _shinDir: [lsv[0]/lsLen, lsv[1]/lsLen, lsv[2]/lsLen],
            _shinLen: lsLen
          };
        }
      }

      // Right thigh: hierarchical pivot from torso Kabsch (same as left)
      {
        const R_thigh = legState.rThighR;
        const R_noTwist = legState.rSwingOnlyR;
        const hip = P.r_hip;
        // Hierarchical pivot: where the torso Kabsch places the hip joint.
        const kabschHip = mat3_mulVec3(R_t, hip[0], hip[1], hip[2]);
        const hipWorld = [kabschHip[0]+t_t[0], kabschHip[1]+t_t[1], kabschHip[2]+t_t[2]];
        const Rth_hip = mat3_mulVec3(R_thigh, hip[0], hip[1], hip[2]);
        const Rnt_hip = mat3_mulVec3(R_noTwist, hip[0], hip[1], hip[2]);
        const rkn = P.r_knee;
        const thighVec = [rkn[0]-hip[0], rkn[1]-hip[1], rkn[2]-hip[2]];
        const thighLen = Math.sqrt(thighVec[0]**2 + thighVec[1]**2 + thighVec[2]**2);
        transforms[2] = {
          R: R_thigh,
          t: [hipWorld[0]-Rth_hip[0], hipWorld[1]-Rth_hip[1], hipWorld[2]-Rth_hip[2]],
          R_noTwist: R_noTwist,
          t_noTwist: [hipWorld[0]-Rnt_hip[0], hipWorld[1]-Rnt_hip[1], hipWorld[2]-Rnt_hip[2]],
          _hip: hip, _thighDir: [thighVec[0]/thighLen, thighVec[1]/thighLen, thighVec[2]/thighLen], _thighLen: thighLen,
          cRest: transforms[0].cRest, cCurr: transforms[0].cCurr
        };

        // Right shin: analytical chain (R_kneeBend * R_thigh) with hierarchical pivot at knee.
        {
          const knee = P.r_knee;
          const kneeT = mat3_mulVec3(R_thigh, knee[0], knee[1], knee[2]);
          const kneeWorld = [kneeT[0]+transforms[2].t[0], kneeT[1]+transforms[2].t[1], kneeT[2]+transforms[2].t[2]];

          const Rb = mat3_rotAxis(legState.rKneeBendAxis[0], legState.rKneeBendAxis[1], legState.rKneeBendAxis[2], legState.rKneeBendAngle);
          const R_shin = mat3_mul(Rb, R_thigh);
          const Rsh_knee = mat3_mulVec3(R_shin, knee[0], knee[1], knee[2]);
          // Bend gradient: at knee use thigh rotation (zero seam), at ankle full bend.
          const ra = P.r_ankle;
          const rsv = [ra[0]-knee[0], ra[1]-knee[1], ra[2]-knee[2]];
          const rsLen = Math.sqrt(rsv[0]*rsv[0]+rsv[1]*rsv[1]+rsv[2]*rsv[2]);
          transforms[4] = {
            R: R_shin,
            t: [kneeWorld[0]-Rsh_knee[0], kneeWorld[1]-Rsh_knee[1], kneeWorld[2]-Rsh_knee[2]],
            cRest: transforms[0].cRest, cCurr: transforms[0].cCurr,
            R_noBend: R_thigh,
            t_noBend: transforms[2].t,
            _kneeRest: [knee[0], knee[1], knee[2]],
            _shinDir: [rsv[0]/rsLen, rsv[1]/rsLen, rsv[2]/rsLen],
            _shinLen: rsLen
          };
        }
      }
    }

    // Feet: analytical ankle rotation (position mode with toe data) or copy shin
    if (legAnalytical && legState.lAnkleBendAngle > 1e-4 && transforms[3]) {
      const Ra = mat3_rotAxis(legState.lAnkleBendAxis[0], legState.lAnkleBendAxis[1], legState.lAnkleBendAxis[2], legState.lAnkleBendAngle);
      const R_foot = mat3_mul(Ra, transforms[3].R);
      const ankle = P.l_ankle;
      const ankleT = mat3_mulVec3(transforms[3].R, ankle[0], ankle[1], ankle[2]);
      const ankleWorld = [ankleT[0]+transforms[3].t[0], ankleT[1]+transforms[3].t[1], ankleT[2]+transforms[3].t[2]];
      const Rf_ankle = mat3_mulVec3(R_foot, ankle[0], ankle[1], ankle[2]);
      transforms[5] = {
        R: R_foot,
        t: [ankleWorld[0]-Rf_ankle[0], ankleWorld[1]-Rf_ankle[1], ankleWorld[2]-Rf_ankle[2]],
        cRest: transforms[0].cRest, cCurr: transforms[0].cCurr
      };
    } else if (transforms[3]) {
      transforms[5] = transforms[3];
    }
    if (legAnalytical && legState.rAnkleBendAngle > 1e-4 && transforms[4]) {
      const Ra = mat3_rotAxis(legState.rAnkleBendAxis[0], legState.rAnkleBendAxis[1], legState.rAnkleBendAxis[2], legState.rAnkleBendAngle);
      const R_foot = mat3_mul(Ra, transforms[4].R);
      const ankle = P.r_ankle;
      const ankleT = mat3_mulVec3(transforms[4].R, ankle[0], ankle[1], ankle[2]);
      const ankleWorld = [ankleT[0]+transforms[4].t[0], ankleT[1]+transforms[4].t[1], ankleT[2]+transforms[4].t[2]];
      const Rf_ankle = mat3_mulVec3(R_foot, ankle[0], ankle[1], ankle[2]);
      transforms[6] = {
        R: R_foot,
        t: [ankleWorld[0]-Rf_ankle[0], ankleWorld[1]-Rf_ankle[1], ankleWorld[2]-Rf_ankle[2]],
        cRest: transforms[0].cRest, cCurr: transforms[0].cCurr
      };
    } else if (transforms[4]) {
      transforms[6] = transforms[4];
    }

    // Toe regions: inherit from foot transform
    if (transforms[5]) transforms[21] = transforms[5];
    if (transforms[6]) transforms[22] = transforms[6];

    // Head: analytical in position mode (R_headIK * R_chest, pinned at neck)
    // Use chest/spine2 transform for head pivot (neck sits at top of chest)
    const headParent = transforms[17] || transforms[16] || transforms[0];
    if (headState && headParent) {
      const R_t = headParent.R, t_t = headParent.t;
      // Head IK directly (no R_t) — R_head includes body lean via R_bodyUpper composition
      const R_head = headState;
      const neck = P.neck;
      const neckT = mat3_mulVec3(R_t, neck[0], neck[1], neck[2]);
      const neckWorld = [neckT[0]+t_t[0], neckT[1]+t_t[1], neckT[2]+t_t[2]];
      const Rh_neck = mat3_mulVec3(R_head, neck[0], neck[1], neck[2]);
      transforms[11] = {
        R: R_head,
        t: [neckWorld[0]-Rh_neck[0], neckWorld[1]-Rh_neck[1], neckWorld[2]-Rh_neck[2]],
        cRest: transforms[0].cRest, cCurr: transforms[0].cCurr
      };
    }

    // Upper arms: compose from analytical arm rotations, pinned at body-rotated shoulder.
    // Use movedShoulderL/R from armState when available — these match the cage vertex
    // pipeline (lean + spine twist around P.pelvis), giving the correct shoulder position.
    // Fallback to chest-transform-derived position if movedShoulder not available.
    const armParent = transforms[17] || transforms[16] || transforms[0];
    if (armState && armParent && cageShoulderL && cageArmDirL) {
      const R_t = armParent.R, t_t = armParent.t;
      let R_u;
      if (armState.positionBased) {
        R_u = armState.lUpperR;
      } else {
        const Rz = mat3_rotZ(armState.lTposeZ);
        const Rx = mat3_rotX(armState.lArmSwing);
        R_u = mat3_mul(Rx, mat3_mul(Rz, R_t));
      }
      const sh = cageShoulderL;
      // Use movedShoulderL directly — matches cage vertex pipeline (lean + twist).
      // Previously derived from chest transform which lacked twist → 117mm displacement.
      let actualSh;
      if (armState.movedShoulderL) {
        actualSh = armState.movedShoulderL.slice();
      } else {
        const kabschShL = mat3_mulVec3(R_t, sh[0], sh[1], sh[2]);
        actualSh = [kabschShL[0]+t_t[0], kabschShL[1]+t_t[1], kabschShL[2]+t_t[2]];
      }
      const Ru_sh = mat3_mulVec3(R_u, sh[0], sh[1], sh[2]);
      transforms[7] = {
        R: R_u,
        t: [actualSh[0]-Ru_sh[0], actualSh[1]-Ru_sh[1], actualSh[2]-Ru_sh[2]],
        cRest: transforms[0].cRest,
        cCurr: transforms[0].cCurr
      };
    }
    if (armState && armParent && cageShoulderR && cageArmDirR) {
      const R_t = armParent.R, t_t = armParent.t;
      let R_u;
      if (armState.positionBased) {
        R_u = armState.rUpperR;
      } else {
        const Rz = mat3_rotZ(armState.rTposeZ);
        const Rx = mat3_rotX(armState.rArmSwing);
        R_u = mat3_mul(Rx, mat3_mul(Rz, R_t));
      }
      const sh = cageShoulderR;
      // Use movedShoulderR directly — matches cage vertex pipeline (lean + twist).
      let actualSh;
      if (armState.movedShoulderR) {
        actualSh = armState.movedShoulderR.slice();
      } else {
        const kabschShR = mat3_mulVec3(R_t, sh[0], sh[1], sh[2]);
        actualSh = [kabschShR[0]+t_t[0], kabschShR[1]+t_t[1], kabschShR[2]+t_t[2]];
      }
      const Ru_sh = mat3_mulVec3(R_u, sh[0], sh[1], sh[2]);
      transforms[8] = {
        R: R_u,
        t: [actualSh[0]-Ru_sh[0], actualSh[1]-Ru_sh[1], actualSh[2]-Ru_sh[2]],
        cRest: transforms[0].cRest,
        cCurr: transforms[0].cCurr
      };
      if (!window._rShoulderDiag) {
        window._rShoulderDiag = true;
        const t8 = transforms[8].t;
        const tMag = Math.sqrt(t8[0]*t8[0]+t8[1]*t8[1]+t8[2]*t8[2]);
        console.log('[R_SHOULDER] sh(rest)=[' + sh.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)) + ']');
        console.log('[R_SHOULDER] actualSh=[' + actualSh.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)) + ']');
        console.log('[R_SHOULDER] Ru*sh=[' + Ru_sh.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)) + ']');
        console.log('[R_SHOULDER] t=[' + t8.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)) + '] |t|=' + tMag.toFixed(4));
        console.log('[R_SHOULDER] positionBased=' + armState.positionBased + ' movedShoulderR=' + (armState.movedShoulderR ? armState.movedShoulderR.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)) : 'null'));
        // Also log L shoulder for comparison
        const t7 = transforms[7].t;
        const t7Mag = Math.sqrt(t7[0]*t7[0]+t7[1]*t7[1]+t7[2]*t7[2]);
        console.log('[L_SHOULDER] t=[' + t7.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)) + '] |t|=' + t7Mag.toFixed(4));
      }
    }

    // Forearms: upper arm analytical + elbow bend + forearm twist.
    // R_forearm = [R_forearmTwist *] R_bend * R_upper, pinned at elbow.
    if (armState && transforms[7] && cageShoulderL && cageArmDirL && armState.lBendAxis) {
      const ba = armState.lBendAxis;
      const lBendAng = armState.positionBased ? armState.lBendAngle : armState.lElbowAngle;
      const Rb = mat3_rotAxis(ba[0], ba[1], ba[2], lBendAng);
      const R_u = transforms[7].R;
      const t_u = transforms[7].t;
      const uaLen = +$('upperArmLen').value;
      const erx = cageShoulderL[0] + cageArmDirL[0]*uaLen;
      const ery = cageShoulderL[1] + cageArmDirL[1]*uaLen;
      const erz = cageShoulderL[2] + cageArmDirL[2]*uaLen;
      const ae = mat3_mulVec3(R_u, erx, ery, erz);
      const ep = [ae[0]+t_u[0], ae[1]+t_u[1], ae[2]+t_u[2]];
      let R_f = mat3_mul(Rb, R_u);
      // Forearm twist (pronation/supination) — quaternion LOCAL extraction
      if (armState.positionBased && Math.abs(armState.lForearmTwist || 0) > 1e-4) {
        const fd = vnorm(mat3_mulVec3(R_f, cageArmDirL[0], cageArmDirL[1], cageArmDirL[2]));
        R_f = mat3_mul(mat3_rotAxis(fd[0], fd[1], fd[2], armState.lForearmTwist), R_f);
      }
      const Rf_re = mat3_mulVec3(R_f, erx, ery, erz);
      transforms[9] = {
        R: R_f,
        t: [ep[0] - Rf_re[0], ep[1] - Rf_re[1], ep[2] - Rf_re[2]],
        cRest: transforms[7].cRest,
        cCurr: transforms[7].cCurr,
        // Bend gradient: at elbow end use upper arm rotation (zero seam),
        // smoothly transition to full forearm rotation at wrist.
        R_noBend: transforms[7].R,
        t_noBend: transforms[7].t,
        _elbowRest: [erx, ery, erz],
        _forearmDir: cageArmDirL,
        _forearmLen: +$('forearmLen').value
      };
      if (!window._farmTransDiag) {
        window._farmTransDiag = true;
        const Ru = transforms[7].R, Rf = transforms[9].R;
        const qU = mat3_to_quat(Ru), qF = mat3_to_quat(Rf);
        const qdot = qU[0]*qF[0]+qU[1]*qF[1]+qU[2]*qF[2]+qU[3]*qF[3];
        const angDiff = 2*Math.acos(Math.min(1, Math.abs(qdot))) * 180/Math.PI;
        console.log('[FARM TRANSFORM] lBendAng=' + (lBendAng*180/Math.PI).toFixed(1) +
          '° ba=[' + ba.map(v=>v.toFixed(3)) + '] positionBased=' + armState.positionBased);
        console.log('[FARM TRANSFORM] R_u vs R_f angular diff=' + angDiff.toFixed(1) + '°');
        console.log('[FARM TRANSFORM] R_f=[' + Array.from(Rf).map(v=>v.toFixed(3)).join(',') + ']');
