        console.log('[FARM TRANSFORM] R_u=[' + Array.from(Ru).map(v=>v.toFixed(3)).join(',') + ']');
      }
    }
    if (armState && transforms[8] && cageShoulderR && cageArmDirR && armState.rBendAxis) {
      const ba = armState.rBendAxis;
      const rBendAng = armState.positionBased ? armState.rBendAngle : armState.rElbowAngle;
      const Rb = mat3_rotAxis(ba[0], ba[1], ba[2], rBendAng);
      const R_u = transforms[8].R;
      const t_u = transforms[8].t;
      const uaLen = +$('upperArmLen').value;
      const erx = cageShoulderR[0] + cageArmDirR[0]*uaLen;
      const ery = cageShoulderR[1] + cageArmDirR[1]*uaLen;
      const erz = cageShoulderR[2] + cageArmDirR[2]*uaLen;
      const ae = mat3_mulVec3(R_u, erx, ery, erz);
      const ep = [ae[0]+t_u[0], ae[1]+t_u[1], ae[2]+t_u[2]];
      let R_f = mat3_mul(Rb, R_u);
      // Forearm twist (right — pronation/supination) — quaternion LOCAL extraction
      if (armState.positionBased && Math.abs(armState.rForearmTwist || 0) > 1e-4) {
        const fd = vnorm(mat3_mulVec3(R_f, cageArmDirR[0], cageArmDirR[1], cageArmDirR[2]));
        R_f = mat3_mul(mat3_rotAxis(fd[0], fd[1], fd[2], armState.rForearmTwist), R_f);
      }
      const Rf_re = mat3_mulVec3(R_f, erx, ery, erz);
      transforms[10] = {
        R: R_f,
        t: [ep[0] - Rf_re[0], ep[1] - Rf_re[1], ep[2] - Rf_re[2]],
        cRest: transforms[8].cRest,
        cCurr: transforms[8].cCurr,
        R_noBend: transforms[8].R,
        t_noBend: transforms[8].t,
        _elbowRest: [erx, ery, erz],
        _forearmDir: cageArmDirR,
        _forearmLen: +$('forearmLen').value
      };
    }
    // Hands: forearm transform + analytical wrist rotation around bone-local axis.
    if (armState && transforms[9] && cageShoulderL && cageArmDirL && armState.lBendAxis) {
      // Position mode: use real wrist flex from FBX finger data, or fallback to elbow counter-rotation
      let ba, lWristAng;
      if (armState.positionBased && armState.lWristFlexAxis && armState.lWristFlexAngle > 1e-4) {
        ba = armState.lWristFlexAxis;
        lWristAng = armState.lWristFlexAngle;
      } else if (armState.positionBased) {
        ba = armState.lBendAxis;
        lWristAng = -armState.lBendAngle * 0.3;
      } else {
        ba = armState.lBendAxis;
        lWristAng = armState.lWristAngle;
      }
      const Rb = mat3_rotAxis(ba[0], ba[1], ba[2], lWristAng);
      const R_f = transforms[9].R;
      const t_f = transforms[9].t;
      const uaLen = +$('upperArmLen').value;
      const faLen = +$('forearmLen').value;
      const wrx = cageShoulderL[0] + cageArmDirL[0]*(uaLen+faLen);
      const wry = cageShoulderL[1] + cageArmDirL[1]*(uaLen+faLen);
      const wrz = cageShoulderL[2] + cageArmDirL[2]*(uaLen+faLen);
      const aw = mat3_mulVec3(R_f, wrx, wry, wrz);
      const wp = [aw[0]+t_f[0], aw[1]+t_f[1], aw[2]+t_f[2]];
      const R_h = mat3_mul(Rb, R_f);
      const Rb_tf = mat3_mulVec3(Rb, t_f[0], t_f[1], t_f[2]);
      const Rb_wp = mat3_mulVec3(Rb, wp[0], wp[1], wp[2]);
      transforms[12] = {
        R: R_h,
        t: [Rb_tf[0] + wp[0] - Rb_wp[0], Rb_tf[1] + wp[1] - Rb_wp[1], Rb_tf[2] + wp[2] - Rb_wp[2]],
        cRest: transforms[9].cRest,
        cCurr: transforms[9].cCurr
      };
    }
    if (armState && transforms[10] && cageShoulderR && cageArmDirR && armState.rBendAxis) {
      let ba, rWristAng;
      if (armState.positionBased && armState.rWristFlexAxis && armState.rWristFlexAngle > 1e-4) {
        ba = armState.rWristFlexAxis;
        rWristAng = armState.rWristFlexAngle;
      } else if (armState.positionBased) {
        ba = armState.rBendAxis;
        rWristAng = -armState.rBendAngle * 0.3;
      } else {
        ba = armState.rBendAxis;
        rWristAng = armState.rWristAngle;
      }
      const Rb = mat3_rotAxis(ba[0], ba[1], ba[2], rWristAng);
      const R_f = transforms[10].R;
      const t_f = transforms[10].t;
      const uaLen = +$('upperArmLen').value;
      const faLen = +$('forearmLen').value;
      const wrx = cageShoulderR[0] + cageArmDirR[0]*(uaLen+faLen);
      const wry = cageShoulderR[1] + cageArmDirR[1]*(uaLen+faLen);
      const wrz = cageShoulderR[2] + cageArmDirR[2]*(uaLen+faLen);
      const aw = mat3_mulVec3(R_f, wrx, wry, wrz);
      const wp = [aw[0]+t_f[0], aw[1]+t_f[1], aw[2]+t_f[2]];
      const R_h = mat3_mul(Rb, R_f);
      const Rb_tf = mat3_mulVec3(Rb, t_f[0], t_f[1], t_f[2]);
      const Rb_wp = mat3_mulVec3(Rb, wp[0], wp[1], wp[2]);
      transforms[13] = {
        R: R_h,
        t: [Rb_tf[0] + wp[0] - Rb_wp[0], Rb_tf[1] + wp[1] - Rb_wp[1], Rb_tf[2] + wp[2] - Rb_wp[2]],
        cRest: transforms[10].cRest,
        cCurr: transforms[10].cCurr
      };
    }
    // One-time: report any regions with verts but no transform (structural gate for tuner)
    if (!window._regionXformDiagDone) {
      window._regionXformDiagDone = true;
      const missing = [];
      for (const rid in regionIndices) {
        if (regionIndices[rid].length > 0 && !transforms[+rid]) {
          missing.push(rid + '(' + regionIndices[rid].length + 'v)');
        }
      }
      console.log('REGION_TRANSFORM_MISSING: [' + missing.join(', ') + ']');
    }
    return transforms;
  }

  // Precompute per-mesh-vertex region weights from bind data
  // Returns array of flat pairs: [rid1, w1, rid2, w2, ...] per vertex
  // Build per-vertex EMA alpha from meshVertRegions (primary region lookup).
  // Returns Float32Array with one alpha per vertex for fast per-frame smoothing.
  function buildPerVertexEmaAlpha(mvr) {
    const EMA_PER_REGION = {
      0: 0.55,   // spine: strong smoothing
      1: 0.8, 2: 0.8,    // thighs
      3: 0.75, 4: 0.75,  // shins
      5: 0.8, 6: 0.8,    // feet
      7: 0.6, 8: 0.6,    // upper arms
      9: 0.8, 10: 0.8,   // forearms
      11: 0.7,            // head
      12: 0.9, 13: 0.9,  // hands
      14: 0.65,           // pelvis
      15: 0.55, 16: 0.55, // spine1, spine2
      17: 0.55,           // chest
      18: 0.65,           // neck
      19: 0.6, 20: 0.6,  // collars
      21: 0.8, 22: 0.8   // toes
    };
    const n = mvr.length;
    const alpha = new Float32Array(n);
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const rw = mvr[i];
      if (!rw || typeof rw[Symbol.iterator] !== 'function') { alpha[i] = 0.85; continue; }
      let primaryRid = -1, maxW = 0;
      try {
        for (const [rid, w] of rw) {
          if (w > maxW) { maxW = w; primaryRid = rid; }
        }
      } catch(e) { alpha[i] = 0.85; continue; }
      alpha[i] = primaryRid >= 0 && EMA_PER_REGION[primaryRid] !== undefined
        ? EMA_PER_REGION[primaryRid] : 0.85;
    }
    return alpha;
  }

  function precomputeMeshRegionWeights(meshRestPos, bindIdx, bindW, K, regionId) {
    const nMesh = meshRestPos.length / 3;
    const result = new Array(nMesh);
    let _bpSuppressed = 0, _bpTotalDelta = 0;
    let _pelvisInjected = 0;
    let _neckInjected = 0;
    let _hipThighInjected = 0;
    let _kneeShinInjected = 0;
    const _bpTracked = {}; // track specific vert indices for diagnostic

    // Adjacency filtering diagnostics
    let _adjFiltered = 0;

    for (let vi = 0; vi < nMesh; vi++) {
      const rw = new Map();
      const base = vi * K;
      for (let j = 0; j < K; j++) {
        const ci = bindIdx[base + j];
        const w = bindW[base + j];
        const rid = regionId[ci];
        rw.set(rid, (rw.get(rid) || 0) + w);
      }

      // Temporarily merge all spine sub-regions (14, 15, 16, 17, 18, 19, 20) into
      // torso (0) so existing shoulder/hip smoothstep code works unchanged.
      // Track which sub-regions existed and their proportions for restoration at end.
      const _spineSubRegions = {}; // rid → original weight
      const _spineRids = [14, 15, 16, 17, 18, 19, 20];
      for (const sr of _spineRids) {
        if (rw.has(sr)) {
          _spineSubRegions[sr] = rw.get(sr);
          rw.set(0, (rw.get(0) || 0) + rw.get(sr));
          rw.delete(sr);
        }
      }
      const _hadSpineSubs = Object.keys(_spineSubRegions).length > 0;

      // Smoothstep elbow weight override: replace KNN-derived upper/forearm split
      // with smooth projection-based interpolation centered on the elbow.
      if (cageShoulderL && cageArmDirL && cageShoulderR && cageArmDirR) {
        const vx = meshRestPos[vi*3], vy = meshRestPos[vi*3+1], vz = meshRestPos[vi*3+2];
        const uaLen = +$('upperArmLen').value;
        const EBAND = CFG('EBAND_FRAC', 0.18) * uaLen;

        // Left arm: regions 7 (upper) + 9 (forearm)
        if (rw.has(7) && rw.has(9)) {
          const sh = cageShoulderL, d = cageArmDirL;
          const tp = (vx-sh[0])*d[0] + (vy-sh[1])*d[1] + (vz-sh[2])*d[2];
          if (Math.abs(tp - uaLen) < EBAND * 2) {
            const totalW = rw.get(7) + rw.get(9);
            const f = clamp((tp - (uaLen - EBAND)) / (2 * EBAND), 0, 1);
            const sf = f * f * (3 - 2 * f); // smoothstep
            rw.set(7, totalW * (1 - sf));
            rw.set(9, totalW * sf);
          }
        }
        // Right arm: regions 8 (upper) + 10 (forearm)
        if (rw.has(8) && rw.has(10)) {
          const sh = cageShoulderR, d = cageArmDirR;
          const tp = (vx-sh[0])*d[0] + (vy-sh[1])*d[1] + (vz-sh[2])*d[2];
          if (Math.abs(tp - uaLen) < EBAND * 2) {
            const totalW = rw.get(8) + rw.get(10);
            const f = clamp((tp - (uaLen - EBAND)) / (2 * EBAND), 0, 1);
            const sf = f * f * (3 - 2 * f); // smoothstep
            rw.set(8, totalW * (1 - sf));
            rw.set(10, totalW * sf);
          }
        }

        // Shoulder weight injection: upper arm verts near the shoulder that have
        // NO torso weight need some injected so the smoothstep can create a blend.
        // Without this, arm verts whose K nearest cage verts are all arm get 100%
        // arm transform, causing a hard pivot/dislocation at the shoulder.
        const SINJECT_DEPTH = 0.25 * uaLen; // inject zone extends 25% down the upper arm
        // Left shoulder injection
        if (cageShoulderL && cageArmDirL && rw.has(7) && !rw.has(0)) {
          const sh = cageShoulderL, d = cageArmDirL;
          const tp = (vx-sh[0])*d[0] + (vy-sh[1])*d[1] + (vz-sh[2])*d[2];
          if (tp > -0.02 && tp < SINJECT_DEPTH) {
            const f = clamp(tp / SINJECT_DEPTH, 0, 1);
            const sf = f * f * (3 - 2 * f);
            const inject = (1 - sf) * 0.4; // up to 40% torso at shoulder, fading to 0
            if (inject > 0.02) {
              const armW = rw.get(7);
              const take = armW * inject;
              rw.set(7, armW - take);
              rw.set(0, take);
            }
          }
        }
        // Right shoulder injection
        if (cageShoulderR && cageArmDirR && rw.has(8) && !rw.has(0)) {
          const sh = cageShoulderR, d = cageArmDirR;
          const tp = (vx-sh[0])*d[0] + (vy-sh[1])*d[1] + (vz-sh[2])*d[2];
          if (tp > -0.02 && tp < SINJECT_DEPTH) {
            const f = clamp(tp / SINJECT_DEPTH, 0, 1);
            const sf = f * f * (3 - 2 * f);
            const inject = (1 - sf) * 0.4;
            if (inject > 0.02) {
              const armW = rw.get(8);
              const take = armW * inject;
              rw.set(8, armW - take);
              rw.set(0, take);
            }
          }
        }

        // Shoulder smoothstep: torso (0) ↔ upper arm (7/8)
        // Same idea as elbow — smooth projection-based weight at shoulder boundary.
        const SBAND = 0.20 * uaLen; // wider blend zone for smoother shoulder transition

        // Left shoulder: regions 0 (torso) + 7 (upper arm)
        if (rw.has(0) && rw.has(7)) {
          const sh = cageShoulderL, d = cageArmDirL;
          const tp = (vx-sh[0])*d[0] + (vy-sh[1])*d[1] + (vz-sh[2])*d[2];
          if (Math.abs(tp) < SBAND * 2) {
            const totalW = rw.get(0) + rw.get(7);
            // t<0 = torso side, t>0 = arm side. Blend centered at t=0 (shoulder).
            const f = clamp((tp + SBAND) / (2 * SBAND), 0, 1);
            const sf = f * f * (3 - 2 * f);
            rw.set(0, totalW * (1 - sf));
            rw.set(7, totalW * sf);
          }
        }
        // Right shoulder: regions 0 (torso) + 8 (upper arm)
        if (rw.has(0) && rw.has(8)) {
          const sh = cageShoulderR, d = cageArmDirR;
          const tp = (vx-sh[0])*d[0] + (vy-sh[1])*d[1] + (vz-sh[2])*d[2];
          if (Math.abs(tp) < SBAND * 2) {
            const totalW = rw.get(0) + rw.get(8);
            const f = clamp((tp + SBAND) / (2 * SBAND), 0, 1);
            const sf = f * f * (3 - 2 * f);
            rw.set(0, totalW * (1 - sf));
            rw.set(8, totalW * sf);
          }
        }
      }

      // Smoothstep knee weight override: thigh (1/2) ↔ shin (3/4)
      // Only redistribute existing thigh+shin weight — never inject shin weight
      // into thigh-only verts. Thigh verts must follow the thigh transform fully.
      if (P.l_hip && P.l_knee && P.l_ankle) {
        const vx = meshRestPos[vi*3], vy = meshRestPos[vi*3+1], vz = meshRestPos[vi*3+2];
        // Left knee: regions 1 (thigh) + 3 (shin)
        if (rw.has(1) && rw.has(3)) {
          const hip = P.l_hip, knee = P.l_knee;
          const thighLen = Math.sqrt((knee[0]-hip[0])**2 + (knee[1]-hip[1])**2 + (knee[2]-hip[2])**2);
          const d = [(knee[0]-hip[0])/thighLen, (knee[1]-hip[1])/thighLen, (knee[2]-hip[2])/thighLen];
          const tp = (vx-hip[0])*d[0] + (vy-hip[1])*d[1] + (vz-hip[2])*d[2];
          const KBAND = 0.12 * thighLen;
          if (Math.abs(tp - thighLen) < KBAND * 2) {
            const totalW = rw.get(1) + rw.get(3);
            const f = clamp((tp - (thighLen - KBAND)) / (2 * KBAND), 0, 1);
            const sf = f * f * (3 - 2 * f);
            rw.set(1, totalW * (1 - sf));
            rw.set(3, totalW * sf);
          }
        }
        // Right knee: regions 2 (thigh) + 4 (shin)
        if (rw.has(2) && rw.has(4) && P.r_hip && P.r_knee) {
          const hip = P.r_hip, knee = P.r_knee;
          const thighLen = Math.sqrt((knee[0]-hip[0])**2 + (knee[1]-hip[1])**2 + (knee[2]-hip[2])**2);
          const d = [(knee[0]-hip[0])/thighLen, (knee[1]-hip[1])/thighLen, (knee[2]-hip[2])/thighLen];
          const tp = (vx-hip[0])*d[0] + (vy-hip[1])*d[1] + (vz-hip[2])*d[2];
          const KBAND = 0.12 * thighLen;
          if (Math.abs(tp - thighLen) < KBAND * 2) {
            const totalW = rw.get(2) + rw.get(4);
            const f = clamp((tp - (thighLen - KBAND)) / (2 * KBAND), 0, 1);
            const sf = f * f * (3 - 2 * f);
            rw.set(2, totalW * (1 - sf));
            rw.set(4, totalW * sf);
          }
        }
      }

      // Thigh dominance enforcement: convert shin weight to thigh for verts that
      // are geometrically in the thigh zone. Inner thigh skin extends past the
      // knee joint anatomically, so for medial (inner) verts we enforce up to
      // proj < 1.15, while outer verts use proj < 1.0. This catches the shin-
      // dominant verts at proj 1.0-1.15 on the inner thigh that cause visible
      // stretching when the knee bends.
      if ((rw.has(3) || rw.has(1)) && P.l_hip && P.l_knee && P.r_hip) {
        const vx = meshRestPos[vi*3], vy = meshRestPos[vi*3+1], vz = meshRestPos[vi*3+2];
        const hip = P.l_hip, knee = P.l_knee;
        const tLen = Math.sqrt((knee[0]-hip[0])**2 + (knee[1]-hip[1])**2 + (knee[2]-hip[2])**2);
        const td = [(knee[0]-hip[0])/tLen, (knee[1]-hip[1])/tLen, (knee[2]-hip[2])/tLen];
        const proj = ((vx-hip[0])*td[0] + (vy-hip[1])*td[1] + (vz-hip[2])*td[2]) / tLen;
        // Medial factor: how far toward body midline (inner thigh)
        const hipHalfSpread = Math.abs(P.l_hip[0] - P.r_hip[0]) / 2;
        const medialFrac = (vx - hip[0]) / Math.max(hipHalfSpread, 0.01);
        // Inner thigh extends past knee: enforce up to proj 1.2 for medial verts
        const maxProj = medialFrac > 0.15 ? 1.2 : 1.0;
        if (rw.has(3) && proj < maxProj) {
          rw.set(1, (rw.get(1) || 0) + rw.get(3));
          rw.delete(3);
        } else if (rw.has(1) && rw.has(3) && rw.get(1) >= rw.get(3)) {
          rw.set(1, rw.get(1) + rw.get(3));
          rw.delete(3);
        }
      }
      if ((rw.has(4) || rw.has(2)) && P.r_hip && P.r_knee && P.l_hip) {
        const vx = meshRestPos[vi*3], vy = meshRestPos[vi*3+1], vz = meshRestPos[vi*3+2];
        const hip = P.r_hip, knee = P.r_knee;
        const tLen = Math.sqrt((knee[0]-hip[0])**2 + (knee[1]-hip[1])**2 + (knee[2]-hip[2])**2);
        const td = [(knee[0]-hip[0])/tLen, (knee[1]-hip[1])/tLen, (knee[2]-hip[2])/tLen];
        const proj = ((vx-hip[0])*td[0] + (vy-hip[1])*td[1] + (vz-hip[2])*td[2]) / tLen;
        const hipHalfSpread = Math.abs(P.l_hip[0] - P.r_hip[0]) / 2;
        const medialFrac = (hip[0] - vx) / Math.max(hipHalfSpread, 0.01);
        const maxProj = medialFrac > 0.15 ? 1.2 : 1.0;
        if (rw.has(4) && proj < maxProj) {
          rw.set(2, (rw.get(2) || 0) + rw.get(4));
          rw.delete(4);
        } else if (rw.has(2) && rw.has(4) && rw.get(2) >= rw.get(4)) {
          rw.set(2, rw.get(2) + rw.get(4));
          rw.delete(4);
        }
      }

      // Crotch weight injection: ensure mesh verts between the hips have SOME
      // thigh weight so the hip smoothstep below can blend them properly.
      // IMPORTANT: inject only the NEARER thigh (based on X position) to avoid
      // opposing-rotation cancellation that causes crotch webbing.
      // Also inject into verts that already have weak thigh weight (<15%) —
      // without this, 14+ crotch verts end up torso-only and create webbing.
      if (P.l_hip && P.r_hip && P.pelvis && P.l_knee && P.r_knee && rw.has(0)) {
        const vx = meshRestPos[vi*3], vy = meshRestPos[vi*3+1];
        const pelvisY = P.pelvis[1];
        const CROTCH_TOP = pelvisY + 0.02;
        const CROTCH_DEPTH = 0.12; // 12cm below pelvis (was 8cm)

        if (vy < CROTCH_TOP && vy > pelvisY - CROTCH_DEPTH) {
          const lhx = P.l_hip[0], rhx = P.r_hip[0];
          const midX = (lhx + rhx) / 2;
          const halfSpread = Math.abs(lhx - rhx) / 2;
          const margin = halfSpread * 0.8; // wider lateral margin (was 0.5)

          if (vx > lhx - margin && vx < rhx + margin) {
            const vertFade = clamp((CROTCH_TOP - vy) / CROTCH_DEPTH, 0, 1);
            const vertSS = vertFade * vertFade * (3 - 2 * vertFade);
            const normX = clamp((vx - midX) / halfSpread, -1, 1);
            const WEAK_THRESH = 0.15; // inject if thigh weight below 15%

            // Inject only the NEARER thigh based on lateral position
            if (normX <= 0 && (!rw.has(1) || rw.get(1) < WEAK_THRESH) && vertSS > 0.05) {
              const lateralW = clamp(0.5 - normX * 0.5, 0.15, 0.5);
              const inject = vertSS * lateralW * 0.5; // stronger injection (was 0.4)
              if (inject > 0.02) {
                const take = Math.min(inject, rw.get(0) * 0.55);
                rw.set(0, rw.get(0) - take);
                rw.set(1, (rw.get(1) || 0) + take);
              }
            } else if (normX > 0 && (!rw.has(2) || rw.get(2) < WEAK_THRESH) && vertSS > 0.05) {
              const lateralW = clamp(0.5 + normX * 0.5, 0.15, 0.5);
              const inject = vertSS * lateralW * 0.5;
              if (inject > 0.02) {
                const take = Math.min(inject, rw.get(0) * 0.55);
                rw.set(0, rw.get(0) - take);
                rw.set(2, (rw.get(2) || 0) + take);
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }

      // Hip thigh injection: torso verts near the hip need some thigh weight
      // so the hip smoothstep below can blend them. Without this, butt/back-of-
      // hip verts with 100% torso from KNN (because the butt protrudes beyond
      // the thigh cage region) stay locked to the torso when the thigh rotates,
      // causing "hanging mesh" on the back of the legs.
      // Also inject for verts with very weak thigh weight (<10%) to prevent
      // posterior mesh from hanging.
      const _existingThighW = (rw.get(1) || 0) + (rw.get(2) || 0);
      if (rw.has(0) && _existingThighW < 0.10 && P.l_hip && P.r_hip && P.l_knee && P.r_knee) {
        const vx = meshRestPos[vi*3], vy = meshRestPos[vi*3+1], vz = meshRestPos[vi*3+2];
        const hipY = Math.max(P.l_hip[1], P.r_hip[1]);
        // Verts at/below hip + 6cm (catches upper buttock/sacrum area)
        if (vy <= hipY + 0.06) {
          const midX = (P.l_hip[0] + P.r_hip[0]) / 2;
          const isL = (vx - midX) <= 0;
          const hip = isL ? P.l_hip : P.r_hip;
          const knee = isL ? P.l_knee : P.r_knee;
          const thighLen = Math.sqrt((knee[0]-hip[0])**2 + (knee[1]-hip[1])**2 + (knee[2]-hip[2])**2);
          const hdx = vx-hip[0], hdy = vy-hip[1], hdz = vz-hip[2];
          const hipDist = Math.sqrt(hdx*hdx + hdy*hdy + hdz*hdz);
          const maxDist = 0.85 * thighLen; // ~14.5cm from hip
          if (hipDist < maxDist) {
            const f = 1 - hipDist / maxDist;
            const sf = f * f * (3 - 2 * f); // smoothstep
            const inject = sf * 0.45; // up to 45% thigh at hip
            if (inject > 0.02) {
              const thighRid = isL ? 1 : 2;
              const take = Math.min(inject, rw.get(0) * 0.6);
              rw.set(0, rw.get(0) - take);
              rw.set(thighRid, (rw.get(thighRid) || 0) + take);
              _hipThighInjected++;
            }
          }
        }
      }

      // Smoothstep hip weight override: torso (0) ↔ thigh (1/2)
      // Without this, the torso-thigh boundary has no smooth transition,
      // causing stretching when hip twist or thigh rotations differ from torso.
      // HBAND = 0.25 * thighLen → gradient spans 25% above hip to 25% below hip.
      // Narrow band keeps thigh verts mostly pure-thigh so they follow the leg.
      // Too wide (0.60) caused thigh mesh to stick out — verts 30%+ down the
      // thigh still had 25%+ torso weight, making them lag behind the leg.
      if (P.l_hip && P.l_knee) {
        const vx = meshRestPos[vi*3], vy = meshRestPos[vi*3+1], vz = meshRestPos[vi*3+2];
        // Left hip: regions 0 (torso) + 1 (l_thigh)
        if (rw.has(0) && rw.has(1)) {
          const hip = P.l_hip, knee = P.l_knee;
          const thighLen = Math.sqrt((knee[0]-hip[0])**2 + (knee[1]-hip[1])**2 + (knee[2]-hip[2])**2);
          const d = [(knee[0]-hip[0])/thighLen, (knee[1]-hip[1])/thighLen, (knee[2]-hip[2])/thighLen];
          const tp = (vx-hip[0])*d[0] + (vy-hip[1])*d[1] + (vz-hip[2])*d[2];
          const HBAND = CFG('HBAND_FRAC', 0.35) * thighLen;
          if (Math.abs(tp) < HBAND * 2) {
            const totalW = rw.get(0) + rw.get(1);
            const f = clamp((tp + HBAND) / (2 * HBAND), 0, 1);
            const sf = f * f * (3 - 2 * f); // smoothstep
            rw.set(0, totalW * (1 - sf));
            rw.set(1, totalW * sf);
          }
        }
        // Right hip: regions 0 (torso) + 2 (r_thigh)
        if (rw.has(0) && rw.has(2) && P.r_hip && P.r_knee) {
          const hip = P.r_hip, knee = P.r_knee;
          const thighLen = Math.sqrt((knee[0]-hip[0])**2 + (knee[1]-hip[1])**2 + (knee[2]-hip[2])**2);
          const d = [(knee[0]-hip[0])/thighLen, (knee[1]-hip[1])/thighLen, (knee[2]-hip[2])/thighLen];
          const tp = (vx-hip[0])*d[0] + (vy-hip[1])*d[1] + (vz-hip[2])*d[2];
          const HBAND = CFG('HBAND_FRAC', 0.35) * thighLen;
          if (Math.abs(tp) < HBAND * 2) {
            const totalW = rw.get(0) + rw.get(2);
            const f = clamp((tp + HBAND) / (2 * HBAND), 0, 1);
            const sf = f * f * (3 - 2 * f); // smoothstep
            rw.set(0, totalW * (1 - sf));
            rw.set(2, totalW * sf);
          }
        }
      }

      // Thigh consolidation: if a vert has both L_thigh(1) and R_thigh(2)
      // weight, redistribute based on proximity to each thigh bone.
      // GENTLE sigmoid (factor 3) creates a WIDE blend zone at the midline.
      // Midline verts keep ~50/50 weight → deform to average of both legs
      // → naturally stays at crotch. No strand. This mimics FBX smooth
      // weight painting where crotch verts blend both thighs.
      if (P.l_hip && P.r_hip && P.l_knee && P.r_knee && rw.has(1) && rw.has(2)) {
        const vx = meshRestPos[vi*3], vy = meshRestPos[vi*3+1], vz = meshRestPos[vi*3+2];
        const dL = pointToSegmentDist(vx, vy, vz, P.l_hip, P.l_knee);
        const dR = pointToSegmentDist(vx, vy, vz, P.r_hip, P.r_knee);
        const lw = rw.get(1), rwv = rw.get(2);
        const totalThigh = lw + rwv;
        const sum = dL + dR;
        if (sum > 1e-6) {
          // rFrac: 0 = on left bone, 0.5 = equidistant, 1 = on right bone
          const rFrac = dL / sum;
          // Gentle sigmoid: wide blend zone so midline verts keep both-thigh weight.
          // Factor 3: blend zone spans ~rFrac 0.33 to 0.67 (vs factor 20 at 0.475-0.525).
          // At rFrac=0.33: 100% left. At 0.45: ~80% left 20% right. At 0.50: 50/50.
          const steepT = clamp((rFrac - 0.5) * 3 + 0.5, 0, 1);
          const sf = steepT * steepT * (3 - 2 * steepT); // smoothstep
          const newR = totalThigh * sf;
          const newL = totalThigh * (1 - sf);
          if (newL > 0.001) rw.set(1, newL); else rw.delete(1);
          if (newR > 0.001) rw.set(2, newR); else rw.delete(2);
          // Track consolidation for diagnostics
          const transferred = Math.abs((lw - newL)) + Math.abs((rwv - newR));
          if (transferred > 0.01) {
            _bpSuppressed++;
            _bpTotalDelta += transferred;
            if (vi === 52138 || vi === 51922 || vi === 1819 || _bpSuppressed <= 5) {
              _bpTracked[vi] = { dL: dL.toFixed(4), dR: dR.toFixed(4),
                origL: lw.toFixed(3), origR: rwv.toFixed(3),
                newL: newL.toFixed(3), newR: newR.toFixed(3) };
            }
          }
        }
      }

      // Cross-thigh weight INJECTION: for midline verts that only have ONE thigh
      // weight, inject opposite thigh weight based on bone proximity.
      // The consolidation above only works on verts that ALREADY have both thigh
      // weights. KNN assigns most midline verts to a single thigh. Without this
      // injection, the midline has a razor-sharp boundary → crotch strand.
      // With injection, midline verts get ~50/50 split → deform to average of
      // both legs → naturally stay at the crotch. This is how FBX skinning works.
      if (P.l_hip && P.r_hip && P.l_knee && P.r_knee) {
        const hasL = rw.has(1), hasR = rw.has(2);
        if ((hasL || hasR) && !(hasL && hasR)) { // has one thigh but not both
          const vx = meshRestPos[vi*3], vy = meshRestPos[vi*3+1], vz = meshRestPos[vi*3+2];
          const hipY = Math.max(P.l_hip[1], P.r_hip[1]);
          const kneeY = Math.min(P.l_knee[1], P.r_knee[1]);
          // Crotch zone: hip to 80% down the thigh
          const lowerBound = hipY + 0.80 * (kneeY - hipY);
          if (vy <= hipY + 0.02 && vy >= lowerBound) {
            const dL = pointToSegmentDist(vx, vy, vz, P.l_hip, P.l_knee);
            const dR = pointToSegmentDist(vx, vy, vz, P.r_hip, P.r_knee);
            const sum = dL + dR;
            if (sum > 1e-6) {
              const rFrac = dL / sum; // 0=on left bone, 0.5=equidistant, 1=on right
              // Only inject in the blend zone (0.30 to 0.70 ratio range)
              if (rFrac > 0.30 && rFrac < 0.70) {
                const existingRid = hasL ? 1 : 2;
                const oppositeRid = hasL ? 2 : 1;
                const existingW = rw.get(existingRid);
                // Compute ideal split using same gentle sigmoid as consolidation
                const steepT = clamp((rFrac - 0.5) * 3 + 0.5, 0, 1);
                const sf = steepT * steepT * (3 - 2 * steepT);
                // sf: ideal fraction for right thigh. (1-sf) for left thigh.
                const idealOpposite = hasL ? sf : (1 - sf);
                const inject = existingW * idealOpposite;
                if (inject > 0.01) {
                  rw.set(existingRid, existingW - inject);
                  rw.set(oppositeRid, inject);
                }
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }

      // Crotch bulge correction: for midline verts with balanced cross-thigh
      // weight, add small pelvis weight to pull them inward. Linear blending of
      // two rotated thigh positions overshoots outward; pelvis acts as anchor.
      // Max 20% pelvis at exact midline, tapering to 0 at blend zone edges.
      if (rw.has(1) && rw.has(2) && P.pelvis) {
        const ltw = rw.get(1), rtw = rw.get(2);
        const totalThigh = ltw + rtw;
        if (totalThigh > 0.05) {
          const balance = 1.0 - 2.0 * Math.abs(ltw - rtw) / totalThigh; // 1=50/50, 0=100/0
          if (balance > 0.3) {
            const pelvisInject = balance * 0.20 * totalThigh; // up to 20% at midline
            if (pelvisInject > 0.01) {
              const halfTake = pelvisInject / 2;
              rw.set(1, ltw - halfTake);
              rw.set(2, rtw - halfTake);
              rw.set(14, (rw.get(14) || 0) + pelvisInject);
            }
          }
        }
      }

      // Knee boundary weight blending: thigh (1/2) ↔ shin (3/4)
      // The knee boundary has 16.5x stretch — verts at 100% thigh vs 100% shin
      // rip apart when the knee bends. Fix: inject missing adjacent weight and
      // smoothstep the distribution near the knee joint. Same approach as the
      // crotch cross-thigh injection that fixed the strand.
      // Left knee: thigh(1) ↔ shin(3)
      if ((rw.has(1) || rw.has(3)) && P.l_hip && P.l_knee && P.l_ankle) {
        const vx = meshRestPos[vi*3], vy = meshRestPos[vi*3+1], vz = meshRestPos[vi*3+2];
        const hip = P.l_hip, knee = P.l_knee;
        const thighLen = Math.sqrt((knee[0]-hip[0])**2+(knee[1]-hip[1])**2+(knee[2]-hip[2])**2);
        if (thighLen > 1e-6) {
          const td = [(knee[0]-hip[0])/thighLen, (knee[1]-hip[1])/thighLen, (knee[2]-hip[2])/thighLen];
          const tProj = (vx-hip[0])*td[0] + (vy-hip[1])*td[1] + (vz-hip[2])*td[2];
          const tFrac = tProj / thighLen; // 0=hip, 1=knee, >1=past knee into shin

          // Blend band: tFrac 0.82 to 1.18 (knee ± 18% of thigh length)
          const KBAND_LO = CFG('KBAND_LO', 0.70), KBAND_HI = CFG('KBAND_HI', 1.30);
          if (tFrac > KBAND_LO && tFrac < KBAND_HI) {
            const thighW = rw.get(1) || 0;
            const shinW = rw.get(3) || 0;
            const totalKnee = thighW + shinW;
            if (totalKnee > 0.01) {
              // f: 0 at KBAND_LO (pure thigh), 1 at KBAND_HI (pure shin)
              const f = clamp((tFrac - KBAND_LO) / (KBAND_HI - KBAND_LO), 0, 1);
              const sf = f * f * (3 - 2 * f);
              const newShin = totalKnee * sf;
              const newThigh = totalKnee * (1 - sf);
              if (newThigh > 0.001) rw.set(1, newThigh); else rw.delete(1);
              if (newShin > 0.001) rw.set(3, newShin); else rw.delete(3);
            }
          }
        }
      }
      // Right knee: thigh(2) ↔ shin(4)
      if ((rw.has(2) || rw.has(4)) && P.r_hip && P.r_knee && P.r_ankle) {
        const vx = meshRestPos[vi*3], vy = meshRestPos[vi*3+1], vz = meshRestPos[vi*3+2];
        const hip = P.r_hip, knee = P.r_knee;
        const thighLen = Math.sqrt((knee[0]-hip[0])**2+(knee[1]-hip[1])**2+(knee[2]-hip[2])**2);
        if (thighLen > 1e-6) {
          const td = [(knee[0]-hip[0])/thighLen, (knee[1]-hip[1])/thighLen, (knee[2]-hip[2])/thighLen];
          const tProj = (vx-hip[0])*td[0] + (vy-hip[1])*td[1] + (vz-hip[2])*td[2];
          const tFrac = tProj / thighLen;

          const KBAND_LO = CFG('KBAND_LO', 0.70), KBAND_HI = CFG('KBAND_HI', 1.30);
          if (tFrac > KBAND_LO && tFrac < KBAND_HI) {
            const thighW = rw.get(2) || 0;
            const shinW = rw.get(4) || 0;
            const totalKnee = thighW + shinW;
            if (totalKnee > 0.01) {
              const f = clamp((tFrac - KBAND_LO) / (KBAND_HI - KBAND_LO), 0, 1);
              const sf = f * f * (3 - 2 * f);
              const newShin = totalKnee * sf;
              const newThigh = totalKnee * (1 - sf);
              if (newThigh > 0.001) rw.set(2, newThigh); else rw.delete(2);
              if (newShin > 0.001) rw.set(4, newShin); else rw.delete(4);
            }
          }
        }
      }

      // Smoothstep ankle weight override: shin (3/4) ↔ foot (5/6)
      if (P.l_knee && P.l_ankle) {
        const vx = meshRestPos[vi*3], vy = meshRestPos[vi*3+1], vz = meshRestPos[vi*3+2];
        // Left ankle: regions 3 (shin) + 5 (foot)
        if (rw.has(3) && rw.has(5)) {
          const knee = P.l_knee, ankle = P.l_ankle;
          const shinLen = Math.sqrt((ankle[0]-knee[0])**2 + (ankle[1]-knee[1])**2 + (ankle[2]-knee[2])**2);
          const d = [(ankle[0]-knee[0])/shinLen, (ankle[1]-knee[1])/shinLen, (ankle[2]-knee[2])/shinLen];
          const tp = (vx-knee[0])*d[0] + (vy-knee[1])*d[1] + (vz-knee[2])*d[2];
          const ABAND = CFG('ABAND_FRAC', 0.20) * shinLen;
          if (Math.abs(tp - shinLen) < ABAND * 2) {
            const totalW = rw.get(3) + rw.get(5);
            const f = clamp((tp - (shinLen - ABAND)) / (2 * ABAND), 0, 1);
            const sf = f * f * (3 - 2 * f);
            rw.set(3, totalW * (1 - sf));
            rw.set(5, totalW * sf);
          }
        }
        // Right ankle: regions 4 (shin) + 6 (foot)
        if (rw.has(4) && rw.has(6) && P.r_knee && P.r_ankle) {
          const knee = P.r_knee, ankle = P.r_ankle;
          const shinLen = Math.sqrt((ankle[0]-knee[0])**2 + (ankle[1]-knee[1])**2 + (ankle[2]-knee[2])**2);
          const d = [(ankle[0]-knee[0])/shinLen, (ankle[1]-knee[1])/shinLen, (ankle[2]-knee[2])/shinLen];
          const tp = (vx-knee[0])*d[0] + (vy-knee[1])*d[1] + (vz-knee[2])*d[2];
          const ABAND = CFG('ABAND_FRAC', 0.20) * shinLen;
          if (Math.abs(tp - shinLen) < ABAND * 2) {
            const totalW = rw.get(4) + rw.get(6);
            const f = clamp((tp - (shinLen - ABAND)) / (2 * ABAND), 0, 1);
            const sf = f * f * (3 - 2 * f);
            rw.set(4, totalW * (1 - sf));
            rw.set(6, totalW * sf);
          }
        }
      }

      // Smoothstep foot↔toe boundary: foot (5/6) ↔ toe (21/22)
      if (P.l_toe && P.l_ankle) {
        const vx = meshRestPos[vi*3], vy = meshRestPos[vi*3+1], vz = meshRestPos[vi*3+2];
        if (rw.has(5) && rw.has(21)) {
          const ankle = P.l_ankle, toe = P.l_toe;
          const footLen = Math.sqrt((toe[0]-ankle[0])**2+(toe[1]-ankle[1])**2+(toe[2]-ankle[2])**2);
          const d = [(toe[0]-ankle[0])/footLen,(toe[1]-ankle[1])/footLen,(toe[2]-ankle[2])/footLen];
          const tp = (vx-ankle[0])*d[0]+(vy-ankle[1])*d[1]+(vz-ankle[2])*d[2];
          const TBAND = 0.15 * footLen;
          const toeThresh = footLen * 0.8;
          if (Math.abs(tp - toeThresh) < TBAND * 2) {
            const totalW = rw.get(5) + rw.get(21);
            const f = clamp((tp - (toeThresh - TBAND)) / (2 * TBAND), 0, 1);
            const sf = f * f * (3 - 2 * f);
            rw.set(5, totalW * (1 - sf));
            rw.set(21, totalW * sf);
          }
        }
        if (rw.has(6) && rw.has(22) && P.r_toe && P.r_ankle) {
          const ankle = P.r_ankle, toe = P.r_toe;
          const footLen = Math.sqrt((toe[0]-ankle[0])**2+(toe[1]-ankle[1])**2+(toe[2]-ankle[2])**2);
          const d = [(toe[0]-ankle[0])/footLen,(toe[1]-ankle[1])/footLen,(toe[2]-ankle[2])/footLen];
          const tp = (vx-ankle[0])*d[0]+(vy-ankle[1])*d[1]+(vz-ankle[2])*d[2];
          const TBAND = 0.15 * footLen;
          const toeThresh = footLen * 0.8;
          if (Math.abs(tp - toeThresh) < TBAND * 2) {
            const totalW = rw.get(6) + rw.get(22);
            const f = clamp((tp - (toeThresh - TBAND)) / (2 * TBAND), 0, 1);
            const sf = f * f * (3 - 2 * f);
            rw.set(6, totalW * (1 - sf));
            rw.set(22, totalW * sf);
          }
        }
      }

      // Neck weight injection: head verts near the neck need some torso weight
      // so the neck creates a smooth rotation transition. Without this, face verts
      // get 100% head transform and rotate rigidly around the neck pivot with no
      // gradient. The zone extends from well below the neck into the lower face
      // (chin/jaw area) to create a natural neck bending effect.
      if (P.neck && P.head && P.pelvis && rw.has(11) && !rw.has(0)) {
        const vy = meshRestPos[vi*3+1];
        const neckY = P.neck[1];
        const headY = P.head[1];
        const neckToHead = headY - neckY;
        // Injection zone: from 8cm below neck to 40% of the way up toward head center
        // This covers the entire neck cylinder + chin + lower jaw
        const NECK_INJECT_TOP = neckToHead * 0.4;
        const NECK_INJECT_DEPTH = 0.08;
        if (vy < neckY + NECK_INJECT_TOP && vy > neckY - NECK_INJECT_DEPTH) {
          const f = clamp((neckY + NECK_INJECT_TOP - vy) / (NECK_INJECT_TOP + NECK_INJECT_DEPTH), 0, 1);
          const sf = f * f * (3 - 2 * f); // smoothstep
          const inject = sf * 0.45; // up to 45% torso at bottom of zone
          if (inject > 0.02) {
            const headW = rw.get(11);
            const take = headW * inject;
            rw.set(11, headW - take);
            rw.set(0, take);
            _neckInjected++;
          }
        }
      }

      // Smoothstep neck weight override: torso (0) ↔ head (11)
      if (P.pelvis && P.neck) {
        const vx = meshRestPos[vi*3], vy = meshRestPos[vi*3+1], vz = meshRestPos[vi*3+2];
        if (rw.has(0) && rw.has(11)) {
          const pelvis = P.pelvis, neck = P.neck;
          const spineLen = Math.sqrt((neck[0]-pelvis[0])**2 + (neck[1]-pelvis[1])**2 + (neck[2]-pelvis[2])**2);
          const d = [(neck[0]-pelvis[0])/spineLen, (neck[1]-pelvis[1])/spineLen, (neck[2]-pelvis[2])/spineLen];
          const tp = (vx-pelvis[0])*d[0] + (vy-pelvis[1])*d[1] + (vz-pelvis[2])*d[2];
          const NBAND = 0.18 * spineLen; // wider neck blend band to match expanded injection
          if (Math.abs(tp - spineLen) < NBAND * 2) {
            const totalW = rw.get(0) + rw.get(11);
            const f = clamp((tp - (spineLen - NBAND)) / (2 * NBAND), 0, 1);
            const sf = f * f * (3 - 2 * f);
            rw.set(0, totalW * (1 - sf));
            rw.set(11, totalW * sf);
          }
        }
      }

      // Restore spine sub-regions: distribute the combined region-0 weight back to
      // the original spine sub-regions using Y-based smoothstep boundaries.
      if (_hadSpineSubs && rw.has(0) && P.pelvis && P.neck) {
        const vy = meshRestPos[vi*3+1];
        const bodyW = rw.get(0);
        rw.delete(0);

        if (P.spine_joint && P.spine1_joint && P.spine2_joint) {
          // Full spine split: Y-based smoothstep into 5 spine sub-regions
          const spineY  = (P.pelvis[1] + P.spine_joint[1]) / 2;
          const spine1Y = (P.spine_joint[1] + P.spine1_joint[1]) / 2;
          const spine2Y = (P.spine1_joint[1] + P.spine2_joint[1]) / 2;
          const chestY  = (P.spine2_joint[1] + P.neck[1]) / 2;
          const SBAND = CFG('SBAND_SPINE', 0.05); // 5cm blend band between adjacent spine segments

          // Compute fraction for each spine sub-region using overlapping smoothsteps
          const ss = (y, center) => {
            const f = clamp((y - (center - SBAND)) / (2 * SBAND), 0, 1);
            return f * f * (3 - 2 * f);
          };
          // Cumulative smoothsteps: each boundary transitions from lower to upper
          const f0 = ss(vy, spineY);   // 0→1: pelvis→spine
          const f1 = ss(vy, spine1Y);  // 0→1: spine→spine1
          const f2 = ss(vy, spine2Y);  // 0→1: spine1→spine2
          const f3 = ss(vy, chestY);   // 0→1: spine2→chest

          // Weight for each segment = (transition in) - (transition out)
          const wPelvis = (1 - f0);
          const wSpine  = f0 - f1;
          const wSpine1 = f1 - f2;
          const wSpine2 = f2 - f3;
          const wChest  = f3;

          if (wPelvis > 0.001) rw.set(14, bodyW * wPelvis);
          if (wSpine > 0.001)  rw.set(0,  bodyW * wSpine);
          if (wSpine1 > 0.001) rw.set(15, bodyW * wSpine1);
          if (wSpine2 > 0.001) rw.set(16, bodyW * wSpine2);
          if (wChest > 0.001)  rw.set(17, bodyW * wChest);
        } else {
          // Fallback: simple pelvis/torso split
          const pelvisThreshY = P.pelvis[1] + 0.07;
          const BLEND_BAND = 0.06;
          const f = clamp((vy - (pelvisThreshY - BLEND_BAND/2)) / BLEND_BAND, 0, 1);
          const sf = f * f * (3 - 2 * f);
          if (sf > 0.001) rw.set(0, bodyW * sf);
          if ((1-sf) > 0.001) rw.set(14, bodyW * (1 - sf));
        }

        // Restore collar/neck sub-regions if they were merged
        // (collar and neck verts should keep their sub-region identity)
        if (_spineSubRegions[18] && rw.has(11)) {
          // Neck verts near head: already have head weight, add neck weight
          // This is handled by the neck injection code above
        }
        if (_spineSubRegions[19]) rw.set(19, _spineSubRegions[19]);
        if (_spineSubRegions[20]) rw.set(20, _spineSubRegions[20]);
      } else if (_hadSpineSubs && rw.has(0)) {
        // Fallback: binary restore to pelvis
        rw.set(14, rw.get(0));
        rw.delete(0);
      }

      // Pelvis weight injection for inner-thigh/crotch verts: verts near the
      // midline between the two thighs need pelvis weight to prevent stretching.
      // Uses bone-axis projection for distance + medial factor so only inner-
      // thigh (crotch) verts get heavy injection. Outer/front/back thigh verts
      // get minimal injection to avoid "hanging mesh" when the thigh swings.
      if ((rw.has(1) || rw.has(2)) && P.l_hip && P.r_hip && P.l_knee && P.r_knee) {
        const vx = meshRestPos[vi*3], vy = meshRestPos[vi*3+1], vz = meshRestPos[vi*3+2];
        const hipY = Math.max(P.l_hip[1], P.r_hip[1]);
        const kneeY = Math.min(P.l_knee[1], P.r_knee[1]);
        const midThighY = (hipY + kneeY) / 2;
        if (vy >= midThighY) {
          const isL = rw.has(1) && (!rw.has(2) || rw.get(1) >= rw.get(2));
          const hip = isL ? P.l_hip : P.r_hip;
          const knee = isL ? P.l_knee : P.r_knee;
          const thighLen = Math.sqrt((knee[0]-hip[0])**2 + (knee[1]-hip[1])**2 + (knee[2]-hip[2])**2);
          // Thigh bone direction (hip → knee)
          const bdx = (knee[0]-hip[0])/thighLen, bdy = (knee[1]-hip[1])/thighLen, bdz = (knee[2]-hip[2])/thighLen;
          // Project vert onto thigh bone axis
          const vhx = vx-hip[0], vhy = vy-hip[1], vhz = vz-hip[2];
          const proj = vhx*bdx + vhy*bdy + vhz*bdz;
          const projFrac = proj / thighLen;
          if (false) { // DISABLED — zero pelvis on thighs
            // Pelvis injection disabled: thigh verts follow thighs only.
            const hipHalfSpread = Math.abs(P.r_hip[0] - P.l_hip[0]) / 2;
            const medialDist = isL ? (vx - hip[0]) : (hip[0] - vx);
            const medialFrac = clamp(medialDist / (hipHalfSpread * 0.7), 0, 1);
            // Inner groin: up to 15% pelvis. Outer: up to 5%
            const maxPelvis = 0.05 + 0.10 * medialFrac;
            const axialTaper = 1 - Math.max(0, projFrac) / 0.15;
            const desiredPelvis = axialTaper * maxPelvis;
            const existingPelvis = rw.get(14) || 0;
            // Convert existing torso(0) to pelvis(14) for medial hip-area verts
            if (rw.has(0) && medialFrac > 0.3) {
              const t0 = rw.get(0);
              rw.set(14, existingPelvis + t0);
              rw.delete(0);
            }
            const curPelvis = rw.get(14) || 0;
            const addPelvis = desiredPelvis - curPelvis;
            if (addPelvis > 0.01) {
              const thighRid = isL ? 1 : 2;
              const thighW = rw.get(thighRid) || 0;
              const take = Math.min(addPelvis, thighW * 0.20);
              if (take > 0.01) {
                rw.set(thighRid, thighW - take);
                rw.set(14, curPelvis + take);
                _pelvisInjected++;
              }
            } else if (rw.has(14)) {
              _pelvisInjected++;
            }
          }
        }
      }

      // THIGH WEIGHT ENFORCEMENT: removed. The cross-thigh injection above
      // handles the crotch midline, and the hip boundary smoothing handles
      // the torso→thigh gradient. The old enforcement forced 60%+ thigh on
      // butt/hip verts that should be torso-dominant, creating holes.

      // Adjacency filter → cap max-4 → rigid core → renormalize.
      // Dominant region = highest weight in rw (deterministic at bind time).
      {
        // Step 1: Determine dominant region from computed weights
        let domRid = 0, domW = 0;
        for (const [rid, w] of rw) {
          if (w > domW) { domRid = rid; domW = w; }
        }
        const adjSet = new Set(REGION_ADJ[domRid] || [domRid]);

        // Step 2: Collect entries, filter to adjacent, sort descending
        const sorted = [];
        for (const [rid, w] of rw) {
          if (adjSet.has(rid)) {
            sorted.push({ rid, w });
          } else {
            _adjFiltered++;
          }
        }
        sorted.sort((a, b) => b.w - a.w);

        // Step 3: Cap at 4 influences
        if (sorted.length > 4) sorted.length = 4;

        // Step 4: Rigid core — if dominant weight > 0.9, keep only 1 influence
        if (sorted.length > 1 && sorted[0].w > 0.9) sorted.length = 1;

        // Step 5: Renormalize
        let sum = 0;
        for (const e of sorted) sum += e.w;

        // Flatten to [rid, weight, rid, weight, ...]
        const entries = [];
        if (sum > 0) {
          for (const e of sorted) entries.push(e.rid, e.w / sum);
        }
        result[vi] = entries;
      }
    }

    // Mesh influence diagnostic
    {
      let totalInfluences = 0, maxInfluences = 0, rigidCount = 0;
      for (let vi = 0; vi < nMesh; vi++) {
        const e = result[vi];
        const nInf = e ? e.length / 2 : 0;
        totalInfluences += nInf;
        if (nInf > maxInfluences) maxInfluences = nInf;
        if (nInf === 1) rigidCount++;
      }
      console.log('[MESH INFLUENCES] avg=' + (totalInfluences / nMesh).toFixed(2) +
        ', max=' + maxInfluences + ', rigid(1-inf)=' + rigidCount + '/' + nMesh +
        ', adjFiltered=' + _adjFiltered);
    }

    // Safety-net: force-inject thigh weight for any crotch vert that still has none.
    // This catches the ~14 verts that slip through both crotch and hip injection.
    if (P.l_hip && P.r_hip && P.pelvis && P.l_knee && P.r_knee) {
      const pelvisY = P.pelvis[1];
      const midX = (P.l_hip[0] + P.r_hip[0]) / 2;
      let forced = 0;
      for (let vi = 0; vi < nMesh; vi++) {
        const vx = meshRestPos[vi*3], vy = meshRestPos[vi*3+1];
        if (vy > pelvisY - 0.12 && vy < pelvisY + 0.02 &&
            vx > P.l_hip[0] * 0.8 && vx < P.r_hip[0] * 0.8) {
          const e = result[vi];
          let thighW = 0, torsoW = 0;
          for (let i = 0; i < e.length; i += 2) {
            if (e[i] === 1 || e[i] === 2) thighW += e[i+1];
            if (e[i] === 0) torsoW += e[i+1];
          }
          if (thighW < 0.01 && torsoW > 0.1) {
            // Force inject: pick nearer thigh, take 40% from torso
            const isL = (vx - midX) <= 0;
            const thighRid = isL ? 1 : 2;
            const take = torsoW * 0.4;
            // Rebuild entry array
            const rw = new Map();
            for (let i = 0; i < e.length; i += 2) rw.set(e[i], e[i+1]);
            rw.set(0, torsoW - take);
            rw.set(thighRid, (rw.get(thighRid) || 0) + take);
            const flat = [];
            for (const [rid, w] of rw) { if (w > 0.001) { flat.push(rid, w); } }
            result[vi] = new Float32Array(flat);
            forced++;
          }
        }
      }
      if (forced > 0) console.log('[CROTCH SAFETY] Force-injected thigh weight on ' + forced + ' torso-only crotch verts');
    }

    // One-shot crotch weight diagnostic
    if (P.l_hip && P.r_hip && P.pelvis) {
      const pelvisY = P.pelvis[1];
      let crotchVerts = 0, torsoOnly = 0, hasThigh = 0;
      let minThighW = 1, maxThighW = 0, sumThighW = 0;
      for (let vi = 0; vi < nMesh; vi++) {
        const vy = meshRestPos[vi*3+1], vx = meshRestPos[vi*3];
        if (vy > pelvisY - 0.08 && vy < pelvisY + 0.02 &&
            vx > P.l_hip[0] * 0.8 && vx < P.r_hip[0] * 0.8) {
          crotchVerts++;
          const e = result[vi];
          let thighW = 0, torsoW = 0;
          for (let i = 0; i < e.length; i += 2) {
            if (e[i] === 1 || e[i] === 2) thighW += e[i+1];
            if (e[i] === 0) torsoW += e[i+1];
          }
          if (thighW < 0.01) torsoOnly++;
          else {
            hasThigh++;
            sumThighW += thighW;
            if (thighW < minThighW) minThighW = thighW;
            if (thighW > maxThighW) maxThighW = thighW;
          }
        }
      }
      console.log('=== CROTCH WEIGHT DIAGNOSTIC ===');
      console.log('  Crotch verts: ' + crotchVerts + ' (torso-only: ' + torsoOnly + ', has thigh: ' + hasThigh + ')');
      if (hasThigh > 0) console.log('  Thigh weight: min=' + minThighW.toFixed(3) + ' max=' + maxThighW.toFixed(3) + ' avg=' + (sumThighW/hasThigh).toFixed(3));
      if (torsoOnly > 0) console.log('  WARNING: ' + torsoOnly + ' crotch verts have NO thigh weight — they will stretch as webbing!');
    }

    // Thigh consolidation diagnostic
    console.log('=== THIGH CONSOLIDATION (nearest-bone assignment) ===');
    console.log('  Verts consolidated: ' + _bpSuppressed + '  total weight moved: ' + _bpTotalDelta.toFixed(3));
    for (const [vid, info] of Object.entries(_bpTracked)) {
      console.log('  v' + vid + ' dL=' + info.dL + ' dR=' + info.dR +
        ' orig[L=' + info.origL + ' R=' + info.origR + '] → new[L=' + info.newL + ' R=' + info.newR + ']');
    }

    // Cross-thigh blend zone diagnostic: count verts with significant both-thigh weight
    {
      let blendVerts = 0, blendMin = 999, blendMax = 0, blendSum = 0;
      let sharpBoundary = 0; // verts with >90% one thigh & near midline
      for (let vi = 0; vi < nMesh; vi++) {
        const e = result[vi];
        if (!e) continue;
        let ltw = 0, rtw = 0;
        for (let j = 0; j < e.length; j += 2) {
          if (e[j] === 1) ltw = e[j + 1];
          else if (e[j] === 2) rtw = e[j + 1];
        }
        if (ltw > 0.05 && rtw > 0.05) {
          blendVerts++;
          const minW = Math.min(ltw, rtw);
          if (minW < blendMin) blendMin = minW;
          if (minW > blendMax) blendMax = minW;
          blendSum += minW;
        }
        // Sharp boundary: >90% one thigh, but near midline (inner thigh)
        const tot = ltw + rtw;
        if (tot > 0.5) {
          const vx = meshRestPos[vi * 3];
          const midX = P.pelvis ? P.pelvis[0] : 0;
          const hipSpread = Math.abs(P.l_hip[0] - P.r_hip[0]);
          if (Math.abs(vx - midX) < hipSpread * 0.3) {
            const dominant = Math.max(ltw, rtw) / tot;
            if (dominant > 0.9) sharpBoundary++;
          }
        }
      }
      console.log('=== CROSS-THIGH BLEND ZONE ===');
      console.log('  Verts with both L+R thigh >5%: ' + blendVerts);
      if (blendVerts > 0) {
        console.log('  Minor thigh weight: min=' + blendMin.toFixed(3) + ' max=' + blendMax.toFixed(3) +
          ' avg=' + (blendSum / blendVerts).toFixed(3));
      }
      console.log('  Sharp boundary verts near midline (>90% one thigh): ' + sharpBoundary);
      if (sharpBoundary > 0 && blendVerts < 20) {
        console.log('  WARNING: blend zone too narrow! Increase sigmoid factor or check consolidation.');
      }
    }

    // Posterior weight diagnostic: show final weights for back-of-hip verts
    if (P.l_hip && P.r_hip) {
      const hipMidZ = (P.l_hip[2] + P.r_hip[2]) / 2;
      const hipY = Math.max(P.l_hip[1], P.r_hip[1]);
      let posteriorCount = 0, posteriorWithThigh = 0, posteriorAvgThigh = 0;
      for (let vi = 0; vi < nMesh; vi++) {
        const vz = meshRestPos[vi*3+2], vy = meshRestPos[vi*3+1];
        if (vz < hipMidZ - 0.02 && vy <= hipY + 0.02 && vy >= hipY - 0.15) {
          posteriorCount++;
          const rw = result[vi];
          if (rw) {
            let thighW = 0;
            for (let e = 0; e < rw.length; e += 2) {
              if (rw[e] === 1 || rw[e] === 2) thighW += rw[e+1];
            }
            if (thighW > 0.01) { posteriorWithThigh++; posteriorAvgThigh += thighW; }
          }
        }
      }
      console.log('=== POSTERIOR (BUTT) VERT DIAGNOSTIC ===');
      console.log('  Posterior verts (Z < hipZ-2cm, near hip height): ' + posteriorCount);
      console.log('  With thigh weight > 1%: ' + posteriorWithThigh +
        ' avg thighW: ' + (posteriorWithThigh > 0 ? (posteriorAvgThigh/posteriorWithThigh).toFixed(3) : 'N/A'));
    }

    // Hip thigh injection diagnostic
    console.log('=== HIP THIGH INJECTION (butt/back-of-hip) ===');
    console.log('  Torso verts given thigh weight: ' + _hipThighInjected);

    // Knee shin injection diagnostic
    console.log('=== KNEE SHIN INJECTION (thigh-only verts given shin weight) ===');
    console.log('  Verts injected: ' + _kneeShinInjected);

    // Knee boundary blend diagnostic
    if (P.l_hip && P.l_knee && P.l_ankle) {
      let kneeBlended = 0, kneeThighOnly = 0, kneeShinOnly = 0;
      const knee = P.l_knee, hip2 = P.l_hip;
      const thighLen2 = Math.sqrt((knee[0]-hip2[0])**2+(knee[1]-hip2[1])**2+(knee[2]-hip2[2])**2);
      const td2 = [(knee[0]-hip2[0])/thighLen2, (knee[1]-hip2[1])/thighLen2, (knee[2]-hip2[2])/thighLen2];
      for (let vi = 0; vi < nMesh; vi++) {
        const vx = meshRestPos[vi*3], vy = meshRestPos[vi*3+1], vz = meshRestPos[vi*3+2];
        const tProj = (vx-hip2[0])*td2[0] + (vy-hip2[1])*td2[1] + (vz-hip2[2])*td2[2];
        const tFrac = tProj / thighLen2;
        if (tFrac > 0.75 && tFrac < 1.25) {
          const e = result[vi];
          if (!e) continue;
          let tw = 0, sw = 0;
          for (let j = 0; j < e.length; j += 2) {
            if (e[j] === 1) tw = e[j+1];
            else if (e[j] === 3) sw = e[j+1];
          }
          if (tw > 0.05 && sw > 0.05) kneeBlended++;
          else if (tw > 0.05) kneeThighOnly++;
          else if (sw > 0.05) kneeShinOnly++;
        }
      }
      console.log('=== LEFT KNEE BOUNDARY BLEND ===');
      console.log('  Verts in knee band (tFrac 0.75-1.25): ' +
        (kneeBlended + kneeThighOnly + kneeShinOnly) +
        ' | blended(thigh+shin): ' + kneeBlended +
        ' | thigh-only: ' + kneeThighOnly +
        ' | shin-only: ' + kneeShinOnly);
    }

    // Pelvis injection diagnostic
    console.log('=== PELVIS WEIGHT INJECTION (upper-thigh) ===');
    console.log('  Verts injected: ' + _pelvisInjected);
    console.log('=== NECK WEIGHT INJECTION (head→torso blend) ===');
    console.log('  Head verts with torso weight injected: ' + _neckInjected);

    return result;
  }

  // ---------------- Bone-envelope weights (replaces cage KNN region weights) ----------------
  // Nearest-bone + inverse-distance half-life blending + projection disambiguation
  // for regions sharing the same bone segment (head/neck, spine/pelvis).
  function computeBoneEnvelopeWeights(meshRestPos) {
    const nMesh = meshRestPos.length / 3;
    const result = new Array(nMesh);

    // Per-region half-life (meters): controls blend zone width at joint boundaries
    // Smaller = tighter transitions (less seam gap at cost of harder boundaries)
    const HL = {
      0: 0.02, 14: 0.02,               // spine/pelvis: 20mm
      15: 0.02, 16: 0.02, 17: 0.02,    // spine sub-regions: 20mm
      1: 0.012, 2: 0.012,              // thighs: 12mm (tight — transforms disagree at knee)
      3: 0.012, 4: 0.012,              // shins: 12mm
      5: 0.010, 6: 0.010,              // feet: 10mm
      7: 0.012, 8: 0.012,              // upper arms: 12mm
      9: 0.012, 10: 0.012,             // forearms: 12mm
      11: 0.02, 18: 0.02,              // head/neck: 20mm
      12: 0.010, 13: 0.010,            // hands: 10mm
      19: 0.02, 20: 0.02,              // collars: 20mm
      21: 0.008, 22: 0.008             // toes: 8mm
    };

    // Identify regions that share the SAME bone segment (need projection disambiguation)
    // Known pairs: spine(0)+pelvis(14) share ['pelvis','spine_joint'],
    //              head(11)+neck(18) share ['neck','head']
    const sharedBoneKey = {};
    const boneKeys = {};
    for (const ridStr of Object.keys(REGION_BONE_MAP)) {
      const rid = +ridStr;
      const [jA, jB] = REGION_BONE_MAP[rid];
      const key = jA + ',' + jB;
      if (!boneKeys[key]) boneKeys[key] = [];
      boneKeys[key].push(rid);
    }
    for (const key of Object.keys(boneKeys)) {
      if (boneKeys[key].length > 1) {
        for (const rid of boneKeys[key]) sharedBoneKey[rid] = key;
      }
    }

    // Build bone segments from P joint positions
    const segments = [];
    for (const ridStr of Object.keys(REGION_BONE_MAP)) {
      const rid = +ridStr;
      const [jA, jB] = REGION_BONE_MAP[rid];
      if (!P[jA] || !P[jB]) continue;
      const A = P[jA], B = P[jB];
      const abx = B[0]-A[0], aby = B[1]-A[1], abz = B[2]-A[2];
      const ab2 = abx*abx + aby*aby + abz*abz;
      segments.push({ rid, A, B, abx, aby, abz, ab2, hl: HL[rid] || 0.025 });
    }

    // Y thresholds for pelvis(14)/spine(0) overlap
    const spineJointY = P.spine_joint ? P.spine_joint[1] : null;
    const pelvisY = P.pelvis ? P.pelvis[1] : null;

    // Pre-allocate arrays
    const distArr = new Float64Array(segments.length);
    const projArr = new Float64Array(segments.length); // parametric t along bone

    for (let vi = 0; vi < nMesh; vi++) {
      const px = meshRestPos[vi * 3];
      const py = meshRestPos[vi * 3 + 1];
      const pz = meshRestPos[vi * 3 + 2];

      // Step 1: Compute distance + projection to every bone segment
      let dMin = Infinity, primaryIdx = 0;

      for (let si = 0; si < segments.length; si++) {
        const seg = segments[si];
        const rid = seg.rid;

        // Pelvis(14)/spine(0) Y-separation
        if (rid === 14 && spineJointY !== null && py > spineJointY) {
          distArr[si] = 1e9; continue;
        }
        if (rid === 0 && pelvisY !== null && py < pelvisY) {
          distArr[si] = 1e9; continue;
        }

        const d = pointToSegmentDist(px, py, pz, seg.A, seg.B);
        distArr[si] = d;

        // Compute parametric projection t for shared-bone disambiguation
        if (seg.ab2 > 1e-12) {
          const apx = px-seg.A[0], apy = py-seg.A[1], apz = pz-seg.A[2];
          projArr[si] = Math.max(0, Math.min(1,
            (apx*seg.abx + apy*seg.aby + apz*seg.abz) / seg.ab2));
        } else {
          projArr[si] = 0.5;
        }

        if (d < dMin) { dMin = d; primaryIdx = si; }
      }

      const primaryRid = segments[primaryIdx].rid;

      // Step 2: REGION_ADJ filter + inverse-distance half-life weighting
      const adjArr = REGION_ADJ[primaryRid];
      const pairs = [];

      for (let si = 0; si < segments.length; si++) {
        const d = distArr[si];
        if (d >= 1e8) continue;
        const rid = segments[si].rid;
        if (!adjArr || adjArr.indexOf(rid) === -1) continue;

        // Inverse quadratic: w = 1 / (1 + (d/halfLife)^2)
        const h = segments[si].hl;
        const r = d / h;
        let w = 1.0 / (1.0 + r * r);

        // Projection-based split for regions sharing the same bone segment
        // Higher rid "owns" the A-end (t=0), lower rid "owns" the B-end (t=1)
        // spine(0)/pelvis(14): pelvis(14,higher)→A-end(pelvis), spine(0,lower)→B-end(spine_joint)
        // head(11)/neck(18): neck(18,higher)→A-end(neck), head(11,lower)→B-end(head)
        if (sharedBoneKey[rid]) {
          const t = projArr[si];
          const peers = boneKeys[sharedBoneKey[rid]];
          const isHigher = peers.every(or => rid >= or);
          w *= isHigher ? Math.max(0.01, 1 - t) : Math.max(0.01, t);
        }

        if (w > 0.003) pairs.push({ rid, w });
      }

      // Step 3: Sort, cap at 4 influences
      pairs.sort((a, b) => b.w - a.w);
      if (pairs.length > 4) pairs.length = 4;

      // Rigid core: if dominant > 90% after normalization, keep only primary
      if (pairs.length > 1) {
        let preSum = 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < pairs.length; i++) preSum += pairs[i].w;
        if (pairs[0].w / preSum > 0.90) pairs.length = 1;
      }

      // Normalize
      let sum = 0;
      for (let i = 0; i < pairs.length; i++) sum += pairs[i].w;
      const entries = [];
      if (sum > 0) {
        for (let i = 0; i < pairs.length; i++) entries.push(pairs[i].rid, pairs[i].w / sum);
      } else {
        entries.push(primaryRid, 1.0);
      }
      result[vi] = entries;
    }

    // ── Diagnostic output ──
    {
      const influenceCounts = [0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0]; // [0]=0 influences, [1]=1, ..., [6]=6+
      const regionStats = {}; // rid → {count, sumW, minW, maxW}
      for (let vi = 0; vi < nMesh; vi++) {
        const e = result[vi];
        const nInf = e ? e.length / 2 : 0;
        influenceCounts[Math.min(nInf, 6)]++;
        if (e) {
          for (let j = 0; j < e.length; j += 2) {
            const rid = e[j], w = e[j + 1];
            if (!regionStats[rid]) regionStats[rid] = { count: 0, sumW: 0, minW: 1, maxW: 0 };
            const s = regionStats[rid];
            s.count++;
            s.sumW += w;
            if (w < s.minW) s.minW = w;
            if (w > s.maxW) s.maxW = w;
          }
        }
      }

      const regionNames = {0:'spine',1:'l_thigh',2:'r_thigh',3:'l_shin',4:'r_shin',
        5:'l_foot',6:'r_foot',7:'l_uarm',8:'r_uarm',9:'l_farm',10:'r_farm',
        11:'head',12:'l_hand',13:'r_hand',14:'pelvis',15:'spine1',16:'spine2',
        17:'chest',18:'neck',19:'l_collar',20:'r_collar',21:'l_toe',22:'r_toe'};

      console.log('=== BONE-ENVELOPE WEIGHTS ===');
      console.log('  Total verts: ' + nMesh);
      console.log('  Influence distribution: ' +
        '1-inf=' + influenceCounts[1] +
        ' 2-inf=' + influenceCounts[2] +
        ' 3-inf=' + influenceCounts[3] +
        ' 4+inf=' + (influenceCounts[4] + influenceCounts[5] + influenceCounts[6]) +
        ' 0-inf=' + influenceCounts[0]);

      const rids = Object.keys(regionStats).map(Number).sort((a, b) => a - b);
      for (const rid of rids) {
        const s = regionStats[rid];
        const name = regionNames[rid] || ('r' + rid);
        const avg = s.sumW / s.count;
        console.log('  ' + name.padEnd(12) +
          ' verts=' + String(s.count).padStart(6) +
          ' avgW=' + avg.toFixed(3) +
          ' min=' + s.minW.toFixed(3) +
          ' max=' + s.maxW.toFixed(3));
      }

      // Boundary quality: for mesh edges where dominant region differs,
      // measure how smooth the weight transition is
      if (typeof edgeA !== 'undefined' && edgeA && edgeA.length > 0) {
        let smoothEdges = 0, abruptEdges = 0, totalCrossEdges = 0;
        for (let e = 0; e < edgeA.length; e++) {
          const a = edgeA[e], b = edgeB[e];
          const wA = result[a], wB = result[b];
          if (!wA || !wB) continue;
          let domA = -1, domAw = 0, domB = -1, domBw = 0;
          for (let j = 0; j < wA.length; j += 2) { if (wA[j+1] > domAw) { domAw = wA[j+1]; domA = wA[j]; } }
          for (let j = 0; j < wB.length; j += 2) { if (wB[j+1] > domBw) { domBw = wB[j+1]; domB = wB[j]; } }
          if (domA === domB) continue;
          totalCrossEdges++;
          // Check: does vert A have any weight for domB's region, and vice versa?
          let aHasB = 0, bHasA = 0;
          for (let j = 0; j < wA.length; j += 2) { if (wA[j] === domB) aHasB = wA[j+1]; }
          for (let j = 0; j < wB.length; j += 2) { if (wB[j] === domA) bHasA = wB[j+1]; }
          if (aHasB > 0.05 && bHasA > 0.05) smoothEdges++;
          else abruptEdges++;
        }
        console.log('  Boundary quality: ' + totalCrossEdges + ' cross-region edges, ' +
          smoothEdges + ' smooth (' + (totalCrossEdges > 0 ? (smoothEdges/totalCrossEdges*100).toFixed(1) : '0') + '%), ' +
          abruptEdges + ' abrupt');
      }
    }

    return result;
  }

  // ---------------- FBX skin weight transfer ----------------
  // Transfer skin weights from FBX mesh to our mesh via nearest-neighbor vertex matching.
  // Uses fbxSkinData extracted during FBX load.
  // Optimized with spatial grid to avoid O(n*m) brute force.
  function computeFBXTransferredWeights(meshRestPos) {
    if (!fbxSkinData || !meshRestPos) return null;
    const nMesh = meshRestPos.length / 3;
    const result = new Array(nMesh);
    const fbxPos = fbxSkinData.positions;
    const fbxWts = fbxSkinData.weights;
    const nFBX = fbxSkinData.nVerts;

    // Build spatial grid over FBX vertices for fast nearest-neighbor lookup
    // Find bounding box
    let minX = Infinity, minY = Infinity, minZ = Infinity;
    let maxX = -Infinity, maxY = -Infinity, maxZ = -Infinity;
    for (let j = 0; j < nFBX; j++) {
      const x = fbxPos[j*3], y = fbxPos[j*3+1], z = fbxPos[j*3+2];
      if (x < minX) minX = x; if (x > maxX) maxX = x;
      if (y < minY) minY = y; if (y > maxY) maxY = y;
      if (z < minZ) minZ = z; if (z > maxZ) maxZ = z;
    }
    // Cell size: aim for ~20 FBX verts per cell on average
    const cellSize = Math.max(0.005, Math.cbrt((maxX-minX)*(maxY-minY)*(maxZ-minZ) * 20 / Math.max(nFBX, 1)));
    const invCell = 1 / cellSize;
    const nX = Math.ceil((maxX - minX) * invCell) + 1;
    const nY = Math.ceil((maxY - minY) * invCell) + 1;
    const nZ = Math.ceil((maxZ - minZ) * invCell) + 1;
    const grid = new Map();
    for (let j = 0; j < nFBX; j++) {
      const cx = Math.floor((fbxPos[j*3] - minX) * invCell);
      const cy = Math.floor((fbxPos[j*3+1] - minY) * invCell);
      const cz = Math.floor((fbxPos[j*3+2] - minZ) * invCell);
      const key = cx + cy * nX + cz * nX * nY;
      let cell = grid.get(key);
      if (!cell) { cell = []; grid.set(key, cell); }
      cell.push(j);
    }

    // For each mesh vertex, find K nearest FBX vertices and blend weights
    // using inverse-distance interpolation. This produces smooth weight
    // transitions at joint boundaries (vs single nearest-neighbor which
    // creates hard boundaries → 42x stretch).
    const K_INTERP = 8;
    let totalDist = 0, maxDist = 0;
    const kDist2 = new Float64Array(K_INTERP);
    const kIdx = new Int32Array(K_INTERP);

    // Also store the K-neighbor mapping for FBX deform transfer
    if (!window._fbxVertMapping) window._fbxVertMapping = {};
    const mapping = window._fbxVertMapping;
    mapping.fbxIdx = new Int32Array(nMesh * K_INTERP);
    mapping.interpW = new Float32Array(nMesh * K_INTERP);
    mapping.K = K_INTERP;
    mapping.nMesh = nMesh;

    for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
      const mx = meshRestPos[i*3], my = meshRestPos[i*3+1], mz = meshRestPos[i*3+2];
      kDist2.fill(Infinity);
      kIdx.fill(-1);
      let kCount = 0;

      const gx = Math.floor((mx - minX) * invCell);
      const gy = Math.floor((my - minY) * invCell);
      const gz = Math.floor((mz - minZ) * invCell);

      for (let radius = 1; radius <= 20; radius++) {
        for (let ddx = -radius; ddx <= radius; ddx++) {
          for (let ddy = -radius; ddy <= radius; ddy++) {
            for (let ddz = -radius; ddz <= radius; ddz++) {
              if (radius > 1 && Math.abs(ddx) < radius && Math.abs(ddy) < radius && Math.abs(ddz) < radius) continue;
              const cx = gx + ddx, cy = gy + ddy, cz = gz + ddz;
              if (cx < 0 || cy < 0 || cz < 0) continue;
              const key = cx + cy * nX + cz * nX * nY;
              const cell = grid.get(key);
              if (!cell) continue;
              for (let c = 0; c < cell.length; c++) {
                const j = cell[c];
                const ex = mx - fbxPos[j*3], ey = my - fbxPos[j*3+1], ez = mz - fbxPos[j*3+2];
                const d2 = ex*ex + ey*ey + ez*ez;
                if (d2 < kDist2[K_INTERP - 1]) {
                  // Insert into sorted K-nearest list
                  let p = K_INTERP - 1;
                  while (p > 0 && d2 < kDist2[p - 1]) p--;
                  for (let q = K_INTERP - 1; q > p; q--) {
                    kDist2[q] = kDist2[q - 1];
                    kIdx[q] = kIdx[q - 1];
                  }
                  kDist2[p] = d2;
                  kIdx[p] = j;
                  if (kCount < K_INTERP) kCount++;
                }
              }
            }
          }
        }
        // Stop expanding if K nearest are all within next shell's boundary
        if (kCount >= K_INTERP && kDist2[K_INTERP - 1] < ((radius + 1) * cellSize) * ((radius + 1) * cellSize)) break;
      }

      // Fallback: brute force if grid search found fewer than K
      if (kCount < 1) {
        for (let j = 0; j < nFBX; j++) {
          const ex = mx - fbxPos[j*3], ey = my - fbxPos[j*3+1], ez = mz - fbxPos[j*3+2];
          const d2 = ex*ex + ey*ey + ez*ez;
          if (d2 < kDist2[K_INTERP - 1]) {
            let p = K_INTERP - 1;
            while (p > 0 && d2 < kDist2[p - 1]) p--;
            for (let q = K_INTERP - 1; q > p; q--) {
              kDist2[q] = kDist2[q - 1];
              kIdx[q] = kIdx[q - 1];
            }
            kDist2[p] = d2;
            kIdx[p] = j;
            if (kCount < K_INTERP) kCount++;
          }
        }
      }

      // Blend weights from K neighbors using inverse-distance weighting
      const regionWeightMap = {};
      let totalInterpW = 0;
      for (let k = 0; k < kCount; k++) {
        const dist = Math.sqrt(kDist2[k]);
        const iw = 1.0 / (dist + 0.001); // 1mm epsilon
        totalInterpW += iw;
        const nw = fbxWts[kIdx[k]];
        for (let e = 0; e < nw.length; e += 2) {
          const rid = nw[e];
          regionWeightMap[rid] = (regionWeightMap[rid] || 0) + iw * nw[e + 1];
        }
        // Store in mapping for deform transfer
        mapping.fbxIdx[i * K_INTERP + k] = kIdx[k];
        mapping.interpW[i * K_INTERP + k] = iw;
      }
      // Normalize mapping interpolation weights
      if (totalInterpW > 0) {
        for (let k = 0; k < kCount; k++) {
          mapping.interpW[i * K_INTERP + k] /= totalInterpW;
        }
      }
      // Zero out unused mapping slots
      for (let k = kCount; k < K_INTERP; k++) {
        mapping.fbxIdx[i * K_INTERP + k] = -1;
        mapping.interpW[i * K_INTERP + k] = 0;
      }

      // Build flat weight array, filter tiny weights, normalize
      const flat = [];
      for (const ridStr of Object.keys(regionWeightMap)) {
        const w = regionWeightMap[ridStr] / totalInterpW;
        if (w > 0.005) flat.push(parseInt(ridStr), w);
      }
      let totalW = 0;
      for (let e = 1; e < flat.length; e += 2) totalW += flat[e];
      if (totalW > 0) for (let e = 1; e < flat.length; e += 2) flat[e] /= totalW;
      result[i] = flat.length > 0 ? flat : [14, 1.0];

      const d = Math.sqrt(kDist2[0]);
      totalDist += d;
      if (d > maxDist) maxDist = d;
    }

    console.log('[FBX WEIGHT TRANSFER] K=' + K_INTERP + ' interpolation. ' + nMesh + ' vertices. ' +
      'Avg nearest distance: ' + (totalDist / nMesh * 1000).toFixed(1) + 'mm, ' +
      'Max: ' + (maxDist * 1000).toFixed(1) + 'mm');

    // Per-region stats
    const regionCounts = {};
    for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
      const entries = result[i];
      for (let e = 0; e < entries.length; e += 2) {
        const rid = entries[e];
        regionCounts[rid] = (regionCounts[rid] || 0) + 1;
      }
    }
    const regionNames = {0:'spine',1:'l_thigh',2:'r_thigh',3:'l_shin',4:'r_shin',
      5:'l_foot',6:'r_foot',7:'l_uarm',8:'r_uarm',9:'l_farm',10:'r_farm',
      11:'head',12:'l_hand',13:'r_hand',14:'pelvis',15:'spine1',16:'spine2',
      17:'chest',18:'neck',19:'l_collar',20:'r_collar',21:'l_toe',22:'r_toe'};
    const sorted = Object.keys(regionCounts).sort((a, b) => regionCounts[b] - regionCounts[a]);
    console.log('[FBX WEIGHT TRANSFER] Region coverage: ' +
      sorted.map(r => (regionNames[r] || r) + '=' + regionCounts[r]).join(', '));

    return result;
  }

  // ---------------- Post-deformation stretch clamping (PBD) ----------------
  function buildStretchConstraints() {
    if (!meshGeo || !meshRestPos || !meshVertRegions) return;
    const idx = meshGeo.index;
    if (!idx) { console.warn('[STRETCH CLAMP] No index buffer'); return; }
    const indices = idx.array;
    const nTri = indices.length / 3;
    const nMesh = meshRestPos.length / 3;

    // Build unique edges from triangle index buffer
    const edgeSet = new Set();
    const edges = [];
    for (let t = 0; t < nTri; t++) {
      const a = indices[t * 3], b = indices[t * 3 + 1], c = indices[t * 3 + 2];
      const pairs = [[a, b], [b, c], [a, c]];
      for (const [v0, v1] of pairs) {
        const lo = Math.min(v0, v1), hi = Math.max(v0, v1);
        const key = lo * nMesh + hi;
        if (!edgeSet.has(key)) {
          edgeSet.add(key);
          edges.push(lo, hi); // flat array for speed
        }
      }
    }

    // Crotch zone bounds from skeleton pivots
    const midX = P.pelvis ? P.pelvis[0] : 0;
    const hipY = Math.max(P.l_hip[1], P.r_hip[1]);
    const kneeY = Math.min(P.l_knee[1], P.r_knee[1]);
    const midThighY = (hipY + kneeY) / 2;
    const hipSpread = Math.abs(P.l_hip[0] - P.r_hip[0]);
    const xRange = hipSpread * 1.5;

    // Per-vert: mobility (inverse mass) and crotch-zone flag
    const vertMobility = new Float32Array(nMesh);
    const inZone = new Uint8Array(nMesh);
    let zoneCount = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
      const vx = meshRestPos[i * 3], vy = meshRestPos[i * 3 + 1];
      const entries = meshVertRegions[i];
      let thighW = 0, torsoW = 0;
      for (let e = 0; e < entries.length; e += 2) {
        const rid = entries[e];
        if (rid === 1 || rid === 2) thighW += entries[e + 1];
        else if (rid === 0 || rid === 14) torsoW += entries[e + 1];
      }

      // Crotch zone: near midline, between hip and mid-thigh
      if (Math.abs(vx - midX) < xRange && vy <= hipY + 0.03 && vy >= midThighY) {
        inZone[i] = 1;
        zoneCount++;
      }

      // Mobility: boundary verts most mobile, zone verts moderate, rest low
      if (thighW > 0.01 && torsoW > 0.01) {
        vertMobility[i] = 1.0;
      } else if (inZone[i]) {
        vertMobility[i] = 0.4; // zone pure-region verts: moderate mobility
      } else {
        vertMobility[i] = 0.1;
      }
    }

    // Precompute per-vert crotch blend factor (post-DQS torso-pull correction)
    // Verts near the midline between both thighs get pulled toward torso transform
    const crotchBlend = new Float32Array(nMesh);
    let blendCount = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
      if (!inZone[i]) continue;
      const vx = meshRestPos[i * 3], vy = meshRestPos[i * 3 + 1], vz = meshRestPos[i * 3 + 2];
      const dL = pointToSegmentDist(vx, vy, vz, P.l_hip, P.l_knee);
      const dR = pointToSegmentDist(vx, vy, vz, P.r_hip, P.r_knee);
      const dMin = Math.min(dL, dR), dMax = Math.max(dL, dR);
      const ratio = dMax > 1e-6 ? dMin / dMax : 1.0;
      if (ratio > 0.4) {
        const rt = (ratio - 0.4) / (1.0 - 0.4);
        const sf = rt * rt * (3 - 2 * rt); // smoothstep
        crotchBlend[i] = sf * 0.65; // max 65% torso pull at midline
        blendCount++;
      }
    }

    // Filter to constraint edges: crotch zone + ALL cross-region boundary edges
    const cEdgeA = [], cEdgeB = [], cEdgeRestLen = [];
    const nEdges = edges.length / 2;
    let crossRegionCount = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < nEdges; i++) {
      const a = edges[i * 2], b = edges[i * 2 + 1];
      const ax = meshRestPos[a * 3], ay = meshRestPos[a * 3 + 1], az = meshRestPos[a * 3 + 2];
      const bx = meshRestPos[b * 3], by = meshRestPos[b * 3 + 1], bz = meshRestPos[b * 3 + 2];
      const restLen = Math.sqrt((bx - ax) ** 2 + (by - ay) ** 2 + (bz - az) ** 2);
      if (restLen < 1e-8) continue;

      // Include if in crotch zone
      if (inZone[a] || inZone[b]) {
        cEdgeA.push(a); cEdgeB.push(b); cEdgeRestLen.push(restLen);
        continue;
      }

      // Include if edge crosses a region boundary (different dominant regions)
      const eA = meshVertRegions[a], eB = meshVertRegions[b];
      if (eA && eB) {
        let maxA = 0, ridA = -1, maxB = 0, ridB = -1;
        for (let k = 0; k < eA.length; k += 2) { if (eA[k+1] > maxA) { maxA = eA[k+1]; ridA = eA[k]; } }
        for (let k = 0; k < eB.length; k += 2) { if (eB[k+1] > maxB) { maxB = eB[k+1]; ridB = eB[k]; } }
        if (ridA !== ridB) {
          cEdgeA.push(a); cEdgeB.push(b); cEdgeRestLen.push(restLen);
          crossRegionCount++;
          // Give boundary verts moderate mobility if they have low mobility
          if (vertMobility[a] < 0.3) vertMobility[a] = 0.3;
          if (vertMobility[b] < 0.3) vertMobility[b] = 0.3;
          continue;
        }

        // Include short edges (< 10mm rest length) — fragile, amplify any error
        if (restLen < 0.010) {
          cEdgeA.push(a); cEdgeB.push(b); cEdgeRestLen.push(restLen);
          if (vertMobility[a] < 0.2) vertMobility[a] = 0.2;
          if (vertMobility[b] < 0.2) vertMobility[b] = 0.2;
          continue;
        }

        // Include edges near arm-torso junction (shoulder/armpit zone)
        // These are within-region edges that get torn by diverging transforms
        if (P.l_shoulder && P.r_shoulder) {
          const shRadius = 0.08; // ~8cm around shoulder joint
          for (const sh of [P.l_shoulder, P.r_shoulder]) {
            const dA = Math.sqrt((ax-sh[0])**2 + (ay-sh[1])**2 + (az-sh[2])**2);
            const dB = Math.sqrt((bx-sh[0])**2 + (by-sh[1])**2 + (bz-sh[2])**2);
            if (dA < shRadius || dB < shRadius) {
              cEdgeA.push(a); cEdgeB.push(b); cEdgeRestLen.push(restLen);
              if (vertMobility[a] < 0.3) vertMobility[a] = 0.3;
              if (vertMobility[b] < 0.3) vertMobility[b] = 0.3;
              break;
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }

    stretchConstraints = {
      edgeA: cEdgeA,
      edgeB: cEdgeB,
      restLen: cEdgeRestLen,
      mobility: vertMobility,
      crotchBlend: crotchBlend,
      nEdges: cEdgeA.length
    };

    console.log('[STRETCH CLAMP] Built ' + cEdgeA.length + ' constraint edges (' +
      crossRegionCount + ' cross-region) from ' +
      (edges.length / 2) + ' total edges, ' + zoneCount + ' crotch zone verts, ' +
      blendCount + ' blend verts (max blend=' + Math.max(...crotchBlend).toFixed(3) + ')');
  }

  // ---------------- Cross-boundary edge detection + strand collapse ----------------
  function buildCrossBoundaryEdges() {
    if (!meshGeo || !meshVertRegions || !meshRestPos) return;
    const idx = meshGeo.index;
    if (!idx) return;
    const indices = idx.array;
    const nTri = indices.length / 3;
    const nMesh = meshRestPos.length / 3;

    // Determine dominant region for each mesh vert
    const domRegion = new Int8Array(nMesh);
    for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
      const entries = meshVertRegions[i];
      if (!entries) continue;
      let maxW = 0, maxR = -1;
      for (let e = 0; e < entries.length; e += 2) {
        if (entries[e + 1] > maxW) { maxW = entries[e + 1]; maxR = entries[e]; }
      }
      domRegion[i] = maxR;
    }

    // Find unique edges that cross l_thigh(1) / r_thigh(2) boundary
    const edgeSet = new Set();
    const eA = [], eB = [], eRestLen = [];
    for (let t = 0; t < nTri; t++) {
      const a = indices[t * 3], b = indices[t * 3 + 1], c = indices[t * 3 + 2];
      const pairs = [[a, b], [b, c], [a, c]];
      for (const [v0, v1] of pairs) {
