        const r0 = domRegion[v0], r1 = domRegion[v1];
        // One vert is l_thigh(1), the other is r_thigh(2)
        if (!((r0 === 1 && r1 === 2) || (r0 === 2 && r1 === 1))) continue;
        const lo = Math.min(v0, v1), hi = Math.max(v0, v1);
        const key = lo * nMesh + hi;
        if (edgeSet.has(key)) continue;
        edgeSet.add(key);
        const ax = meshRestPos[lo * 3], ay = meshRestPos[lo * 3 + 1], az = meshRestPos[lo * 3 + 2];
        const bx = meshRestPos[hi * 3], by = meshRestPos[hi * 3 + 1], bz = meshRestPos[hi * 3 + 2];
        const restLen = Math.sqrt((bx - ax) ** 2 + (by - ay) ** 2 + (bz - az) ** 2);
        eA.push(lo);
        eB.push(hi);
        eRestLen.push(restLen);
      }
    }

    crossBoundaryEdges = { edgeA: eA, edgeB: eB, restLen: eRestLen, nEdges: eA.length };
    console.log('[CROSS-BOUNDARY] Found ' + eA.length + ' edges crossing l_thigh/r_thigh boundary');
  }

  // Post-deformation: collapse stretched edges across ALL region boundaries
  // and in high-stress zones (crotch, shoulder/armpit).
  // Uses mobility weights to distribute correction.
  function collapseStretchedEdges(pos) {
    if (!stretchConstraints || stretchConstraints.nEdges === 0) return;
    const { edgeA, edgeB, restLen, mobility, nEdges } = stretchConstraints;
    const MAX_STRETCH = CFG('MAX_STRETCH', 1.5);       // normal edges: allow 1.5x before clamping
    const MAX_STRETCH_SHORT = CFG('MAX_STRETCH_SHORT', 1.2); // very short edges (< 3mm): tighter limit
    const SHORT_THRESH = 0.003;    // 3mm rest length threshold
    const ITERATIONS = CFG('PBD_ITERATIONS', 12);

    for (let iter = 0; iter < ITERATIONS; iter++) {
      for (let i = 0; i < nEdges; i++) {
        const a = edgeA[i], b = edgeB[i];
        const a3 = a * 3, b3 = b * 3;
        const dx = pos[b3] - pos[a3], dy = pos[b3 + 1] - pos[a3 + 1], dz = pos[b3 + 2] - pos[a3 + 2];
        const defLenSq = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz;
        const rl = restLen[i];
        const stretch = rl < SHORT_THRESH ? MAX_STRETCH_SHORT : MAX_STRETCH;
        const maxLen = rl * stretch;
        const maxLenSq = maxLen * maxLen;
        if (defLenSq > maxLenSq) {
          const defLen = Math.sqrt(defLenSq);
          const excess = 1.0 - maxLen / defLen;
          // Distribute correction by mobility weights
          const mA = mobility[a], mB = mobility[b];
          const mTotal = mA + mB;
          if (mTotal < 1e-6) continue;
          const wA = mA / mTotal;  // how much A moves
          const wB = mB / mTotal;  // how much B moves
          pos[a3]     += dx * excess * wA;
          pos[a3 + 1] += dy * excess * wA;
          pos[a3 + 2] += dz * excess * wA;
          pos[b3]     -= dx * excess * wB;
          pos[b3 + 1] -= dy * excess * wB;
          pos[b3 + 2] -= dz * excess * wB;
        }
      }
    }

    // Targeted cross-boundary collapse: pull L/R thigh boundary edges together
    if (crossBoundaryEdges && crossBoundaryEdges.nEdges > 0) {
      const MAX_CB_STRETCH = CFG('MAX_CB_STRETCH', 1.3);
      const cb = crossBoundaryEdges;
      for (let iter = 0; iter < 4; iter++) {
        for (let i = 0; i < cb.nEdges; i++) {
          const a = cb.edgeA[i], b = cb.edgeB[i];
          const a3 = a*3, b3 = b*3;
          const dx = pos[b3]-pos[a3], dy = pos[b3+1]-pos[a3+1], dz = pos[b3+2]-pos[a3+2];
          const defLenSq = dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz;
          const maxLen = cb.restLen[i] * MAX_CB_STRETCH;
          if (defLenSq > maxLen * maxLen) {
            const defLen = Math.sqrt(defLenSq);
            const excess = 1.0 - maxLen / defLen;
            pos[a3]   += dx * excess * 0.5;
            pos[a3+1] += dy * excess * 0.5;
            pos[a3+2] += dz * excess * 0.5;
            pos[b3]   -= dx * excess * 0.5;
            pos[b3+1] -= dy * excess * 0.5;
            pos[b3+2] -= dz * excess * 0.5;
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // ---------------- Mesh adjacency + strain diagnostic + Delta Mush ----------------
  function buildMeshAdjacency() {
    if (!meshGeo || !meshRestPos) return;
    const idx = meshGeo.index;
    if (!idx) { console.warn('[ADJACENCY] No index buffer'); return; }
    const indices = idx.array;
    const nTri = indices.length / 3;
    const nMesh = meshRestPos.length / 3;

    // Build unique edges from triangle index buffer
    const edgeSet = new Set();
    const edgeA = [], edgeB = [], edgeRestLen = [];
    for (let t = 0; t < nTri; t++) {
      const a = indices[t*3], b = indices[t*3+1], c = indices[t*3+2];
      const pairs = [[a,b],[b,c],[a,c]];
      for (const [v0,v1] of pairs) {
        const lo = Math.min(v0,v1), hi = Math.max(v0,v1);
        const key = lo * nMesh + hi;
        if (!edgeSet.has(key)) {
          edgeSet.add(key);
          edgeA.push(lo); edgeB.push(hi);
          const dx = meshRestPos[hi*3]-meshRestPos[lo*3];
          const dy = meshRestPos[hi*3+1]-meshRestPos[lo*3+1];
          const dz = meshRestPos[hi*3+2]-meshRestPos[lo*3+2];
          edgeRestLen.push(Math.sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy+dz*dz));
        }
      }
    }

    // Build per-vertex adjacency (neighbor lists)
    const adjCount = new Uint16Array(nMesh);
    for (let i = 0; i < edgeA.length; i++) {
      adjCount[edgeA[i]]++; adjCount[edgeB[i]]++;
    }
    const adjStart = new Uint32Array(nMesh);
    for (let i = 1; i < nMesh; i++) adjStart[i] = adjStart[i-1] + adjCount[i-1];
    const totalAdj = adjStart[nMesh-1] + adjCount[nMesh-1];
    const adjList = new Uint32Array(totalAdj);
    const adjFill = new Uint16Array(nMesh);
    for (let i = 0; i < edgeA.length; i++) {
      const a = edgeA[i], b = edgeB[i];
      adjList[adjStart[a] + adjFill[a]++] = b;
      adjList[adjStart[b] + adjFill[b]++] = a;
    }

    meshAdjacency = {
      edgeA, edgeB, edgeRestLen,
      nEdges: edgeA.length,
      adjList, adjStart, adjCount,
      nVerts: nMesh
    };

    console.log('[ADJACENCY] Built ' + edgeA.length + ' edges, ' +
      nMesh + ' verts, avg ' + (totalAdj/nMesh).toFixed(1) + ' neighbors/vert');

    // ── Weight smoothness diagnostic ──
    // For each edge, check if the dominant region changes abruptly (weight jump > threshold)
    if (meshVertRegions) {
      const REGION_NAMES = ['spine','l_thigh','r_thigh','l_shin','r_shin','l_foot','r_foot',
        'l_uarm','r_uarm','l_farm','r_farm','head','l_hand','r_hand','pelvis',
        'spine1','spine2','chest','neck_reg','l_collar','r_collar','l_toe_reg','r_toe_reg'];
      const jumpThresh = 0.5; // weight jump > 50% on an edge = abrupt
      let abruptEdges = 0;
      const regionJumps = {}; // "ridA→ridB": count
      const worstJumps = []; // {a, b, ridA, ridB, wA, wB, jump}
      for (let e = 0; e < edgeA.length; e++) {
        const a = edgeA[e], b = edgeB[e];
        const wA = meshVertRegions[a], wB = meshVertRegions[b];
        if (!wA || !wB) continue;
        // Find dominant region for each vert
        let maxA = 0, ridA = -1, maxB = 0, ridB = -1;
        for (let k = 0; k < wA.length; k += 2) { if (wA[k+1] > maxA) { maxA = wA[k+1]; ridA = wA[k]; } }
        for (let k = 0; k < wB.length; k += 2) { if (wB[k+1] > maxB) { maxB = wB[k+1]; ridB = wB[k]; } }
        if (ridA === ridB) continue;
        // For cross-region edges, check how smooth the transition is
        // Get weight of ridA in vert B, and weight of ridB in vert A
        let wA_inB = 0, wB_inA = 0;
        for (let k = 0; k < wB.length; k += 2) { if (wB[k] === ridA) wA_inB = wB[k+1]; }
        for (let k = 0; k < wA.length; k += 2) { if (wA[k] === ridB) wB_inA = wA[k+1]; }
        // Jump = how much the weight changes across this edge for the transitioning region
        const jump = Math.max(maxA - wA_inB, maxB - wB_inA);
        if (jump > jumpThresh) {
          abruptEdges++;
          const key = REGION_NAMES[ridA] + '→' + REGION_NAMES[ridB];
          regionJumps[key] = (regionJumps[key] || 0) + 1;
          if (worstJumps.length < 200) worstJumps.push({a, b, ridA, ridB, wA: maxA, wB: maxB, jump});
        }
      }
      console.log('=== WEIGHT SMOOTHNESS ===');
      console.log('  Abrupt transition edges (>' + (jumpThresh*100) + '% jump): ' + abruptEdges + ' / ' + edgeA.length);
      const sorted = Object.entries(regionJumps).sort((a,b) => b[1] - a[1]);
      for (const [key, count] of sorted.slice(0, 10)) {
        console.log('  ' + key + ': ' + count + ' edges');
      }
      worstJumps.sort((a,b) => b.jump - a.jump);
      for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(5, worstJumps.length); i++) {
        const w = worstJumps[i];
        console.log('  worst: v' + w.a + '(' + REGION_NAMES[w.ridA] + '=' + w.wA.toFixed(3) + ') ↔ v' + w.b +
          '(' + REGION_NAMES[w.ridB] + '=' + w.wB.toFixed(3) + ') jump=' + w.jump.toFixed(3));
      }
    }

    // Precompute Delta Mush rest-pose data
    buildDeltaMushData();
  }

  function buildDeltaMushData() {
    if (!meshAdjacency || !meshRestPos) return;
    const { adjList, adjStart, adjCount, nVerts } = meshAdjacency;
    const SMOOTH_ITERS = 3;

    // Laplacian smooth the rest mesh N times
    let smoothed = meshRestPos.slice();
    const tmp = new Float32Array(nVerts * 3);
    for (let iter = 0; iter < SMOOTH_ITERS; iter++) {
      for (let i = 0; i < nVerts; i++) {
        const nc = adjCount[i];
        if (nc === 0) { tmp[i*3]=smoothed[i*3]; tmp[i*3+1]=smoothed[i*3+1]; tmp[i*3+2]=smoothed[i*3+2]; continue; }
        let sx=0, sy=0, sz=0;
        const start = adjStart[i];
        for (let j = 0; j < nc; j++) {
          const ni = adjList[start+j];
          sx += smoothed[ni*3]; sy += smoothed[ni*3+1]; sz += smoothed[ni*3+2];
        }
        tmp[i*3] = sx/nc; tmp[i*3+1] = sy/nc; tmp[i*3+2] = sz/nc;
      }
      const swap = smoothed; smoothed = tmp.slice(); // copy for next iter
    }

    // Per-vertex delta: rest - smoothed (the mesh detail to preserve)
    const deltas = new Float32Array(nVerts * 3);
    for (let i = 0; i < nVerts * 3; i++) deltas[i] = meshRestPos[i] - smoothed[i];

    // Per-vertex local coordinate frame from rest-pose neighbors (tangent space)
    // We store a 3x3 frame per vert for rotating deltas into deformed space
    const frames = new Float32Array(nVerts * 9); // [t0x,t0y,t0z, t1x,t1y,t1z, nx,ny,nz]
    for (let i = 0; i < nVerts; i++) {
      const nc = adjCount[i];
      if (nc < 2) { frames[i*9]=1; frames[i*9+4]=1; frames[i*9+8]=1; continue; }
      // Normal: average of (neighbor - vert) cross products
      const vx=meshRestPos[i*3], vy=meshRestPos[i*3+1], vz=meshRestPos[i*3+2];
      let nx=0, ny=0, nz=0;
      const start = adjStart[i];
      const n0 = adjList[start];
      const e0x=meshRestPos[n0*3]-vx, e0y=meshRestPos[n0*3+1]-vy, e0z=meshRestPos[n0*3+2]-vz;
      for (let j = 1; j < nc; j++) {
        const nj = adjList[start+j];
        const ejx=meshRestPos[nj*3]-vx, ejy=meshRestPos[nj*3+1]-vy, ejz=meshRestPos[nj*3+2]-vz;
        nx += e0y*ejz-e0z*ejy; ny += e0z*ejx-e0x*ejz; nz += e0x*ejy-e0y*ejx;
      }
      let nLen = Math.sqrt(nx*nx+ny*ny+nz*nz);
      if (nLen < 1e-10) { frames[i*9]=1; frames[i*9+4]=1; frames[i*9+8]=1; continue; }
      nx/=nLen; ny/=nLen; nz/=nLen;
      // Tangent: first neighbor direction, orthogonalized to normal
      let tx=e0x, ty=e0y, tz=e0z;
      const dot = tx*nx+ty*ny+tz*nz;
      tx-=dot*nx; ty-=dot*ny; tz-=dot*nz;
      let tLen = Math.sqrt(tx*tx+ty*ty+tz*tz);
      if (tLen < 1e-10) { frames[i*9]=1; frames[i*9+4]=1; frames[i*9+8]=1; continue; }
      tx/=tLen; ty/=tLen; tz/=tLen;
      // Bitangent: cross(normal, tangent)
      const bx=ny*tz-nz*ty, by=nz*tx-nx*tz, bz=nx*ty-ny*tx;
      frames[i*9]=tx; frames[i*9+1]=ty; frames[i*9+2]=tz;
      frames[i*9+3]=bx; frames[i*9+4]=by; frames[i*9+5]=bz;
      frames[i*9+6]=nx; frames[i*9+7]=ny; frames[i*9+8]=nz;
    }

    deltaMushData = { smoothedRest: smoothed, deltas, frames, smoothIters: SMOOTH_ITERS };
    console.log('[DELTA MUSH] Precomputed: ' + nVerts + ' verts, ' +
      SMOOTH_ITERS + ' smooth iters, avg delta=' +
      (Math.sqrt(deltas.reduce((s,v)=>s+v*v,0)/nVerts)/3).toFixed(5));
  }

  // Per-frame Delta Mush correction (called after deformMesh)
  // STRAIN-WEIGHTED: only corrects vertices with actual deformation problems.
  // Verts with <5% edge strain get NO correction (preserves texture).
  // Verts with >20% strain get full correction (fixes dimples/artifacts).
  function applyDeltaMush(pos) {
    if (!deltaMushData || !meshAdjacency || !$('enableDeltaMush').checked) return;
    const { deltas, frames, smoothIters } = deltaMushData;
    const { adjList, adjStart, adjCount, nVerts, edgeA, edgeB, edgeRestLen, nEdges } = meshAdjacency;

    // Step 0: Compute per-vertex strain to weight the correction
    const strainW = new Float32Array(nVerts);
    for (let i = 0; i < nEdges; i++) {
      const a = edgeA[i], b = edgeB[i];
      const dx = pos[b*3]-pos[a*3], dy = pos[b*3+1]-pos[a*3+1], dz = pos[b*3+2]-pos[a*3+2];
      const curLen = Math.sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy+dz*dz);
      const rest = edgeRestLen[i];
      if (rest < 1e-8) continue;
      const dev = Math.abs(curLen/rest - 1.0);
      if (dev > strainW[a]) strainW[a] = dev;
      if (dev > strainW[b]) strainW[b] = dev;
    }
    // Map strain to correction weight: <5% = none, 5-20% = ramp, >20% = full
    const SL = 0.05, SH = 0.20;
    let corrCount = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < nVerts; i++) {
      if (strainW[i] <= SL) { strainW[i] = 0; continue; }
      if (strainW[i] >= SH) { strainW[i] = 1; corrCount++; continue; }
      const t = (strainW[i] - SL) / (SH - SL);
      strainW[i] = t * t * (3 - 2*t);
      corrCount++;
    }

    // Save original deformed positions (before smoothing overwrites)
    const origPos = new Float32Array(pos);

    // Step 1: Laplacian smooth the deformed positions (3 iterations)
    let smoothed = pos.slice();
    const tmp = new Float32Array(nVerts * 3);
    for (let iter = 0; iter < smoothIters; iter++) {
      for (let i = 0; i < nVerts; i++) {
        const nc = adjCount[i];
        if (nc === 0) { tmp[i*3]=smoothed[i*3]; tmp[i*3+1]=smoothed[i*3+1]; tmp[i*3+2]=smoothed[i*3+2]; continue; }
        let sx=0, sy=0, sz=0;
        const start = adjStart[i];
        for (let j = 0; j < nc; j++) {
          const ni = adjList[start+j];
          sx += smoothed[ni*3]; sy += smoothed[ni*3+1]; sz += smoothed[ni*3+2];
        }
        tmp[i*3] = sx/nc; tmp[i*3+1] = sy/nc; tmp[i*3+2] = sz/nc;
      }
      smoothed = tmp.slice();
    }

    // Step 2: Compute corrected position per vert, then blend by strain weight
    for (let i = 0; i < nVerts; i++) {
      const sw = strainW[i];
      if (sw < 0.001) continue; // skip verts with no strain — no texture distortion
      const nc = adjCount[i];
      if (nc < 2) continue;
      const vx=smoothed[i*3], vy=smoothed[i*3+1], vz=smoothed[i*3+2];
      const start = adjStart[i];
      const n0 = adjList[start];
      const e0x=smoothed[n0*3]-vx, e0y=smoothed[n0*3+1]-vy, e0z=smoothed[n0*3+2]-vz;
      let nx=0, ny=0, nz=0;
      for (let j = 1; j < nc; j++) {
        const nj = adjList[start+j];
        const ejx=smoothed[nj*3]-vx, ejy=smoothed[nj*3+1]-vy, ejz=smoothed[nj*3+2]-vz;
        nx += e0y*ejz-e0z*ejy; ny += e0z*ejx-e0x*ejz; nz += e0x*ejy-e0y*ejx;
      }
      let nLen = Math.sqrt(nx*nx+ny*ny+nz*nz);
      if (nLen < 1e-10) continue;
      nx/=nLen; ny/=nLen; nz/=nLen;
      let tx=e0x, ty=e0y, tz=e0z;
      const dot = tx*nx+ty*ny+tz*nz;
      tx-=dot*nx; ty-=dot*ny; tz-=dot*nz;
      let tLen = Math.sqrt(tx*tx+ty*ty+tz*tz);
      if (tLen < 1e-10) continue;
      tx/=tLen; ty/=tLen; tz/=tLen;
      const bx=ny*tz-nz*ty, by=nz*tx-nx*tz, bz=nx*ty-ny*tx;

      const fi = i*9;
      const rt0x=frames[fi], rt0y=frames[fi+1], rt0z=frames[fi+2];
      const rb0x=frames[fi+3], rb0y=frames[fi+4], rb0z=frames[fi+5];
      const rn0x=frames[fi+6], rn0y=frames[fi+7], rn0z=frames[fi+8];

      const dx=deltas[i*3], dy=deltas[i*3+1], dz=deltas[i*3+2];
      const lx = rt0x*dx+rt0y*dy+rt0z*dz;
      const ly = rb0x*dx+rb0y*dy+rb0z*dz;
      const lz = rn0x*dx+rn0y*dy+rn0z*dz;

      // Mush-corrected position
      const mx = vx + tx*lx + bx*ly + nx*lz;
      const my = vy + ty*lx + by*ly + ny*lz;
      const mz = vz + tz*lx + bz*ly + nz*lz;

      // Blend: original * (1-sw) + corrected * sw
      pos[i*3]   = origPos[i*3]   + sw * (mx - origPos[i*3]);
      pos[i*3+1] = origPos[i*3+1] + sw * (my - origPos[i*3+1]);
      pos[i*3+2] = origPos[i*3+2] + sw * (mz - origPos[i*3+2]);
    }
  }

  // Precompute rest-pose vertex normals for surface health diagnostic
  function computeRestVertexNormals() {
    if (!meshGeo || !meshRestPos) return;
    const idx = meshGeo.index;
    if (!idx) return;
    const indices = idx.array;
    const nTri = indices.length / 3;
    const nVerts = meshRestPos.length / 3;
    meshRestNormals = new Float32Array(nVerts * 3);
    for (let t = 0; t < nTri; t++) {
      const a = indices[t*3], b = indices[t*3+1], c = indices[t*3+2];
      const a3 = a*3, b3 = b*3, c3 = c*3;
      const e1x = meshRestPos[b3]-meshRestPos[a3], e1y = meshRestPos[b3+1]-meshRestPos[a3+1], e1z = meshRestPos[b3+2]-meshRestPos[a3+2];
      const e2x = meshRestPos[c3]-meshRestPos[a3], e2y = meshRestPos[c3+1]-meshRestPos[a3+1], e2z = meshRestPos[c3+2]-meshRestPos[a3+2];
      const nx = e1y*e2z - e1z*e2y, ny = e1z*e2x - e1x*e2z, nz = e1x*e2y - e1y*e2x;
      meshRestNormals[a3]+=nx; meshRestNormals[a3+1]+=ny; meshRestNormals[a3+2]+=nz;
      meshRestNormals[b3]+=nx; meshRestNormals[b3+1]+=ny; meshRestNormals[b3+2]+=nz;
      meshRestNormals[c3]+=nx; meshRestNormals[c3+1]+=ny; meshRestNormals[c3+2]+=nz;
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < nVerts; i++) {
      const i3 = i*3;
      const len = Math.sqrt(meshRestNormals[i3]**2 + meshRestNormals[i3+1]**2 + meshRestNormals[i3+2]**2);
      if (len > 1e-8) { meshRestNormals[i3]/=len; meshRestNormals[i3+1]/=len; meshRestNormals[i3+2]/=len; }
    }
    console.log('[REST NORMALS] Computed for ' + nVerts + ' vertices');
  }

  // Per-frame strain diagnostic (colors mesh by edge stretch quality)
  function computeStrainColors(pos, colors) {
    if (!meshAdjacency || !colors) return;
    const { edgeA, edgeB, edgeRestLen, nEdges, nVerts } = meshAdjacency;

    // Per-vert: track worst stretch ratio
    const worstStrain = new Float32Array(nVerts);
    for (let i = 0; i < worstStrain.length; i++) worstStrain[i] = 1.0; // 1.0 = perfect

    for (let i = 0; i < nEdges; i++) {
      const a = edgeA[i], b = edgeB[i];
      const dx = pos[b*3]-pos[a*3], dy = pos[b*3+1]-pos[a*3+1], dz = pos[b*3+2]-pos[a*3+2];
      const curLen = Math.sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy+dz*dz);
      const rest = edgeRestLen[i];
      if (rest < 1e-8) continue;
      const ratio = curLen / rest;
      // Store worst deviation from 1.0
      const deviation = Math.abs(ratio - 1.0);
      if (deviation > Math.abs(worstStrain[a] - 1.0)) worstStrain[a] = ratio;
      if (deviation > Math.abs(worstStrain[b] - 1.0)) worstStrain[b] = ratio;
    }

    // Color by strain: green (good) → yellow (mild) → red (bad)
    let badCount = 0, totalDeviation = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < nVerts; i++) {
      const r = worstStrain[i];
      const dev = Math.abs(r - 1.0);
      totalDeviation += dev;
      if (dev > 0.3) badCount++;
      // Map: 0-10% = green, 10-30% = yellow, 30%+ = red
      if (dev < 0.1) {
        colors[i*3] = 0; colors[i*3+1] = 0.8; colors[i*3+2] = 0; // green
      } else if (dev < 0.3) {
        const t = (dev - 0.1) / 0.2;
        colors[i*3] = t; colors[i*3+1] = 0.8*(1-t); colors[i*3+2] = 0; // green→red
      } else {
        colors[i*3] = 1; colors[i*3+1] = 0; colors[i*3+2] = 0; // red
      }
    }

    // One-shot diagnostic log
    if (!window._strainDiagDone) {
      window._strainDiagDone = true;
      console.log('=== STRAIN DIAGNOSTIC ===');
      console.log('  Total edges: ' + nEdges);
      console.log('  Avg deviation from rest length: ' + (totalDeviation/nVerts*100).toFixed(1) + '%');
      console.log('  Verts with >30% strain: ' + badCount + ' / ' + nVerts +
        ' (' + (badCount/nVerts*100).toFixed(1) + '%)');
    }
  }

  // ---------------- Motion interpolation (from your old code) ----------------
  function getRotationAtTime(boneName, t, axis = 'x_rot') {
    if (!motionData || !motionData[boneName]) return 0;
    const data = motionData[boneName];
    const times = data.times;
    const values = data[axis];
    if (!values || values.length === 0) return 0;

    const cycleT = t % cycleDuration;
    const firstTime = times[0];
    const lastTime = times[times.length - 1];

    if (cycleT < firstTime || cycleT > lastTime) {
      const v0 = values[values.length - 1];
      const v1 = values[0];
      const t0 = lastTime;
      const t1 = firstTime + cycleDuration;
      const adjustedT = cycleT < firstTime ? cycleT + cycleDuration : cycleT;
      const frac = (adjustedT - t0) / (t1 - t0);
      const degrees = v0 + (v1 - v0) * clamp(frac,0,1);
      return degrees * Math.PI / 180;
    }

    let i = 0;
    while (i < times.length - 1 && times[i + 1] < cycleT) i++;
    const t0 = times[i], t1 = times[i + 1];
    const v0 = values[i], v1 = values[i + 1];
    const frac = (cycleT - t0) / (t1 - t0);
    const degrees = v0 + (v1 - v0) * frac;
    return degrees * Math.PI / 180;
  }

  function getArmRotationAtTime(boneName, t, axis = 'x_rot') {
    if (!armMotionData || !armMotionData[boneName]) return 0;
    const data = armMotionData[boneName];
    const times = data.times;
    const values = data[axis];
    if (!values || values.length === 0) return 0;

    const cycleT = t % cycleDuration;
    const firstTime = times[0];
    const lastTime = times[times.length - 1];
    const n = values.length;

    if (cycleT < firstTime || cycleT > lastTime) {
      const v0 = values[n - 1];
      const v1 = values[0];
      const t0 = lastTime;
      const t1 = firstTime + cycleDuration;
      const adjustedT = cycleT < firstTime ? cycleT + cycleDuration : cycleT;
      const frac = clamp((adjustedT - t0) / (t1 - t0), 0, 1);
      // Catmull-Rom at cycle boundary
      const p0 = values[n - 2];
      const p3 = values[1];
      const tt = frac, tt2 = tt*tt, tt3 = tt2*tt;
      const degrees = 0.5*((2*v0)+(-p0+v1)*tt+(2*p0-5*v0+4*v1-p3)*tt2+(-p0+3*v0-3*v1+p3)*tt3);
      return degrees * Math.PI / 180;
    }

    let i = 0;
    while (i < times.length - 1 && times[i + 1] < cycleT) i++;
    const t0 = times[i], t1 = times[i + 1];
    const frac = (cycleT - t0) / (t1 - t0);

    // Catmull-Rom spline: smooth velocity at each keyframe (no abrupt direction changes)
    const p0 = values[i > 0 ? i - 1 : n - 1];
    const p1 = values[i];
    const p2 = values[i + 1 < n ? i + 1 : 0];
    const p3 = values[i + 2 < n ? i + 2 : (i + 2) % n];
    const tt = frac, tt2 = tt*tt, tt3 = tt2*tt;
    const degrees = 0.5*((2*p1)+(-p0+p2)*tt+(2*p0-5*p1+4*p2-p3)*tt2+(-p0+3*p1-3*p2+p3)*tt3);
    return degrees * Math.PI / 180;
  }

  function getSpineDataAtTime(boneName, t, axis = 'x_rot') {
    if (!spineMotionData || !spineMotionData[boneName]) return 0;
    const data = spineMotionData[boneName];
    let times = data.times;
    const values = data[axis];
    if (!values || values.length === 0) return 0;

    // Some bones (spine, neck, head) have only 2 time entries but 32 values.
    // Generate uniform times from first to last when there's a mismatch.
    if (values.length > times.length) {
      const t0 = times[0], tN = times[times.length - 1];
      times = [];
      for (let k = 0; k < values.length; k++) {
        times.push(t0 + (tN - t0) * k / (values.length - 1));
      }
    }

    const cycleT = t % cycleDuration;
    const firstTime = times[0];
    const lastTime = times[times.length - 1];

    if (cycleT < firstTime || cycleT > lastTime) {
      const v0 = values[values.length - 1];
      const v1 = values[0];
      const t0 = lastTime;
      const t1 = firstTime + cycleDuration;
      const adjustedT = cycleT < firstTime ? cycleT + cycleDuration : cycleT;
      const frac = (adjustedT - t0) / (t1 - t0);
      return v0 + (v1 - v0) * clamp(frac,0,1);
    }

    let i = 0;
    while (i < times.length - 1 && times[i + 1] < cycleT) i++;
    const t0 = times[i], t1 = times[i + 1];
    const v0 = values[i], v1 = values[i + 1];
    const frac = (cycleT - t0) / (t1 - t0);
    return v0 + (v1 - v0) * frac;
  }

  // ---------------- Position-based interpolation & limb IK ----------------

  // Get hips-local joint position at time t (Catmull-Rom, cyclic)
  // Returns [x, y, z] in hips-local cage-scale coordinates
  function getJointPositionAtTime(jointName, t) {
    if (!jointPosData || !jointPosData.joints[jointName]) return null;
    const jd = jointPosData.joints[jointName];
    const times = jd.times;
    const positions = jd.positions;
    const n = positions.length;
    if (n === 0) return null;
    if (n === 1) return positions[0].slice();

    const duration = jointPosData.duration;
    const cycleT = t % duration;

    // Find bracketing frames
    let i = 0;
    while (i < n - 1 && times[i + 1] < cycleT) i++;

    // Catmull-Rom with 4 control points (cyclic wrapping)
    const i0 = (i - 1 + n) % n;
    const i1 = i;
    const i2 = (i + 1) % n;
    const i3 = (i + 2) % n;

    const t0 = times[i1];
    const t1 = (i2 === 0) ? duration : times[i2];
    let frac;
    if (cycleT < t0) {
      // Before first frame — interpolate from last to first
      const tLast = times[n - 1];
      frac = (cycleT + duration - tLast) / (times[0] + duration - tLast);
      frac = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, frac));
      // Use last→first segment
      const p0 = positions[(n - 2 + n) % n];
      const p1 = positions[n - 1];
      const p2 = positions[0];
      const p3 = positions[Math.min(1, n - 1)];
      return catmullRomVec3(p0, p1, p2, p3, frac);
    }

    frac = (t1 > t0) ? (cycleT - t0) / (t1 - t0) : 0;
    frac = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, frac));

    return catmullRomVec3(positions[i0], positions[i1], positions[i2], positions[i3], frac);
  }

  function catmullRomVec3(p0, p1, p2, p3, t) {
    const t2 = t * t, t3 = t2 * t;
    const result = [0, 0, 0];
    for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) {
      result[c] = 0.5 * (
        (2 * p1[c]) +
        (-p0[c] + p2[c]) * t +
        (2*p0[c] - 5*p1[c] + 4*p2[c] - p3[c]) * t2 +
        (-p0[c] + 3*p1[c] - 3*p2[c] + p3[c]) * t3
      );
    }
    return result;
  }

  // Interpolate joint quaternion at time t using SLERP (cyclic)
  function getJointQuaternionAtTime(jointName, t) {
    if (!jointPosData || !jointPosData.joints[jointName]) return null;
    const jd = jointPosData.joints[jointName];
    const quats = jd.quaternions;
    if (!quats || quats.length === 0) return null;
    if (quats.length === 1) return quats[0].slice();

    const times = jd.times;
    const n = quats.length;
    const duration = jointPosData.duration;
    const cycleT = t % duration;

    // Find bracketing frames
    let i = 0;
    while (i < n - 1 && times[i + 1] < cycleT) i++;

    const i1 = i;
    const i2 = (i + 1) % n;

    const t0 = times[i1];
    const t1 = (i2 === 0) ? duration : times[i2];
    let frac;
    if (cycleT < t0) {
      const tLast = times[n - 1];
      frac = (cycleT + duration - tLast) / (times[0] + duration - tLast);
      frac = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, frac));
      return quat_slerp(quats[n - 1], quats[0], frac);
    }
    frac = (t1 > t0) ? (cycleT - t0) / (t1 - t0) : 0;
    frac = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, frac));
    return quat_slerp(quats[i1], quats[i2], frac);
  }

  // Get twist angle for a bone at time t using quaternion swing-twist decomposition.
  // boneName: the joint whose twist we want (e.g. 'l_shoulder')
  // childName: the child joint, used to compute rest bone direction
  function getJointTwistAtTime(boneName, childName, t) {
    const curQ = getJointQuaternionAtTime(boneName, t);
    if (!curQ || !jointPosData.rest_quats[boneName]) return 0;
    const restQ = jointPosData.rest_quats[boneName];
    // Bone direction from rest-pose positions (already X-mirrored & scaled)
    const rp = jointPosData.rest_pose;
    if (!rp[boneName] || !rp[childName]) return 0;
    const boneDir = vnorm(vsub(rp[childName], rp[boneName]));
    return extractQuatTwist(restQ, curQ, boneDir);
  }

  // Transform hips-local position to world via torso Kabsch transform
  function hipsLocalToWorld(localPos, torsoR, torsoT) {
    const w = mat3_mulVec3(torsoR, localPos[0], localPos[1], localPos[2]);
    return [w[0] + torsoT[0], w[1] + torsoT[1], w[2] + torsoT[2]];
  }

  // Interpolate root (hips world) position at time t — for body bob/sway
  function getRootPositionAtTime(t) {
    if (!jointPosData || !jointPosData.root_positions) return null;
    const rp = jointPosData.root_positions;
    const n = rp.length;
    if (n === 0) return null;
    if (n === 1) return rp[0].slice();

    const duration = jointPosData.duration;
    const cycleT = t % duration;
    const fps = jointPosData.fps;

    const fExact = cycleT * fps;
    const i1 = Math.min(Math.floor(fExact), n - 1);
    const i2 = Math.min(i1 + 1, n - 1);
    const frac = fExact - Math.floor(fExact);

    return [
      rp[i1][0] + (rp[i2][0] - rp[i1][0]) * frac,
      rp[i1][1] + (rp[i2][1] - rp[i1][1]) * frac,
      rp[i1][2] + (rp[i2][2] - rp[i1][2]) * frac
    ];
  }

  // Helper: cross product
  function vcross(a, b) {
    return [
      a[1]*b[2] - a[2]*b[1],
      a[2]*b[0] - a[0]*b[2],
      a[0]*b[1] - a[1]*b[0]
    ];
  }

  // Stable bend axis: returns normalized cross product of two directions,
  // falling back to previous frame's axis when the cross product is degenerate
  // (near-straight or near-180° limb). Updates prevBendAxis[key] in-place.
  function stableBendAxis(dir1, dir2, key, fallback) {
    const c = vcross(dir1, dir2);
    const len = vlen(c);
    if (len > 1e-4) {
      const axis = vnorm(c);
      // Ensure consistent sign with previous frame (prevent 180° flips)
      if (prevBendAxis[key] && vdot(axis, prevBendAxis[key]) < 0) {
        const flipped = [-axis[0], -axis[1], -axis[2]];
        prevBendAxis[key] = flipped;
        return flipped;
      }
      prevBendAxis[key] = axis;
      return axis;
    }
    // Degenerate: use previous frame or fallback
    if (prevBendAxis[key]) return prevBendAxis[key];
    return fallback || [0, 1, 0];
  }

  // Extract twist around a bone axis by comparing actual vs expected bend plane normals.
  // The bend plane normal (cross product of parent and child bone directions) encodes
  // the axial rotation that position-only retargeting misses (e.g. forearm pronation,
  // thigh internal rotation). Returns signed twist angle in radians.
  function extractBoneTwist(restBoneDir, curBoneDir, restChildDir, curChildDir) {
    const restCross = vcross(restBoneDir, restChildDir);
    const curCross = vcross(curBoneDir, curChildDir);

    // Need both normals to be non-degenerate (bend > ~0.3°)
    // T-pose always has a tiny natural bend, giving a clean reference
    if (vlen(restCross) < 0.002 || vlen(curCross) < 0.05) return 0;

    const restBendN = vnorm(restCross);
    const curBendN = vnorm(curCross);

    // Expected bend normal if NO twist occurred (just swing from rest to current)
    const expectedBendN = applyDeltaRotation(restBoneDir, curBoneDir, restBendN);

    // Project both onto plane perpendicular to current bone direction
    const projE = vsub(expectedBendN, vscale(curBoneDir, vdot(expectedBendN, curBoneDir)));
    const projC = vsub(curBendN, vscale(curBoneDir, vdot(curBendN, curBoneDir)));

    if (vlen(projE) < 1e-6 || vlen(projC) < 1e-6) return 0;

    const pE = vnorm(projE), pC = vnorm(projC);
    const dotTw = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, vdot(pE, pC)));
    let twist = Math.acos(dotTw);
    const crossTw = vcross(pE, pC);
    if (vdot(crossTw, curBoneDir) < 0) twist = -twist;
    return twist;
  }

  // Position-based twist extraction from bend-axis comparison.
  // Compares actual joint bend axis (from positions, character-independent) with
  // the expected "no-twist" bend axis (swing rotation applied to a canonical reference).
  // Returns signed twist angle in radians. Returns 0 if bend is too small for stable axis.
  function extractPositionTwist(swingR, targetBoneDir, refPerp, actualBendAxis, bendAngle) {
    if (Math.abs(bendAngle) < 0.1) return 0; // need ~6° bend for stable axis
    const expected = vnorm(mat3_mulVec3(swingR, refPerp[0], refPerp[1], refPerp[2]));
    // Project both onto plane perpendicular to target bone direction (ensure orthogonality)
    const projE = vsub(expected, vscale(targetBoneDir, vdot(expected, targetBoneDir)));
    const projA = vsub(actualBendAxis, vscale(targetBoneDir, vdot(actualBendAxis, targetBoneDir)));
    if (vlen(projE) < 1e-6 || vlen(projA) < 1e-6) return 0;
    const eN = vnorm(projE), aN = vnorm(projA);
    const cosT = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, vdot(eN, aN)));
    const sinT = vdot(vcross(eN, aN), targetBoneDir);
    return Math.atan2(sinT, cosT);
  }

  // Compute a canonical reference perpendicular for a bone rest direction.
  // rawRef is the anatomical "natural bend" direction (e.g., [0,1,0] for elbow up).
  // Orthogonalizes rawRef to restBoneDir to ensure perpendicularity.
  function canonicalBendRef(rawRef, restBoneDir) {
    const d = vdot(rawRef, restBoneDir);
    const orth = vsub(rawRef, vscale(restBoneDir, d));
    return (vlen(orth) > 1e-6) ? vnorm(orth) : rawRef;
  }

  // For position-based driving: given target world positions of 3 joints,
  // rotate cage vertices so the limb matches. Works by computing the full
  // rotation chain: rest→T-pose→target for each segment.
  //
  // This replaces the Euler-based applyRotZ(tPose)+applyRotX(swing)+applyRotAxis(bend) chain.
  function driveArmFromPositions_world(
    shoulderW, elbowW, wristW,    // target world positions
    shoulderRest,                   // rest-pose shoulder position [x,y,z]
    restArmDir,                     // rest-pose arm direction (±X normalized)
    upperLen, foreLen,              // segment lengths
    upperIdx, foreIdx, handIdx,     // cage vert index arrays
    side                            // 'l' or 'r' for stableBendAxis tracking
  ) {
    const allIdx = [].concat(upperIdx, foreIdx, handIdx || []);
    const foreHandIdx = [].concat(foreIdx, handIdx || []);

    // Target upper arm direction
    const targetUpperDir = vnorm(vsub(elbowW, shoulderW));

    // Rest-pose upper arm direction (from cage)
    // The rest-pose arm points along restArmDir from shoulderRest
    const restDir = restArmDir;

    // Rotation from rest direction to target direction (axis-angle)
    let axis1 = vcross(restDir, targetUpperDir);
    const axis1Len = vlen(axis1);
    const dot1 = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, vdot(restDir, targetUpperDir)));
    const angle1 = Math.acos(dot1);

    if (axis1Len > 1e-6 && Math.abs(angle1) > 1e-4) {
      axis1 = vnorm(axis1);
      applyRotAxis(allIdx, shoulderRest, axis1[0], axis1[1], axis1[2], angle1);
    }

    // Elbow pivot after upper rotation
    const elbowPivot = [
      shoulderRest[0] + targetUpperDir[0] * upperLen,
      shoulderRest[1] + targetUpperDir[1] * upperLen,
      shoulderRest[2] + targetUpperDir[2] * upperLen
    ];

    // Forearm bend: angle between upper and lower segments
    const targetLowerDir = vnorm(vsub(wristW, elbowW));
    const dotBend = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, vdot(targetUpperDir, targetLowerDir)));
    const bendAngle = Math.acos(dotBend);

    let stableAxis = [0, 1, 0];
    if (Math.abs(bendAngle) > 1e-4) {
      // Use stableBendAxis to prevent axis flipping at small angles
      stableAxis = stableBendAxis(targetUpperDir, targetLowerDir, (side||'') + 'Elbow', [0, 1, 0]);
      applyRotAxis(foreHandIdx, elbowPivot, stableAxis[0], stableAxis[1], stableAxis[2], bendAngle);
    }

    // Wrist pivot after all rotations
    const wristPivot = [
      elbowPivot[0] + targetLowerDir[0] * foreLen,
      elbowPivot[1] + targetLowerDir[1] * foreLen,
      elbowPivot[2] + targetLowerDir[2] * foreLen
    ];

    return {
      shoulderPivot: shoulderRest,
      elbowPivot,
      wristPivot,
      upperDir: targetUpperDir,
      lowerDir: targetLowerDir,
      bendAngle,
      bendAxis: stableAxis
    };
  }

  // Drive a leg from positions: hip→knee→ankle, plus foot
  function driveLegFromPositions_world(
    hipW, kneeW, ankleW,
    hipRest, restLegDir,
    thighLen, shinLen,
    thighIdx, shinIdx, footIdx,
    side,
    restFootDir  // optional: rest-pose foot direction (body-rotated), tracked through rotations
  ) {
    const allIdx = [].concat(thighIdx, shinIdx, footIdx);
    const shinFootIdx = [].concat(shinIdx, footIdx);

    // Target thigh direction
    const targetThighDir = vnorm(vsub(kneeW, hipW));
    const restDir = restLegDir; // should be [0, -1, 0] (straight down)

    // Track foot direction through both rotations (for direct ankle driving)
    let postFootDir = restFootDir ? [restFootDir[0], restFootDir[1], restFootDir[2]] : null;

    // Rotation from rest to target for whole leg
    let axis1 = vcross(restDir, targetThighDir);
    const axis1Len = vlen(axis1);
    const dot1 = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, vdot(restDir, targetThighDir)));
    const angle1 = Math.acos(dot1);

    if (axis1Len > 1e-6 && Math.abs(angle1) > 1e-4) {
      axis1 = vnorm(axis1);
      applyRotAxis(allIdx, hipRest, axis1[0], axis1[1], axis1[2], angle1);
      // Track foot direction through R1 (thigh rotation)
      if (postFootDir) {
        const rf = rotateAroundAxis(postFootDir[0], postFootDir[1], postFootDir[2],
          0, 0, 0, axis1[0], axis1[1], axis1[2], angle1);
        postFootDir = [rf[0], rf[1], rf[2]];
      }
    }

    // Knee pivot
    const kneePivot = [
      hipRest[0] + targetThighDir[0] * thighLen,
      hipRest[1] + targetThighDir[1] * thighLen,
      hipRest[2] + targetThighDir[2] * thighLen
    ];

    // Shin bend
    const targetShinDir = vnorm(vsub(ankleW, kneeW));
    const dotBend = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, vdot(targetThighDir, targetShinDir)));
    const bendAngle = Math.acos(dotBend);

    if (Math.abs(bendAngle) > 1e-4) {
      let bendAxis = vcross(targetThighDir, targetShinDir);
      if (vlen(bendAxis) < 1e-6) {
        bendAxis = [1, 0, 0]; // default: bend forward
      }
      bendAxis = vnorm(bendAxis);
      applyRotAxis(shinFootIdx, kneePivot, bendAxis[0], bendAxis[1], bendAxis[2], bendAngle);
      // Track foot direction through R2 (knee bend)
      if (postFootDir) {
        const rf = rotateAroundAxis(postFootDir[0], postFootDir[1], postFootDir[2],
          0, 0, 0, bendAxis[0], bendAxis[1], bendAxis[2], bendAngle);
        postFootDir = [rf[0], rf[1], rf[2]];
      }
    }

    const anklePivot = [
      kneePivot[0] + targetShinDir[0] * shinLen,
      kneePivot[1] + targetShinDir[1] * shinLen,
      kneePivot[2] + targetShinDir[2] * shinLen
    ];

    return {
      hipPivot: hipRest, kneePivot, anklePivot,
      thighDir: targetThighDir, shinDir: targetShinDir,
      bendAngle,
      postFootDir: postFootDir ? vnorm(postFootDir) : null
    };
  }

  // ---------------- Cage -> Mesh deformation (Kabsch rigid-per-region) ----------------
  let lastRegionTransforms = null; // cached for debug logging
  let prevSmoothedTransforms = null; // previous frame's smoothed transforms (for temporal smoothing)
  let armDiagDone = false;         // one-shot arm comparison diagnostic

  // Per-region temporal smoothing factors.
  // Small regions (few cage verts) get more smoothing to dampen Kabsch noise.
  // 0 = full smoothing (frozen), 1 = no smoothing (raw current frame).
  const REGION_SMOOTH_ALPHA = {
    0: 0.7,   // torso: Kabsch noise on complex motion → strong smoothing
    1: 1.0,   // l_thigh: analytical → no temporal smoothing
    2: 1.0,   // r_thigh: analytical → no temporal smoothing
    3: 0.9,   // l_shin: analytical chain, light smoothing for walking stability
    4: 0.9,   // r_shin: analytical chain, light smoothing for walking stability
    5: 0.85,  // l_foot: analytical chain, moderate smoothing for ground contact
    6: 0.85,  // r_foot: analytical chain, moderate smoothing for ground contact
    7: 0.8,   // l_uarm: shoulder junction noise → moderate smoothing
    8: 0.8,   // r_uarm: shoulder junction noise → moderate smoothing
    9: 0.85,  // l_farm: responsive motion
    10: 0.85, // r_farm: responsive motion
    11: 0.80, // head: moderate smoothing
    12: 0.70, // l_hand: moderate smoothing
    13: 0.70, // r_hand: moderate smoothing
    14: 0.9   // pelvis: light smoothing for torso-pelvis junction stability
  };

  function smoothTransformsTemporal(transforms) {
    if (!prevSmoothedTransforms) {
      // First frame: just store and return as-is
      prevSmoothedTransforms = {};
      for (const rid in transforms) {
        const tf = transforms[rid];
        if (!tf) continue;
        prevSmoothedTransforms[rid] = {
          q: mat3_to_quat(tf.R),
          t: [tf.t[0], tf.t[1], tf.t[2]]
        };
      }
      return transforms;
    }

    const smoothed = {};
    for (const rid in transforms) {
      const tf = transforms[rid];
      if (!tf) continue;

      const alpha = REGION_SMOOTH_ALPHA[rid] !== undefined ? REGION_SMOOTH_ALPHA[rid] : 1.0;
      if (alpha >= 0.999) {
        // No smoothing needed
        smoothed[rid] = tf;
        prevSmoothedTransforms[rid] = {
          q: mat3_to_quat(tf.R),
          t: [tf.t[0], tf.t[1], tf.t[2]]
        };
        continue;
      }

      const prev = prevSmoothedTransforms[rid];
      if (!prev) {
        smoothed[rid] = tf;
        prevSmoothedTransforms[rid] = {
          q: mat3_to_quat(tf.R),
          t: [tf.t[0], tf.t[1], tf.t[2]]
        };
        continue;
      }

      // Slerp rotation: prev.q → current q with weight alpha
      const curQ = mat3_to_quat(tf.R);
      const blendQ = quat_slerp(prev.q, curQ, alpha);
      const blendR = quat_to_mat3(blendQ);

      // Lerp translation
      const blendT = [
        prev.t[0] + alpha * (tf.t[0] - prev.t[0]),
        prev.t[1] + alpha * (tf.t[1] - prev.t[1]),
        prev.t[2] + alpha * (tf.t[2] - prev.t[2])
      ];

      // Build smoothed transform, preserving any extra fields (like _hip, _thighDir, etc.)
      const stf = Object.assign({}, tf, { R: blendR, t: blendT });
      smoothed[rid] = stf;

      prevSmoothedTransforms[rid] = { q: blendQ, t: [blendT[0], blendT[1], blendT[2]] };
    }
    return smoothed;
  }

  // Maps each region to its bone segment [parentJoint, childJoint] for bone-skinned comparison
  const REGION_BONE_MAP = {
    0:  ['pelvis','spine_joint'],   // spine (lowest spine segment)
    1:  ['l_hip','l_knee'],         // l_thigh
    2:  ['r_hip','r_knee'],         // r_thigh
    3:  ['l_knee','l_ankle'],       // l_shin
    4:  ['r_knee','r_ankle'],       // r_shin
    5:  ['l_ankle','l_toe'],        // l_foot
    6:  ['r_ankle','r_toe'],        // r_foot
    7:  ['l_shoulder','l_elbow'],   // l_upperarm
    8:  ['r_shoulder','r_elbow'],   // r_upperarm
    9:  ['l_elbow','l_wrist'],      // l_forearm
    10: ['r_elbow','r_wrist'],      // r_forearm
    11: ['neck','head'],            // head
    12: ['l_wrist','l_mid_knuckle'],// l_hand
    13: ['r_wrist','r_mid_knuckle'],// r_hand
    14: ['pelvis','spine_joint'],   // pelvis
    15: ['spine_joint','spine1_joint'],  // spine1
    16: ['spine1_joint','spine2_joint'], // spine2
    17: ['spine2_joint','neck'],    // chest
    18: ['neck','head'],            // neck (lower part)
    19: ['spine2_joint','l_shoulder'],   // l_collar
    20: ['spine2_joint','r_shoulder'],   // r_collar
    21: ['l_toe','l_toe'],          // l_toe (uses foot end)
    22: ['r_toe','r_toe']           // r_toe (uses foot end)
  };

  // Region color map for debug visualization
  const REGION_COLORS = {
    0:  [0.5, 0.5, 0.5],  // spine: gray
    1:  [1.0, 0.5, 0.0],  // l_thigh: orange
    2:  [1.0, 0.5, 0.0],  // r_thigh: orange
    3:  [0.0, 1.0, 1.0],  // l_shin: cyan
    4:  [0.0, 1.0, 1.0],  // r_shin: cyan
    5:  [1.0, 0.0, 1.0],  // l_foot: magenta
    6:  [1.0, 0.0, 1.0],  // r_foot: magenta
    7:  [1.0, 0.0, 0.0],  // l_upperarm: red
    8:  [1.0, 0.0, 0.0],  // r_upperarm: red
    9:  [0.0, 1.0, 0.0],  // l_forearm: green
    10: [0.0, 1.0, 0.0],  // r_forearm: green
    11: [1.0, 1.0, 1.0],  // head: white
    12: [1.0, 1.0, 0.0],  // l_hand: yellow
    13: [1.0, 1.0, 0.0],  // r_hand: yellow
    14: [0.8, 0.4, 0.8],  // pelvis: purple
    15: [0.6, 0.6, 0.6],  // spine1: lighter gray
    16: [0.7, 0.7, 0.7],  // spine2: even lighter gray
    17: [0.4, 0.6, 0.8],  // chest: steel blue
    18: [0.9, 0.8, 0.7],  // neck: tan
    19: [0.8, 0.2, 0.2],  // l_collar: dark red
    20: [0.8, 0.2, 0.2],  // r_collar: dark red
    21: [0.8, 0.0, 0.8],  // l_toe: bright magenta
    22: [0.8, 0.0, 0.8],  // r_toe: bright magenta
  };

  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // BONE vs CAGE COMPARISON (diagnostic heatmap)
  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

  // Compute rigid transform for a bone segment: rest (parentRest→childRest) to current (parentCur→childCur)
  // Auto-detect toe positions from mesh geometry instead of synthetic floor projection.
  // Finds the distal end (furthest from ankle) of each foot region and uses the
  // centroid of the top 20% furthest vertices as the toe position.
  function autoDetectToeFromMesh() {
    if (!meshVertRegions || !meshRestPos || !P.l_ankle || !P.r_ankle) return;
    const nMesh = meshRestPos.length / 3;

    for (const [side, footRid, ankleKey, toeKey] of [
      ['L', 5, 'l_ankle', 'l_toe'],
      ['R', 6, 'r_ankle', 'r_toe']
    ]) {
      const ankle = P[ankleKey];
      if (!ankle) continue;

      // Collect foot region vertices with distance from ankle
      const footVerts = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
        const entries = meshVertRegions[i];
        if (!entries) continue;
        let footW = 0;
        for (let k = 0; k < entries.length; k += 2) {
          if (entries[k] === footRid) { footW = entries[k+1]; break; }
        }
        if (footW < 0.5) continue; // only dominant foot verts
        const vx = meshRestPos[i*3], vy = meshRestPos[i*3+1], vz = meshRestPos[i*3+2];
        const dx = vx - ankle[0], dy = vy - ankle[1], dz = vz - ankle[2];
        const dist = Math.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz);
        footVerts.push({ x: vx, y: vy, z: vz, dist });
      }

      if (footVerts.length < 10) continue;

      // Sort by distance from ankle, take top 20% as "toe area"
      footVerts.sort((a, b) => b.dist - a.dist);
      const topN = Math.max(5, Math.floor(footVerts.length * 0.2));
      let cx = 0, cy = 0, cz = 0;
      for (let i = 0; i < topN; i++) {
        cx += footVerts[i].x;
        cy += footVerts[i].y;
        cz += footVerts[i].z;
      }
      const oldToe = P[toeKey] ? P[toeKey].slice() : null;
      P[toeKey] = [cx / topN, cy / topN, cz / topN];
      const footLen = vlen(vsub(P[toeKey], ankle));
      console.log('[TOE AUTO-DETECT] ' + side + ': ' + toeKey + '=[' +
        P[toeKey].map(v => v.toFixed(4)).join(',') + '] from ' + topN + '/' + footVerts.length +
        ' verts, footLen=' + (footLen * 1000).toFixed(1) + 'mm' +
        (oldToe ? ' (was [' + oldToe.map(v => v.toFixed(4)).join(',') + '])' : ''));
    }
  }

  function computeBoneRigidTransform(parentRest, childRest, parentCur, childCur) {
    const restDir = vnorm(vsub(childRest, parentRest));
    const curDir  = vnorm(vsub(childCur, parentCur));
    const R = computeIKRotMatrix(restDir, curDir);
    // Translation: parentCur - R * parentRest
    const Rp = [
      R[0]*parentRest[0] + R[1]*parentRest[1] + R[2]*parentRest[2],
      R[3]*parentRest[0] + R[4]*parentRest[1] + R[5]*parentRest[2],
      R[6]*parentRest[0] + R[7]*parentRest[1] + R[8]*parentRest[2]
    ];
    return { R: R, t: [parentCur[0]-Rp[0], parentCur[1]-Rp[1], parentCur[2]-Rp[2]] };
  }

  // Compute per-region transforms using FBX joint quaternions (includes twist).
  // Unlike computeAllRegionTransforms (cage-derived, swing-only for most bones),
  // this uses the FBX animation's world quaternions which capture the FULL rotation
  // including axial twist. Produces standard bone skinning quality.
  //
  // Uses cage retargetedJoints for position anchoring (includes ground correction, IK)
  // but FBX quaternion rotations for the rotation matrix (includes twist).
  function computeFBXQuatTransforms(t) {
    if (!jointPosData || !jointPosData.rest_quats || !retargetedJoints || !retargetedJoints.pelvis) return null;

    // Step 1: Compute world-space quaternion deltas and rotation matrices for all joints
    const jointQDelta = {};  // joint name → quaternion delta [x,y,z,w]
    const jointRMat = {};    // joint name → 3x3 rotation matrix
    const allJoints = Object.keys(jointPosData.rest_quats);
    for (const jn of allJoints) {
      const curQ = getJointQuaternionAtTime(jn, t);
      const restQ = jointPosData.rest_quats[jn];
      if (!curQ || !restQ) continue;
      let qd = quat_mul(curQ, quat_conjugate(restQ));
      if (qd[3] < 0) qd = [-qd[0], -qd[1], -qd[2], -qd[3]];
      jointQDelta[jn] = qd;
      jointRMat[jn] = quat_to_mat3(qd);
    }

    // Step 2: FK-consistent positions chained from pelvis through skeleton hierarchy.
    // retargetedJoints doesn't include ground correction — add lastGroundCorrection to Y.
    // FK ensures parent/child transforms agree at shared joints (zero seam at boundaries).
    const gc = lastGroundCorrection;
    const fkPos = {};
    fkPos['pelvis'] = [retargetedJoints.pelvis[0], retargetedJoints.pelvis[1] + gc, retargetedJoints.pelvis[2]];

    const FK_CHILDREN = {
      'pelvis': ['spine_joint', 'l_hip', 'r_hip'],
      'spine_joint': ['spine1_joint'],
      'spine1_joint': ['spine2_joint'],
      'spine2_joint': ['neck', 'l_collar', 'r_collar'],
      'neck': ['head'],
      'l_collar': ['l_shoulder'], 'r_collar': ['r_shoulder'],
      'l_hip': ['l_knee'], 'r_hip': ['r_knee'],
      'l_knee': ['l_ankle'], 'r_knee': ['r_ankle'],
      'l_ankle': ['l_toe'], 'r_ankle': ['r_toe'],
      'l_shoulder': ['l_elbow'], 'r_shoulder': ['r_elbow'],
      'l_elbow': ['l_wrist'], 'r_elbow': ['r_wrist'],
      'l_wrist': ['l_mid_knuckle'], 'r_wrist': ['r_mid_knuckle']
    };

    // BFS: childPos = parentPos + R_parentDelta * (P[child] - P[parent])
    const queue = ['pelvis'];
    while (queue.length > 0) {
      const parent = queue.shift();
      const children = FK_CHILDREN[parent];
      if (!children) continue;
      const R_p = jointRMat[parent];
      if (!R_p || !P[parent]) continue;
      for (const child of children) {
        if (!P[child]) continue;
        const dx = P[child][0] - P[parent][0], dy = P[child][1] - P[parent][1], dz = P[child][2] - P[parent][2];
        const rd = mat3_mulVec3(R_p, dx, dy, dz);
        fkPos[child] = [fkPos[parent][0] + rd[0], fkPos[parent][1] + rd[1], fkPos[parent][2] + rd[2]];
        queue.push(child);
      }
    }

    // Step 3: Build per-region transforms using FBX quaternion rotation + FK positions.
    // Each region uses the controlling joint's world quaternion delta (full rotation with twist).
    const QUAT_JOINT = {
      0: 'spine_joint',       // spine segment
      1: 'l_hip', 2: 'r_hip',
      3: 'l_knee', 4: 'r_knee',
      5: 'l_ankle', 6: 'r_ankle',
      7: 'l_shoulder', 8: 'r_shoulder',
      9: 'l_elbow', 10: 'r_elbow',
      11: 'head',
      12: 'l_wrist', 13: 'r_wrist',
      14: 'pelvis',
      15: 'spine1_joint',
      16: 'spine2_joint',
      17: 'spine2_joint',     // chest = spine2
      18: 'neck',
      19: 'l_collar', 20: 'r_collar',  // collar joints (not spine2)
      21: 'l_toe', 22: 'r_toe'         // toe joints (fallback to ankle)
    };

    const transforms = {};
    for (let rid = 0; rid <= 22; rid++) {
      const bm = REGION_BONE_MAP[rid];
      if (!bm) continue;
      // Try primary joint, then REGION_BONE_MAP parent as fallback
      const qJoint = QUAT_JOINT[rid] || bm[0];
      let R = jointRMat[qJoint];
      let anchorJoint = qJoint;
      // If primary joint has no quaternion data, try REGION_BONE_MAP parent
      if (!R && qJoint !== bm[0]) {
        R = jointRMat[bm[0]];
        anchorJoint = bm[0];
      }
      const jRest = P[anchorJoint];
      const jCur = fkPos[anchorJoint];

      if (R && jRest && jCur) {
        const Rj = mat3_mulVec3(R, jRest[0], jRest[1], jRest[2]);
        transforms[rid] = { R: R, t: [jCur[0]-Rj[0], jCur[1]-Rj[1], jCur[2]-Rj[2]] };
      } else {
        // Fallback: direction-only from FK positions
        const pRest = P[bm[0]], cRest = P[bm[1]];
        const pCur = fkPos[bm[0]] || retargetedJoints[bm[0]];
        const cCur = fkPos[bm[1]] || retargetedJoints[bm[1]];
        if (pRest && cRest && pCur && cCur) {
          transforms[rid] = computeBoneRigidTransform(pRest, cRest, pCur, cCur);
        }
      }
    }

    // NOTE: Twist/bend gradient support (R_noTwist, R_noBend) intentionally omitted.
    // World quaternion deltas don't decompose cleanly into bend+twist because they bake
    // in the rest-pose joint orientation. Using them with deformMesh's gradient code
    // causes 177x stretch vs 42x without. The analytical pipeline's gradients work because
    // it constructs R_shin = R_kneeBend * R_thigh (clean incremental composition).

    // One-shot FK diagnostic
    if (!window._fkDiagDone) {
      window._fkDiagDone = true;
      console.log('=== FBX QUAT FK DIAGNOSTIC ===');
      console.log('  lastGroundCorrection=' + gc.toFixed(4));
      const checkJoints = ['pelvis','l_hip','l_knee','l_ankle','l_toe','r_hip','r_knee','r_ankle','r_toe',
        'spine_joint','spine2_joint','neck','head','l_shoulder','l_elbow','l_wrist'];
      for (const jn of checkJoints) {
        const fk = fkPos[jn], rj = retargetedJoints[jn];
        if (fk && rj) {
          const d = Math.sqrt((fk[0]-rj[0])**2+(fk[1]-rj[1]-gc)**2+(fk[2]-rj[2])**2);
          console.log('  ' + jn + ': FK=[' + fk.map(v=>v.toFixed(3)) + '] rj=[' + rj.map(v=>v.toFixed(3)) + '] diff=' + (d*1000).toFixed(1) + 'mm');
        }
      }
    }

    return transforms;
  }

  // ── FBX DEFORMATION TRANSFER ──
  // Deforms the FBX mesh using its own skeleton (FBX rest positions as pivots),
  // then transfers displacement to our mesh via K-neighbor interpolation.
  // This bypasses our weight/transform pipeline entirely and gives the closest
  // possible match to three.js's own GPU skinning of the FBX mesh.
  let _fbxDeformBuf = null;  // cached Float32Array for deformed FBX positions
  let _fbxJointRest = null;  // FBX joint rest positions in our coordinate space

  function computeFBXDeformTransfer(t, outPos) {
    if (!fbxSkinData || !jointPosData || !jointPosData.rest_quats ||
        !retargetedJoints || !retargetedJoints.pelvis || !meshRestPos ||
        !window._fbxVertMapping) return false;

    const mapping = window._fbxVertMapping;
    const K = mapping.K;
    const nMesh = mapping.nMesh;
    const fbxPos = fbxSkinData.positions;
    const fbxWts = fbxSkinData.weights;
    const nFBX = fbxSkinData.nVerts;

    // Build FBX joint rest positions (with P.pelvis offset) once
    if (!_fbxJointRest && jointPosData.rest_pose && P.pelvis) {
      _fbxJointRest = {};
      for (const jn of Object.keys(jointPosData.rest_pose)) {
        const rp = jointPosData.rest_pose[jn];
        _fbxJointRest[jn] = [
          rp[0] + P.pelvis[0],
          rp[1] + P.pelvis[1],
          rp[2] + P.pelvis[2]
        ];
      }
    }
    if (!_fbxJointRest) return false;

    // Step 1: Compute world-space quaternion deltas + rotation matrices
    const jointRMat = {};
    const allJoints = Object.keys(jointPosData.rest_quats);
    for (const jn of allJoints) {
      const curQ = getJointQuaternionAtTime(jn, t);
      const restQ = jointPosData.rest_quats[jn];
      if (!curQ || !restQ) continue;
      let qd = quat_mul(curQ, quat_conjugate(restQ));
      if (qd[3] < 0) qd = [-qd[0], -qd[1], -qd[2], -qd[3]];
      jointRMat[jn] = quat_to_mat3(qd);
    }

    // Step 2: FK chain using FBX rest-pose bone vectors (not cage P vectors).
    // This ensures FK joint positions match FBX skeleton proportions.
    const gc = lastGroundCorrection;
    const fkPos = {};
    fkPos['pelvis'] = [retargetedJoints.pelvis[0], retargetedJoints.pelvis[1] + gc, retargetedJoints.pelvis[2]];

    const FK_CHILDREN = {
      'pelvis': ['spine_joint', 'l_hip', 'r_hip'],
      'spine_joint': ['spine1_joint'],
      'spine1_joint': ['spine2_joint'],
      'spine2_joint': ['neck', 'l_collar', 'r_collar'],
      'neck': ['head'],
      'l_collar': ['l_shoulder'], 'r_collar': ['r_shoulder'],
      'l_hip': ['l_knee'], 'r_hip': ['r_knee'],
      'l_knee': ['l_ankle'], 'r_knee': ['r_ankle'],
      'l_ankle': ['l_toe'], 'r_ankle': ['r_toe'],
      'l_shoulder': ['l_elbow'], 'r_shoulder': ['r_elbow'],
      'l_elbow': ['l_wrist'], 'r_elbow': ['r_wrist'],
      'l_wrist': ['l_mid_knuckle'], 'r_wrist': ['r_mid_knuckle']
    };

    const queue = ['pelvis'];
    while (queue.length > 0) {
      const parent = queue.shift();
      const children = FK_CHILDREN[parent];
      if (!children) continue;
      const R_p = jointRMat[parent];
      if (!R_p || !_fbxJointRest[parent]) continue;
      for (const child of children) {
        if (!_fbxJointRest[child]) continue;
        // Use FBX rest-pose bone vectors (FBX proportions)
        const dx = _fbxJointRest[child][0] - _fbxJointRest[parent][0];
        const dy = _fbxJointRest[child][1] - _fbxJointRest[parent][1];
        const dz = _fbxJointRest[child][2] - _fbxJointRest[parent][2];
        const rd = mat3_mulVec3(R_p, dx, dy, dz);
        fkPos[child] = [fkPos[parent][0] + rd[0], fkPos[parent][1] + rd[1], fkPos[parent][2] + rd[2]];
        queue.push(child);
      }
    }

    // Step 3: Build per-region transforms using FBX rest positions as pivots
    const QUAT_JOINT = {
      0: 'spine_joint', 1: 'l_hip', 2: 'r_hip',
      3: 'l_knee', 4: 'r_knee', 5: 'l_ankle', 6: 'r_ankle',
      7: 'l_shoulder', 8: 'r_shoulder', 9: 'l_elbow', 10: 'r_elbow',
      11: 'head', 12: 'l_wrist', 13: 'r_wrist', 14: 'pelvis',
      15: 'spine1_joint', 16: 'spine2_joint', 17: 'spine2_joint',
      18: 'neck', 19: 'l_collar', 20: 'r_collar', 21: 'l_toe', 22: 'r_toe'
    };

    const transforms = {};
    for (let rid = 0; rid <= 22; rid++) {
      const bm = REGION_BONE_MAP[rid];
      if (!bm) continue;
      const qJoint = QUAT_JOINT[rid] || bm[0];
      let R = jointRMat[qJoint];
      let anchorJoint = qJoint;
      if (!R && qJoint !== bm[0]) {
        R = jointRMat[bm[0]];
        anchorJoint = bm[0];
      }
      const jRest = _fbxJointRest[anchorJoint]; // FBX rest position (not cage P)
      const jCur = fkPos[anchorJoint];
      if (R && jRest && jCur) {
        const Rj = mat3_mulVec3(R, jRest[0], jRest[1], jRest[2]);
        transforms[rid] = { R: R, t: [jCur[0]-Rj[0], jCur[1]-Rj[1], jCur[2]-Rj[2]] };
      }
    }

    // Step 4: Deform FBX vertices via standard LBS using FBX weights
    if (!_fbxDeformBuf || _fbxDeformBuf.length !== nFBX * 3) {
      _fbxDeformBuf = new Float32Array(nFBX * 3);
    }

    // Collect unique referenced FBX vertices for efficient deformation
    const refSet = new Set();
    for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
      for (let k = 0; k < K; k++) {
        const fi = mapping.fbxIdx[i * K + k];
        if (fi >= 0) refSet.add(fi);
      }
    }

    // Deform only referenced FBX vertices
    for (const fi of refSet) {
      const rx = fbxPos[fi*3], ry = fbxPos[fi*3+1], rz = fbxPos[fi*3+2];
      const entries = fbxWts[fi];
      let px = 0, py = 0, pz = 0, totalW = 0;
      for (let e = 0; e < entries.length; e += 2) {
        const rid = entries[e];
        const w = entries[e + 1];
        const bt = transforms[rid];
        if (!bt) continue;
        const R = bt.R, tr = bt.t;
        px += w * (R[0]*rx + R[1]*ry + R[2]*rz + tr[0]);
        py += w * (R[3]*rx + R[4]*ry + R[5]*rz + tr[1]);
        pz += w * (R[6]*rx + R[7]*ry + R[8]*rz + tr[2]);
        totalW += w;
      }
      if (totalW > 1e-6) {
        _fbxDeformBuf[fi*3]   = px / totalW;
        _fbxDeformBuf[fi*3+1] = py / totalW;
        _fbxDeformBuf[fi*3+2] = pz / totalW;
      } else {
        _fbxDeformBuf[fi*3] = rx; _fbxDeformBuf[fi*3+1] = ry; _fbxDeformBuf[fi*3+2] = rz;
      }
    }

    // Step 5: Transfer displacement from FBX mesh to our mesh
    // ourDeformed = ourRest + sum_k(w_k * (fbxDeformed_k - fbxRest_k))
    for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
      let dx = 0, dy = 0, dz = 0;
      for (let k = 0; k < K; k++) {
        const fi = mapping.fbxIdx[i * K + k];
        const w = mapping.interpW[i * K + k];
        if (fi < 0 || w < 1e-6) continue;
        dx += w * (_fbxDeformBuf[fi*3]   - fbxPos[fi*3]);
        dy += w * (_fbxDeformBuf[fi*3+1] - fbxPos[fi*3+1]);
        dz += w * (_fbxDeformBuf[fi*3+2] - fbxPos[fi*3+2]);
      }
      outPos[i*3]   = meshRestPos[i*3]   + dx;
      outPos[i*3+1] = meshRestPos[i*3+1] + dy;
      outPos[i*3+2] = meshRestPos[i*3+2] + dz;
    }

    // One-shot diagnostic
    if (!window._fbxDeformDiagDone) {
      window._fbxDeformDiagDone = true;
      console.log('=== FBX DEFORM TRANSFER DIAGNOSTIC ===');
      console.log('  FBX vertices referenced: ' + refSet.size + ' / ' + nFBX);
      console.log('  Regions with transforms: ' + Object.keys(transforms).length + '/23');
      for (let rid = 0; rid <= 22; rid++) {
        if (!transforms[rid]) console.log('  MISSING transform for region ' + rid);
      }
    }
    return true;
  }

  // Compute bone-skinned reference positions for all mesh verts using retargetedJoints
  let boneRefPos = null; // cached Float32Array
  function computeBoneSkinnedPositions(outPos) {
    if (!meshVertRegions || !retargetedJoints.pelvis) return false;
    const nMesh = meshRestPos.length / 3;

    // Precompute rigid transforms per region
    const boneTransforms = {};
    for (let rid = 0; rid <= 22; rid++) {
      const bm = REGION_BONE_MAP[rid];
      if (!bm) continue;
      const pRest = P[bm[0]], cRest = P[bm[1]];
      const pCur = retargetedJoints[bm[0]], cCur = retargetedJoints[bm[1]];
      if (!pRest || !cRest || !pCur || !cCur) continue;
      boneTransforms[rid] = computeBoneRigidTransform(pRest, cRest, pCur, cCur);
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
      const rx = meshRestPos[i*3], ry = meshRestPos[i*3+1], rz = meshRestPos[i*3+2];
      const entries = meshVertRegions[i];
      let px = 0, py = 0, pz = 0;
      let totalW = 0;

      for (let e = 0; e < entries.length; e += 2) {
        const rid = entries[e];
        const w   = entries[e + 1];
        const bt  = boneTransforms[rid];
        if (!bt) continue;
        const R = bt.R, t = bt.t;
        px += w * (R[0]*rx + R[1]*ry + R[2]*rz + t[0]);
        py += w * (R[3]*rx + R[4]*ry + R[5]*rz + t[1]);
        pz += w * (R[6]*rx + R[7]*ry + R[8]*rz + t[2]);
        totalW += w;
      }

      if (totalW > 1e-6) {
        outPos[i*3]   = px / totalW;
        outPos[i*3+1] = py / totalW;
        outPos[i*3+2] = pz / totalW;
      } else {
        outPos[i*3] = rx; outPos[i*3+1] = ry; outPos[i*3+2] = rz;
      }
    }
    return true;
  }

  // Compute FBX ground truth positions using direction-based LBS with FBX bone directions.
  // Same as computeBoneSkinnedPositions but uses FBX animation's actual bone directions
  // (from getJointPositionAtTime) instead of cage retargetedJoints directions.
  // Parent positions are anchored to retargetedJoints so global position matches the cage.
  // The difference isolates: "how much does the cage's bone direction differ from FBX intent?"
  let fbxGTBuf = null; // cached Float32Array for FBX ground truth positions
  function computeFBXGroundTruth(outPos, t) {
    if (!meshVertRegions || !meshRestPos || !jointPosData || !retargetedJoints || !retargetedJoints.pelvis) return false;
    const nMesh = meshRestPos.length / 3;

    // Use FK-consistent quaternion transforms (same as main mesh when useFBXTransforms is on).
    // This gives a proper ground truth with full bone rotations (including twist).
    const boneTransforms = computeFBXQuatTransforms(t);
    if (!boneTransforms) return false;

    // Standard LBS using meshVertRegions weights
    for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
      const rx = meshRestPos[i*3], ry = meshRestPos[i*3+1], rz = meshRestPos[i*3+2];
      const entries = meshVertRegions[i];
      let px = 0, py = 0, pz = 0;
      let totalW = 0;

      for (let e = 0; e < entries.length; e += 2) {
        const rid = entries[e];
        const w   = entries[e + 1];
        const bt  = boneTransforms[rid];
        if (!bt) continue;
        const R = bt.R, tr = bt.t;
        px += w * (R[0]*rx + R[1]*ry + R[2]*rz + tr[0]);
        py += w * (R[3]*rx + R[4]*ry + R[5]*rz + tr[1]);
        pz += w * (R[6]*rx + R[7]*ry + R[8]*rz + tr[2]);
        totalW += w;
      }

      if (totalW > 1e-6) {
        outPos[i*3]   = px / totalW;
        outPos[i*3+1] = py / totalW;
        outPos[i*3+2] = pz / totalW;
      } else {
        outPos[i*3] = rx; outPos[i*3+1] = ry; outPos[i*3+2] = rz;
      }
    }
    return true;
  }

  // Compute comparison heatmap colors: cage-deformed vs bone-skinned reference
  function computeComparisonColors(deformedPos, refPos, colors) {
    if (!colors) return;
    const nMesh = meshRestPos.length / 3;
    const regionNames = {0:'spine',1:'l_thigh',2:'r_thigh',3:'l_shin',4:'r_shin',
      5:'l_foot',6:'r_foot',7:'l_uarm',8:'r_uarm',9:'l_farm',10:'r_farm',
      11:'head',12:'l_hand',13:'r_hand',14:'pelvis',15:'spine1',16:'spine2',
      17:'chest',18:'neck',19:'l_collar',20:'r_collar',21:'l_toe',22:'r_toe'};

    // Per-region error stats (for one-shot diagnostic)
    const regionStats = {};

    for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
      const dx = deformedPos[i*3] - refPos[i*3];
      const dy = deformedPos[i*3+1] - refPos[i*3+1];
      const dz = deformedPos[i*3+2] - refPos[i*3+2];
      const err = Math.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz);

      // Get dominant region
      const entries = meshVertRegions[i];
      let domRid = 0, domW = -1;
      for (let e = 0; e < entries.length; e += 2) {
        if (entries[e+1] > domW) { domRid = entries[e]; domW = entries[e+1]; }
      }
      if (!regionStats[domRid]) regionStats[domRid] = { sum: 0, count: 0, max: 0, maxVert: 0 };
      regionStats[domRid].sum += err;
      regionStats[domRid].count++;
      if (err > regionStats[domRid].max) { regionStats[domRid].max = err; regionStats[domRid].maxVert = i; }

      // Color: <1mm green, 1-5mm green→yellow, 5-15mm yellow→red, >15mm red
      const errMM = err * 1000; // convert to mm (assuming model units = meters)
      let r, g, b;
      if (errMM < 1) {
        r = 0; g = 0.8; b = 0;
      } else if (errMM < 5) {
        const t = (errMM - 1) / 4;
        r = t; g = 0.8; b = 0;
      } else if (errMM < 15) {
        const t = (errMM - 5) / 10;
        r = 1; g = 0.8 * (1 - t); b = 0;
      } else {
        r = 1; g = 0; b = 0;
      }
      colors[i*3] = r; colors[i*3+1] = g; colors[i*3+2] = b;
    }

    // One-shot diagnostic log
    if (!window._boneCompDiagDone) {
      window._boneCompDiagDone = true;
      console.log('=== BONE vs CAGE COMPARISON ===');
      let overallSum = 0, overallCount = 0, worstRegion = '', worstMax = 0;
      const sorted = Object.entries(regionStats).sort((a,b) => b[1].max - a[1].max);
      for (const [rid, s] of sorted) {
        const avg = (s.sum / s.count * 1000).toFixed(1);
        const max = (s.max * 1000).toFixed(1);
        const name = regionNames[rid] || rid;
        console.log('Region ' + rid + ' (' + name + '): avg ' + avg + 'mm, max ' + max + 'mm (vert ' + s.maxVert + ')');
        overallSum += s.sum;
        overallCount += s.count;
        if (s.max > worstMax) { worstMax = s.max; worstRegion = name; }
      }
      const overallAvg = (overallSum / overallCount * 1000).toFixed(1);
      console.log('Overall: avg ' + overallAvg + 'mm | Worst region: ' + worstRegion + ' (' + (worstMax * 1000).toFixed(1) + 'mm max)');
    }
  }

  // ── Diagnostic HUD: real-time per-region error display ──
  let _hudFrameCounter = 0;
  let _hudWorstFrames = {};  // { rid: { rmse, time } }
  const HUD_REGION_NAMES = {0:'spine',1:'l_thigh',2:'r_thigh',3:'l_shin',4:'r_shin',
    5:'l_foot',6:'r_foot',7:'l_uarm',8:'r_uarm',9:'l_farm',10:'r_farm',
    11:'head',12:'l_hand',13:'r_hand',14:'pelvis',15:'spine1',16:'spine2',
    17:'chest',18:'neck',19:'l_collar',20:'r_collar',21:'l_toe',22:'r_toe'};

  function updateDiagHUD(pos, refPos, nMesh) {
    _hudFrameCounter++;
    if (_hudFrameCounter % 3 !== 0) return; // throttle to every 3rd frame

    // Per-region accumulation
    const regionStats = {};
    let overallSum = 0, overallSumSq = 0, overallCount = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
      const dx = pos[i*3] - refPos[i*3];
      const dy = pos[i*3+1] - refPos[i*3+1];
      const dz = pos[i*3+2] - refPos[i*3+2];
      const errMM = Math.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz) * 1000;
      const entries = meshVertRegions[i];
      let domRid = 0, domW = -1;
      for (let e = 0; e < entries.length; e += 2) {
        if (entries[e+1] > domW) { domRid = entries[e]; domW = entries[e+1]; }
      }
      if (!regionStats[domRid]) regionStats[domRid] = { sum: 0, sumSq: 0, count: 0, max: 0 };
      regionStats[domRid].sum += errMM;
      regionStats[domRid].sumSq += errMM * errMM;
      regionStats[domRid].count++;
      if (errMM > regionStats[domRid].max) regionStats[domRid].max = errMM;
      overallSum += errMM;
      overallSumSq += errMM * errMM;
      overallCount++;
    }

    // Per-region rotation and translation error decomposition
    const regionDecomp = {};
    for (let rid = 0; rid <= 22; rid++) {
      const bm = REGION_BONE_MAP[rid];
      if (!bm) continue;
      const pRest = P[bm[0]], cRest = P[bm[1]];
      const pCur = retargetedJoints[bm[0]], cCur = retargetedJoints[bm[1]];
      if (!pRest || !cRest || !pCur || !cCur) continue;
      // Cage transform (from Kabsch)
      const cageTf = lastRegionTransforms ? lastRegionTransforms[rid] : null;
      // Bone reference transform
      const boneTf = computeBoneRigidTransform(pRest, cRest, pCur, cCur);
      if (!cageTf || !boneTf) continue;
      // Rotation error decomposed into swing + twist
      const Rc = cageTf.R, Rb = boneTf.R;
      // R_diff = R_cage * R_bone^T (row-major 3x3)
      const Rd = new Float64Array(9);
      for (let row = 0; row < 3; row++) {
        for (let col = 0; col < 3; col++) {
          Rd[row*3+col] = Rc[row*3]*Rb[col*3] + Rc[row*3+1]*Rb[col*3+1] + Rc[row*3+2]*Rb[col*3+2];
        }
      }
      // Total rotation error from trace
      const trR = Math.max(-1, Math.min(3, Rd[0] + Rd[4] + Rd[8]));
      const rotErr = Math.acos(Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, (trR - 1) / 2))) * 180 / Math.PI;
      // Swing-twist decomposition
      let swingErr = rotErr, twistErr = 0;
      const isSelfRef = (bm[0] === bm[1]); // toe regions 21/22
      if (!isSelfRef) {
        const qDiff = mat3_to_quat(Rd);
        const boneAxis = vnorm(vsub(cRest, pRest));
        const twistAngle = swingTwistDecompose(qDiff, boneAxis);
        twistErr = Math.abs(twistAngle) * 180 / Math.PI;
        // Swing = total quaternion with twist removed
        const proj = qDiff[0]*boneAxis[0] + qDiff[1]*boneAxis[1] + qDiff[2]*boneAxis[2];
        let tx = proj*boneAxis[0], ty = proj*boneAxis[1], tz = proj*boneAxis[2];
        const tw = qDiff[3];
        const tlen = Math.hypot(tx, ty, tz, tw);
        if (tlen > 1e-10) {
          tx /= tlen; ty /= tlen; tz /= tlen;
          const twn = tw / tlen;
          // q_swing = q_diff * conj(q_twist)
          const qTwist = [tx, ty, tz, twn];
          const qSwing = quat_mul(qDiff, quat_conjugate(qTwist));
          const swingSinHalf = Math.hypot(qSwing[0], qSwing[1], qSwing[2]);
          swingErr = 2 * Math.atan2(swingSinHalf, Math.abs(qSwing[3])) * 180 / Math.PI;
        }
      }
      // Translation error
      const tdx = cageTf.t[0] - boneTf.t[0];
      const tdy = cageTf.t[1] - boneTf.t[1];
      const tdz = cageTf.t[2] - boneTf.t[2];
      const transErr = Math.sqrt(tdx*tdx + tdy*tdy + tdz*tdz) * 1000;
      regionDecomp[rid] = { rotErr, transErr, swingErr, twistErr };
    }

    // Overall RMSE
    const overallRMSE = overallCount > 0 ? Math.sqrt(overallSumSq / overallCount) : 0;

    // Sort regions by RMSE descending
    const sorted = Object.entries(regionStats).map(([rid, s]) => {
      const rmse = Math.sqrt(s.sumSq / s.count);
      return { rid: +rid, name: HUD_REGION_NAMES[+rid] || ('r' + rid), rmse, max: s.max, count: s.count, decomp: regionDecomp[+rid] };
    }).sort((a, b) => b.rmse - a.rmse);

    // Track worst RMSE per region across all frames
    const curTime = typeof time !== 'undefined' ? time % cycleDuration : 0;
    for (const r of sorted) {
      const prev = _hudWorstFrames[r.rid];
      if (!prev || r.rmse > prev.rmse) {
        _hudWorstFrames[r.rid] = { rmse: r.rmse, time: curTime };
      }
      r.worstTime = _hudWorstFrames[r.rid].time;
      r.worstRmse = _hudWorstFrames[r.rid].rmse;
    }

    // Build HTML
    const frameEl = document.getElementById('hudFrameInfo');
    const overallEl = document.getElementById('hudOverallError');
    const listEl = document.getElementById('hudRegionList');

    const totalFrames = (typeof jointPosData !== 'undefined' && jointPosData && jointPosData.fps) ? Math.round(cycleDuration * jointPosData.fps) : '?';
    const curFrame = (typeof jointPosData !== 'undefined' && jointPosData && jointPosData.fps) ? Math.round(curTime * jointPosData.fps) : '?';
    frameEl.textContent = 'Frame ' + curFrame + '/' + totalFrames + ' t=' + curTime.toFixed(3) + 's';
    // Sync scrubber position (without triggering input event)
    const scrubber = document.getElementById('hudScrubber');
    if (scrubber && cycleDuration > 0) {
      scrubber.value = curTime / cycleDuration;
    }
    const oc = overallRMSE < 1 ? '#4ecdc4' : overallRMSE < 5 ? '#ffe066' : '#ff6b6b';
    overallEl.innerHTML = '<span style="color:' + oc + '">Overall RMSE: ' + overallRMSE.toFixed(2) + 'mm</span>';

    let html = '<table style="border-collapse:collapse;width:100%">';
    html += '<tr style="color:#888"><td>Region</td><td>RMSE</td><td>Max</td><td>Sw/Tw/T</td><td></td></tr>';
    for (const r of sorted) {
      const c = r.rmse < 1 ? '#4ecdc4' : r.rmse < 5 ? '#ffe066' : '#ff6b6b';
      const barW = Math.min(60, r.rmse * 4);
      let decomp = '';
      if (r.decomp) {
        decomp = 'sw:' + r.decomp.swingErr.toFixed(1) + '\u00b0 tw:' + r.decomp.twistErr.toFixed(1) + '\u00b0';
        // Per-bone twist delta for limb regions
        const REGION_TWIST_MAP = {1:'l_thigh',2:'r_thigh',7:'l_uarm',8:'r_uarm',9:'l_farm',10:'r_farm'};
        const twistBone = REGION_TWIST_MAP[r.rid];
        if (twistBone && retargetedTwists[twistBone] !== undefined && fbxFrameTwists[twistBone] !== undefined) {
          const deltaTw = Math.abs(retargetedTwists[twistBone] - fbxFrameTwists[twistBone]) * 180 / Math.PI;
          decomp += ' \u0394tw:' + deltaTw.toFixed(1) + '\u00b0';
        }
        decomp += ' t:' + r.decomp.transErr.toFixed(1) + 'mm';
      }
      const worstTip = r.worstTime !== undefined ? 'Worst: ' + r.worstRmse.toFixed(1) + 'mm @ t=' + r.worstTime.toFixed(3) + 's (click to seek)' : '';
      const wtAttr = r.worstTime !== undefined ? ' data-worst-time="' + r.worstTime.toFixed(4) + '"' : '';
      html += '<tr style="color:' + c + ';cursor:pointer"' + wtAttr + ' title="' + worstTip + '">' +
        '<td style="padding:1px 4px">' + r.name + '</td>' +
        '<td style="padding:1px 4px;text-align:right">' + r.rmse.toFixed(1) + '</td>' +
        '<td style="padding:1px 4px;text-align:right">' + r.max.toFixed(1) + '</td>' +
        '<td style="padding:1px 4px;font-size:9px">' + decomp + '</td>' +
        '<td style="padding:1px 0"><div style="background:' + c + ';height:6px;width:' + barW + 'px;border-radius:3px"></div></td>' +
        '</tr>';
    }
    html += '</table>';
    listEl.innerHTML = html;
  }

  // ── HUD click-to-seek and scrubber handlers ──
  function _hudSeekToTime(t) {
    // Pause playback and seek to specific time
    const playCheck = document.getElementById('play');
    if (playCheck) playCheck.checked = false;
    time = t;
    if (typeof driveCageFromMixamo === 'function') driveCageFromMixamo(time);
    if (typeof deformMesh === 'function') deformMesh();
    if (typeof updateCageMesh === 'function' && cageMesh) updateCageMesh();
  }
  (function() {
    // Click handler: seek to worst frame when clicking a region row
    document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
      const tr = e.target.closest('#hudRegionList tr[data-worst-time]');
      if (!tr) return;
      const wt = parseFloat(tr.getAttribute('data-worst-time'));
      if (!isNaN(wt)) _hudSeekToTime(wt);
    });
    // Scrubber input handler
    document.addEventListener('input', function(e) {
      if (e.target.id !== 'hudScrubber') return;
      const frac = parseFloat(e.target.value);
      if (isNaN(frac)) return;
      _hudSeekToTime(frac * cycleDuration);
    });
  })();

  // ── Ghost/reference mesh overlay ──
  let ghostMesh = null;
  function ensureGhostMesh() {
    if (ghostMesh) return;
    if (!meshGeo || !meshGeo.index) return;
    const nMesh = meshRestPos.length / 3;
    const ghostGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    ghostGeo.setIndex(meshGeo.index);
    const ghostPositions = new Float32Array(nMesh * 3);
    ghostGeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(ghostPositions, 3));
    const ghostMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
      color: 0x00aaff, wireframe: true, transparent: true, opacity: 0.35
    });
    ghostMesh = new THREE.Mesh(ghostGeo, ghostMat);
    ghostMesh.renderOrder = 998;
    scene.add(ghostMesh);
  }

  function deformMesh(){
    if (!meshVertRegions) return;
    const alpha = +$('alpha').value;
    const pos = meshGeo.attributes.position.array;
    const nMesh = meshRestPos.length / 3;

    // Debug region coloring / strain / bone error visualization
    const debugRegions = $('debugRegions').checked;
    const showStrainCheck = $('showStrain').checked;
    const showBoneError = $('showBoneError').checked;
    const showSurfaceHealth = $('showSurfaceHealth').checked;
    const showFBXGT = $('showFBXGT').checked;
    const useVertexColors = debugRegions || showStrainCheck || showBoneError || showSurfaceHealth || showFBXGT;
    const colors = meshGeo.attributes.color ? meshGeo.attributes.color.array : null;
    if (meshObj) {
      meshObj.material.vertexColors = useVertexColors;
      // When debug regions are on, hide the texture so vertex colors show as
      // pure flat colors. Otherwise the texture multiplies with vertex colors,
      // making it impossible to distinguish regions on textured meshes.
      if (useVertexColors && meshObj.material.map) {
        meshObj.material._savedMap = meshObj.material.map;
        meshObj.material.map = null;
      } else if (!useVertexColors && meshObj.material._savedMap) {
        meshObj.material.map = meshObj.material._savedMap;
        delete meshObj.material._savedMap;
      }
      meshObj.material.needsUpdate = true;
    }

    // ── FBX deform transfer: bypass entire weight/transform pipeline ──
    // Deforms FBX mesh using its own skeleton, transfers displacement to our mesh.
    if ($('fbxDeformTransfer').checked && fbxSkinData && jointPosData && jointPosData.rest_quats && window._fbxVertMapping) {
      if (computeFBXDeformTransfer(time, pos)) {
        meshGeo.attributes.position.needsUpdate = true;
        // Still compute transforms for diagnostics
        let transforms;
        if ($('useFBXTransforms').checked && jointPosData && jointPosData.rest_quats) {
          transforms = computeFBXQuatTransforms(time);
        }
