          l_thigh: bLen(rj.l_hip, rj.l_knee), r_thigh: bLen(rj.r_hip, rj.r_knee),
          l_shin: bLen(rj.l_knee, rj.l_ankle), r_shin: bLen(rj.r_knee, rj.r_ankle),
          l_foot: bLen(rj.l_ankle, rj.l_toe), r_foot: bLen(rj.r_ankle, rj.r_toe),
          l_uarm: bLen(rj.l_shoulder, rj.l_elbow), r_uarm: bLen(rj.r_shoulder, rj.r_elbow),
          l_farm: bLen(rj.l_elbow, rj.l_wrist), r_farm: bLen(rj.r_elbow, rj.r_wrist),
          spine: bLen(rj.pelvis, rj.spine_joint),
          spine1: bLen(rj.spine_joint, rj.spine1_joint),
          spine2: bLen(rj.spine1_joint, rj.spine2_joint),
          chest: bLen(rj.spine2_joint, rj.neck),
          l_collar: bLen(rj.spine2_joint, rj.l_shoulder),
          r_collar: bLen(rj.spine2_joint, rj.r_shoulder),
        };
        fbxFrameBoneLens = {
          l_thigh: bLen(jp.l_hip, jp.l_knee), r_thigh: bLen(jp.r_hip, jp.r_knee),
          l_shin: bLen(jp.l_knee, jp.l_ankle), r_shin: bLen(jp.r_knee, jp.r_ankle),
          l_foot: bLen(jp.l_ankle, jp.l_toe), r_foot: bLen(jp.r_ankle, jp.r_toe),
          l_uarm: bLen(jp.l_shoulder, jp.l_elbow), r_uarm: bLen(jp.r_shoulder, jp.r_elbow),
          l_farm: bLen(jp.l_elbow, jp.l_wrist), r_farm: bLen(jp.r_elbow, jp.r_wrist),
          spine: bLen(jp.pelvis, jp.spine_joint),
          spine1: bLen(jp.spine_joint, jp.spine1_joint),
          spine2: bLen(jp.spine1_joint, jp.spine2_joint),
          chest: bLen(jp.spine2_joint, jp.neck),
          l_collar: bLen(jp.spine2_joint, jp.l_shoulder),
          r_collar: bLen(jp.spine2_joint, jp.r_shoulder),
        };

        // ─── RELATIVE POSITIONS (pelvis-relative, height-normalized — coordinate-agnostic) ───
        const bodyH = rj.head && rj.pelvis ? Math.max(0.01, vlen(vsub(rj.head, rj.pelvis))) : 1;
        const fbxBodyH = jp.head && jp.pelvis ? Math.max(0.01, vlen(vsub(jp.head, jp.pelvis))) : 1;
        const rPos = (joint, root, h) => {
          if (!joint || !root) return null;
          const d = vsub(joint, root);
          return [d[0]/h, d[1]/h, d[2]/h];
        };
        retargetedRelPos = {}; fbxFrameRelPos = {}; fbxFrameRelPosMM = {};
        const relJoints = ['l_knee','r_knee','l_ankle','r_ankle','l_toe','r_toe',
                           'l_elbow','r_elbow','l_wrist','r_wrist','l_shoulder','r_shoulder','head',
                           'spine_joint','spine1_joint','spine2_joint','l_collar','r_collar','neck'];
        for (const jn of relJoints) {
          retargetedRelPos[jn] = rPos(rj[jn], rj.pelvis, bodyH);
          fbxFrameRelPos[jn] = rPos(jp[jn], jp.pelvis, fbxBodyH);
          // Raw FBX pelvis-relative positions in mm (for frame-by-frame comparison)
          if (jp[jn] && jp.pelvis) {
            const d = vsub(jp[jn], jp.pelvis);
            fbxFrameRelPosMM[jn] = [d[0] * 1000, d[1] * 1000, d[2] * 1000];
          }
        }
        // Save FBX joint positions for shape fidelity diagnostic
        fbxFrameJointPos = {};
        for (const jn of Object.keys(jp)) {
          if (jp[jn]) fbxFrameJointPos[jn] = jp[jn].slice ? jp[jn].slice() : jp[jn];
        }
      }

    } else {
      // No joint position data — skip animation (position-based driving requires FBX)
      return;
    }

    // ── ARM-TORSO COLLISION AVOIDANCE ──
    // Approximate the torso as a capsule (spine axis + radius) computed from
    // the deformed torso cage verts. Push arm cage verts outward if they penetrate.
    if ((allLeftArmCage.length || allRightArmCage.length) && R.torso && R.torso.length > 10) {
      const neckRY = P.neck ? P.neck[1] : P.pelvis[1] + 0.2;
      const midRY = (P.pelvis[1] + neckRY) / 2;
      // Compute deformed spine axis from torso cage vert centroids
      let bx = 0, by = 0, bz = 0, bn = 0; // lower torso centroid
      let ux = 0, uy = 0, uz = 0, un = 0; // upper torso centroid
      for (const ci of R.torso) {
        const ry = cageRest[ci][1];
        const px = cagePos[ci*3], py = cagePos[ci*3+1], pz = cagePos[ci*3+2];
        if (ry < midRY) { bx += px; by += py; bz += pz; bn++; }
        else { ux += px; uy += py; uz += pz; un++; }
      }
      if (bn > 0 && un > 0) {
        bx /= bn; by /= bn; bz /= bn;
        ux /= un; uy /= un; uz /= un;
        const capAx = ux - bx, capAy = uy - by, capAz = uz - bz;
        const capLen = Math.sqrt(capAx*capAx + capAy*capAy + capAz*capAz);
        if (capLen > 0.005) {
          const nax = capAx/capLen, nay = capAy/capLen, naz = capAz/capLen;
          // Capsule radius: compute from average torso vert distance to spine axis
          let radSum = 0, radN = 0;
          for (const ci of R.torso) {
            const px = cagePos[ci*3]-bx, py = cagePos[ci*3+1]-by, pz = cagePos[ci*3+2]-bz;
            const proj = px*nax + py*nay + pz*naz;
            const perpX = px - proj*nax, perpY = py - proj*nay, perpZ = pz - proj*naz;
            const perpD = Math.sqrt(perpX*perpX + perpY*perpY + perpZ*perpZ);
            radSum += perpD; radN++;
          }
          // Use 85% of average distance as collision radius + 5% margin beyond surface.
          // 60% was too conservative — arms passing through outer torso weren't detected.
          const avgR = (radN > 0) ? (radSum / radN) : 0.05;
          const capR = avgR * 0.85;
          const capRMargin = avgR * 0.90; // push verts slightly beyond surface to avoid clipping
          const capR2 = capRMargin * capRMargin;

          // Push arm verts outward if inside capsule
          const armIdx = [].concat(allLeftArmCage, allRightArmCage,
            R.l_hand || [], R.r_hand || []);
          let pushCount = 0;
          for (const ci of armIdx) {
            const px = cagePos[ci*3], py = cagePos[ci*3+1], pz = cagePos[ci*3+2];
            const dx = px - bx, dy = py - by, dz = pz - bz;
            let proj = dx*nax + dy*nay + dz*naz;
            proj = Math.max(0, Math.min(capLen, proj));
            const closestX = bx + nax*proj, closestY = by + nay*proj, closestZ = bz + naz*proj;
            const rx = px - closestX, ry2 = py - closestY, rz = pz - closestZ;
            const d2 = rx*rx + ry2*ry2 + rz*rz;
            if (d2 < capR2 && d2 > 1e-10) {
              const d = Math.sqrt(d2);
              const push = capRMargin - d;
              const invD = 1 / d;
              cagePos[ci*3]   += rx * invD * push;
              cagePos[ci*3+1] += ry2 * invD * push;
              cagePos[ci*3+2] += rz * invD * push;
              pushCount++;
            }
          }
          // One-shot diagnostic (resets when FBX loaded via window._armCollisionDiag = false)
          if (!window._armCollisionDiag) {
            window._armCollisionDiag = true;
            console.log('=== ARM-TORSO COLLISION ===');
            console.log('  capsule: bottom=['+[bx,by,bz].map(v=>v.toFixed(4))+'] top=['+[ux,uy,uz].map(v=>v.toFixed(4))+'] len='+capLen.toFixed(4));
            console.log('  avg torso radius: '+avgR.toFixed(4)+' collision radius: '+capR.toFixed(4)+' margin: '+capRMargin.toFixed(4));
            console.log('  arm verts checked: '+armIdx.length+' pushed: '+pushCount);
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // ----- HEAD/NECK motion -----
    // Position mode: handled analytically via headState (computed above).
    // No vert-level rotation needed — avoids pivot-mismatch at P.neck after body rotation.
    // Euler fallback: vert-level rotation from spine_keyframes.json.
    if (!jointPosData && spineMotionData && P.neck && P.head && R.head && R.head.length) {
      // Euler fallback: head from spine_keyframes.json
      const neckX = getSpineDataAtTime('neck', t, 'x_rot') * Math.PI/180;
      const neckY = getSpineDataAtTime('neck', t, 'y_rot') * Math.PI/180;
      const neckZ = getSpineDataAtTime('neck', t, 'z_rot') * Math.PI/180;

      applyRotX(R.head, P.neck, neckX);
      applyRotY(R.head, P.neck, neckY);
      applyRotZ(R.head, P.neck, neckZ);

      let headPivot = rotateAroundAxis(
        P.head[0], P.head[1], P.head[2],
        P.neck[0], P.neck[1], P.neck[2], 1,0,0, neckX);
      headPivot = rotateAroundAxis(
        headPivot[0], headPivot[1], headPivot[2],
        P.neck[0], P.neck[1], P.neck[2], 0,1,0, neckY);
      headPivot = rotateAroundAxis(
        headPivot[0], headPivot[1], headPivot[2],
        P.neck[0], P.neck[1], P.neck[2], 0,0,1, neckZ);

      const headX = getSpineDataAtTime('head', t, 'x_rot') * Math.PI/180;
      const headY = getSpineDataAtTime('head', t, 'y_rot') * Math.PI/180;
      const headZ = getSpineDataAtTime('head', t, 'z_rot') * Math.PI/180;

      applyRotX(R.head, headPivot, headX);
      applyRotY(R.head, headPivot, headY);
      applyRotZ(R.head, headPivot, headZ);
    }

    // ----- Foot + hand plant correction (joint-space with hysteresis) -----
    footPlantJointSpace(t, allIdx);
    handPlantJointSpace(t, allIdx);

    // ----- Ground compensation (position mode) -----
    // Translate entire cage so the lowest foot vertex sits at rest-pose floor level.
    // Uses the lowest vertex across both feet — simple and reliable.
    if (jointPosData && R.l_foot && R.r_foot) {
      const footIdx = [].concat(R.l_foot, R.r_foot);
      let minFootY = Infinity;
      for (const ci of footIdx) { if (cagePos[ci*3+1] < minFootY) minFootY = cagePos[ci*3+1]; }
      let minFootRestY = Infinity;
      for (const ci of footIdx) { if (cageRest[ci][1] < minFootRestY) minFootRestY = cageRest[ci][1]; }
      const groundCorrection = minFootRestY - minFootY;
      if (Math.abs(groundCorrection) > 0.001) {
        translate(allIdx, 0, groundCorrection, 0);
        lastGroundCorrection = groundCorrection;
      } else {
        lastGroundCorrection = 0;
      }
    } else {
      lastGroundCorrection = 0;
    }

    // [REMOVED] Floor clamp, foot containment, and self-collision post-processing.
    // These per-vertex position pushes created frame discontinuities causing high jitter.
    // TODO: Reintroduce as soft constraints (spring/damper, joint-space corrections)
    // instead of per-vertex shoves.

    // One-shot: log ground comp + pivot alignment diagnostic
    if (!window._groundDiagDone && jointPosData) {
      window._groundDiagDone = true;
      console.log('=== GROUND COMP + PIVOT DIAG ===');
      console.log('  lastGroundCorrection=' + lastGroundCorrection.toFixed(4));
      if (R.l_foot && R.r_foot) {
        const footIdx = [].concat(R.l_foot, R.r_foot);
        let minY = Infinity, minRestY = Infinity;
        for (const ci of footIdx) {
          if (cagePos[ci*3+1] < minY) minY = cagePos[ci*3+1];
          if (cageRest[ci][1] < minRestY) minRestY = cageRest[ci][1];
        }
        console.log('  foot cage minY=' + minY.toFixed(4) + ' rest minY=' + minRestY.toFixed(4));
      }
      const _mhL = legState && legState.movedHipL;
      const _mhR = legState && legState.movedHipR;
      if (_mhL) console.log('  movedHipL=[' + _mhL.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)).join(',') + ']');
      if (_mhR) console.log('  movedHipR=[' + _mhR.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)).join(',') + ']');
      console.log('  P.l_hip=[' + P.l_hip.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)).join(',') + ']');
      console.log('  P.r_hip=[' + P.r_hip.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)).join(',') + ']');
    }

    applyCageSmoothing();
  }


function normalizeRegionId(regionIdInput, cageLen) {
  if (!Array.isArray(regionIdInput)) {
    console.warn("regions.regionId missing or invalid. Filling with zeros.");
    return new Array(cageLen).fill(0);
  }
  if (regionIdInput.length === cageLen) return regionIdInput.slice();
  if (regionIdInput.length > cageLen) {
    console.warn(`regions.regionId longer than cageRest (${regionIdInput.length} > ${cageLen}). Truncating.`);
    return regionIdInput.slice(0, cageLen);
  }
  console.warn(`regions.regionId shorter than cageRest (${regionIdInput.length} < ${cageLen}). Padding.`);
  const padded = regionIdInput.slice();
  const fillVal = regionIdInput.length ? regionIdInput[regionIdInput.length - 1] : 0;
  while (padded.length < cageLen) padded.push(fillVal);
  return padded;
}

// Auto-derived region adjacency map from REGION_BONE_MAP + skeleton joint hierarchy.
// Two regions are adjacent if any of their joints are connected in the skeleton tree
// (same joint, parent-child, or siblings sharing the same parent joint).
const REGION_ADJ = (function() {
  const JOINT_PARENT = {
    'spine_joint': 'pelvis', 'l_hip': 'pelvis', 'r_hip': 'pelvis',
    'spine1_joint': 'spine_joint', 'spine2_joint': 'spine1_joint',
    'neck': 'spine2_joint', 'l_shoulder': 'spine2_joint', 'r_shoulder': 'spine2_joint',
    'head': 'neck',
    'l_knee': 'l_hip', 'r_knee': 'r_hip',
    'l_ankle': 'l_knee', 'r_ankle': 'r_knee',
    'l_toe': 'l_ankle', 'r_toe': 'r_ankle',
    'l_elbow': 'l_shoulder', 'r_elbow': 'r_shoulder',
    'l_wrist': 'l_elbow', 'r_wrist': 'r_elbow',
    'l_mid_knuckle': 'l_wrist', 'r_mid_knuckle': 'r_wrist'
  };
  // Check if two joints are directly connected (same or parent-child).
  // Sibling check (shared parent) is excluded — it creates cross-body adjacencies
  // (e.g., l_shoulder↔r_shoulder both parent=spine2_joint would make arms adjacent).
  function jointsConnected(jA, jB) {
    if (jA === jB) return true;
    if (JOINT_PARENT[jA] === jB || JOINT_PARENT[jB] === jA) return true;
    return false;
  }
  const adj = {};
  const rids = Object.keys(REGION_BONE_MAP).map(Number);
  for (const rid of rids) {
    const set = new Set([rid]); // always include self
    const [jA0, jA1] = REGION_BONE_MAP[rid];
    for (const other of rids) {
      if (other === rid) continue;
      const [jB0, jB1] = REGION_BONE_MAP[other];
      if (jointsConnected(jA0, jB0) || jointsConnected(jA0, jB1) ||
          jointsConnected(jA1, jB0) || jointsConnected(jA1, jB1)) {
        set.add(other);
      }
    }
    adj[rid] = Array.from(set);
  }
  // Cross-thigh adjacency: l_thigh(1) and r_thigh(2) physically meet at the crotch.
  // Without this, cross-thigh crotch blending weights get stripped by the adjacency filter.
  if (adj[1] && !adj[1].includes(2)) adj[1].push(2);
  if (adj[2] && !adj[2].includes(1)) adj[2].push(1);
  return adj;
})();

function classifyMeshRegion(meta, x, y, z){
  // Always return null to use nearest-cage-vert classification.
  // The cage regions are properly classified using joint positions (regenerate_cage.py).
  // Assigning each mesh vert the region of its nearest cage vert guarantees consistency.
  return null;
}

function buildBindKNNRegion(meshRestPos, cageRest, regionIdInput, regionMeta, K = 8) {
  const nMesh = meshRestPos.length / 3;
  const nCage = cageRest.length;
  const regionIdLocal = normalizeRegionId(regionIdInput, nCage);

  const regionToIndices = new Map();
  for (let ci = 0; ci < nCage; ci++) {
    const r = regionIdLocal[ci];
    if (!regionToIndices.has(r)) regionToIndices.set(r, []);
    regionToIndices.get(r).push(ci);
  }

  const idx = new Int32Array(nMesh * K);
  const w   = new Float32Array(nMesh * K);
  let reclassCount = 0, clampCount = 0;
  let thighReclassCount = 0, thighClampCount = 0;
  let headReclassCount = 0;

  for (let vi = 0; vi < nMesh; vi++) {
    const x = meshRestPos[vi*3], y = meshRestPos[vi*3+1], z = meshRestPos[vi*3+2];

    let meshRegion = classifyMeshRegion(regionMeta, x, y, z);
    if (meshRegion === null) {
      let nearestRegion = 0;
      let nearestD2 = 1e30;
      for (let ci = 0; ci < nCage; ci++) {
        const cx = cageRest[ci][0], cy = cageRest[ci][1], cz = cageRest[ci][2];
        const dx = x - cx, dy = y - cy, dz = z - cz;
        const d2 = dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz;
        if (d2 < nearestD2) {
          nearestD2 = d2;
          nearestRegion = regionIdLocal[ci];
        }
      }
      meshRegion = nearestRegion;
    }

    let allowedRegions = REGION_ADJ[meshRegion] || [meshRegion];

    // Geometry-based arm detection + torso clamp (Fix A + Fix B).
    // Catches bicep verts misclassified as torso by nearest-cage-vert.
    // Uses point-to-arm-axis distance + projection range to detect arm verts,
    // then clamps torso influence past a shoulder band.
    if (cageShoulderL && cageShoulderR && cageArmDirL && cageArmDirR) {
      const uaLen = +$('upperArmLen').value;
      const faLen = +$('forearmLen').value;
      const totalArmLen = uaLen + faLen;
      const ARM_RADIUS = 0.06;              // 6cm from axis = "on the arm"
      const SHOULDER_BAND = 0.15 * uaLen;   // torso blending within wider band for smoother shoulder
      const ELBOW_BAND = 0.12 * uaLen;      // cross-arm blending zone around elbow (smoothstep-weighted)

      // Check left arm
      const lsh = cageShoulderL, ld = cageArmDirL;
      const ltProj = (x-lsh[0])*ld[0] + (y-lsh[1])*ld[1] + (z-lsh[2])*ld[2];
      if (ltProj > 0 && ltProj < totalArmLen) {
        const lpx = lsh[0]+ld[0]*ltProj, lpy = lsh[1]+ld[1]*ltProj, lpz = lsh[2]+ld[2]*ltProj;
        const lAxDist = Math.sqrt((x-lpx)*(x-lpx) + (y-lpy)*(y-lpy) + (z-lpz)*(z-lpz));
        if (lAxDist < ARM_RADIUS) {
          if (meshRegion === 0) {
            meshRegion = (ltProj <= uaLen) ? 7 : 9;
            allowedRegions = REGION_ADJ[meshRegion] || [meshRegion];
            reclassCount++;
          }
          // Shoulder clamp: no torso past shoulder band
          if (ltProj > SHOULDER_BAND) {
            allowedRegions = allowedRegions.filter(r => r !== 0);
            clampCount++;
          }
          // Elbow clamp: upper arm verts far from elbow -> no forearm
          if (meshRegion === 7 && ltProj < uaLen - ELBOW_BAND) {
            allowedRegions = allowedRegions.filter(r => r !== 9);
            clampCount++;
          }
          // Elbow clamp: forearm verts far from elbow -> no upper arm
          if (meshRegion === 9 && ltProj > uaLen + ELBOW_BAND) {
            allowedRegions = allowedRegions.filter(r => r !== 7);
            clampCount++;
          }
        }
      }

      // Check right arm
      const rsh = cageShoulderR, rd = cageArmDirR;
      const rtProj = (x-rsh[0])*rd[0] + (y-rsh[1])*rd[1] + (z-rsh[2])*rd[2];
      if (rtProj > 0 && rtProj < totalArmLen) {
        const rpx = rsh[0]+rd[0]*rtProj, rpy = rsh[1]+rd[1]*rtProj, rpz = rsh[2]+rd[2]*rtProj;
        const rAxDist = Math.sqrt((x-rpx)*(x-rpx) + (y-rpy)*(y-rpy) + (z-rpz)*(z-rpz));
        if (rAxDist < ARM_RADIUS) {
          if (meshRegion === 0) {
            meshRegion = (rtProj <= uaLen) ? 8 : 10;
            allowedRegions = REGION_ADJ[meshRegion] || [meshRegion];
            reclassCount++;
          }
          if (rtProj > SHOULDER_BAND) {
            allowedRegions = allowedRegions.filter(r => r !== 0);
            clampCount++;
          }
          // Elbow clamp: upper arm far from elbow -> no forearm
          if (meshRegion === 8 && rtProj < uaLen - ELBOW_BAND) {
            allowedRegions = allowedRegions.filter(r => r !== 10);
            clampCount++;
          }
          // Elbow clamp: forearm far from elbow -> no upper arm
          if (meshRegion === 10 && rtProj > uaLen + ELBOW_BAND) {
            allowedRegions = allowedRegions.filter(r => r !== 8);
            clampCount++;
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // Geometry-based thigh detection + torso clamp (same pattern as arms).
    // Catches groin/upper-thigh verts misclassified as torso by nearest-cage-vert.
    // Without this, the cage's near-vertical right thigh (3° outward) means r_thigh
    // cage verts are so close to torso that KNN assigns zero thigh weight to groin mesh verts.
    if (P.l_hip && P.l_knee && P.r_hip && P.r_knee) {
      // Left thigh
      const lh = P.l_hip, lk = P.l_knee;
      const lThLen = Math.sqrt((lk[0]-lh[0])**2 + (lk[1]-lh[1])**2 + (lk[2]-lh[2])**2);
      const lThD = [(lk[0]-lh[0])/lThLen, (lk[1]-lh[1])/lThLen, (lk[2]-lh[2])/lThLen];
      const THIGH_RADIUS = 0.30 * lThLen; // proportional to thigh length
      const THIGH_RADIUS_HIP_L = 0.60 * lThLen; // wider near hip for crotch/groin
      const lHipBand = 0.15 * lThLen;
      const HIP_EXTEND_L = 0.12 * lThLen; // detect slightly above hip for crotch
      const lThProj = (x-lh[0])*lThD[0] + (y-lh[1])*lThD[1] + (z-lh[2])*lThD[2];
      if (lThProj > -HIP_EXTEND_L && lThProj < lThLen) {
        const projCL = Math.max(0, lThProj);
        const lpx = lh[0]+lThD[0]*projCL, lpy = lh[1]+lThD[1]*projCL, lpz = lh[2]+lThD[2]*projCL;
        const lThAxDist = Math.sqrt((x-lpx)*(x-lpx) + (y-lpy)*(y-lpy) + (z-lpz)*(z-lpz));
        // Tapered radius: wide near hip (crotch), normal further down thigh
        const hipFracL = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, 1 - lThProj / (0.3 * lThLen)));
        const effectiveRadiusL = THIGH_RADIUS + (THIGH_RADIUS_HIP_L - THIGH_RADIUS) * hipFracL;
        if (lThAxDist < effectiveRadiusL) {
          if (meshRegion === 0) {
            meshRegion = 1;
            allowedRegions = REGION_ADJ[meshRegion] || [meshRegion];
            thighReclassCount++;
          }
          if (lThProj > lHipBand) {
            allowedRegions = allowedRegions.filter(r => r !== 0);
            thighClampCount++;
          }
        }
      }
      // Right thigh
      const rh = P.r_hip, rk = P.r_knee;
      const rThLen = Math.sqrt((rk[0]-rh[0])**2 + (rk[1]-rh[1])**2 + (rk[2]-rh[2])**2);
      const rThD = [(rk[0]-rh[0])/rThLen, (rk[1]-rh[1])/rThLen, (rk[2]-rh[2])/rThLen];
      const THIGH_RADIUS_R = 0.30 * rThLen; // proportional to thigh length
      const THIGH_RADIUS_HIP_R = 0.60 * rThLen; // wider near hip for crotch/groin
      const rHipBand = 0.15 * rThLen;
      const HIP_EXTEND_R = 0.12 * rThLen; // detect slightly above hip for crotch
      const rThProj = (x-rh[0])*rThD[0] + (y-rh[1])*rThD[1] + (z-rh[2])*rThD[2];
      if (rThProj > -HIP_EXTEND_R && rThProj < rThLen) {
        const projCR = Math.max(0, rThProj);
        const rpx = rh[0]+rThD[0]*projCR, rpy = rh[1]+rThD[1]*projCR, rpz = rh[2]+rThD[2]*projCR;
        const rThAxDist = Math.sqrt((x-rpx)*(x-rpx) + (y-rpy)*(y-rpy) + (z-rpz)*(z-rpz));
        // Tapered radius: wide near hip (crotch), normal further down thigh
        const hipFracR = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, 1 - rThProj / (0.3 * rThLen)));
        const effectiveRadiusR = THIGH_RADIUS_R + (THIGH_RADIUS_HIP_R - THIGH_RADIUS_R) * hipFracR;
        if (rThAxDist < effectiveRadiusR) {
          if (meshRegion === 0) {
            meshRegion = 2;
            allowedRegions = REGION_ADJ[meshRegion] || [meshRegion];
            thighReclassCount++;
          }
          if (rThProj > rHipBand) {
            allowedRegions = allowedRegions.filter(r => r !== 0);
            thighClampCount++;
          }
        }
      }
      // Midline catch: inner-thigh verts between the two hip joints that fall
      // outside both cylindrical radii. These verts are close to the body midline
      // and neither thigh cylinder is wide enough to catch them. Without this,
      // torso cage verts remain in the KNN candidate pool → ~70% torso weight.
      // Handles BOTH cases:
      //   meshRegion === 0 (torso): reclassify to nearer thigh + clamp torso
      //   meshRegion === 1/2 (thigh but outside cylinder): just clamp torso
      {
        const lhx = lh[0], rhx = rh[0];
        const hipY = Math.max(lh[1], rh[1]);
        // Between the two hips in X and at/below hip level
        if (x > lhx && x < rhx && y < hipY + 0.02) {
          const midX = (lhx + rhx) / 2;
          const isL = x <= midX;
          const hip = isL ? lh : rh;
          const tLen = isL ? lThLen : rThLen;
          const td = isL ? lThD : rThD;
          const proj = ((x-hip[0])*td[0] + (y-hip[1])*td[1] + (z-hip[2])*td[2]);
          // Reclassify all midline verts from hip down to knee as thigh.
          // Previously excluded top 15% (hipBand) which left crotch verts as torso.
          if (proj > -0.02 * tLen && proj < tLen) {
            if (meshRegion === 0) {
              meshRegion = isL ? 1 : 2;
              allowedRegions = REGION_ADJ[meshRegion] || [meshRegion];
              thighReclassCount++;
            }
            // Clamp torso from allowed regions (catches both reclassified and
            // thigh-classified verts that the cylinder check missed)
            if (allowedRegions.includes(0)) {
              allowedRegions = allowedRegions.filter(r => r !== 0);
              thighClampCount++;
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // Bone-distance-based reclassification: for any torso vert, compute distance
    // to the nearest point on each bone (thigh/shin segments). If the vert is
    // closer to a limb bone than to the spine, reclassify it.
    // This is the fundamental fix: the KNN cage classification assigns verts based
    // on nearest cage vertex, but the cage's torso region is huge and grabs verts
    // that anatomically belong to thighs. Bone-distance classification matches
    // what FBX skinning does natively.
    if (meshRegion === 0 && P.l_hip && P.r_hip && P.pelvis) {
      // Distance to nearest point on a line segment
      function distToSeg(px,py,pz, ax,ay,az, bx,by,bz) {
        const dx=bx-ax, dy=by-ay, dz=bz-az;
        const len2 = dx*dx+dy*dy+dz*dz;
        if (len2 < 1e-10) return Math.sqrt((px-ax)**2+(py-ay)**2+(pz-az)**2);
        let t = ((px-ax)*dx+(py-ay)*dy+(pz-az)*dz) / len2;
        t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, t));
        const cx=ax+t*dx, cy=ay+t*dy, cz=az+t*dz;
        return Math.sqrt((px-cx)**2+(py-cy)**2+(pz-cz)**2);
      }
      // Distance to spine (pelvis → head roughly, use pelvis→spine1 if available)
      const spineTop = P.spine1 || P.neck || [P.pelvis[0], P.pelvis[1]+0.2, P.pelvis[2]];
      const dSpine = distToSeg(x,y,z, P.pelvis[0],P.pelvis[1],P.pelvis[2], spineTop[0],spineTop[1],spineTop[2]);
      // Distance to left thigh bone
      let dLThigh = Infinity;
      if (P.l_hip && P.l_knee) dLThigh = distToSeg(x,y,z, P.l_hip[0],P.l_hip[1],P.l_hip[2], P.l_knee[0],P.l_knee[1],P.l_knee[2]);
      // Distance to right thigh bone
      let dRThigh = Infinity;
      if (P.r_hip && P.r_knee) dRThigh = distToSeg(x,y,z, P.r_hip[0],P.r_hip[1],P.r_hip[2], P.r_knee[0],P.r_knee[1],P.r_knee[2]);
      const dNearThigh = Math.min(dLThigh, dRThigh);
      // If closer to thigh than spine (with small margin to avoid boundary noise)
      if (dNearThigh < dSpine * 0.95) {
        meshRegion = dLThigh < dRThigh ? 1 : 2;
        allowedRegions = REGION_ADJ[meshRegion] || [meshRegion];
        thighReclassCount++;
      }
    }

    // Geometry-based head detection (same pattern as arms/thighs).
    // Catches face/head verts misclassified as torso by nearest-cage-vert.
    // Without this, face mesh verts get 100% torso transform and deform
    // with the body instead of rotating rigidly with the head.
    if (P.neck && P.head && meshRegion === 0) {
      const neckToHead = [P.head[0]-P.neck[0], P.head[1]-P.neck[1], P.head[2]-P.neck[2]];
      const nthLen = Math.sqrt(neckToHead[0]**2 + neckToHead[1]**2 + neckToHead[2]**2);
      if (nthLen > 0.001) {
        const hd = [neckToHead[0]/nthLen, neckToHead[1]/nthLen, neckToHead[2]/nthLen];
        const dx = x - P.neck[0], dy = y - P.neck[1], dz = z - P.neck[2];
        const proj = dx*hd[0] + dy*hd[1] + dz*hd[2];
        const HEAD_RADIUS = 0.45 * nthLen;  // tighter: only actual head/face, not upper chest
        const NECK_BAND = 0.4 * nthLen;     // wide blend zone for smooth torso→head transition

        if (proj > 0.02) { // above neck (2cm margin above to avoid chest verts)
          const px = P.neck[0]+hd[0]*proj, py = P.neck[1]+hd[1]*proj, pz = P.neck[2]+hd[2]*proj;
          const axDist = Math.sqrt((x-px)*(x-px) + (y-py)*(y-py) + (z-pz)*(z-pz));

          if (axDist < HEAD_RADIUS) {
            meshRegion = 11;
            headReclassCount++;
            if (proj < NECK_BAND) {
              // Neck band: allow both head and torso cage verts for smooth blend
              allowedRegions = [11, 0];
            } else {
              // Above neck band: pure head, no torso
              allowedRegions = [11];
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }

    const candidateIndices = [];
    for (const r of allowedRegions) {
      const list = regionToIndices.get(r);
      if (list) candidateIndices.push(...list);
    }

    if (!candidateIndices.length) {
      for (let ci = 0; ci < nCage; ci++) candidateIndices.push(ci);
    }

    const bestD2 = new Float32Array(K);
    const bestI  = new Int32Array(K);
    for (let k = 0; k < K; k++) { bestD2[k] = 1e30; bestI[k] = candidateIndices[0] ?? 0; }

    for (const ci of candidateIndices) {
      const cx = cageRest[ci][0], cy = cageRest[ci][1], cz = cageRest[ci][2];
      const dx = x - cx, dy = y - cy, dz = z - cz;
      const d2 = dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz;

      let worst = 0;
      for (let k = 1; k < K; k++) if (bestD2[k] > bestD2[worst]) worst = k;

      if (d2 < bestD2[worst]) {
        bestD2[worst] = d2;
        bestI[worst]  = ci;
      }
    }

    let sum = 0;
    for (let k = 0; k < K; k++) {
      const d = Math.sqrt(bestD2[k]) + 1e-6;
      const ww = 1.0 / d;
      w[vi*K + k] = ww;
      idx[vi*K + k] = bestI[k];
      sum += ww;
    }
    for (let k = 0; k < K; k++) w[vi*K + k] /= sum;
  }

  if (reclassCount > 0 || clampCount > 0) {
    console.log('Arm geometry clamp: reclassified', reclassCount, 'torso→arm verts, clamped torso on', clampCount, 'arm verts');
  }
  if (thighReclassCount > 0 || thighClampCount > 0) {
    console.log('Thigh geometry clamp: reclassified', thighReclassCount, 'torso→thigh verts, clamped torso on', thighClampCount, 'thigh verts');
  }
  if (headReclassCount > 0) {
    console.log('Head geometry clamp: reclassified', headReclassCount, 'torso→head verts');
  }
  return { K, idx, w, regionId: regionIdLocal };
}

  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // JOINT PLACEMENT SYSTEM — Mixamo-style drag from panel onto mesh
  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  let placementMode = false;
  const placedMarkers = {};   // key → { el: HTMLElement, worldPos: [x,y,z], color: string }
  let draggedJoint = null;
  let dragSource = null;      // 'panel' or 'marker'
  let ghostEl = null;
  const jRaycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();
  const jMouse = new THREE.Vector2();

  const JOINT_DEFS = {
    chin:       { mirror: null,         label: 'CHIN' },
    l_wrist:    { mirror: 'r_wrist',    label: 'WRISTS' },
    l_elbow:    { mirror: 'r_elbow',    label: 'ELBOWS' },
    l_shoulder: { mirror: 'r_shoulder', label: 'SHOULDERS' },
    l_knee:     { mirror: 'r_knee',     label: 'KNEES' },
    groin:      { mirror: null,         label: 'GROIN' },
    l_ankle:    { mirror: 'r_ankle',    label: 'ANKLES' }
  };

  function mirrorX(pos) { return [-pos[0], pos[1], pos[2]]; }

  // Interior projection: find bone center from surface click.
  // Uses iterative local clustering: start tight around click, expand only if needed.
  // This prevents the centroid from collapsing toward the body center at joints
  // where the cross-section includes the torso (shoulders, hips).
  function projectToInterior(hitPos, hitNormal) {
    if (!meshRestPos) return [hitPos.x, hitPos.y, hitPos.z];
    const nMesh = meshRestPos.length / 3;
    const hp = [hitPos.x, hitPos.y, hitPos.z];

    // Collect vertices in a horizontal band at hit Y
    const sliceVal = hitPos.y;
    let bandVerts = [];
    for (const bw of [0.005, 0.010, 0.020]) {
      bandVerts = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
        if (Math.abs(meshRestPos[i*3+1] - sliceVal) <= bw)
          bandVerts.push([meshRestPos[i*3], meshRestPos[i*3+1], meshRestPos[i*3+2]]);
      }
      if (bandVerts.length >= 10) break;
    }
    if (bandVerts.length < 3) {
      // Fallback: push inward along surface normal
      return [hp[0] + hitNormal.x * -0.02, hp[1] + hitNormal.y * -0.02, hp[2] + hitNormal.z * -0.02];
    }

    // Sort band verts by distance to click point
    const dists = bandVerts.map(v =>
      Math.sqrt((v[0]-hp[0])**2 + (v[1]-hp[1])**2 + (v[2]-hp[2])**2));

    // Find a natural break in distances (gap detection) to isolate the local limb
    // Sort indices by distance
    const sortedIdx = dists.map((d,i) => i).sort((a,b) => dists[a] - dists[b]);

    // Start with the closest 20% of verts (or minimum 10), then look for a gap
    const minCount = Math.max(10, Math.floor(bandVerts.length * 0.05));
    let cutoff = minCount;

    // Look for the first large gap in sorted distances (>2x the median spacing)
    for (let i = 1; i < Math.min(sortedIdx.length - 1, bandVerts.length * 0.5); i++) {
      const d1 = dists[sortedIdx[i]];
      const d0 = dists[sortedIdx[i-1]];
      const gap = d1 - d0;
      // If we have enough verts and hit a big gap, stop here
      if (i >= minCount && gap > d1 * 0.3 && gap > 0.01) {
        cutoff = i;
        break;
      }
      cutoff = i + 1;
    }

    // Also cap at a maximum radius from click point (limb diameter estimate)
    // Use the mesh height to estimate: typical limb is ~8-12% of mesh height in diameter
    const meshH = bounds ? bounds.height : 1.0;
    const maxLimbRadius = meshH * 0.12; // generous: covers thick thighs
    let localVerts = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < cutoff && i < sortedIdx.length; i++) {
      if (dists[sortedIdx[i]] <= maxLimbRadius) {
        localVerts.push(bandVerts[sortedIdx[i]]);
      }
    }

    // If gap detection gave too few, use radius-based fallback
    if (localVerts.length < 5) {
      localVerts = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < bandVerts.length; i++) {
        if (dists[i] <= maxLimbRadius) localVerts.push(bandVerts[i]);
      }
    }

    // Final fallback: just use the closest verts
    if (localVerts.length < 3) {
      localVerts = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(20, sortedIdx.length); i++) {
        localVerts.push(bandVerts[sortedIdx[i]]);
      }
    }

    // Compute centroid of local cluster
    let fx = 0, fy = 0, fz = 0;
    for (const v of localVerts) { fx += v[0]; fy += v[1]; fz += v[2]; }
    const result = [fx / localVerts.length, fy / localVerts.length, fz / localVerts.length];

    console.log('[INTERIOR] ' + localVerts.length + '/' + bandVerts.length + ' verts, ' +
      'click=[' + hp.map(v=>v.toFixed(3)).join(',') + '] → interior=[' + result.map(v=>v.toFixed(3)).join(',') + ']');
    return result;
  }

  // Project interior pos outward to mesh surface
  function projectToSurface(interiorPos) {
    if (!meshObj) return interiorPos;
    const origin = new THREE.Vector3(interiorPos[0], interiorPos[1], interiorPos[2]);
    const dirs = [[0,0,1],[0,0,-1],[1,0,0],[-1,0,0],[0,1,0],[0,-1,0],
      [.707,0,.707],[-.707,0,.707],[.707,0,-.707],[-.707,0,-.707],[0,.5,.866],[0,-.5,.866]];
    const rc = new THREE.Raycaster();
    let bestDist = Infinity, bestPt = null;
    for (const d of dirs) {
      rc.set(origin, new THREE.Vector3(d[0],d[1],d[2]));
      const hits = rc.intersectObject(meshObj);
      if (hits.length > 0 && hits[0].distance < bestDist) { bestDist = hits[0].distance; bestPt = hits[0].point; }
    }
    return bestPt ? [bestPt.x, bestPt.y, bestPt.z] : [interiorPos[0], interiorPos[1], interiorPos[2] + 0.02];
  }

  // --- HTML overlay markers (always visible on top of mesh) ---
  function createPlacedMarkerEl(key, color) {
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = 'jp-placed';
    el.style.borderColor = color;
    el.style.background = color.replace(')', ',0.35)').replace('rgb', 'rgba');
    el.dataset.jointKey = key;
    const dot = document.createElement('div');
    dot.className = 'jp-dot';
    dot.style.background = color;
    el.appendChild(dot);
    document.body.appendChild(el);
    // Allow re-dragging placed markers
    el.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e) {
      if (!placementMode || !meshObj) return;
      draggedJoint = key;
      dragSource = 'marker'; // distinguish from panel drag
      ghostEl = null;        // no ghost needed, we move the marker directly
      controls.enabled = false;
      el.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();
    });
    return el;
  }

  function placeMarkerHTML(key, worldPos, color) {
    if (placedMarkers[key]) {
      placedMarkers[key].worldPos = worldPos;
      updateMarkerScreenPos(key);
      return;
    }
    const el = createPlacedMarkerEl(key, color);
    placedMarkers[key] = { el, worldPos, color };
    updateMarkerScreenPos(key);
  }

  function updateMarkerScreenPos(key) {
    const m = placedMarkers[key];
    if (!m) return;
    const v = new THREE.Vector3(m.worldPos[0], m.worldPos[1], m.worldPos[2]);
    v.project(camera);
    const rect = renderer.domElement.getBoundingClientRect();
    const x = (v.x * 0.5 + 0.5) * rect.width + rect.left;
    const y = (-v.y * 0.5 + 0.5) * rect.height + rect.top;
    m.el.style.left = x + 'px';
    m.el.style.top = y + 'px';
    m.el.style.display = (v.z > 1) ? 'none' : 'flex';  // hide if behind camera
  }

  function updateAllMarkerScreenPositions() {
    for (const key of Object.keys(placedMarkers)) updateMarkerScreenPos(key);
  }

  function removeMarkerHTML(key) {
    if (placedMarkers[key]) {
      if (placedMarkers[key].el.parentNode) placedMarkers[key].el.parentNode.removeChild(placedMarkers[key].el);
      delete placedMarkers[key];
    }
  }

  function clearAllMarkers() {
    for (const key of Object.keys(placedMarkers)) removeMarkerHTML(key);
  }

  function getRowColor(row) {
    const c = row.querySelector('.jp-circle');
    return c ? c.style.borderColor : '#fff';
  }

  // Apply joint placement to P
  function applyJointToP(key, interiorPos) {
    const useSymmetry = $('symmetryToggle').checked;
    const def = JOINT_DEFS[key];
    if (key === 'chin') {
      P.neck = [interiorPos[0], interiorPos[1] - 0.01, interiorPos[2]];
    } else if (key === 'groin') {
      P.pelvis = interiorPos.slice();
    } else {
      P[key] = interiorPos.slice();
      if (def && def.mirror && useSymmetry) P[def.mirror] = mirrorX(interiorPos);
    }
  }

  // Build reverse lookup: mirror key → primary key (e.g., 'r_wrist' → 'l_wrist')
  const MIRROR_REVERSE = {};
  for (const k of Object.keys(JOINT_DEFS)) {
    if (JOINT_DEFS[k].mirror) MIRROR_REVERSE[JOINT_DEFS[k].mirror] = k;
  }

  // Find the actual joint key when dragging a circle
  // Primary circles → left/center key; mirror circles → right key
  function getDragKey(row, circle) {
    const leftKey = row.dataset.joint;
    if (!circle.classList.contains('jp-mirror')) return leftKey;
    // Mirror circle → get the right-side key
    const def = JOINT_DEFS[leftKey];
    return def && def.mirror ? def.mirror : leftKey;
  }

  // Get the row for any joint key (left or right)
  function getRowForKey(key) {
    // Direct match
    let row = document.querySelector('.jp-row[data-joint="'+key+'"]');
    if (row) return row;
    // Reverse: key is a mirror target (e.g., 'r_wrist'), find the row for 'l_wrist'
    const primary = MIRROR_REVERSE[key];
    if (primary) return document.querySelector('.jp-row[data-joint="'+primary+'"]');
    return null;
  }

  // --- Drag from panel onto mesh ---
  // Get the mirror key for any joint key (works both directions)
  function getMirrorKey(key) {
    const def = JOINT_DEFS[key];
    if (def && def.mirror) return def.mirror;       // left → right
    if (MIRROR_REVERSE[key]) return MIRROR_REVERSE[key]; // right → left
    return null;
  }

  // Place a joint on the mesh: set surface marker + update P + handle mirror
  function placeJointOnMesh(key, surfPt, hitNormal, color) {
    const interior = projectToInterior(
      new THREE.Vector3(surfPt[0], surfPt[1], surfPt[2]), hitNormal);

    // Place primary marker on surface
    placeMarkerHTML(key, surfPt, color);

    // Update P
    if (key === 'chin') {
      P.neck = [interior[0], interior[1] - 0.01, interior[2]];
    } else if (key === 'groin') {
      P.pelvis = interior.slice();
    } else {
      P[key] = interior.slice();
    }

    // Auto-mirror if symmetry is on
    if ($('symmetryToggle').checked) {
      const mirrorKey = getMirrorKey(key);
      if (mirrorKey) {
        // Simple mirror: negate X of both surface point and interior
        const mirrorSurf = [-surfPt[0], surfPt[1], surfPt[2]];
        const mirrorInt = mirrorX(interior);
        placeMarkerHTML(mirrorKey, mirrorSurf, color);
        if (mirrorKey === 'chin' || mirrorKey === 'groin') {
          // these don't have a P key matching the mirror key
        } else {
          P[mirrorKey] = mirrorInt.slice();
        }
        // Mark mirror panel circle
        const row = getRowForKey(mirrorKey);
        if (row) {
          const mc = row.querySelector('.jp-mirror');
          const pc = row.querySelector('.jp-circle:not(.jp-mirror)');
          if (mc) mc.classList.add('placed');
          if (pc) pc.classList.add('placed');
        }
      }
    }

    // Mark panel circle as placed
    const row = getRowForKey(key);
    if (row) {
      const def = JOINT_DEFS[key];
      if (def) {
        row.querySelector('.jp-circle:not(.jp-mirror)').classList.add('placed');
      } else if (MIRROR_REVERSE[key]) {
        const mc = row.querySelector('.jp-mirror');
        if (mc) mc.classList.add('placed');
      }
    }
  }

  // --- Drag from panel ---
  function onJointPointerDown(event) {
    if (!placementMode || !meshObj) return;
    const row = event.target.closest('.jp-row');
    if (!row) return;
    const circle = event.target.closest('.jp-circle');
    if (!circle) return;
    const key = getDragKey(row, circle);
    if (!key) return;

    draggedJoint = key;
    dragSource = 'panel';
    circle.classList.add('dragging');

    // Create ghost circle following cursor
    ghostEl = document.createElement('div');
    ghostEl.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;width:32px;height:32px;border-radius:50%;border:3px solid ' +
      circle.style.borderColor + ';background:' + circle.style.background +
      ';pointer-events:none;z-index:1000;transform:translate(-50%,-50%);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center';
    const dot = document.createElement('div');
    dot.style.cssText = 'width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;background:' + circle.style.borderColor;
    ghostEl.appendChild(dot);
    document.body.appendChild(ghostEl);
    ghostEl.style.left = event.clientX + 'px';
    ghostEl.style.top = event.clientY + 'px';

    controls.enabled = false;
    event.preventDefault();
  }

  function onDocPointerMove(event) {
    if (!draggedJoint) return;

    // Move ghost if panel drag
    if (ghostEl) {
      ghostEl.style.left = event.clientX + 'px';
      ghostEl.style.top = event.clientY + 'px';
    }

    // Raycast to mesh surface for preview
    const rect = renderer.domElement.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (event.clientX >= rect.left && event.clientX <= rect.right &&
        event.clientY >= rect.top && event.clientY <= rect.bottom) {
      jMouse.x =  ((event.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 2 - 1;
      jMouse.y = -((event.clientY - rect.top)  / rect.height) * 2 + 1;
      jRaycaster.setFromCamera(jMouse, camera);
      const hits = jRaycaster.intersectObject(meshObj);
      if (hits.length > 0) {
        const surfPt = [hits[0].point.x, hits[0].point.y, hits[0].point.z];
        const hitN = hits[0].face ? hits[0].face.normal.clone().transformDirection(meshObj.matrixWorld) : new THREE.Vector3(0,0,1);
        const row = getRowForKey(draggedJoint);
        const color = row ? getRowColor(row) : '#fff';

        // Preview: place marker at surface point
        placeMarkerHTML(draggedJoint, surfPt, color);

        // Preview mirror
        if ($('symmetryToggle').checked) {
          const mirrorKey = getMirrorKey(draggedJoint);
          if (mirrorKey) {
            const mirrorSurf = [-surfPt[0], surfPt[1], surfPt[2]];
            placeMarkerHTML(mirrorKey, mirrorSurf, color);
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  function onDocPointerUp(event) {
    if (!draggedJoint) return;
    const key = draggedJoint;

    // Remove ghost
    if (ghostEl && ghostEl.parentNode) ghostEl.parentNode.removeChild(ghostEl);
    ghostEl = null;

    // Un-dim panel circles
    const row = getRowForKey(key);
    if (row) row.querySelectorAll('.jp-circle').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('dragging'));

    // Check if dropped on mesh
    const rect = renderer.domElement.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (event.clientX >= rect.left && event.clientX <= rect.right &&
        event.clientY >= rect.top && event.clientY <= rect.bottom) {
      jMouse.x =  ((event.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 2 - 1;
      jMouse.y = -((event.clientY - rect.top)  / rect.height) * 2 + 1;
      jRaycaster.setFromCamera(jMouse, camera);
      const hits = jRaycaster.intersectObject(meshObj);
      if (hits.length > 0) {
        const surfPt = [hits[0].point.x, hits[0].point.y, hits[0].point.z];
        const hitN = hits[0].face ? hits[0].face.normal.clone().transformDirection(meshObj.matrixWorld) : new THREE.Vector3(0,0,1);
        const color = row ? getRowColor(row) : '#fff';
        placeJointOnMesh(key, surfPt, hitN, color);
      }
    } else if (dragSource === 'panel') {
      // Dropped outside canvas from panel — cancel
      removeMarkerHTML(key);
      const mirrorKey = getMirrorKey(key);
      if (mirrorKey) removeMarkerHTML(mirrorKey);
    }

    draggedJoint = null;
    dragSource = null;
    controls.enabled = true;
    // Reset cursor on placed markers
    document.querySelectorAll('.jp-placed').forEach(el => el.style.cursor = 'grab');
  }

  // Attach panel drag events
  document.getElementById('jointPanelLeft').addEventListener('pointerdown', onJointPointerDown);
  document.addEventListener('pointermove', onDocPointerMove);
  document.addEventListener('pointerup', onDocPointerUp);

  // Enter placement mode
  function startPlacement() {
    placementMode = true;
    draggedJoint = null;
    ghostEl = null;
    window._boneCompDiagDone = false;
    window._seamGapDiagDone = false;
    window._rShoulderDiag = false;
    $('play').checked = false;
    $('doneJoints').style.display = '';
    $('replaceJoints').style.display = 'none';
    $('jointPanel').style.display = '';
    document.querySelectorAll('.jp-circle').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('placed','dragging'));
    clearAllMarkers();
    $('jointStatus').textContent = 'Drag LEFT circles onto the LEFT side of the mesh. Right mirrors automatically.';
  }

  // Exit placement mode, derive secondary joints, rebuild
  function finishPlacement() {
    // Record which joints were directly placed before clearing markers
    const directlyPlaced = new Set(Object.keys(placedMarkers));

    placementMode = false;
    draggedJoint = null;
    $('doneJoints').style.display = 'none';
    $('replaceJoints').style.display = '';
    $('jointPanel').style.display = 'none';
    clearAllMarkers();

    // Derive shoulder from elbow + mesh geometry ONLY if not directly placed
    if (!directlyPlaced.has('l_shoulder') && P.l_elbow) {
      let shoulderY = P.l_elbow[1];
      if (meshRestPos) {
        const nMesh = meshRestPos.length / 3;
        let maxY = -Infinity;
        for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
          if (meshRestPos[i*3] < -0.02 && meshRestPos[i*3+1] > maxY) maxY = meshRestPos[i*3+1];
        }
        shoulderY = maxY - 0.03;
      }
      const sX = P.l_elbow[0] * 0.35;
      P.l_shoulder = [sX, shoulderY, P.l_elbow[2]];
      P.r_shoulder = mirrorX(P.l_shoulder);
    }

    // Derive hips from groin — anatomically, the hip joint is:
    //   - BELOW the groin surface (the ball joint is inside the pelvis)
    //   - WIDER than the groin centroid (the femoral heads are lateral)
    // Strategy: scan the mesh at groin height to find leg root width,
    // then push hips downward toward the knees.
    if (P.pelvis) {
      const meshH = bounds ? bounds.height : 1.0;
      let hipSpread = meshH * 0.08; // fallback: 8% of mesh height
      if (meshRestPos) {
        const nMesh = meshRestPos.length / 3;
        // Scan a wider band around groin Y to find the widest point of the hip area
        const bandH = meshH * 0.03; // 3% band
        let minX = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity;
        for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
          if (Math.abs(meshRestPos[i*3+1] - P.pelvis[1]) < bandH) {
            minX = Math.min(minX, meshRestPos[i*3]); maxX = Math.max(maxX, meshRestPos[i*3]);
          }
        }
        if (maxX > minX) {
          // Hip joint centers are roughly at 1/3 of the way from center to edge
          // (the femoral head is about 1/3 into the pelvis width from outside)
          hipSpread = (maxX - minX) / 3;
        }
      }
      // Center pelvis X between knees to avoid asymmetry from off-center groin click
      if (P.l_knee && P.r_knee) {
        P.pelvis[0] = (P.l_knee[0] + P.r_knee[0]) / 2;
      }
      // Hip Y = pelvis Y (groin click is already at hip joint level anatomically)
      P.l_hip = [P.pelvis[0] - hipSpread, P.pelvis[1], P.pelvis[2]];
      P.r_hip = [P.pelvis[0] + hipSpread, P.pelvis[1], P.pelvis[2]];
      console.log('[HIP DERIVE] spread=' + (hipSpread*1000).toFixed(1) + 'mm, pelvisX=' + P.pelvis[0].toFixed(4) +
        ' hipY=' + P.pelvis[1].toFixed(4));
    }

    // Derive chest, head, toes, knuckles
    if (P.pelvis && P.neck) {
      P.chest = [P.pelvis[0], (P.pelvis[1] + P.neck[1]) * 0.5 + 0.03, P.pelvis[2]];
    }
    if (P.neck) {
      let headTop = P.neck[1] + 0.12;
      if (meshRestPos) {
        const nMesh = meshRestPos.length / 3;
        let maxY = -Infinity;
        for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) { if (meshRestPos[i*3+1] > maxY) maxY = meshRestPos[i*3+1]; }
        headTop = maxY;
      }
      P.head = [P.neck[0], (P.neck[1] + headTop) * 0.5, P.neck[2]];
    }
    if (P.l_ankle) {
      const floorY = bounds ? bounds.min[1] : 0;
      P.l_toe = [P.l_ankle[0], floorY, P.l_ankle[2] + 0.04];
    }
    if (P.r_ankle) {
      const floorY = bounds ? bounds.min[1] : 0;
      P.r_toe = [P.r_ankle[0], floorY, P.r_ankle[2] + 0.04];
    }
    if (P.l_wrist && P.l_elbow) {
      const d = vnorm(vsub(P.l_wrist, P.l_elbow));
      P.l_mid_knuckle = vadd(P.l_wrist, vscale(d, 0.04));
    }
    if (P.r_wrist && P.r_elbow) {
      const d = vnorm(vsub(P.r_wrist, P.r_elbow));
      P.r_mid_knuckle = vadd(P.r_wrist, vscale(d, 0.04));
    }

    // Update sliders
    if (P.l_hip && P.l_knee) {
      const tLen = vlen(vsub(P.l_knee, P.l_hip));
      $('thighLen').value = tLen.toFixed(3); $('thighLenVal').textContent = tLen.toFixed(3);
    }
    if (P.l_knee && P.l_ankle) {
      const sLen = vlen(vsub(P.l_ankle, P.l_knee));
      $('shinLen').value = sLen.toFixed(3); $('shinLenVal').textContent = sLen.toFixed(3);
    }
    if (P.l_shoulder && P.l_elbow) {
      const uLen = vlen(vsub(P.l_elbow, P.l_shoulder));
      $('upperArmLen').value = uLen.toFixed(3); $('upperArmLenVal').textContent = uLen.toFixed(3);
    }
    if (P.l_elbow && P.l_wrist) {
      const fLen = vlen(vsub(P.l_wrist, P.l_elbow));
      $('forearmLen').value = fLen.toFixed(3); $('forearmLenVal').textContent = fLen.toFixed(3);
    }

    // Update ALL proportion ratios (leg, arm, shoulder spread, hip spread)
    // These are critical for retargeting — stale ratios cause terrible animation
    if (jointPosData) {
      const rp = jointPosData.rest_pose;

      // Leg proportion ratio
      if (P.l_hip && P.l_knee && P.l_ankle && rp.l_hip && rp.l_knee && rp.l_ankle) {
        const cageLeg = vlen(vsub(P.l_knee, P.l_hip)) + vlen(vsub(P.l_ankle, P.l_knee));
        const fbxLeg = vlen(vsub(rp.l_knee, rp.l_hip)) + vlen(vsub(rp.l_ankle, rp.l_knee));
        if (fbxLeg > 1e-6) fbxLegPropRatio = cageLeg / fbxLeg;
      }

      // Arm proportion ratio
      if (P.l_shoulder && P.l_elbow && P.l_wrist && rp.l_shoulder && rp.l_elbow && rp.l_wrist) {
        const cageArm = vlen(vsub(P.l_elbow, P.l_shoulder)) + vlen(vsub(P.l_wrist, P.l_elbow));
        const fbxArm = vlen(vsub(rp.l_elbow, rp.l_shoulder)) + vlen(vsub(rp.l_wrist, rp.l_elbow));
        if (fbxArm > 0.01 && cageArm > 0.01) fbxArmPropRatio = cageArm / fbxArm;
      }

      // Shoulder spread ratio (FBX / cage)
      if (P.l_shoulder && P.r_shoulder && rp.l_shoulder && rp.r_shoulder) {
        const fbxSh = vlen(vsub(rp.r_shoulder, rp.l_shoulder));
        const cageSh = vlen(vsub(P.r_shoulder, P.l_shoulder));
        if (fbxSh > 0.01 && cageSh > 0.01) fbxShoulderSpreadRatio = fbxSh / cageSh;
      }

      // Hip spread ratio (FBX / cage)
      if (P.l_hip && P.r_hip && rp.l_hip && rp.r_hip) {
        const fbxHip = vlen(vsub(rp.r_hip, rp.l_hip));
        const cageHip = vlen(vsub(P.r_hip, P.l_hip));
        if (fbxHip > 0.01 && cageHip > 0.01) fbxHipSpreadRatio = fbxHip / cageHip;
      }

      console.log('[PLACEMENT] Ratios updated: leg=' + fbxLegPropRatio.toFixed(3) +
        ' arm=' + fbxArmPropRatio.toFixed(3) +
        ' shoulder=' + fbxShoulderSpreadRatio.toFixed(3) +
        ' hip=' + fbxHipSpreadRatio.toFixed(3));
    }

    // Validate bone lengths before saving
    const meshH = bounds ? bounds.height : 1.0;
    const minBone = meshH * 0.07, maxBone = meshH * 0.50;
    const bonePairs = [
      ['l_hip','l_knee','l_thigh'], ['l_knee','l_ankle','l_shin'],
      ['l_shoulder','l_elbow','l_uarm'], ['l_elbow','l_wrist','l_farm']
    ];
    let badBones = [];
    for (const [a, b, name] of bonePairs) {
      if (P[a] && P[b]) {
        const len = vlen(vsub(P[b], P[a]));
        if (len < minBone || len > maxBone) badBones.push(name + '=' + len.toFixed(4));
      }
    }
    if (badBones.length > 0) {
      console.warn('WARNING: Bad bone lengths after placement: ' + badBones.join(', '));
      console.warn('Expected range: ' + minBone.toFixed(4) + ' - ' + maxBone.toFixed(4) + ' (meshH=' + meshH.toFixed(3) + ')');
      $('jointStatus').textContent = 'Warning: some bones look too short/long. Try re-placing.';
    }

    saveJointsToStorage();
    rebuildFromSkeleton();

    console.log('=== JOINT PLACEMENT COMPLETE ===');
    for (const k of Object.keys(P)) {
      if (P[k]) console.log('  P.' + k + ' = [' + P[k].map(v => v.toFixed(4)).join(', ') + ']');
    }
    const lens = bonePairs.map(([a,b,n]) => P[a]&&P[b] ? n+'='+vlen(vsub(P[b],P[a])).toFixed(4) : '').filter(Boolean);
    console.log('  Bone lengths: ' + lens.join(', '));
    if (!badBones.length) $('jointStatus').textContent = 'Joints placed. Rebuild complete.';
  }

  // Save joint data to localStorage keyed by mesh identity
  function saveJointsToStorage() {
    if (!meshRestPos) return;
    const nVerts = meshRestPos.length / 3;
    let bHash = 0;
    if (bounds) {
      bHash = Math.round((bounds.min[0]+bounds.min[1]+bounds.min[2]+bounds.max[0]+bounds.max[1]+bounds.max[2]) * 10000);
    }
    const meshKey = 'cageJoints_' + nVerts + '_' + bHash;
    const data = {};
    for (const k of Object.keys(P)) {
      if (P[k]) data[k] = P[k].slice();
    }
    localStorage.setItem(meshKey, JSON.stringify({ P: data }));
    console.log('Saved joints to localStorage key: ' + meshKey);
  }

  // Load joint data from localStorage with sanity validation
  function loadJointsFromStorage() {
    if (!meshRestPos) return false;
    const nVerts = meshRestPos.length / 3;
    let bHash = 0;
    if (bounds) {
      bHash = Math.round((bounds.min[0]+bounds.min[1]+bounds.min[2]+bounds.max[0]+bounds.max[1]+bounds.max[2]) * 10000);
    }
    const meshKey = 'cageJoints_' + nVerts + '_' + bHash;
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(meshKey);
    if (!saved) return false;
    try {
      const data = JSON.parse(saved);
      if (data.P) {
        // Sanity check: validate bone lengths relative to mesh height
        const meshH = bounds ? bounds.height : 1.0;
        const minBone = meshH * 0.07;  // bone must be at least 7% of mesh height
        const maxBone = meshH * 0.50;  // bone must be less than 50% of mesh height
        const sp = data.P;
        const checks = [];
        if (sp.l_hip && sp.l_knee) checks.push({ name: 'l_thigh', len: vlen(vsub(sp.l_knee, sp.l_hip)) });
        if (sp.l_knee && sp.l_ankle) checks.push({ name: 'l_shin', len: vlen(vsub(sp.l_ankle, sp.l_knee)) });
        if (sp.l_shoulder && sp.l_elbow) checks.push({ name: 'l_uarm', len: vlen(vsub(sp.l_elbow, sp.l_shoulder)) });
        if (sp.l_elbow && sp.l_wrist) checks.push({ name: 'l_farm', len: vlen(vsub(sp.l_wrist, sp.l_elbow)) });
        let failed = [];
        for (const c of checks) {
          if (c.len < minBone || c.len > maxBone) failed.push(c.name + '=' + c.len.toFixed(4) + ' (range: ' + minBone.toFixed(4) + '-' + maxBone.toFixed(4) + ')');
        }
        if (failed.length > 0) {
          console.warn('Saved joints FAILED sanity check — discarding. Bad bones: ' + failed.join(', '));
          console.warn('Removing bad localStorage key: ' + meshKey);
          localStorage.removeItem(meshKey);
          return false;
        }
        for (const k of Object.keys(data.P)) {
          P[k] = data.P[k];
        }
        console.log('Restored joints from localStorage key: ' + meshKey + ' (all bone lengths OK)');
        return true;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('Failed to parse saved joints:', e);
    }
    return false;
  }

  // Shrink cage foot/toe vertices toward the ankle to match the mesh foot extent.
  // The cage character has larger feet than the Mixamo mesh, creating visible
  // "flipper" shapes in the wireframe that extend far beyond the actual mesh feet.
  function shrinkCageFeetToMesh() {
    console.log('[FOOT SHRINK] called — cageRest=' + !!cageRest + ' meshRestPos=' + !!meshRestPos +
      ' ankle=' + !!P.l_ankle + ' foot=' + !!(R.l_foot && R.l_foot.length));
    if (!cageRest || !meshRestPos || !P.l_ankle || !R.l_foot) return;
    const nMesh = meshRestPos.length / 3;

    for (const [side, ankleKey, footReg, toeReg] of [
      ['L', 'l_ankle', 'l_foot', 'l_toe_reg'],
      ['R', 'r_ankle', 'r_foot', 'r_toe_reg']
    ]) {
      const ankle = P[ankleKey];
      if (!ankle) continue;
      const footIdx = [].concat(R[footReg] || [], R[toeReg] || []);
      if (footIdx.length === 0) continue;

      // Find max mesh vertex distance from ankle
      // Use all mesh verts within 300mm XZ radius, no Y restriction
      let maxMeshDist = 0, meshCount = 0;
      for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
        const mx = meshRestPos[i*3], my = meshRestPos[i*3+1], mz = meshRestPos[i*3+2];
        // Only verts below the knee level and within XZ radius of ankle
        if (my > ankle[1] + 0.15) continue;
        const dxz = Math.sqrt((mx - ankle[0])**2 + (mz - ankle[2])**2);
        if (dxz > 0.3) continue;
        const d = Math.sqrt((mx-ankle[0])**2 + (my-ankle[1])**2 + (mz-ankle[2])**2);
        if (d > maxMeshDist) maxMeshDist = d;
        meshCount++;
      }

      // Find max cage foot distance from ankle
      let maxCageDist = 0;
      for (const ci of footIdx) {
        const d = vlen(vsub(cageRest[ci], ankle));
        if (d > maxCageDist) maxCageDist = d;
      }

      console.log('[FOOT SHRINK] ' + side + ': ankle=[' + ankle.map(v=>v.toFixed(3)).join(',') +
        '] footVerts=' + footIdx.length + ' meshNear=' + meshCount +
        ' meshMax=' + (maxMeshDist*1000).toFixed(0) + 'mm cageMax=' + (maxCageDist*1000).toFixed(0) + 'mm');

      if (maxCageDist < 0.001 || maxMeshDist < 0.001) continue;

      // Scale cage foot toward ankle to match mesh foot extent (+ 20% margin)
      const targetMax = maxMeshDist * 1.2;
      const scale = Math.min(1.0, targetMax / maxCageDist);
      if (scale >= 0.95) continue;

      for (const ci of footIdx) {
        const offset = vsub(cageRest[ci], ankle);
        const newPos = vadd(ankle, vscale(offset, scale));
        cageRest[ci] = [newPos[0], newPos[1], newPos[2]];
      }

      // Update cagePos for these vertices
      for (const ci of footIdx) {
        cagePos[ci*3]   = cageRest[ci][0];
        cagePos[ci*3+1] = cageRest[ci][1];
        cagePos[ci*3+2] = cageRest[ci][2];
      }

      console.log('[FOOT SHRINK] ' + side + ': meshMax=' + (maxMeshDist*1000).toFixed(0) +
        'mm cageMax=' + (maxCageDist*1000).toFixed(0) + 'mm → scale=' + scale.toFixed(3) +
        ' (' + footIdx.length + ' verts)');
    }
  }

  // Rebuild all region splits, bindings, and weights from current P
  function rebuildFromSkeleton() {
    if (!cageRest || !regionId || !meshRestPos) return;

    // Reset spine/collar/toe/neck regions back to their parent regions
    for (let i = 0; i < regionId.length; i++) {
      const r = regionId[i];
      if (r === 14 || r === 15 || r === 16 || r === 17) regionId[i] = 0;  // spine sub-regions → torso
      else if (r === 18) regionId[i] = 11;  // neck → head
      else if (r === 19 || r === 20) regionId[i] = 17;  // collar → chest (will be re-split from torso)
      else if (r === 21) regionId[i] = 5;   // l_toe → l_foot
      else if (r === 22) regionId[i] = 6;   // r_toe → r_foot
    }
    // Now all spine sub-regions are back to 0, re-split
    if (P.spine_joint && P.spine1_joint && P.spine2_joint && P.neck) {
      const spineY  = (P.pelvis[1] + P.spine_joint[1]) / 2;
      const spine1Y = (P.spine_joint[1] + P.spine1_joint[1]) / 2;
      const spine2Y = (P.spine1_joint[1] + P.spine2_joint[1]) / 2;
      const chestY  = (P.spine2_joint[1] + P.neck[1]) / 2;
      for (let i = 0; i < regionId.length; i++) {
        if (regionId[i] !== 0) continue;
        const y = cageRest[i][1];
        if (y < spineY)        regionId[i] = 14;
        else if (y < spine1Y)  regionId[i] = 0;
        else if (y < spine2Y)  regionId[i] = 15;
        else if (y < chestY)   regionId[i] = 16;
        else                    regionId[i] = 17;
      }
    } else {
      const pelvisThreshY = P.pelvis[1] + 0.07;
      for (let i = 0; i < regionId.length; i++) {
        if (regionId[i] === 0 && cageRest[i][1] < pelvisThreshY) regionId[i] = 14;
      }
    }

    // Neck split
    if (P.neck && P.head) {
      const neckHeadMidY = (P.neck[1] + P.head[1]) / 2;
      for (let i = 0; i < regionId.length; i++) {
        if (regionId[i] === 11 && cageRest[i][1] < neckHeadMidY) regionId[i] = 18;
      }
    }
    // Collar split
    if (P.spine2_joint && P.l_shoulder && P.r_shoulder) {
      for (let i = 0; i < regionId.length; i++) {
        if (regionId[i] !== 17) continue;
        const pos = cageRest[i];
        if (pos[1] < P.spine2_joint[1]) continue;
        const isLeft = pos[0] < P.spine2_joint[0];
        const shoulder = isLeft ? P.l_shoulder : P.r_shoulder;
        const collarDir = vnorm(vsub(shoulder, P.spine2_joint));
        const toVert = vsub(pos, P.spine2_joint);
        const proj = vdot(toVert, collarDir);
        const collarLen = vlen(vsub(shoulder, P.spine2_joint));
        if (proj < 0 || proj > collarLen * 0.8) continue;
        if (vlen(vsub(toVert, vscale(collarDir, proj))) > 0.05) continue;
        regionId[i] = isLeft ? 19 : 20;
      }
    }
    // Toe split
    if (P.l_toe && P.l_ankle && P.r_toe && P.r_ankle) {
      for (let i = 0; i < regionId.length; i++) {
        const r = regionId[i];
        if (r !== 5 && r !== 6) continue;
        const isLeft = (r === 5);
        const toeJoint = isLeft ? P.l_toe : P.r_toe;
        const ankleJoint = isLeft ? P.l_ankle : P.r_ankle;
        const footDir = vnorm(vsub(toeJoint, ankleJoint));
        const projVal = vdot(vsub(cageRest[i], ankleJoint), footDir);
        const toeProj = vdot(vsub(toeJoint, ankleJoint), footDir);
        if (projVal > toeProj * 0.8) regionId[i] = isLeft ? 21 : 22;
      }
    }

    R.torso = indicesByRegion(0);
    R.pelvis = indicesByRegion(14);
    R.spine1 = indicesByRegion(15);
    R.spine2 = indicesByRegion(16);
    R.chest = indicesByRegion(17);
    R.neck_reg = indicesByRegion(18);
    R.l_collar = indicesByRegion(19);
    R.r_collar = indicesByRegion(20);
    R.l_thigh = indicesByRegion(1);
    R.r_thigh = indicesByRegion(2);
    R.l_shin  = indicesByRegion(3);
    R.r_shin  = indicesByRegion(4);
    R.l_foot  = indicesByRegion(5);
    R.r_foot  = indicesByRegion(6);
    R.l_toe_reg = indicesByRegion(21);
    R.r_toe_reg = indicesByRegion(22);
    R.l_upperarm = indicesByRegion(7);
    R.r_upperarm = indicesByRegion(8);
    R.l_forearm  = indicesByRegion(9);
    R.r_forearm  = indicesByRegion(10);
    R.head       = indicesByRegion(11);

    allLeftArmCage  = [].concat(R.l_upperarm, R.l_forearm, indicesByRegion(12));
    allRightArmCage = [].concat(R.r_upperarm, R.r_forearm, indicesByRegion(13));
    allLeftLegCage  = [].concat(R.l_thigh, R.l_shin, R.l_foot, R.l_toe_reg);
    allRightLegCage = [].concat(R.r_thigh, R.r_shin, R.r_foot, R.r_toe_reg);
    allPelvisCage = R.pelvis.slice();
    if (P.l_hip && P.r_hip) {
      const hipY = Math.max(P.l_hip[1], P.r_hip[1]);
      for (const ci of R.torso) {
        if (cageRest[ci][1] <= hipY + 0.12) allPelvisCage.push(ci);
      }
    }

    regionIndices[0]  = R.torso;
    if (R.pelvis.length >= 4) regionIndices[14] = R.pelvis;
    if (R.spine1.length >= 4) regionIndices[15] = R.spine1;
    if (R.spine2.length >= 4) regionIndices[16] = R.spine2;
    if (R.chest.length >= 4) regionIndices[17] = R.chest;
    if (R.neck_reg.length >= 4) regionIndices[18] = R.neck_reg;
    if (R.l_collar.length >= 4) regionIndices[19] = R.l_collar;
    if (R.r_collar.length >= 4) regionIndices[20] = R.r_collar;
    if (R.l_toe_reg.length >= 4) regionIndices[21] = R.l_toe_reg;
    if (R.r_toe_reg.length >= 4) regionIndices[22] = R.r_toe_reg;
    regionIndices[1]  = R.l_thigh;
    regionIndices[2]  = R.r_thigh;
    regionIndices[3]  = R.l_shin;
    regionIndices[4]  = R.r_shin;
    regionIndices[5]  = R.l_foot;
    regionIndices[6]  = R.r_foot;
    if (R.head.length) regionIndices[11] = R.head;

    resplitArmRegions();
    resplitLegRegions();
    autoDetectToeFromMesh();
    shrinkCageFeetToMesh();
    buildStretchConstraints();
    buildCrossBoundaryEdges();
    buildMeshAdjacency();
    computeRestVertexNormals();

    if (meshRestPos && bindIdx && bindW && K && regionId) {
      meshVertRegions = $('useFBXWeights').checked && fbxSkinData
        ? computeFBXTransferredWeights(meshRestPos)
        : $('boneEnvWeights').checked
          ? computeBoneEnvelopeWeights(meshRestPos)
          : precomputeMeshRegionWeights(meshRestPos, bindIdx, bindW, K, regionId);
      meshVertEmaAlpha = buildPerVertexEmaAlpha(meshVertRegions);
      console.log('[REBUILD] Mesh region weights recomputed for ' + (meshRestPos.length/3) + ' verts');
    }

    _prevShinDirL = null; _prevShinDirR = null;
    _prevFootDirL = null; _prevFootDirR = null;
    _smoothedShinDirL = null; _smoothedShinDirR = null;
    _prevAnkleBendAxisL = null; _prevAnkleBendAxisR = null;
    _prevAnkleAngleL = 0; _prevAnkleAngleR = 0;
    _prevAnkleAxisCageL = null; _prevAnkleAxisCageR = null;
    prevMeshPos = null;
    _jitterVel = null;
    prevSmoothedTransforms = null;

    console.log('[REBUILD] Regions re-split, bindings rebuilt');
  }

  // Button handlers
  $('replaceJoints').addEventListener('click', startPlacement);
  $('doneJoints').addEventListener('click', finishPlacement);
  $('clearJoints').addEventListener('click', function() {
    if (!meshRestPos) return;
    const nVerts = meshRestPos.length / 3;
    let bHash = 0;
    if (bounds) {
      bHash = Math.round((bounds.min[0]+bounds.min[1]+bounds.min[2]+bounds.max[0]+bounds.max[1]+bounds.max[2]) * 10000);
    }
    const meshKey = 'cageJoints_' + nVerts + '_' + bHash;
    localStorage.removeItem(meshKey);
    $('jointStatus').textContent = 'Saved joints cleared. Reload page to use defaults.';
    console.log('Cleared saved joints from localStorage key: ' + meshKey);
  });

  // Export joints to placed_joints.json (download)
  // Saves both the full P AND the primary placements (user-clicked joints)
  // so that secondary joints can be re-derived with improved algorithms on reload.
  $('exportJoints').addEventListener('click', function() {
    const data = {};
    for (const k of Object.keys(P)) {
      if (P[k]) data[k] = P[k].slice();
    }
    // Primary placements: the joints the user directly clicked
    // (secondary joints like hips, head, chest, toes are derived from these)
    const primaryKeys = ['pelvis', 'neck', 'l_knee', 'r_knee', 'l_ankle', 'r_ankle',
      'l_shoulder', 'r_shoulder', 'l_elbow', 'r_elbow', 'l_wrist', 'r_wrist'];
    const primary = {};
    for (const k of primaryKeys) {
      if (P[k]) primary[k] = P[k].slice();
    }
    // Include bone lengths for reference
    const boneLens = {};
    const pairs = [['l_hip','l_knee','l_thigh'],['l_knee','l_ankle','l_shin'],
      ['l_shoulder','l_elbow','l_uarm'],['l_elbow','l_wrist','l_farm'],
      ['r_hip','r_knee','r_thigh'],['r_knee','r_ankle','r_shin'],
      ['r_shoulder','r_elbow','r_uarm'],['r_elbow','r_wrist','r_farm']];
    for (const [a,b,name] of pairs) {
      if (P[a] && P[b]) boneLens[name] = vlen(vsub(P[b], P[a]));
    }
    const exported = { P: data, primary: primary, bone_lengths: boneLens, mesh_height: bounds ? bounds.height : null };
    const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(exported, null, 2)], { type: 'application/json' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = 'placed_joints.json';
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    $('jointStatus').textContent = 'Exported placed_joints.json';
    console.log('Exported joints to placed_joints.json');
  });

  // Re-derive secondary joints from primary placements using current algorithms.
  // This allows the derivation code to be improved without re-placing markers.
  function rederiveSecondaryJoints() {
    const meshH = bounds ? bounds.height : 1.0;

    // Derive hips from pelvis (wider spread, centered between knees)
    if (P.pelvis && P.l_knee) {
      // Center pelvis X between knees to avoid asymmetry from off-center groin click
      if (P.r_knee) {
        P.pelvis[0] = (P.l_knee[0] + P.r_knee[0]) / 2;
      }
      let hipSpread = meshH * 0.08;
      if (meshRestPos) {
        const nMesh = meshRestPos.length / 3;
        const bandH = meshH * 0.03;
        let minX = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity;
        for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) {
          if (Math.abs(meshRestPos[i*3+1] - P.pelvis[1]) < bandH) {
            minX = Math.min(minX, meshRestPos[i*3]); maxX = Math.max(maxX, meshRestPos[i*3]);
          }
        }
        if (maxX > minX) hipSpread = (maxX - minX) / 3;
      }
      // Hip Y = pelvis Y (groin click is already at hip joint level anatomically)
      P.l_hip = [P.pelvis[0] - hipSpread, P.pelvis[1], P.pelvis[2]];
      P.r_hip = [P.pelvis[0] + hipSpread, P.pelvis[1], P.pelvis[2]];
      console.log('[REDERIVE] hip spread=' + (hipSpread*1000).toFixed(1) + 'mm, pelvisX=' + P.pelvis[0].toFixed(4) +
        ' hipY=' + P.pelvis[1].toFixed(4));
    }

    // Derive chest
    if (P.pelvis && P.neck) {
      P.chest = [P.pelvis[0], (P.pelvis[1] + P.neck[1]) * 0.5 + 0.03, P.pelvis[2]];
    }

    // Derive head
    if (P.neck) {
      let headTop = P.neck[1] + 0.12;
      if (meshRestPos) {
        const nMesh = meshRestPos.length / 3;
        let maxY = -Infinity;
        for (let i = 0; i < nMesh; i++) { if (meshRestPos[i*3+1] > maxY) maxY = meshRestPos[i*3+1]; }
        headTop = maxY;
      }
      P.head = [P.neck[0], (P.neck[1] + headTop) * 0.5, P.neck[2]];
    }

    // Derive toes
    if (P.l_ankle) {
      const floorY = bounds ? bounds.min[1] : 0;
      P.l_toe = [P.l_ankle[0], floorY, P.l_ankle[2] + 0.04];
    }
    if (P.r_ankle) {
      const floorY = bounds ? bounds.min[1] : 0;
      P.r_toe = [P.r_ankle[0], floorY, P.r_ankle[2] + 0.04];
    }

    // Derive knuckles
    if (P.l_wrist && P.l_elbow) {
      const d = vnorm(vsub(P.l_wrist, P.l_elbow));
      P.l_mid_knuckle = vadd(P.l_wrist, vscale(d, 0.04));
    }
    if (P.r_wrist && P.r_elbow) {
      const d = vnorm(vsub(P.r_wrist, P.r_elbow));
      P.r_mid_knuckle = vadd(P.r_wrist, vscale(d, 0.04));
    }
  }

  // Rescale cage rest positions to match FBX skeleton proportions.
  // Stretches limb regions along bone axes so cage bone lengths match FBX.
  // Called once when FBX is loaded. After rescaling, proportion ratios → ~1.0.
  let _cageRescaled = false;
  function rescaleCageToFBX(restPose) {
    // DISABLED: Rescaling the cage geometry breaks the spatial alignment between
    // cage and mesh vertices, destroying the KNN binding. The cage must stay at
    // its original proportions (matching the mesh it wraps). Proportion differences
    // between cage and FBX skeletons are handled by retargeting ratios instead.
    console.log('[RESCALE] Disabled — proportion differences handled by retargeting ratios');
    return;
    if (!restPose || !cageRest || !P.l_hip || !R.l_thigh) return;
    if (_cageRescaled) {
      console.log('[RESCALE] Already rescaled — skipping');
      return;
    }

    console.log('=== RESCALING CAGE TO MATCH FBX PROPORTIONS ===');

    // Bone chains to rescale: process parent→child top-down so deltas cascade.
    // Each entry: parent joint, child joint, region to stretch,
    //             downstream regions to shift, downstream joints to shift.
    const chains = [
      // Left leg
      { parent: 'l_hip', child: 'l_knee', region: 'l_thigh',
        downstream: ['l_shin', 'l_foot', 'l_toe_reg'],
        downJoints: ['l_ankle', 'l_toe'] },
      { parent: 'l_knee', child: 'l_ankle', region: 'l_shin',
        downstream: ['l_foot', 'l_toe_reg'],
        downJoints: ['l_toe'] },
      // Right leg
      { parent: 'r_hip', child: 'r_knee', region: 'r_thigh',
        downstream: ['r_shin', 'r_foot', 'r_toe_reg'],
        downJoints: ['r_ankle', 'r_toe'] },
      { parent: 'r_knee', child: 'r_ankle', region: 'r_shin',
        downstream: ['r_foot', 'r_toe_reg'],
        downJoints: ['r_toe'] },
      // Left arm
      { parent: 'l_shoulder', child: 'l_elbow', region: 'l_upperarm',
        downstream: ['l_forearm', 'l_hand'],
        downJoints: ['l_wrist', 'l_mid_knuckle'] },
      { parent: 'l_elbow', child: 'l_wrist', region: 'l_forearm',
        downstream: ['l_hand'],
        downJoints: ['l_mid_knuckle'] },
      // Right arm
      { parent: 'r_shoulder', child: 'r_elbow', region: 'r_upperarm',
        downstream: ['r_forearm', 'r_hand'],
        downJoints: ['r_wrist', 'r_mid_knuckle'] },
      { parent: 'r_elbow', child: 'r_wrist', region: 'r_forearm',
        downstream: ['r_hand'],
        downJoints: ['r_mid_knuckle'] },
    ];

    for (const chain of chains) {
      const parentPos = P[chain.parent];
      const childPos = P[chain.child];
      const rpParent = restPose[chain.parent];
      const rpChild = restPose[chain.child];

      if (!parentPos || !childPos || !rpParent || !rpChild) continue;

      const curLen = vlen(vsub(childPos, parentPos));
      const targetLen = vlen(vsub(rpChild, rpParent));
      if (curLen < 0.001 || targetLen < 0.001) continue;

      const scale = targetLen / curLen;
      if (Math.abs(scale - 1.0) < 0.01) continue; // skip if already close

      const boneDir = vnorm(vsub(childPos, parentPos));

      // Scale vertices in this region along the bone axis.
      // Perpendicular offset (mesh width) is preserved.
      const regionVerts = R[chain.region];
      if (regionVerts) {
        for (const ci of regionVerts) {
          const v = cageRest[ci];
          const offset = vsub(v, parentPos);
          const along = vdot(offset, boneDir);
          const perp = vsub(offset, vscale(boneDir, along));
          const newAlong = along * scale;
          const newPos = vadd(parentPos, vadd(vscale(boneDir, newAlong), perp));
          cageRest[ci] = [newPos[0], newPos[1], newPos[2]];
        }
      }

      // Move child joint to new position
      const newChildPos = vadd(parentPos, vscale(boneDir, targetLen));
      const delta = vsub(newChildPos, childPos);
      P[chain.child] = [newChildPos[0], newChildPos[1], newChildPos[2]];

      // Shift all downstream region vertices by the delta
      for (const regName of chain.downstream) {
        const regVerts = R[regName] || [];
        for (const ci of regVerts) {
          cageRest[ci] = [
            cageRest[ci][0] + delta[0],
            cageRest[ci][1] + delta[1],
            cageRest[ci][2] + delta[2]
          ];
        }
      }

      // Shift downstream joints by the delta
      for (const jn of chain.downJoints) {
        if (P[jn]) {
          P[jn] = [P[jn][0] + delta[0], P[jn][1] + delta[1], P[jn][2] + delta[2]];
        }
      }

      console.log('  ' + chain.parent + ' → ' + chain.child + ': ' +
        (curLen * 1000).toFixed(0) + 'mm → ' + (targetLen * 1000).toFixed(0) +
        'mm (x' + scale.toFixed(3) + ') delta=[' +
        (delta[0]*1000).toFixed(0) + ',' + (delta[1]*1000).toFixed(0) + ',' +
        (delta[2]*1000).toFixed(0) + ']mm');
    }

    // Update cagePos from rescaled cageRest
    for (let i = 0; i < cageRest.length; i++) {
      cagePos[i * 3] = cageRest[i][0];
      cagePos[i * 3 + 1] = cageRest[i][1];
      cagePos[i * 3 + 2] = cageRest[i][2];
    }

    // Update region-split slider thresholds to match rescaled bone lengths.
    // Use PROJECTIONS along hip→ankle / shoulder→wrist axes (not bone lengths)
    // because bone lengths sum to more than the axis length due to knee/elbow bend,
    // which would push the shin boundary past the ankle into the foot region.
    if (P.l_hip && P.l_knee && P.l_ankle) {
      const legDir = vnorm(vsub(P.l_ankle, P.l_hip));
      const kneeProj = vdot(vsub(P.l_knee, P.l_hip), legDir);
      const ankleProj = vdot(vsub(P.l_ankle, P.l_hip), legDir);
      $('thighLen').value = Math.min(0.35, kneeProj);
      $('shinLen').value = Math.min(0.35, ankleProj - kneeProj);
      console.log('[RESCALE] Leg split thresholds: thighL=' + kneeProj.toFixed(4) +
        ' shinL=' + (ankleProj - kneeProj).toFixed(4) + ' total=' + ankleProj.toFixed(4));
    }
    if (P.l_shoulder && P.l_elbow && P.l_wrist) {
      const armDir = vnorm(vsub(P.l_wrist, P.l_shoulder));
      const elbowProj = vdot(vsub(P.l_elbow, P.l_shoulder), armDir);
      const wristProj = vdot(vsub(P.l_wrist, P.l_shoulder), armDir);
      $('upperArmLen').value = Math.min(0.40, elbowProj);
      $('forearmLen').value = Math.min(0.20, wristProj - elbowProj);
    }

    // Save toe positions — rebuildFromSkeleton calls autoDetectToeFromMesh which
    // uses meshRestPos (not rescaled) with the shifted ankle, corrupting the angle
    const savedLToe = P.l_toe ? P.l_toe.slice() : null;
    const savedRToe = P.r_toe ? P.r_toe.slice() : null;

    // Rebuild region assignments (Y-based splits use updated vertex positions)
    rebuildFromSkeleton();

    // Restore toe positions (autoDetectToeFromMesh corrupts them because
    // meshRestPos wasn't rescaled but P.l_ankle was shifted)
    if (savedLToe) P.l_toe = savedLToe;
    if (savedRToe) P.r_toe = savedRToe;

    _cageRescaled = true;

    // Log new bone lengths
    const logLen = (a, b) => P[a] && P[b] ? (vlen(vsub(P[b], P[a])) * 1000).toFixed(0) + 'mm' : 'N/A';
    console.log('  New cage bone lengths:');
    console.log('    l_thigh=' + logLen('l_hip','l_knee') + ' r_thigh=' + logLen('r_hip','r_knee'));
    console.log('    l_shin=' + logLen('l_knee','l_ankle') + ' r_shin=' + logLen('r_knee','r_ankle'));
    console.log('    l_uarm=' + logLen('l_shoulder','l_elbow') + ' r_uarm=' + logLen('r_shoulder','r_elbow'));
    console.log('    l_farm=' + logLen('l_elbow','l_wrist') + ' r_farm=' + logLen('r_elbow','r_wrist'));
    console.log('=== RESCALE COMPLETE ===');
  }

  // Load joints from placed_joints.json file (if it exists alongside index.html).
  // If 'primary' field exists, loads only primary placements and re-derives
  // secondary joints (hips, head, etc.) with current improved algorithms.
  async function loadJointsFromFile() {
    try {
      const resp = await fetch('placed_joints.json');
      if (!resp.ok) return false;
      const data = await resp.json();
      if (!data.P && !data.primary) return false;
