              console.log('  Worst region:               ' + RNAMES[worstRid] + ' (' + (worstMax * 1000).toFixed(1) + 'mm max)');
            }

            for (const rid of gtRegionOrder) {
              const d = gt[rid];
              if (!d || d.count === 0) continue;
              const avg = d.sum / d.count * 1000;
              const max = d.max * 1000;
              const name = RNAMES[rid] || ('r' + rid);
              const status = max < 10 ? 'GOOD' : max < 25 ? 'OK' : max < 50 ? 'WARN' : 'BAD';
              console.log('    ' + (name + '            ').slice(0, 14) +
                'avg ' + avg.toFixed(1).padStart(6) + 'mm  max ' + max.toFixed(1).padStart(6) + 'mm (f' + d.worstFrame + ')  ' + status);
            }

            // FBX ref mesh stretch vs cage stretch comparison
            if (m.fbxRefStretch) {
              const fbxWS = m.fbxRefStretch.worstStretch;
              const fbxAvgStrain = m.fbxRefStretch.totalAvgDev / m.frameCount * 100;
              console.log('  FBX ref mesh stretch:       worst=' + fbxWS.toFixed(1) + 'x  avg strain=' + fbxAvgStrain.toFixed(1) + '%');
            }
            const cageWS = m.worstStretch;
            const cageAvgStrain = m.totalAvgDev / m.frameCount * 100;
            console.log('  Cage mesh stretch:          worst=' + cageWS.toFixed(1) + 'x  avg strain=' + cageAvgStrain.toFixed(1) + '%');
            console.log('=== END FBX GROUND TRUTH ===');
          }

          // (h) Per-frame CSV dump + oscillation/correlation analysis
          if (m.perFrame && m.perFrame.length > 0) {
            const pf = m.perFrame;
            // CSV header + data (every frame for key metrics)
            const csvCols = ['f','l_foot_avg','r_foot_avg','l_foot_max','r_foot_max',
                             'l_shin_avg','r_shin_avg',
                             'l_foot_dir_err','r_foot_dir_err',
                             'l_ankle_err','r_ankle_err',
                             'l_ankle_ours','r_ankle_ours','l_ankle_fbx','r_ankle_fbx',
                             'l_knee_err','r_knee_err',
                             'l_foot_jitter_avg','r_foot_jitter_avg',
                             'l_foot_jitter_max','r_foot_jitter_max',
                             'l_uarm_twist_ours','l_uarm_twist_fbx',
                             'r_uarm_twist_ours','r_uarm_twist_fbx',
                             'l_farm_twist_ours','l_farm_twist_fbx',
                             'r_farm_twist_ours','r_farm_twist_fbx',
                             'l_thigh_twist_ours','l_thigh_twist_fbx',
                             'r_thigh_twist_ours','r_thigh_twist_fbx'];
            console.log('=== PER-FRAME CSV (' + pf.length + ' frames) ===');
            console.log('CSV_HEADER: ' + csvCols.join(','));
            for (let i = 0; i < pf.length; i++) {
              const d = pf[i];
              const vals = csvCols.map(c => {
                const v = d[c];
                return v !== undefined && v !== null ? (typeof v === 'number' ? v.toFixed(2) : v) : '';
              });
              console.log('CSV: ' + vals.join(','));
            }

            // Oscillation analysis: is foot error constant or swinging?
            console.log('=== OSCILLATION ANALYSIS ===');
            for (const region of ['l_foot','r_foot','l_shin','r_shin']) {
              const key = region + '_avg';
              const vals = pf.map(d => d[key]).filter(v => v !== undefined && v !== null);
              if (vals.length < 10) continue;
              const mean = vals.reduce((s,v) => s+v, 0) / vals.length;
              const std = Math.sqrt(vals.reduce((s,v) => s + (v-mean)*(v-mean), 0) / vals.length);
              const min = Math.min(...vals);
              const max = Math.max(...vals);
              const cv = mean > 0.01 ? (std / mean) : 0; // coefficient of variation
              const oscType = cv < 0.05 ? 'CONSTANT' : cv < 0.2 ? 'MILD_OSC' : 'HIGH_OSC';
              console.log('  ' + (region + '          ').slice(0,12) +
                'mean=' + mean.toFixed(1) + 'mm std=' + std.toFixed(1) + 'mm range=[' +
                min.toFixed(1) + '-' + max.toFixed(1) + '] CV=' + cv.toFixed(3) + '  ' + oscType);
            }

            // Correlation: does shin angular velocity predict foot jitter?
            console.log('=== CORRELATION ANALYSIS ===');
            for (const side of ['l','r']) {
              const shinVelKey = side + '_shin_dir_err'; // per-frame shin direction error as proxy for velocity
              const footJitKey = side + '_foot_jitter_avg';
              const footDevKey = side + '_foot_avg';
              // Compute correlation between foot deviation change and shin direction
              const pairs = [];
              for (let i = 1; i < pf.length; i++) {
                const shinErr = pf[i][shinVelKey];
                const footJit = pf[i][footJitKey];
                if (shinErr !== undefined && footJit !== undefined) {
                  pairs.push([shinErr, footJit]);
                }
              }
              if (pairs.length > 10) {
                const meanX = pairs.reduce((s,p) => s+p[0], 0) / pairs.length;
                const meanY = pairs.reduce((s,p) => s+p[1], 0) / pairs.length;
                let num = 0, denX = 0, denY = 0;
                for (const [x,y] of pairs) {
                  num += (x - meanX) * (y - meanY);
                  denX += (x - meanX) * (x - meanX);
                  denY += (y - meanY) * (y - meanY);
                }
                const corr = (denX > 0 && denY > 0) ? num / Math.sqrt(denX * denY) : 0;
                console.log('  ' + side.toUpperCase() + ' shin_dir_err vs foot_jitter: r=' + corr.toFixed(3) +
                  (Math.abs(corr) > 0.5 ? '  CORRELATED' : '  INDEPENDENT'));
              }
              // Also check foot deviation oscillation vs ankle angle
              const devPairs = [];
              for (let i = 1; i < pf.length; i++) {
                const ankleErr = pf[i][side + '_ankle_err'];
                const footDev = pf[i][footDevKey];
                if (ankleErr !== undefined && footDev !== undefined) {
                  devPairs.push([ankleErr, footDev]);
                }
              }
              if (devPairs.length > 10) {
                const mX = devPairs.reduce((s,p) => s+p[0], 0) / devPairs.length;
                const mY = devPairs.reduce((s,p) => s+p[1], 0) / devPairs.length;
                let n = 0, dX = 0, dY = 0;
                for (const [x,y] of devPairs) {
                  n += (x - mX) * (y - mY);
                  dX += (x - mX) * (x - mX);
                  dY += (y - mY) * (y - mY);
                }
                const corr = (dX > 0 && dY > 0) ? n / Math.sqrt(dX * dY) : 0;
                console.log('  ' + side.toUpperCase() + ' ankle_angle_err vs foot_deviation: r=' + corr.toFixed(3) +
                  (Math.abs(corr) > 0.5 ? '  CORRELATED' : '  INDEPENDENT'));
              }
            }
            // Frame-by-frame joint position comparison (every 10 frames, key joints)
            console.log('=== FRAME-BY-FRAME POSITION COMPARISON (pelvis-relative, mm) ===');
            console.log('  Shows cage vs FBX position for key joints every 10 frames. delta=cage-fbx');
            for (let i = 0; i < pf.length; i += 10) {
              const d = pf[i];
              const f = d.f;
              const px = d.pelvis_x, py = d.pelvis_y, pz = d.pelvis_z;
              if (px !== undefined) {
                console.log('  f' + f + ' pelvis_world=[' + px.toFixed(0) + ',' + py.toFixed(0) + ',' + pz.toFixed(0) + ']mm');
              }
              for (const jn of ['l_ankle','r_ankle','l_wrist','r_wrist','head']) {
                const cx = d[jn + '_cage_x'], cy = d[jn + '_cage_y'], cz = d[jn + '_cage_z'];
                const fx = d[jn + '_fbx_x'], fy = d[jn + '_fbx_y'], fz = d[jn + '_fbx_z'];
                if (cx !== undefined && fx !== undefined) {
                  const dx = cx - fx, dy = cy - fy, dz = cz - fz;
                  const dist = Math.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz);
                  console.log('    ' + (jn + '        ').slice(0, 10) +
                    'cage=[' + cx.toFixed(0) + ',' + cy.toFixed(0) + ',' + cz.toFixed(0) + '] ' +
                    'fbx=[' + fx.toFixed(0) + ',' + fy.toFixed(0) + ',' + fz.toFixed(0) + '] ' +
                    'delta=' + dist.toFixed(0) + 'mm');
                }
              }
            }
            console.log('=== END FRAME-BY-FRAME POSITION ===');

            console.log('=== END PER-FRAME ANALYSIS ===');
          }
        }
      }

      // ONE-SHOT EULER DIAGNOSTIC — dumps everything needed to find the bug
      if (!window._eulerDiagDone && time > 0.05 && !jointPosData && armState && lastRegionTransforms) {
        window._eulerDiagDone = true;
        console.log('=== EULER MODE FULL DIAGNOSTIC (t=' + time.toFixed(4) + ') ===');
        console.log('armState:', JSON.stringify({
          lTposeZ: armState.lTposeZ, rTposeZ: armState.rTposeZ,
          lArmSwing: armState.lArmSwing, rArmSwing: armState.rArmSwing,
          lElbowAngle: armState.lElbowAngle, rElbowAngle: armState.rElbowAngle,
          lWristAngle: armState.lWristAngle, rWristAngle: armState.rWristAngle,
          lBendAxis: armState.lBendAxis, rBendAxis: armState.rBendAxis,
          positionBased: armState.positionBased
        }));
        const tf = lastRegionTransforms;
        // Torso Kabsch
        if (tf[0] && tf[0].cRest && tf[0].cCurr) {
          console.log('Torso Kabsch: det=' + mat3_det(tf[0].R).toFixed(6) +
            ' cRest=' + tf[0].cRest.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)) +
            ' cCurr=' + tf[0].cCurr.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)));
        }
        // Shoulder agreement test: shoulder point should map to same world pos under torso and arm transforms
        if (tf[0] && tf[7] && cageShoulderL) {
          const sh = cageShoulderL;
          const t0 = tf[0], t7 = tf[7];
          const shViaTorso = [t0.R[0]*sh[0]+t0.R[1]*sh[1]+t0.R[2]*sh[2]+t0.t[0],
                              t0.R[3]*sh[0]+t0.R[4]*sh[1]+t0.R[5]*sh[2]+t0.t[1],
                              t0.R[6]*sh[0]+t0.R[7]*sh[1]+t0.R[8]*sh[2]+t0.t[2]];
          const shViaArm = [t7.R[0]*sh[0]+t7.R[1]*sh[1]+t7.R[2]*sh[2]+t7.t[0],
                            t7.R[3]*sh[0]+t7.R[4]*sh[1]+t7.R[5]*sh[2]+t7.t[1],
                            t7.R[6]*sh[0]+t7.R[7]*sh[1]+t7.R[8]*sh[2]+t7.t[2]];
          const err = Math.sqrt((shViaTorso[0]-shViaArm[0])**2+(shViaTorso[1]-shViaArm[1])**2+(shViaTorso[2]-shViaArm[2])**2);
          console.log('Shoulder L (cage rest):', sh.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)));
          console.log('Shoulder L via torso:', shViaTorso.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)));
          console.log('Shoulder L via arm:', shViaArm.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)));
          console.log('Shoulder agreement error:', err.toFixed(8));
        }
        // Forearm: compare old formula vs new formula
        if (tf[7] && armState.lBendAxis && cageShoulderL && cageArmDirL) {
          const ba = armState.lBendAxis;
          const ang = armState.lElbowAngle;
          const Rb = mat3_rotAxis(ba[0], ba[1], ba[2], ang);
          const R_u = tf[7].R, t_u = tf[7].t;
          const uaLen = +$('upperArmLen').value;
          const erx = cageShoulderL[0]+cageArmDirL[0]*uaLen;
          const ery = cageShoulderL[1]+cageArmDirL[1]*uaLen;
          const erz = cageShoulderL[2]+cageArmDirL[2]*uaLen;
          const ae = mat3_mulVec3(R_u, erx, ery, erz);
          const ep = [ae[0]+t_u[0], ae[1]+t_u[1], ae[2]+t_u[2]];
          const R_f = mat3_mul(Rb, R_u);
          // NEW formula
          const Rf_re = mat3_mulVec3(R_f, erx, ery, erz);
          const tNew = [ep[0]-Rf_re[0], ep[1]-Rf_re[1], ep[2]-Rf_re[2]];
          // OLD formula
          const Rb_tu = mat3_mulVec3(Rb, t_u[0], t_u[1], t_u[2]);
          const Rb_ep = mat3_mulVec3(Rb, ep[0], ep[1], ep[2]);
          const tOld = [Rb_tu[0]+ep[0]-Rb_ep[0], Rb_tu[1]+ep[1]-Rb_ep[1], Rb_tu[2]+ep[2]-Rb_ep[2]];
          console.log('Forearm L NEW t:', tNew.map(v=>v.toFixed(6)));
          console.log('Forearm L OLD t:', tOld.map(v=>v.toFixed(6)));
          console.log('Formula diff:', Math.sqrt((tNew[0]-tOld[0])**2+(tNew[1]-tOld[1])**2+(tNew[2]-tOld[2])**2).toFixed(12));
          console.log('Elbow rest pos:', [erx,ery,erz].map(v=>v.toFixed(4)));
          console.log('Elbow world pos:', ep.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)));
        }
        // Sample cage verts: check a few from each region
        for (const [name, arr] of [['torso',R.torso],['l_thigh',R.l_thigh],['l_uarm',R.l_upperarm]]) {
          if (arr && arr.length) {
            const ci = arr[0];
            console.log('Cage ' + name + '[0] idx=' + ci +
              ' rest=[' + cageRest[ci].map(v=>v.toFixed(4)) + ']' +
              ' cur=[' + cagePos[ci*3].toFixed(4) + ',' + cagePos[ci*3+1].toFixed(4) + ',' + cagePos[ci*3+2].toFixed(4) + ']');
          }
        }
        // Mesh vert binding check: what regions does v0 actually have?
        if (meshVertRegions && meshVertRegions[0]) {
          const pairs = meshVertRegions[0];
          let str = 'v0 weight pairs:';
          for (let e = 0; e < pairs.length; e += 2) {
            str += ' r' + pairs[e] + '=' + pairs[e+1].toFixed(4);
          }
          console.log(str);
        }
      }

      // One-shot arm diagnostic: compare L vs R transforms after first few frames
      if (!armDiagDone && time > 0.1 && lastRegionTransforms) {
        armDiagDone = true;
        const pairs = [[7,8,'upperarm'],[9,10,'forearm']];
        for (const [lid, rid, name] of pairs) {
          const L = lastRegionTransforms[lid];
          const R_tf = lastRegionTransforms[rid];
          if (!L) { console.warn(`ARM DIAG: L ${name} (region ${lid}) MISSING`); continue; }
          if (!R_tf) { console.warn(`ARM DIAG: R ${name} (region ${rid}) MISSING`); continue; }
          const Ld = mat3_det(L.R), Rd = mat3_det(R_tf.R);
          console.log(`ARM DIAG ${name}: L det=${Ld.toFixed(4)} R det=${Rd.toFixed(4)}`);
          console.log(`  L.R=[${Array.from(L.R).map(v=>v.toFixed(3)).join(',')}]`);
          console.log(`  R.R=[${Array.from(R_tf.R).map(v=>v.toFixed(3)).join(',')}]`);
          console.log(`  L.t=[${L.t.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)).join(',')}]`);
          console.log(`  R.t=[${R_tf.t.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)).join(',')}]`);
        }
      }

      // (det(R) logging removed — was spamming console)

      // One-shot mesh vertex check: verify mesh deformation produces sane values
      if (!window._meshVertCheckDone && time > 0.05 && meshGeo) {
        window._meshVertCheckDone = true;
        const mpos = meshGeo.attributes.position.array;
        const n = meshRestPos.length / 3;
        let nanCount = 0, infCount = 0, bigCount = 0, zeroDeform = 0;
        let maxDist = 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
          const dx = mpos[i*3] - meshRestPos[i*3];
          const dy = mpos[i*3+1] - meshRestPos[i*3+1];
          const dz = mpos[i*3+2] - meshRestPos[i*3+2];
          const dist = Math.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz);
          if (isNaN(mpos[i*3]) || isNaN(mpos[i*3+1]) || isNaN(mpos[i*3+2])) nanCount++;
          if (!isFinite(mpos[i*3]) || !isFinite(mpos[i*3+1]) || !isFinite(mpos[i*3+2])) infCount++;
          if (dist > 1.0) bigCount++;
          if (dist < 1e-6) zeroDeform++;
          if (dist > maxDist) maxDist = dist;
        }
        console.log('[MESH CHECK] verts=' + n + ' NaN=' + nanCount + ' Inf=' + infCount +
          ' bigDeform(>1)=' + bigCount + ' zeroDeform=' + zeroDeform + ' maxDist=' + maxDist.toFixed(4));
        // Sample 5 vertices from different regions
        const samples = [0, Math.floor(n*0.2), Math.floor(n*0.4), Math.floor(n*0.6), Math.floor(n*0.8)];
        for (const si of samples) {
          const ri = meshVertRegions[si] ? meshVertRegions[si][0] : '?';
          console.log('  v' + si + ' region=' + ri + ' rest=[' +
            meshRestPos[si*3].toFixed(4) + ',' + meshRestPos[si*3+1].toFixed(4) + ',' + meshRestPos[si*3+2].toFixed(4) +
            '] cur=[' + mpos[si*3].toFixed(4) + ',' + mpos[si*3+1].toFixed(4) + ',' + mpos[si*3+2].toFixed(4) + ']');
        }
      }
    }

    // Update skeleton overlay
    if ($('showSkeleton').checked && retargetedJoints.pelvis) {
      if (!skeletonLine) {
        // Create line segments for bones
        const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
        const maxPts = SKELETON_BONES.length * 2;
        const posArr = new Float32Array(maxPts * 3);
        geo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(posArr, 3));
        geo.setDrawRange(0, maxPts);
        skeletonLine = new THREE.LineSegments(geo, new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: 0x00ff88, linewidth: 2, depthTest: false }));
        skeletonLine.renderOrder = 999;
        scene.add(skeletonLine);
        // Create small spheres for each joint
        const jointKeys = new Set();
        for (const [a,b] of SKELETON_BONES) { jointKeys.add(a); jointKeys.add(b); }
        const sphereGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.005, 8, 6);
        const sphereMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xffff00, depthTest: false });
        for (const k of jointKeys) {
          const s = new THREE.Mesh(sphereGeo, sphereMat);
          s.renderOrder = 1000;
          s.userData.jointKey = k;
          scene.add(s);
          skeletonJointSpheres.push(s);
        }
      }
      // Update bone line positions
      const posArr = skeletonLine.geometry.attributes.position.array;
      let idx = 0;
      for (const [a, b] of SKELETON_BONES) {
        const pa = retargetedJoints[a], pb = retargetedJoints[b];
        if (pa && pb) {
          posArr[idx++] = pa[0]; posArr[idx++] = pa[1]; posArr[idx++] = pa[2];
          posArr[idx++] = pb[0]; posArr[idx++] = pb[1]; posArr[idx++] = pb[2];
        } else {
          posArr[idx++] = 0; posArr[idx++] = 0; posArr[idx++] = 0;
          posArr[idx++] = 0; posArr[idx++] = 0; posArr[idx++] = 0;
        }
      }
      skeletonLine.geometry.attributes.position.needsUpdate = true;
      skeletonLine.visible = true;
      // Update joint sphere positions
      for (const s of skeletonJointSpheres) {
        const jp = retargetedJoints[s.userData.jointKey];
        if (jp) { s.position.set(jp[0], jp[1], jp[2]); s.visible = true; }
        else { s.visible = false; }
      }
    } else if (skeletonLine) {
      skeletonLine.visible = false;
      for (const s of skeletonJointSpheres) s.visible = false;
    }

    // Diagnostic HUD visibility toggle
    $('diagHUD').style.display = $('showDiagHUD').checked ? 'block' : 'none';

    controls.update();
    renderer.render(scene, camera);
    // Update placed joint marker overlay positions when camera moves
    if (placementMode) updateAllMarkerScreenPositions();
  }
  tick();

  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // FBX DRAG-DROP: extract joint positions in-memory from Mixamo FBX
  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

  const FBX_BONE_MAP = [
    { suffix: 'Hips',          name: 'pelvis' },
    { suffix: 'Spine',         name: 'spine_joint' },
    { suffix: 'Spine1',        name: 'spine1_joint' },
    { suffix: 'Spine2',        name: 'spine2_joint' },
    { suffix: 'Neck',          name: 'neck' },
    { suffix: 'Head',          name: 'head' },
    { suffix: 'LeftShoulder',  name: 'l_collar' },
    { suffix: 'RightShoulder', name: 'r_collar' },
    { suffix: 'LeftArm',       name: 'l_shoulder' },
    { suffix: 'LeftForeArm',   name: 'l_elbow' },
    { suffix: 'LeftHand',      name: 'l_wrist' },
    { suffix: 'LeftHandMiddle1', name: 'l_mid_knuckle', alt: 'LeftHandMiddle2' },
    { suffix: 'LeftUpLeg',     name: 'l_hip' },
    { suffix: 'LeftLeg',       name: 'l_knee' },
    { suffix: 'LeftFoot',      name: 'l_ankle' },
    { suffix: 'LeftToeBase',   name: 'l_toe' },
    { suffix: 'RightArm',      name: 'r_shoulder' },
    { suffix: 'RightForeArm',  name: 'r_elbow' },
    { suffix: 'RightHand',     name: 'r_wrist' },
    { suffix: 'RightHandMiddle1', name: 'r_mid_knuckle', alt: 'RightHandMiddle2' },
    { suffix: 'RightUpLeg',    name: 'r_hip' },
    { suffix: 'RightLeg',      name: 'r_knee' },
    { suffix: 'RightFoot',     name: 'r_ankle' },
    { suffix: 'RightToeBase',  name: 'r_toe' },
  ];

  function matchFBXBone(boneName) {
    const lower = boneName.toLowerCase();
    const sorted = FBX_BONE_MAP.slice().sort((a, b) => b.suffix.length - a.suffix.length);
    for (const entry of sorted) {
      if (lower.endsWith(entry.suffix.toLowerCase())) return entry.name;
      if (entry.alt && lower.endsWith(entry.alt.toLowerCase())) return entry.name;
    }
    return null;
  }

  function extractPositionsFromFBX(arrayBuffer, filename) {
    statusEl.textContent = 'Parsing FBX...';

    const loader = new THREE.FBXLoader();
    let group;
    try {
      group = loader.parse(arrayBuffer, '');
    } catch (e) {
      statusEl.textContent = 'FBX parse error: ' + e.message;
      console.error(e);
      return;
    }

    // Find bones and map them
    const allBones = [];
    group.traverse(obj => { if (obj.isBone) allBones.push(obj); });
    if (!allBones.length) { statusEl.textContent = 'No bones in FBX!'; return; }

    const boneMapping = {};
    let hipsBone = null;
    for (const bone of allBones) {
      const mapped = matchFBXBone(bone.name);
      if (mapped) {
        boneMapping[mapped] = bone;
        if (mapped === 'pelvis') hipsBone = bone;
      }
    }

    if (!hipsBone) { statusEl.textContent = 'No Hips bone found!'; return; }

    const clip = group.animations && group.animations[0];
    if (!clip) { statusEl.textContent = 'No animation clip in FBX!'; return; }

    // ── Extract BIND POSE (T-pose) BEFORE animation overrides bone transforms ──
    // In Mixamo FBX, the skeleton's default pose is the T-pose (bind pose).
    // We need these positions as the "rest" reference for delta retargeting,
    // NOT frame 0 of the walking animation (which is mid-walk).
    group.updateMatrixWorld(true);
    const bindPoseRaw = {};  // hips-centered, X-mirrored, UNscaled
    const hipsBindPos = new THREE.Vector3();
    hipsBindPos.setFromMatrixPosition(hipsBone.matrixWorld);
    for (const jn of Object.keys(boneMapping)) {
      const bone = boneMapping[jn];
      const bp = new THREE.Vector3();
      bp.setFromMatrixPosition(bone.matrixWorld);
      bindPoseRaw[jn] = [
        -(bp.x - hipsBindPos.x),
        bp.y - hipsBindPos.y,
        bp.z - hipsBindPos.z
      ];
    }

    // ── Extract BIND POSE quaternions (world-space, X-mirrored) ──
    const bindQuatRaw = {};
    const tmpQuat = new THREE.Quaternion();
    for (const jn of Object.keys(boneMapping)) {
      const bone = boneMapping[jn];
      bone.getWorldQuaternion(tmpQuat);
      // X-mirror: reflection across YZ plane → negate Y and Z components
      bindQuatRaw[jn] = [tmpQuat.x, -tmpQuat.y, -tmpQuat.z, tmpQuat.w];
    }

    // Setup mixer
    const mixer = new THREE.AnimationMixer(group);
    const action = mixer.clipAction(clip);
    action.play();

    // Measure skeleton height for auto-scale (from animated frame 0)
    mixer.setTime(0);
    group.updateMatrixWorld(true);
    let minY = Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
    group.traverse(obj => {
      if (obj.isBone) {
        const wp = new THREE.Vector3();
        obj.getWorldPosition(wp);
        minY = Math.min(minY, wp.y);
        maxY = Math.max(maxY, wp.y);
      }
    });
    const sourceHeight = maxY - minY;
    const targetHeight = meshHeight || 1.0;
    const scaleFactor = (sourceHeight > 0.01) ? (targetHeight / sourceHeight) : 1.0;
    console.log('FBX skeleton height:', sourceHeight.toFixed(1),
      '→ cage mesh height:', targetHeight.toFixed(3),
      '→ scale:', scaleFactor.toFixed(4));

    // ── Extract FBX SKIN WEIGHTS from SkinnedMesh ──
    // These are artist-quality per-vertex bone weights designed for standard LBS.
    // We'll transfer them to our mesh via nearest-neighbor vertex matching.
    // Must be after scaleFactor is computed (needs it for coordinate transform).
    {
      const JOINT_TO_REGION = {
        'pelvis': 14, 'spine_joint': 0, 'spine1_joint': 15, 'spine2_joint': 17,
        'neck': 18, 'head': 11, 'l_collar': 19, 'r_collar': 20,
        'l_shoulder': 7, 'r_shoulder': 8, 'l_elbow': 9, 'r_elbow': 10,
        'l_wrist': 12, 'r_wrist': 13, 'l_mid_knuckle': 12, 'r_mid_knuckle': 13,
        'l_hip': 1, 'r_hip': 2, 'l_knee': 3, 'r_knee': 4,
        'l_ankle': 5, 'r_ankle': 6, 'l_toe': 21, 'r_toe': 22
      };

      // Find the largest SkinnedMesh in the FBX group
      let bestMesh = null, bestVertCount = 0;
      group.traverse(obj => {
        if (obj.isSkinnedMesh && obj.geometry && obj.geometry.attributes.position) {
          const nv = obj.geometry.attributes.position.count;
          if (nv > bestVertCount) { bestVertCount = nv; bestMesh = obj; }
        }
      });

      if (bestMesh && bestMesh.geometry.attributes.skinIndex && bestMesh.geometry.attributes.skinWeight) {
        const geo = bestMesh.geometry;
        const posAttr = geo.attributes.position;
        const skinIdx = geo.attributes.skinIndex;
        const skinWt = geo.attributes.skinWeight;
        const skeleton = bestMesh.skeleton;
        const nFBXVerts = posAttr.count;

        // Build bone index → region ID mapping
        const boneToRegion = new Int32Array(skeleton.bones.length).fill(-1);
        for (let bi = 0; bi < skeleton.bones.length; bi++) {
          const boneName = skeleton.bones[bi].name;
          const mapped = matchFBXBone(boneName);
          if (mapped && JOINT_TO_REGION[mapped] !== undefined) {
            boneToRegion[bi] = JOINT_TO_REGION[mapped];
          }
        }

        // Extract vertex positions in our coordinate space (hips-centered, X-mirrored, scaled)
        const fbxPositions = new Float32Array(nFBXVerts * 3);
        const meshWorldMat = bestMesh.matrixWorld;
        const tmpV = new THREE.Vector3();
        for (let i = 0; i < nFBXVerts; i++) {
          tmpV.set(posAttr.getX(i), posAttr.getY(i), posAttr.getZ(i));
          tmpV.applyMatrix4(meshWorldMat);
          // Same transform as bind pose joints: hips-center, X-mirror, scale
          fbxPositions[i * 3]     = -(tmpV.x - hipsBindPos.x) * scaleFactor;
          fbxPositions[i * 3 + 1] =  (tmpV.y - hipsBindPos.y) * scaleFactor;
          fbxPositions[i * 3 + 2] =  (tmpV.z - hipsBindPos.z) * scaleFactor;
        }

        // Extract per-vertex region weights
        // three.js 0.128 BufferAttribute uses getX/getY/getZ/getW, not getComponent
        const _getComp = (attr, i, j) => [attr.getX(i), attr.getY(i), attr.getZ(i), attr.getW(i)][j];
        const fbxWeights = new Array(nFBXVerts);
        let unmapped = 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < nFBXVerts; i++) {
          const entries = [];
          for (let j = 0; j < 4; j++) {
            const bi = _getComp(skinIdx, i, j);
            const w = _getComp(skinWt, i, j);
            if (w < 0.001) continue;
            const rid = boneToRegion[bi];
            if (rid < 0) { unmapped++; continue; }
            // Merge duplicate region IDs (can happen when multiple bones map to same region)
            const existing = entries.find(e => e[0] === rid);
            if (existing) { existing[1] += w; }
            else { entries.push([rid, w]); }
          }
          // Normalize
          let totalW = 0;
          for (const e of entries) totalW += e[1];
          if (totalW > 0) for (const e of entries) e[1] /= totalW;
          // Store as flat [rid, w, rid, w, ...]
          const flat = [];
          for (const e of entries) { flat.push(e[0], e[1]); }
          fbxWeights[i] = flat.length > 0 ? flat : [14, 1.0]; // fallback to pelvis
        }

        // Add pelvis offset to match our mesh coordinate system
        if (P.pelvis) {
          for (let i = 0; i < nFBXVerts; i++) {
            fbxPositions[i * 3]     += P.pelvis[0];
            fbxPositions[i * 3 + 1] += P.pelvis[1];
            fbxPositions[i * 3 + 2] += P.pelvis[2];
          }
        }

        fbxSkinData = { positions: fbxPositions, weights: fbxWeights, nVerts: nFBXVerts };
        console.log('[FBX SKIN] Extracted ' + nFBXVerts + ' vertices with skin weights from ' +
          bestMesh.name + '. Unmapped bone refs: ' + unmapped +
          '. Bones: ' + skeleton.bones.length + ' → ' +
          boneToRegion.filter(r => r >= 0).length + ' mapped to regions.');
      } else {
        fbxSkinData = null;
        console.log('[FBX SKIN] No SkinnedMesh with skin weights found in FBX.');
      }
    }

    // Extract positions — use clip's native rate or minimum 60fps for fast animations
    let nativeFPS = 30;
    if (clip.tracks.length > 0 && clip.tracks[0].times.length > 1) {
      const dt = clip.tracks[0].times[1] - clip.tracks[0].times[0];
      if (dt > 0) nativeFPS = Math.round(1 / dt);
    }
    const fps = Math.max(60, nativeFPS);
    const duration = clip.duration;
    const frameCount = Math.ceil(duration * fps) + 1;
    const jointNames = Object.keys(boneMapping);

    const result = {
      duration, fps, frame_count: frameCount,
      rest_pose: {},
      rest_quats: {},
      root_positions: [],
      joints: {}
    };
    for (const jn of jointNames) {
      result.joints[jn] = { times: [], positions: [], quaternions: [] };
    }

    const worldPos = new THREE.Vector3();
    const hipsPos = new THREE.Vector3();

    for (let f = 0; f < frameCount; f++) {
      const t = Math.min(f / fps, duration);
      mixer.setTime(t);
      group.updateMatrixWorld(true);

      // Get hips TRANSLATION only (not full inverse matrix).
      // This removes root motion drift while keeping world-space orientation,
      // so direction vectors between joints stay in world coordinates.
      hipsPos.setFromMatrixPosition(hipsBone.matrixWorld);
      // Negate X: Mixamo has left=+X, cage has left=-X (mirrored convention)
      result.root_positions.push([
        -hipsPos.x * scaleFactor,
        hipsPos.y * scaleFactor,
        hipsPos.z * scaleFactor
      ]);

      for (const jn of jointNames) {
        const bone = boneMapping[jn];
        worldPos.setFromMatrixPosition(bone.matrixWorld);

        // Subtract hips translation only — positions are now
        // "hips-centered but world-oriented"
        // Negate X to match cage convention (Mixamo left=+X, cage left=-X)
        const lx = -(worldPos.x - hipsPos.x) * scaleFactor;
        const ly = (worldPos.y - hipsPos.y) * scaleFactor;
        const lz = (worldPos.z - hipsPos.z) * scaleFactor;

        result.joints[jn].times.push(+t.toFixed(6));
        result.joints[jn].positions.push([
          +lx.toFixed(6), +ly.toFixed(6), +lz.toFixed(6)
        ]);

        // World quaternion (X-mirrored: negate Y and Z)
        bone.getWorldQuaternion(tmpQuat);
        result.joints[jn].quaternions.push([
          +tmpQuat.x.toFixed(6), +(-tmpQuat.y).toFixed(6),
          +(-tmpQuat.z).toFixed(6), +tmpQuat.w.toFixed(6)
        ]);
      }
    }

    // Use BIND POSE (T-pose) as rest_pose, NOT frame 0 of the animation.
    // This is critical: frame 0 of a walking animation is mid-walk, giving tiny
    // deltas. T-pose gives meaningful animation deltas for retargeting.
    for (const jn of jointNames) {
      const raw = bindPoseRaw[jn];
      if (raw) {
        result.rest_pose[jn] = [
          +(raw[0] * scaleFactor).toFixed(6),
          +(raw[1] * scaleFactor).toFixed(6),
          +(raw[2] * scaleFactor).toFixed(6)
        ];
      } else {
        result.rest_pose[jn] = result.joints[jn].positions[0].slice();
      }
    }

    // Rest quaternions from bind pose (T-pose)
    for (const jn of jointNames) {
      result.rest_quats[jn] = bindQuatRaw[jn] ||
        (result.joints[jn].quaternions[0] || [0, 0, 0, 1]).slice();
    }

    // Backward compat: alias 'chest' → spine1_joint (old code references P.chest / retargetedJoints.chest)
    if (result.rest_pose.spine1_joint && !result.rest_pose.chest) {
      result.rest_pose.chest = result.rest_pose.spine1_joint.slice();
      result.rest_quats.chest = result.rest_quats.spine1_joint ? result.rest_quats.spine1_joint.slice() : [0,0,0,1];
      result.joints.chest = result.joints.spine1_joint;
    }

    // Diagnostic: compare bind pose (T-pose) vs frame 0 (animated) positions
    console.log('=== BIND POSE (T-pose) vs FRAME 0 (animated) ===');
    for (const jn of jointNames) {
      const bp = result.rest_pose[jn];
      const f0 = result.joints[jn].positions[0];
      const dist = Math.sqrt((bp[0]-f0[0])**2 + (bp[1]-f0[1])**2 + (bp[2]-f0[2])**2);
      if (dist > 0.001) {
        console.log('  ' + jn + ': T-pose=[' + bp.map(v=>(+v).toFixed(4)).join(',') +
          '] frame0=[' + f0.map(v=>(+v).toFixed(4)).join(',') + '] diff=' + dist.toFixed(4));
      }
    }

    // ── TWIST SANITY CHECK (frame 0 vs bind pose — should be small) ──
    // Position-swing residual method: twist at frame 0 measures how far from
    // T-pose the animation starts. For walking, this is a few degrees.
    // Values > 30° may indicate extraction issues.
    {
      const twistChecks = [
        ['l_shoulder', 'l_elbow'], ['r_shoulder', 'r_elbow'],
        ['l_elbow', 'l_wrist'], ['r_elbow', 'r_wrist'],
        ['l_hip', 'l_knee'], ['r_hip', 'r_knee']
      ];
      const parts = [];
      for (const [bone, child] of twistChecks) {
        const rq = result.rest_quats[bone];
        const fq = result.joints[bone] && result.joints[bone].quaternions[0];
        const rpB = result.rest_pose[bone], rpC = result.rest_pose[child];
        if (rq && fq && rpB && rpC) {
          const bd = vnorm(vsub(rpC, rpB));
          const tw = extractQuatTwist(rq, fq, bd) * 180 / Math.PI;
          parts.push(bone + '=' + tw.toFixed(1) + '°');
        }
      }
      console.log('[TWIST SANITY frame0] ' + parts.join(' '));
    }

    // ── SKELETON PROPORTION CHECK ──
    // Compare FBX rest-pose proportions vs cage pivot proportions.
    // No alignment/rotation is applied — only direction vectors are used from FBX data.
    if (P) {
      console.log('=== FBX vs CAGE PROPORTION CHECK (no alignment applied) ===');
      const cagePelvis = P.pelvis || [0, 0, 0];
      const checkJoints = ['l_hip','r_hip','l_shoulder','r_shoulder','l_knee','r_knee','l_elbow','r_elbow','neck','head'];
      for (const jn of checkJoints) {
        const fp = result.rest_pose[jn];
        const cp = P[jn];
        if (fp && cp) {
          // FBX: hips-centered distance from pelvis
          const fd = Math.sqrt(fp[0]*fp[0] + fp[1]*fp[1] + fp[2]*fp[2]);
          // Cage: distance from cage pelvis
          const cd = Math.sqrt((cp[0]-cagePelvis[0])**2 + (cp[1]-cagePelvis[1])**2 + (cp[2]-cagePelvis[2])**2);
          const ratio = cd > 0.001 ? (fd / cd).toFixed(3) : 'N/A';
          console.log('  ' + jn + ': fbxDist=' + fd.toFixed(4) + ' cageDist=' + cd.toFixed(4) + ' ratio=' + ratio +
            '  fbx=[' + fp.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)).join(',') + '] cage=[' + cp.map(v=>v.toFixed(4)).join(',') + ']');
        }
      }
      // Direction check: FBX l_shoulder direction vs cage l_shoulder direction
      const fpLS = result.rest_pose.l_shoulder, fpRS = result.rest_pose.r_shoulder;
      const cpLS = P.l_shoulder, cpRS = P.r_shoulder;
      if (fpLS && fpRS && cpLS && cpRS) {
        console.log('  FBX shoulder axis: [' + [(fpLS[0]-fpRS[0]).toFixed(4), (fpLS[1]-fpRS[1]).toFixed(4), (fpLS[2]-fpRS[2]).toFixed(4)].join(',') + ']');
        console.log('  Cage shoulder axis: [' + [(cpLS[0]-cpRS[0]).toFixed(4), (cpLS[1]-cpRS[1]).toFixed(4), (cpLS[2]-cpRS[2]).toFixed(4)].join(',') + ']');
      }
      // Forward direction: knee should be in +Z when leg is forward
      const fpLK = result.rest_pose.l_knee, fpLH = result.rest_pose.l_hip;
      if (fpLK && fpLH) {
        console.log('  FBX l_thigh rest dir: [' + [(fpLK[0]-fpLH[0]).toFixed(4), (fpLK[1]-fpLH[1]).toFixed(4), (fpLK[2]-fpLH[2]).toFixed(4)].join(',') + ']');
      }
      const cpLK = P.l_knee, cpLH = P.l_hip;
      if (cpLK && cpLH) {
        console.log('  Cage l_thigh rest dir: [' + [(cpLK[0]-cpLH[0]).toFixed(4), (cpLK[1]-cpLH[1]).toFixed(4), (cpLK[2]-cpLH[2]).toFixed(4)].join(',') + ']');
      }
    }

    // Rescale cage rest positions to match FBX skeleton proportions.
    // Must happen BEFORE proportion ratio computation so ratios reflect rescaled cage.
    rescaleCageToFBX(result.rest_pose);

    // Shrink cage foot/toe vertices to match the mesh foot extent (eliminate flippers)
    shrinkCageFeetToMesh();

    // Compute leg proportion ratio: cage leg length / FBX leg length (in cage-scaled space).
    // Used to scale pelvis translation so bob/sway matches the cage's actual leg reach.
    fbxLegPropRatio = 1.0;
    if (P) {
      const rp = result.rest_pose;
      // FBX leg length (hip→knee + knee→ankle) in cage-scaled space
      let fbxLegLen = 0;
      if (rp.l_hip && rp.l_knee && rp.l_ankle) {
        fbxLegLen = Math.sqrt(rp.l_knee[0]**2+rp.l_knee[1]**2+rp.l_knee[2]**2) > 0.001
          ? Math.sqrt((rp.l_knee[0]-rp.l_hip[0])**2+(rp.l_knee[1]-rp.l_hip[1])**2+(rp.l_knee[2]-rp.l_hip[2])**2) +
            Math.sqrt((rp.l_ankle[0]-rp.l_knee[0])**2+(rp.l_ankle[1]-rp.l_knee[1])**2+(rp.l_ankle[2]-rp.l_knee[2])**2)
          : 0;
      }
      // Cage leg length (hip→knee + knee→ankle)
      let cageLegLen = 0;
      if (P.l_hip && P.l_knee && P.l_ankle) {
        cageLegLen = Math.sqrt((P.l_knee[0]-P.l_hip[0])**2+(P.l_knee[1]-P.l_hip[1])**2+(P.l_knee[2]-P.l_hip[2])**2) +
          Math.sqrt((P.l_ankle[0]-P.l_knee[0])**2+(P.l_ankle[1]-P.l_knee[1])**2+(P.l_ankle[2]-P.l_knee[2])**2);
      }
      if (fbxLegLen > 0.01 && cageLegLen > 0.01) {
        fbxLegPropRatio = cageLegLen / fbxLegLen;
        console.log('=== LEG PROPORTION RATIO ===');
        console.log('  FBX leg len (scaled): ' + fbxLegLen.toFixed(4) +
          '  Cage leg len: ' + cageLegLen.toFixed(4) +
          '  ratio: ' + fbxLegPropRatio.toFixed(3));
      }
    }

    // ── PROPORTION COMPARISON DIAGNOSTIC ──
    // Compare FBX vs cage skeleton proportions: arm lengths, shoulder spread, hip spread, torso height.
    // Mismatches here cause spatial issues (hands not meeting, arms through chest, wrong foot positions).
    {
      const rp = result.rest_pose;

      // Arm proportion ratio (same pattern as leg ratio)
      fbxArmPropRatio = 1.0;
      let fbxArmLen = 0, cageArmLen = 0;
      if (P && P.l_shoulder && P.l_elbow && P.l_wrist &&
          rp.l_shoulder && rp.l_elbow && rp.l_wrist) {
        fbxArmLen = vlen(vsub(rp.l_elbow, rp.l_shoulder)) + vlen(vsub(rp.l_wrist, rp.l_elbow));
        cageArmLen = vlen(vsub(P.l_elbow, P.l_shoulder)) + vlen(vsub(P.l_wrist, P.l_elbow));
        if (fbxArmLen > 0.01 && cageArmLen > 0.01) {
          fbxArmPropRatio = cageArmLen / fbxArmLen;
        }
      }

      // Shoulder spread ratio (FBX / cage)
      fbxShoulderSpreadRatio = 1.0;
      let fbxShSpread = 0, cageShSpread = 0;
      if (P && P.l_shoulder && P.r_shoulder && rp.l_shoulder && rp.r_shoulder) {
        fbxShSpread = vlen(vsub(rp.r_shoulder, rp.l_shoulder));
        cageShSpread = vlen(vsub(P.r_shoulder, P.l_shoulder));
        if (fbxShSpread > 0.01 && cageShSpread > 0.01) {
          fbxShoulderSpreadRatio = fbxShSpread / cageShSpread;
        }
      }

      // Hip spread ratio (FBX / cage)
      fbxHipSpreadRatio = 1.0;
      let fbxHipSpread = 0, cageHipSpread = 0;
      if (P && P.l_hip && P.r_hip && rp.l_hip && rp.r_hip) {
        fbxHipSpread = vlen(vsub(rp.r_hip, rp.l_hip));
        cageHipSpread = vlen(vsub(P.r_hip, P.l_hip));
        if (fbxHipSpread > 0.01 && cageHipSpread > 0.01) {
          fbxHipSpreadRatio = fbxHipSpread / cageHipSpread;
        }
      }

      // Torso height comparison
      let fbxTorsoH = 0, cageTorsoH = 0;
      if (rp.pelvis && rp.neck) fbxTorsoH = Math.abs(rp.neck[1] - rp.pelvis[1]);
      if (P && P.pelvis && P.neck) cageTorsoH = Math.abs(P.neck[1] - P.pelvis[1]);

      // Arm segment breakdown
      let fbxUpperArm = 0, fbxForearm = 0, cageUpperArm = 0, cageForearm = 0;
      if (rp.l_shoulder && rp.l_elbow) fbxUpperArm = vlen(vsub(rp.l_elbow, rp.l_shoulder));
      if (rp.l_elbow && rp.l_wrist) fbxForearm = vlen(vsub(rp.l_wrist, rp.l_elbow));
      if (P && P.l_shoulder && P.l_elbow) cageUpperArm = vlen(vsub(P.l_elbow, P.l_shoulder));
      if (P && P.l_elbow && P.l_wrist) cageForearm = vlen(vsub(P.l_wrist, P.l_elbow));

      // Total reach from spine center
      let fbxTotalReach = 0, cageTotalReach = 0;
      if (rp.l_shoulder && rp.pelvis) {
        const fbxShOffset = Math.abs(rp.l_shoulder[0] - (rp.pelvis[0] || 0));
        fbxTotalReach = fbxShOffset + fbxArmLen;
      }
      if (P && P.l_shoulder && P.pelvis) {
        const cageShOffset = Math.abs(P.l_shoulder[0] - P.pelvis[0]);
        cageTotalReach = cageShOffset + cageArmLen;
      }

      console.log('=== PROPORTION COMPARISON ===');
      console.log('  Arm total:     FBX=' + fbxArmLen.toFixed(4) + '  Cage=' + cageArmLen.toFixed(4) +
        '  ratio=' + fbxArmPropRatio.toFixed(3) + (Math.abs(fbxArmPropRatio - 1) > 0.15 ? '  MISMATCH' : '  OK'));
      console.log('    upper arm:   FBX=' + fbxUpperArm.toFixed(4) + '  Cage=' + cageUpperArm.toFixed(4));
      console.log('    forearm:     FBX=' + fbxForearm.toFixed(4) + '  Cage=' + cageForearm.toFixed(4));
      console.log('  Shoulder spread: FBX=' + fbxShSpread.toFixed(4) + '  Cage=' + cageShSpread.toFixed(4) +
        '  ratio=' + fbxShoulderSpreadRatio.toFixed(3) + (Math.abs(fbxShoulderSpreadRatio - 1) > 0.15 ? '  MISMATCH' : '  OK'));
      console.log('  Hip spread:    FBX=' + fbxHipSpread.toFixed(4) + '  Cage=' + cageHipSpread.toFixed(4) +
        '  ratio=' + fbxHipSpreadRatio.toFixed(3) + (Math.abs(fbxHipSpreadRatio - 1) > 0.15 ? '  MISMATCH' : '  OK'));
      console.log('  Torso height:  FBX=' + fbxTorsoH.toFixed(4) + '  Cage=' + cageTorsoH.toFixed(4) +
        '  ratio=' + (cageTorsoH > 0.01 ? (cageTorsoH/fbxTorsoH).toFixed(3) : 'N/A'));
      console.log('  Total reach (center→wrist): FBX=' + fbxTotalReach.toFixed(4) + '  Cage=' + cageTotalReach.toFixed(4) +
        '  diff=' + ((cageTotalReach - fbxTotalReach) * 1000).toFixed(1) + 'mm' +
        (Math.abs(cageTotalReach - fbxTotalReach) > 0.03 ? '  MISMATCH' : '  OK'));
      // Log active corrections
      const hipAdj = 1.0 + (fbxHipSpreadRatio - 1.0) * 0.4;
      const shAdj = 1.0 + (fbxShoulderSpreadRatio - 1.0) * 0.4;
      console.log('  Active corrections (40% blend):');
      console.log('    hip spread adj=' + hipAdj.toFixed(3) +
        ' (moving hips ' + ((1-hipAdj)*100).toFixed(1) + '% toward center)');
      console.log('    shoulder spread adj=' + shAdj.toFixed(3) +
        ' (moving shoulders ' + ((1-shAdj)*100).toFixed(1) + '% toward center)');
    }

    // ── CROSS-BODY CONTACT DETECTION ──
    // Scan all frames for joints that come close together across different limbs.
    // This detects hand-to-elbow, hands-together (prayer), hand-to-knee, etc.
    {
      const contactPairs = [
        ['l_wrist', 'r_elbow', 'L wrist→R elbow'],
        ['r_wrist', 'l_elbow', 'R wrist→L elbow'],
        ['l_wrist', 'r_wrist', 'L wrist→R wrist'],
        ['l_wrist', 'l_knee',  'L wrist→L knee'],
        ['r_wrist', 'r_knee',  'R wrist→R knee'],
        ['l_wrist', 'r_knee',  'L wrist→R knee'],
        ['r_wrist', 'l_knee',  'R wrist→L knee'],
        ['l_elbow', 'r_elbow', 'L elbow→R elbow'],
      ];
      // Estimate body height from rest pose for threshold
      const rp = result.rest_pose;
      let bodyH = 1.0;
      if (rp.l_ankle && rp.head) {
        bodyH = Math.abs(rp.head[1] - rp.l_ankle[1]) || 1.0;
      }
      const contactThresh = bodyH * 0.12; // 12% of body height = ~20cm for 170cm character
      console.log('=== CROSS-BODY CONTACT ANALYSIS (threshold=' + contactThresh.toFixed(4) + ', bodyH=' + bodyH.toFixed(4) + ') ===');
      let anyContact = false;
      for (const [jA, jB, label] of contactPairs) {
        const dataA = result.joints[jA], dataB = result.joints[jB];
        if (!dataA || !dataB) continue;
        let minDist = Infinity, minFrame = 0;
        const nf = dataA.positions.length;
        let contactFrames = 0;
        for (let f = 0; f < nf; f++) {
          const pA = dataA.positions[f], pB = dataB.positions[f];
          const d = Math.sqrt((pA[0]-pB[0])**2 + (pA[1]-pB[1])**2 + (pA[2]-pB[2])**2);
          if (d < minDist) { minDist = d; minFrame = f; }
          if (d < contactThresh) contactFrames++;
        }
        if (minDist < contactThresh * 2) { // report if within 2x threshold
          const pct = (contactFrames / nf * 100).toFixed(0);
          const contact = minDist < contactThresh;
          console.log('  ' + (contact ? 'CONTACT' : 'near') + ' ' + label +
            ': minDist=' + minDist.toFixed(4) + ' at frame ' + minFrame +
            '/' + nf + ' (' + pct + '% of frames in contact)');
          if (contact) anyContact = true;
        }
      }
      if (!anyContact) console.log('  No cross-body contacts detected');
    }

    // Smooth ALL joint position data at load time (input-side smoothing).
    // This is safe because it doesn't break joint-boundary agreement — it just
    // cleans up micro-noise in the source data before any transforms are computed.
    // Arms already get 8-pass smoothArray on rotation data (line 8188).
    // Position-based joints need position smoothing instead.
    {
      const SMOOTH_PASSES = 8;
      let smoothCount = 0;
      for (const jn of Object.keys(result.joints)) {
        const jd = result.joints[jn];
        if (!jd || !jd.positions || jd.positions.length < 4) continue;
        const n = jd.positions.length;
        for (let axis = 0; axis < 3; axis++) {
          const vals = new Array(n);
          for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) vals[i] = jd.positions[i][axis];
          const smoothed = smoothArray(vals, SMOOTH_PASSES);
          for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) jd.positions[i][axis] = smoothed[i];
        }
        smoothCount++;
      }
      // Also smooth root positions (pelvis translation — base of everything)
      if (result.root_positions && result.root_positions.length > 3) {
        const n = result.root_positions.length;
        for (let axis = 0; axis < 3; axis++) {
          const vals = new Array(n);
          for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) vals[i] = result.root_positions[i][axis];
          const smoothed = smoothArray(vals, SMOOTH_PASSES);
          for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) result.root_positions[i][axis] = smoothed[i];
        }
      }
      console.log('[JOINT SMOOTH] Smoothed ' + smoothCount + ' joints + root positions, ' + SMOOTH_PASSES + ' passes each');

      // Extra smoothing for toe joints — short bones amplify position noise into
      // large angular swings. Additional 16 passes on toe positions only.
      const TOE_EXTRA_PASSES = 16;
      for (const toeName of ['l_toe', 'r_toe']) {
        const jd = result.joints[toeName];
        if (!jd || !jd.positions || jd.positions.length < 4) continue;
        const n = jd.positions.length;
        for (let axis = 0; axis < 3; axis++) {
          const vals = new Array(n);
          for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) vals[i] = jd.positions[i][axis];
          const smoothed = smoothArray(vals, TOE_EXTRA_PASSES);
          for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) jd.positions[i][axis] = smoothed[i];
        }
      }
      console.log('[JOINT SMOOTH] Extra ' + TOE_EXTRA_PASSES + ' passes on toe positions');
    }

    // Reset diagnostics
    window._hipSmoothDiag = false;
    window._hipAnchorDiag = false;
    window._stretchDiagDone = false;

    // Apply to app
    jointPosData = result;
    overridePFromFBX(true);

    // CRITICAL FIX: Recompute proportion ratios AFTER overridePFromFBX.
    // Before override, P contains cage surface anchor positions (wider than skeleton).
    // After override, P contains FBX internal skeleton positions (same as animation data).
    // The ratios computed before override (lines above) compare surface vs internal positions,
    // producing bogus mismatches (e.g. 30% shoulder, 17% hip) even when the mesh is identical.
    // After override, P and rp are in the same coordinate system, so ratios should be ~1.0.
    {
      const rp = result.rest_pose;
      // Leg ratio
      if (P.l_hip && P.l_knee && P.l_ankle && rp.l_hip && rp.l_knee && rp.l_ankle) {
        const cL = vlen(vsub(P.l_knee, P.l_hip)) + vlen(vsub(P.l_ankle, P.l_knee));
        const fL = vlen(vsub(rp.l_knee, rp.l_hip)) + vlen(vsub(rp.l_ankle, rp.l_knee));
        if (fL > 1e-6) fbxLegPropRatio = cL / fL;
      }
      // Arm ratio
      if (P.l_shoulder && P.l_elbow && P.l_wrist && rp.l_shoulder && rp.l_elbow && rp.l_wrist) {
        const cA = vlen(vsub(P.l_elbow, P.l_shoulder)) + vlen(vsub(P.l_wrist, P.l_elbow));
        const fA = vlen(vsub(rp.l_elbow, rp.l_shoulder)) + vlen(vsub(rp.l_wrist, rp.l_elbow));
        if (fA > 0.01 && cA > 0.01) fbxArmPropRatio = cA / fA;
      }
      // Shoulder spread
      if (P.l_shoulder && P.r_shoulder && rp.l_shoulder && rp.r_shoulder) {
        const fSh = vlen(vsub(rp.r_shoulder, rp.l_shoulder));
        const cSh = vlen(vsub(P.r_shoulder, P.l_shoulder));
        if (fSh > 0.01 && cSh > 0.01) fbxShoulderSpreadRatio = fSh / cSh;
      }
      // Hip spread
      if (P.l_hip && P.r_hip && rp.l_hip && rp.r_hip) {
        const fHip = vlen(vsub(rp.r_hip, rp.l_hip));
        const cHip = vlen(vsub(P.r_hip, P.l_hip));
        if (fHip > 0.01 && cHip > 0.01) fbxHipSpreadRatio = fHip / cHip;
      }
      console.log('[PROPORTION FIX] Ratios recomputed after P override: leg=' + fbxLegPropRatio.toFixed(4) +
        ' arm=' + fbxArmPropRatio.toFixed(4) +
        ' shoulder=' + fbxShoulderSpreadRatio.toFixed(4) +
        ' hip=' + fbxHipSpreadRatio.toFixed(4));
    }

    cycleDuration = result.duration;
    time = 0;
    window._posDiagDone = false;  // reset diagnostic for fresh output
    window._kabschDiagDone = false;
    window._armAnalyticalDiagDone = false;
    window._twistDiagCount = 0;
    window._meshVertCheckDone = false;  // re-check mesh after FBX load
    window._armCollisionDiag = false;   // re-report collision with FBX data
    window._pivotDiagDone = false;      // re-report Kabsch vs analytical shin comparison
    window._regionXformDiagDone = false; // re-report transform coverage after FBX
    window._fkDiagDone = false;         // re-report FK position diagnostic
    // Reset temporal coherence state for new animation
    for (const k of Object.keys(prevBendAxis)) prevBendAxis[k] = null;

    const mappedNames = jointNames.join(', ');
    const info = filename + ': ' + frameCount + ' frames, ' +
      jointNames.length + ' joints, ' + duration.toFixed(2) + 's (scale ' + scaleFactor.toFixed(3) + ')';
    $('fbxInfo').textContent = info;
    statusEl.textContent = 'FBX loaded — position-based driving active';
    // Structural status for auto-tuner preflight
    console.log('STRUCTURAL_STATUS: overridePFromFBX=' + (!!jointPosData) +
      ' eulerFallback=' + (!jointPosData && !!spineMotionData) +
      ' positionMode=' + (!!jointPosData));
    // Auto-enable DQS for FBX mode — LBS causes volume collapse at
    // body boundaries (crotch, shoulders) when diverging transforms blend.
    // DQS preserves volume by averaging quaternions instead of matrices.
    $('useDQS').checked = true;
    console.log('FBX extracted:', info);
    console.log('Mapped joints:', mappedNames);

    // Reset multi-frame diagnostic accumulator
    _mfDiag = {
      frameCount: 0, targetFrames: 360, done: false,
      worstStretch: 0, worstStretchFrame: 0, worstStretchEdge: -1,
      totalAvgDev: 0, worstAvgDev: 0,
      worstJitter: 0, worstJitterVert: -1, worstJitterFrame: 0, totalAvgJitter: 0,
      prevPos: null,
      lFootMinY: Infinity, rFootMinY: Infinity,
      lFootMaxMinY: -Infinity, rFootMaxMinY: -Infinity,
      lFootTotalMinY: 0, rFootTotalMinY: 0
    };
    // Reset temporal smoothing state for new animation
    prevSmoothedTransforms = null;
    prevMeshPos = null;
    _jitterVel = null;
    _prevShinDirL = null; _prevShinDirR = null;
    _prevFootDirL = null; _prevFootDirR = null;
    _smoothedShinDirL = null; _smoothedShinDirR = null;
    _prevAnkleBendAxisL = null; _prevAnkleBendAxisR = null;
    _prevAnkleAngleL = 0; _prevAnkleAngleR = 0;
    _prevAnkleAxisCageL = null; _prevAnkleAxisCageR = null;
    // Reset HUD worst-frame tracking and twist tracking
    _hudWorstFrames = {};
    retargetedTwists = {};
    fbxFrameTwists = {};
    // Keep FBX skinned mesh alive for live playback in viewport
    if (fbxLiveGroup) { scene.remove(fbxLiveGroup); fbxLiveGroup = null; }
    fbxLiveMixer = null; fbxLiveAction = null;
    // Scale and position the FBX group to match our mesh coordinate system
    // FBX is in its native units; scaleFactor converts to mesh space.
    // X-mirror: Mixamo left=+X, our mesh left=-X
    group.scale.set(-scaleFactor, scaleFactor, scaleFactor);
    // Offset so FBX hips align with mesh pelvis rest position
    if (P.pelvis) {
      const hBind = hipsBindPos; // hips position in FBX native units
      group.position.set(
        P.pelvis[0] + hBind.x * scaleFactor,
        P.pelvis[1] - hBind.y * scaleFactor,
        P.pelvis[2] - hBind.z * scaleFactor
      );
    }
    // Make all FBX meshes wireframe + semi-transparent so they overlay cleanly
    group.traverse(obj => {
      if (obj.isMesh) {
        obj.material = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
          color: 0x00ff88, wireframe: true, transparent: true, opacity: 0.35
        });
        obj.frustumCulled = false;
      }
    });
    group.visible = false; // hidden by default, toggled by checkbox
    scene.add(group);
    fbxLiveGroup = group;
    // Create a fresh mixer for live playback (the extraction mixer is spent)
    fbxLiveMixer = new THREE.AnimationMixer(group);
    fbxLiveAction = fbxLiveMixer.clipAction(clip);
    fbxLiveAction.play();
    console.log('[FBX LIVE] Skinned mesh added to scene (hidden). Toggle with "Show FBX ghost mesh" checkbox.');

    // Reset one-shot diagnostics so they fire again for new FBX
    window._strainDiagDone = false;
    window._seamGapDiagDone = false;
    window._rShoulderDiag = false;
    window._boneCompDiagDone = false;
    window._innerThighDiagDone = false;
    window._postCollapseDiagDone = false;
    window._groundDiagDone = false;
    window._ankleDiagDone = false;
    window._surfaceHealthDiagDone = false;
    window._armReachDiagDone = false;
  }

  // Drag-drop handlers
  const dropOverlay = $('dropOverlay');
  let dragCounter = 0;
  document.addEventListener('dragenter', e => {
    e.preventDefault();
    dragCounter++;
    dropOverlay.classList.add('active');
  });
  document.addEventListener('dragover', e => { e.preventDefault(); });
  document.addEventListener('dragleave', e => {
    dragCounter--;
    if (dragCounter <= 0) { dragCounter = 0; dropOverlay.classList.remove('active'); }
  });
  document.addEventListener('drop', e => {
    e.preventDefault();
    dragCounter = 0;
    dropOverlay.classList.remove('active');
    const file = e.dataTransfer.files[0];
    if (file && file.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.fbx')) {
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = () => extractPositionsFromFBX(reader.result, file.name);
      reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
    }
  });

  // File picker handler
  $('fbxFile').addEventListener('change', e => {
    const file = e.target.files[0];
    if (file) {
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = () => extractPositionsFromFBX(reader.result, file.name);
      reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
    }
  });

  </script>
</body>
</html>
